  globalIgnores(['dist']),
  {
    files: ['**/*.{js,jsx}'],
    extends: [
      js.configs.recommended,
      reactHooks.configs.flat.recommended,
      reactRefresh.configs.vite,
    ],
    languageOptions: {
      ecmaVersion: 2020,
      globals: globals.browser,
      parserOptions: {
        ecmaVersion: 'latest',
        ecmaFeatures: { jsx: true },
        sourceType: 'module',
      },
    },
    rules: {
      'no-unused-vars': ['error', { varsIgnorePattern: '^[A-Z_]' }],

  },
])

    pkgs.nodejs_20

    # Estensioni di VS Code da installare (opzionale)
    extensions = [
      # "svelte.svelte-vscode"
      # "vue.volar"
    ];
    
    # Configurazione Anteprima

      enable = true;
      previews = {
        web = {
          # Comando per avviare Next.js
          command = [
            "npm"
            "run"
            "dev"
            "--"
            "--port"
            "$PORT"
            "--hostname"
            "0.0.0.0"
          ];
          manager = "web";
          env = {
            # Variabili d'ambiente opzionali per l'anteprima

          };

      };
    };
  };

# 🇰🇷 Annyeong Academy - Complete Platform

## 🚀 Project Status: COMPLETE
All functionalities specified in the requirements have been implemented.

### 🌟 Key Features
1.  **Public Funnel**:
    *   High-conversion Landing Page (Hero, Countdown, Social Proof).
    *   GDPR-compliant Webinar Registration.
    *   Dynamic Blog & FAQ sections.
2.  **Learning Management System (LMS)**:
    *   **Course Player**: Video/Audio support, PDF downloads, Lesson Discussions.
    *   **Interactive Elements**: Vocabulary Matching Game, Quizzes (Text/Voice/Multi-choice), Homework Upload.
    *   **Gamification**: XP, Streaks, Levels, Leaderboard.
    *   **Smart Logic**: Prerequisite locking, Drip content (Release dates).
3.  **E-Commerce**:
    *   Shopping Cart with Coupon support.
    *   Upsells (One-click add to cart).
    *   Subscription System (Premium Membership).
    *   Product Wishlist.
4.  **Community & Interaction**:
    *   Real-time Messaging (Student <-> Tutor).
    *   Tutor Profiles with Social Links.
    *   Event Calendar & Booking (Seat limits).
5.  **Admin Dashboard**:
    *   Comprehensive management for Courses, Lessons, Tutors, Blog, Shop, Events.
    *   **Grading System**: View & grade student homework.
    *   **Analytics**: Sales trends, Student progress.
    *   **CMS**: Edit homepage text without code.
    *   **CRM**: Manage leads and messages (CSV Export).

### 🛠️ Tech Stack
*   **Frontend**: Next.js 13+ (App Router), Tailwind CSS, Lucide React.
*   **Backend**: Supabase (PostgreSQL, Auth, Storage, Realtime).
*   **Security**: Row Level Security (RLS) policies applied to all tables.

### 🏃 How to Run
1.  Ensure `.env.local` is configured with your Supabase credentials.
2.  Run `npm install`.
3.  Run `npm run dev`.
4.  Visit `http://localhost:3000`.

### 📦 Database Setup
All SQL migrations have been generated in the `supabase/` folder. Ensure you have run them in this order:
1.  `db_schema.sql` (Initial)
2.  `full_platform_db.sql` (Blog, Placement)
3.  `interaction_schema.sql` (Messaging)
4.  `gamification_setup.sql` (XP Logic)
5.  `ecommerce_advanced.sql` (Subscriptions)
6.  `learning_interactions.sql` (Homework, Discussions)
7.  `system_schema.sql` (Settings, CMS)
8.  `security_policies.sql` (RLS)
9.  `final_integrations.sql` (Storage, Tutors)

*Annyeong Academy Platform Ready for Launch!* 🚀

# 🇰🇷 Annyeong Akadémia - Web Platform & LMS

## 📋 Project Background & Context
**Annyeong Akadémia** is a Korean language school tailored for the Hungarian market. The core value proposition is teaching Korean **without requiring English knowledge**, using a direct Korean-to-Hungarian method.

**⚠️ Development Context:**
This project was developed as an **emergency pivot** to meet the strict deadline of **December 6th** for the first live webinar. Due to previous delays with other solutions, this platform was built from scratch using a **Modern Tech Stack (React + Supabase)** to ensure speed, stability, and full control over specific custom features that standard CMS plugins could not easily handle.

---

## 🎯 Part 1: Webinar Landing Page (Priority: Immediate)
**Objective:** Capture sign-ups for the "Korean Open Day" free webinar.

### ✅ Business Specifications Implemented
*   **Event Dates:** Users can select between two specific appointments:
    *   📅 **December 6th (Friday)**
    *   📅 **December 7th (Saturday)**
*   **GDPR Compliant Registration:**
    *   Replaced Google Forms with a custom, professional form.
    *   **Data Collected:** Name, Email, Date Preference.
    *   **Consent:** Mandatory checkbox to accept Privacy Policy before submission.
*   **Email Automation Ready:** The system records leads into the `webinar_leads` database table, which can trigger webhook automations (e.g., via Resend or MailerLite) to send the Zoom link confirmation.
*   **Content Anatomy (EduBlink Style):**
    *   **Hero:** "Learn Korean without English" USP with social proof (+500 students).
    *   **Benefits:** Bento-grid layout highlighting Native Teachers and Cultural Experience.
    *   **Teachers:** Profiles for Kim Seon-saeng-nim (Beginner Specialist) and Lee Seon-saeng-nim.
    *   **Agenda:** Timeline (18:00 Intro -> 18:15 Mini-Lesson -> 18:45 Q&A).
    *   **Offer:** "0 Ft" vs "15.000 Ft" value comparison.
    *   **Footer:** Links to Privacy Policy, Terms, and Contact.

---

## 🎯 Part 2: Main LMS Platform (Deadline: Dec 6)
**Objective:** Enable course enrollment, content delivery, and digital product sales immediately following the webinar.

### ✅ Core Functions Implemented

#### 1. Course Management
*   **Regular Group Courses:**
    *   Support for fixed-schedule, curriculum-based courses (e.g., "Coreano Base 1").
    *   **Membership System:** Content is locked. Only users who purchase the course can access the Player.
*   **Standalone Live Lessons (Drop-in):**
    *   Booking system for single sessions (e.g., "Learn with K-Pop").
    *   **Logic:** These do not build on each other; schedules vary weekly.
    *   **Delivery:** Upon purchase, the "Buy" button transforms into a **"Join Zoom"** button.

#### 2. Digital Store (E-Commerce)
*   **Products:** Dedicated section for purchasing **Ebooks** and **Planners**.
*   **Delivery:** "My Downloads" area where students can download PDFs immediately after purchase.

#### 3. Content Delivery (The Player)
*   **Video/Audio:** Supports streaming of pre-recorded lessons.
*   **File Support:** Teachers can upload **PDFs**, **Audio (MP3)**, and **PPT (Presentations)** directly to specific lessons.
*   **Quizlet Integration:** Specific functionality to add external Quizlet Flashcard buttons to any lesson.

#### 4. Institutional Pages
*   **About Us:** Mission statement and team overview.
*   **Our Tutors:** Detailed profiles.
*   **Contact Us:** Functional form saving messages to the database (`contact_messages`).
*   **Legal:** GDPR and Terms & Conditions pages.

---

## 🛠️ Tech Stack

*   **Frontend:** React 18 (Vite)
*   **Styling:** Tailwind CSS (Custom "Brand Purple/Blue" gradient theme)
*   **Routing:** React Router DOM v6 (Protected Routes for Dashboard/Admin)
*   **Backend-as-a-Service:** Supabase
    *   **Database:** PostgreSQL.
    *   **Auth:** Secure Email/Password authentication.
    *   **Storage:** Hosting for Course PDFs, Audio files, and PPTs.
*   **Payments:** Logic ready for Stripe Integration (currently in Simulation Mode).
---

## 🚀 Setup & Installation

### 1. Database Configuration
This project uses **Supabase**.
1. Create a new Supabase project.
2. Go to the SQL Editor in your dashboard.
3. Run the contents of these files in order:
   - `final_db_setup.sql` (Creates Tables & Security Policies)
   - `storage_setup.sql` (Enables File Uploads)
   - `seed_data.sql` (Adds Demo Courses & Products)

### 2. Environment Variables
1. Rename `.env.local.example` (if it exists) to `.env.local`.
2. Fill in your keys from Supabase -> Project Settings -> API:
    ```
    VITE_SUPABASE_URL=https://your-project.supabase.co
    VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY=your-anon-key
    ```

### 3. Run Locally
```bash
npm install
npm run dev
```

### 4. Admin Access
To access the Admin Dashboard at `/admin`:
1. Sign up as a new user in the app.
2. Go to Supabase Dashboard -> Table Editor -> `profiles`.
3. Find your user row and change the `role` column from `student` to `admin`.
4. Refresh the page.

"use client";
import { useState } from 'react'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { 
  LayoutList, Loader2, Users, GraduationCap, DollarSign, Database, Tag,
  Megaphone, PenTool, Brain, Calendar, ShoppingBag, LayoutTemplate, BarChart2, UserCheck, HelpCircle, MessageSquare, Gift, Settings, FileText, MessageCircle
} from 'lucide-react'

import CourseManager from '@/components/admin/CourseManager'
import EventManager from '@/components/admin/EventManager'
import ShopManager from '@/components/admin/ShopManager'
import BlogManager from '@/components/admin/BlogManager'
import StudentManager from '@/components/admin/StudentManager'
import LeadsManager from '@/components/admin/LeadsManager'
import AnnouncementManager from '@/components/admin/AnnouncementManager'
import CMSManager from '@/components/admin/CMSManager'
import AnalyticsManager from '@/components/admin/AnalyticsManager'
import TutorManager from '@/components/admin/TutorManager'
import FAQManager from '@/components/admin/FAQManager'
import ReviewManager from '@/components/admin/ReviewManager'
import TestManager from '@/components/admin/TestManager'
import GiveawayManager from '@/components/admin/GiveawayManager'
import SettingsManager from '@/components/admin/SettingsManager'
import OrderManager from '@/components/admin/OrderManager'
import HomeworkManager from '@/components/admin/HomeworkManager'
import FeedbackViewer from '@/components/admin/FeedbackViewer'
import SeedManager from '@/components/admin/SeedManager'
import CouponManager from '@/components/admin/CouponManager'
import CommentManager from '@/components/admin/CommentManager'

export default function AdminDashboard() {
  const { isAdmin, loading: authLoading } = useAuth()
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('analytics')
  
  if (authLoading || !isAdmin) return <div className="flex h-screen items-center justify-center"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-brand-purple"/></div>

  const tabs = [
    { id: 'analytics', icon: BarChart2, label: 'Overview' },
    { id: 'orders', icon: DollarSign, label: 'Sales' },
    { id: 'coupons', icon: Tag, label: 'Coupons' },
    { id: 'students', icon: GraduationCap, label: 'Students' },
    { id: 'lessons', icon: LayoutList, label: 'Courses' },
    { id: 'grading', icon: FileText, label: 'Homework' },
    { id: 'events', icon: Calendar, label: 'Events' },
    { id: 'tutors', icon: UserCheck, label: 'Tutors' },
    { id: 'shop', icon: ShoppingBag, label: 'Shop' },
    { id: 'blog', icon: PenTool, label: 'Blog' },
    { id: 'comments', icon: MessageCircle, label: 'Comments' }, // NEW
    { id: 'reviews', icon: MessageSquare, label: 'Reviews' },
    { id: 'feedback', icon: MessageSquare, label: 'Feedback' },
    { id: 'giveaway', icon: Gift, label: 'Giveaway' },
    { id: 'tests', icon: Brain, label: 'Tests' },
    { id: 'cms', icon: LayoutTemplate, label: 'CMS' },
    { id: 'faq', icon: HelpCircle, label: 'FAQ' },
    { id: 'leads', icon: Users, label: 'CRM' },
    { id: 'announce', icon: Megaphone, label: 'Alerts' },
    { id: 'settings', icon: Settings, label: 'Settings' },
    { id: 'db', icon: Database, label: 'DB Tools' },
  ]

  return (
    <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto pb-20">
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-6">Admin Dashboard</h1>

      <div className="flex gap-2 mb-8 border-b border-gray-200 overflow-x-auto pb-1 scrollbar-hide">
        {tabs.map(tab => (
          <button key={tab.id} onClick={() => setActiveTab(tab.id)} className={`flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 font-bold rounded-t-lg transition whitespace-nowrap ${activeTab === tab.id ? 'bg-brand-purple text-white' : 'text-gray-500 hover:bg-gray-100'}`}>
            <tab.icon size={18}/> {tab.label}
          </button>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="animate-fade-in">
        {activeTab === 'analytics' && <AnalyticsManager />}
        {activeTab === 'orders' && <OrderManager />}
        {activeTab === 'coupons' && <CouponManager />}
        {activeTab === 'grading' && <HomeworkManager />}
        {activeTab === 'db' && <SeedManager />}
        {activeTab === 'feedback' && <FeedbackViewer />}
        {activeTab === 'lessons' && <CourseManager />}
        {activeTab === 'reviews' && <ReviewManager />}
        {activeTab === 'comments' && <CommentManager />}
        {activeTab === 'tutors' && <TutorManager />}
        {activeTab === 'events' && <EventManager />}
        {activeTab === 'cms' && <CMSManager />}
        {activeTab === 'tests' && <TestManager />}
        {activeTab === 'giveaway' && <GiveawayManager />}
        {activeTab === 'faq' && <FAQManager />}
        {activeTab === 'shop' && <ShopManager />}
        {activeTab === 'blog' && <BlogManager />}
        {activeTab === 'students' && <StudentManager />}
        {activeTab === 'leads' && <LeadsManager />}
        {activeTab === 'announce' && <AnnouncementManager />}
        {activeTab === 'settings' && <SettingsManager />}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

PATH: ./src/app/(app)/corsi/[id]/page.jsx

"use client";
import RelatedProducts from '@/components/course/RelatedProducts'
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { useParams } from 'next/navigation'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { useCart } from '@/context/CartContext'
import { Loader2, FileText, Clock, BarChart, AlertTriangle, Lock, Globe } from 'lucide-react'
import CoursePlayer from '@/components/course/CoursePlayer' 
import { cleanDescription } from '@/lib/helpers'
import Link from 'next/link'
import SaleCountdown from '@/components/ui/SaleCountdown'

export default function CoursePage() {
  const { id } = useParams()
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const { addToCart } = useCart()
  
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [hasAccess, setHasAccess] = useState(false)
  const [course, setCourse] = useState(null)
  const [lessons, setLessons] = useState([])
  const [userLevel, setUserLevel] = useState(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    if(course) document.title = `${course.title} | Annyeong Academy`
  }, [course])

  useEffect(() => {
    const checkAccess = async () => {
      try {
        const { data: product } = await supabase.from('products').select('*, tutors(*)').eq('id', id).single()
        setCourse(product)

        const { data: lessonData } = await supabase.from('lessons').select('*').eq('course_id', id).order('created_at', { ascending: true })
        setLessons(lessonData || [])

        if (user) {
          const { data: purchase } = await supabase.from('purchases').select('*').eq('user_id', user.id).eq('product_id', id).eq('status', 'completed').single()
          const { data: sub } = await supabase.from('subscriptions').select('*').eq('user_id', user.id).eq('status', 'active').single()
          
          if (purchase || sub) setHasAccess(true)

          const { data: placement } = await supabase.from('placement_results').select('recommended_level').eq('user_id', user.id).order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(1).single()
          if (placement) setUserLevel(placement.recommended_level)
        }
      } catch (err) { console.error(err) } finally { setLoading(false) }
    }
    if (id) checkAccess()
  }, [user, id])

  if (loading) return <div className="flex h-screen items-center justify-center"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-brand-purple" size={40} /></div>
  if (!course) return <div className="text-center py-20">Course not found</div>

  if (hasAccess) return <CoursePlayer course={course} lessons={lessons} user={user} />

  const seatsLeft = (course.total_seats || 999) - (course.booked_seats || 0)
  const isFull = seatsLeft <= 0
  const isPrereqLocked = course.level === 'Advanced' && (!userLevel || userLevel !== 'Advanced')
  const isOnSale = course.sale_price && new Date(course.sale_ends_at) > new Date()

  return (
    <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto pb-20 pt-10 px-4">
      <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-8 shadow-sm border border-gray-100 flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-8 items-start mb-8 animate-fade-in">
        <div className="w-full md:w-1/3">
          <div className="aspect-square bg-gray-200 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden relative shadow-md mb-4">
            <img src={course.image_url || 'https://placehold.co/400'} alt={course.title} className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
            {isFull && <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/60 flex items-center justify-center text-white font-bold text-xl uppercase tracking-widest">Sold Out</div>}
            {isOnSale && <SaleCountdown endDate={course.sale_ends_at} />}
          </div>
          {course.tutors && (
            <Link href={`/tutors/${course.tutors.id}`} className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-xl hover:bg-gray-100 transition border border-gray-200">
              <img src={course.tutors.image_url} className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full object-cover"/>
              <div>
                <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 font-bold uppercase">Teached By</p>
                <p className="text-sm font-bold text-brand-dark">{course.tutors.name}</p>
              </div>
            </Link>
          )}
        </div>

        <div className="flex-1">
          <div className="flex gap-2 mb-4">
             <span className="bg-purple-100 text-brand-purple text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-full uppercase tracking-wider">Course</span>
             {course.level && <span className="bg-blue-100 text-brand-blue text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-full uppercase tracking-wider">{course.level}</span>}
             {course.instruction_language && <span className="bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-full flex items-center gap-1"><Globe size={12}/> {course.instruction_language}</span>}
          </div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl md:text-4xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-4">{course.title}</h1>
          <p className="text-gray-600 mb-6 text-lg leading-relaxed">{cleanDescription(course.description)}</p>
          
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-6 mb-8 text-sm font-medium text-gray-500">
            <span className="flex items-center gap-2"><Clock size={18}/> {lessons.length * 15} Mins</span>
            <span className="flex items-center gap-2"><FileText size={18}/> {lessons.length} Lessons</span>
            <span className="flex items-center gap-2"><BarChart size={18}/> {course.level || 'General'}</span>
          </div>

          {isPrereqLocked && (
            <div className="bg-red-50 border-l-4 border-red-500 p-4 mb-6 rounded-r-lg">
              <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                <Lock className="text-red-500 flex-shrink-0" size={20}/>
                <div>
                  <p className="font-bold text-red-800 text-sm">Prerequisite Missing</p>
                  <p className="text-red-700 text-sm">You need <strong>Advanced</strong> proficiency. Your level: <strong>{userLevel || 'Unknown'}</strong>.</p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
            <div className="flex flex-col">
               {isOnSale && <span className="text-sm text-gray-400 line-through">{course.price}</span>}
               <span className={`text-3xl font-bold ${isOnSale ? 'text-red-500' : 'text-brand-dark'}`}>{isOnSale ? course.sale_price : course.price}</span>
            </div>
            <button 
              onClick={() => addToCart({ ...course, price: isOnSale ? course.sale_price : course.price })}
              disabled={isFull || isPrereqLocked}
              className="btn btn-plum-gradient px-8 py-3 rounded-xl shadow-lg flex-1 md:flex-none text-center disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
            >
              {isFull ? "Course Full" : (isPrereqLocked ? "Locked" : "Enroll Now")}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-200 overflow-hidden">
        <div className="p-6 border-b border-gray-100 bg-gray-50/50"><h2 className="font-bold text-xl text-gray-800">Curriculum Preview</h2></div>
        {lessons.map((lesson, idx) => (
          <div key={lesson.id} className="p-4 border-b border-gray-100 last:border-0 flex items-center justify-between hover:bg-gray-50 transition">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
              <div className="w-8 h-8 bg-gray-100 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-gray-400 font-bold text-sm">{idx + 1}</div>
              <div><p className="font-bold text-gray-800">{lesson.title}</p></div>
            </div>
            {lesson.type === 'video' && <span className="text-xs bg-gray-200 text-gray-600 px-2 py-1 rounded">Video</span>}
          </div>
        ))}
        {lessons.length === 0 && <div className="p-8 text-center text-gray-400">No lessons yet.</div>}
      </div>
      <RelatedProducts currentCourseId={course.id} />
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/app/(app)/corsi/page.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { usePayment } from '@/context/PaymentContext'
import { useLanguage } from '@/context/LanguageContext'
import { Video, Loader2, PlayCircle, ShoppingCart, Search, X, Star, Filter, User, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { cleanDescription } from '@/lib/helpers'
import WishlistButton from '@/components/ui/WishlistButton'
import { CourseCardSkeleton } from '@/components/ui/Skeletons'

export default function Courses() {
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const { t } = useLanguage()
  const { initiateCheckout } = usePayment()
  const searchParams = useSearchParams()
  
  const [courses, setCourses] = useState([])
  const [tutors, setTutors] = useState([])
  const [purchasedIds, setPurchasedIds] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  
  // Filters & Pagination
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState('')
  const [ownershipFilter, setOwnershipFilter] = useState('all')
  const [levelFilter, setLevelFilter] = useState('all')
  const [tutorFilter, setTutorFilter] = useState('all')
  const [sortOrder, setSortOrder] = useState('popular')
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1)
  const ITEMS_PER_PAGE = 6

  useEffect(() => {
    const levelParam = searchParams.get('level')
    if (levelParam) setLevelFilter(levelParam)
  }, [searchParams])

  useEffect(() => { fetchData() }, [user])

  const fetchData = async () => {
    try {
      const { data: prods } = await supabase.from('products').select('*, tutors(id, name)').eq('type', 'course')
      setCourses(prods || [])
      
      const { data: tuts } = await supabase.from('tutors').select('id, name')
      setTutors(tuts || [])

      if (user) {
        const { data: purchases } = await supabase.from('purchases').select('product_id').eq('user_id', user.id).eq('status', 'completed')
        if (purchases) setPurchasedIds(purchases.map(p => p.product_id))
      }
    } catch (error) { console.error(error) } finally { setLoading(false) }
  }

  const getPriceVal = (p) => parseFloat(p.replace(/[^0-9.]/g, '') || 0)

  // Client-side filtering because DB is small for demo. 
  const filteredCourses = courses
    .filter(course => {
      const matchesSearch = course.title.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase())
      const isOwned = purchasedIds.includes(course.id)
      
      const matchOwn = ownershipFilter === 'all' 
        || (ownershipFilter === 'owned' && isOwned) 
        || (ownershipFilter === 'not_owned' && !isOwned)
      
      const matchLevel = levelFilter === 'all' || course.level?.toLowerCase() === levelFilter.toLowerCase()
      const matchTutor = tutorFilter === 'all' || course.tutor_id === tutorFilter

      return matchesSearch && matchOwn && matchLevel && matchTutor
    })
    .sort((a, b) => {
      if (sortOrder === 'price_asc') return getPriceVal(a.price) - getPriceVal(b.price)
      if (sortOrder === 'price_desc') return getPriceVal(b.price) - getPriceVal(a.price)
      if (sortOrder === 'newest') return new Date(b.created_at || 0) - new Date(a.created_at || 0)
      return 0 
    })

  const totalPages = Math.ceil(filteredCourses.length / ITEMS_PER_PAGE)
  const displayedCourses = filteredCourses.slice((page - 1) * ITEMS_PER_PAGE, page * ITEMS_PER_PAGE)

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-end md:items-center mb-8 gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">{t('course_catalog')}</h1>
          <p className="text-gray-500">{t('course_desc')}</p>
        </div>
        <div className="relative w-full md:w-64">
          <Search className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 transform -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400" size={18} />
          <input 
            type="text" placeholder={t('course_search')}
            className="w-full pl-10 pr-10 py-2 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl outline-none focus:border-brand-purple transition"
            value={searchTerm} onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)}
          />
          {searchTerm && <button onClick={() => setSearchTerm('')} className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 transform -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600"><X size={14}/></button>}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Advanced Filters Bar */}
      <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-xl border border-gray-100 shadow-sm mb-8 flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-4 overflow-x-auto">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-max">
          <Filter size={16} className="text-brand-purple"/> <span className="font-bold text-sm text-gray-700">Filters:</span>
        </div>
        
        <select className="filter-select" value={ownershipFilter} onChange={e => setOwnershipFilter(e.target.value)}>
          <option value="all">All Ownership</option>
          <option value="owned">My Courses</option>
          <option value="not_owned">Available to Buy</option>
        </select>

        <select className="filter-select" value={levelFilter} onChange={e => setLevelFilter(e.target.value)}>
          <option value="all">All Levels</option>
          <option value="Beginner">Beginner</option>
          <option value="Intermediate">Intermediate</option>
          <option value="Advanced">Advanced</option>
        </select>

        <select className="filter-select" value={tutorFilter} onChange={e => setTutorFilter(e.target.value)}>
          <option value="all">All Tutors</option>
          {tutors.map(t => <option key={t.id} value={t.id}>{t.name}</option>)}
        </select>

        {/* Sorting */}
        <select className="filter-select md:ml-auto" value={sortOrder} onChange={e => setSortOrder(e.target.value)}>
          <option value="popular">Most Popular</option>
          <option value="newest">Newest</option>
          <option value="price_asc">Price: Low to High</option>
          <option value="price_desc">Price: High to Low</option>
        </select>
      </div>

      {loading ? (
        <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
          {[1,2,3,4,5,6].map(i => <CourseCardSkeleton key={i} />)}
        </div>
      ) : filteredCourses.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="text-center py-12 bg-white rounded-xl border border-dashed border-gray-300">
          <p className="text-gray-500 font-medium">{t('course_no_results')}</p>
          <button onClick={() => {setSearchTerm(''); setLevelFilter('all'); setOwnershipFilter('all'); setTutorFilter('all')}} className="text-brand-purple font-bold mt-2 hover:underline">Clear Filters</button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <>
          <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
            {displayedCourses.map(course => {
              const isOwned = purchasedIds.includes(course.id)
              return (
                <div key={course.id} className={`bg-white rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border transition group flex flex-col relative ${isOwned ? 'border-green-200 shadow-sm' : 'border-gray-100 hover:shadow-md'}`}>
                  <div className="h-48 overflow-hidden relative bg-gray-100">
                    <img src={course.image_url || 'https://placehold.co/600x400/6a11cb/ffffff?text=Course'} alt={course.title} className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-105 transition duration-500" />
                    <div className="absolute top-4 right-4 bg-white/90 backdrop-blur px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold text-brand-dark flex items-center gap-1">
                      <Video size={12} className="text-blue-500" /> {course.level || 'All Levels'}
                    </div>
                    <div className="absolute top-4 left-4">
                      <WishlistButton productId={course.id} />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  
                  <div className="p-6 flex-1 flex flex-col">
                    <div className="mb-2">
                      <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-brand-dark line-clamp-1">{course.title}</h3>
                      {course.tutors && <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 flex items-center gap-1 mt-1"><User size={12}/> {course.tutors.name}</p>}
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mb-4 line-clamp-2 flex-1">{cleanDescription(course.description)}</p>
                    <div className="mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-gray-50 flex items-center justify-between">
                      {isOwned ? (
                        <Link href={`/corsi/${course.id}`} className="w-full bg-green-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold hover:bg-green-700 transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                          <PlayCircle size={16} /> {t('course_access')}
                        </Link>
                      ) : (
                        <>
                          <span className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-purple">{course.price}</span>
                          <button onClick={() => initiateCheckout(course)} className="bg-brand-dark text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold hover:bg-gray-800 transition flex items-center gap-2">
                            <ShoppingCart size={16}/> {t('course_enroll')}
                          </button>
                        </>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )
            })}
          </div>

          {/* Pagination */}
          {totalPages > 1 && (
            <div className="flex justify-center items-center gap-4 mt-12">
                <button 
                    disabled={page === 1} 
                    onClick={() => setPage(p => p - 1)} 
                    className="p-3 bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-full hover:bg-gray-50 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transition shadow-sm"
                >
                    <ChevronLeft size={20} />
                </button>
                <span className="font-bold text-gray-600 text-sm">Page {page} of {totalPages}</span>
                <button 
                    disabled={page === totalPages} 
                    onClick={() => setPage(p => p + 1)} 
                    className="p-3 bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-full hover:bg-gray-50 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transition shadow-sm"
                >
                    <ChevronRight size={20} />
                </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </>
      )}
      
      <style jsx>{`
        .filter-select {
          @apply px-4 py-2 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl outline-none bg-white text-gray-600 text-sm font-bold focus:border-brand-purple cursor-pointer;
        }
      `}</style>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/app/(app)/dashboard/page.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useEffect, useState, useRef } from 'react'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useGamification } from '@/hooks/useGamification'
import { useLanguage } from '@/context/LanguageContext'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { BookOpen, Loader2, PlayCircle, Calendar, Download, ArrowRight, Award, Zap, Flame, Clock, StickyNote, ExternalLink } from 'lucide-react'
import ReferralCard from '@/components/dashboard/ReferralCard'
import LevelUpModal from '@/components/ui/LevelUpModal'
import { extractTag } from '@/lib/helpers'

export default function Dashboard() {
  const { user, profile } = useAuth()
  const { t } = useLanguage()
  const { points, streak, level, loading: gameLoading } = useGamification(user?.id)
  
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [courses, setCourses] = useState([])
  const [upcomingEvents, setUpcomingEvents] = useState([])
  const [ebooks, setEbooks] = useState([])
  const [progressMap, setProgressMap] = useState({})
  const [lastAccessed, setLastAccessed] = useState({})
  
  // Level Up State
  const [showLevelUp, setShowLevelUp] = useState(false)
  const prevLevelRef = useRef(1)

  useEffect(() => { 
    if (user) fetchData() 
  }, [user])

  // Track Level Changes
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!gameLoading && level > 1 && level > prevLevelRef.current) {
        setShowLevelUp(true)
    }
    prevLevelRef.current = level
  }, [level, gameLoading])

  const fetchData = async () => {
    try {
      const { data: purchases } = await supabase.from('purchases').select('product_id').eq('user_id', user.id).eq('status', 'completed')
      
      if (purchases && purchases.length > 0) {
        const ids = purchases.map(p => p.product_id)
        const { data: products } = await supabase.from('products').select('*').in('id', ids)

        if (products) {
          const myCourses = products.filter(p => p.type === 'course')
          setCourses(myCourses)
          
          const live = products.filter(p => p.type === 'standalone' && new Date(p.event_date) > new Date())
          setUpcomingEvents(live.sort((a,b) => new Date(a.event_date) - new Date(b.event_date)))
          
          setEbooks(products.filter(p => p.type === 'ebook'))

          const progressData = {}
          const lastAccessData = {}
          
          const { data: userProgress } = await supabase.from('lesson_progress').select('*').eq('user_id', user.id).order('last_accessed', { ascending: false })
          const { data: allLessons } = await supabase.from('lessons').select('id, course_id, title').in('course_id', myCourses.map(c => c.id))

          myCourses.forEach(course => {
             const courseLessons = allLessons.filter(l => l.course_id === course.id)
             const total = courseLessons.length
             const completed = userProgress.filter(p => p.course_id === course.id).length
             progressData[course.id] = total > 0 ? Math.round((completed / total) * 100) : 0
             
             const last = userProgress.find(p => p.course_id === course.id)
             if (last) {
               const lTitle = courseLessons.find(cl => cl.id === last.lesson_id)?.title
               lastAccessData[course.id] = { title: lTitle, id: last.lesson_id }
             }
          })
          setProgressMap(progressData)
          setLastAccessed(lastAccessData)
        }
      }
    } catch (error) { console.error(error) } 
    finally { setLoading(false) }
  }

  if (loading || gameLoading) return <div className="flex h-[50vh] items-center justify-center"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-brand-purple" size={40} /></div>

  const displayName = profile?.full_name ? profile.full_name.split(' ')[0] : user?.email?.split('@')[0]

  return (
    <div className="pb-10 animate-fade-in">
      {showLevelUp && <LevelUpModal level={level} onClose={() => setShowLevelUp(false)} />}
      
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between mb-8 gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-1">{t('dash_hello')}, {displayName}! 👋</h1>
          <p className="text-gray-500">{t('dash_ready')}</p>
        </div>
        
        <div className="flex gap-4">
           <div className="bg-white px-4 py-2 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="bg-orange-100 p-2 rounded-lg"><Flame size={20} className="text-orange-500 fill-orange-500"/></div>
              <div>
                 <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">{t('dash_streak')}</p>
                 <p className="font-bold text-brand-dark text-lg">{streak} <span className="text-sm font-normal text-gray-500">{t('dash_days')}</span></p>
              </div>
           </div>
           <div className="bg-white px-4 py-2 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 flex items-center gap-3">
              <div className="bg-yellow-100 p-2 rounded-lg"><Zap size={20} className="text-yellow-500 fill-yellow-500"/></div>
              <div>
                 <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 font-bold uppercase tracking-wider">{t('dash_xp')}</p>
                 <p className="font-bold text-brand-dark text-lg">{points} <span className="text-sm font-normal text-gray-500">XP</span></p>
              </div>
           </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
        <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-8">
          <section>
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
              <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-brand-dark">{t('dash_my_courses')}</h2>
              {courses.length > 0 && <Link href="/notebook" className="text-sm font-bold text-brand-purple flex items-center gap-1 hover:underline"><StickyNote size={16}/> {t('nb_title')}</Link>}
            </div>
            {courses.length > 0 ? (
              <div className="space-y-4">
                {courses.map(course => {
                  const progress = progressMap[course.id] || 0
                  const last = lastAccessed[course.id]
                  return (
                    <div key={course.id} className="bg-white p-5 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 hover:shadow-md transition group relative overflow-hidden">
                      <div className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 h-1 bg-gray-100 w-full">
                         <div className={`h-full transition-all duration-1000 ${progress === 100 ? 'bg-green-500' : 'bg-brand-purple'}`} style={{ width: `${progress}%` }}></div>
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex items-start gap-5 relative z-10">
                        <div className="w-24 h-24 bg-gray-100 rounded-xl overflow-hidden flex-shrink-0 relative shadow-inner">
                           <img src={course.image_url || 'https://placehold.co/150'} alt={course.title} className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-105 transition duration-500" />
                        </div>
                        <div className="flex-1 min-w-0 py-1 flex flex-col justify-between h-24">
                          <div>
                             <h3 className="font-bold text-brand-dark text-lg truncate">{course.title}</h3>
                             <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1">{last ? `Resume: ${last.title}` : 'Start your journey'}</p>
                          </div>
                          <div className="flex items-end justify-between">
                             <span className={`text-xs font-bold ${progress === 100 ? "text-green-600" : "text-gray-400"}`}>
                                {progress === 100 ? "Completed! 🏆" : `${progress}% Complete`}
                             </span>
                             <Link href={`/corsi/${course.id}`} className="bg-brand-dark text-white pl-3 pr-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold shadow-md hover:bg-brand-purple transition flex items-center gap-2 transform hover:-translate-y-0.5">
                                <PlayCircle size={16} /> {progress === 0 ? "Start" : "Resume"}
                             </Link>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )
                })}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-10 text-center border-2 border-dashed border-gray-200">
                <BookOpen size={32} className="mx-auto text-gray-300 mb-4" />
                <p className="text-gray-500 mb-4 font-medium">{t('dash_no_courses')}</p>
                <Link href="/corsi" className="inline-block bg-brand-purple text-white px-6 py-2.5 rounded-full font-bold text-sm hover:shadow-lg transition">
                  {t('dash_start_now')}
                </Link>
              </div>
            )}
          </section>

          <section>
             <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-4">{t('dash_files')}</h2>
             <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 flex items-center justify-between">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                   <div className="bg-blue-50 p-3 rounded-xl text-brand-blue"><Download size={24}/></div>
                   <div>
                      <p className="font-bold text-gray-800">{ebooks.length} Resources</p>
                      <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">Ebooks & Workbooks available</p>
                   </div>
                </div>
                <Link href="/downloads" className="text-sm font-bold text-brand-purple hover:underline">View All</Link>
             </div>
          </section>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-8">
           <ReferralCard />
           <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm border border-gray-100">
              <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2"><Calendar size={20} className="text-brand-purple"/> My Calendar</h3>
              {upcomingEvents.length > 0 ? (
                 <div className="space-y-4">
                    {upcomingEvents.map(evt => {
                       const zoomLink = extractTag(evt.description, 'ZOOM')
                       return (
                         <div key={evt.id} className="flex gap-3 items-start border-b border-gray-50 pb-3 last:border-0 last:pb-0">
                            <div className="bg-gray-100 rounded-lg p-2 text-center min-w-[50px]">
                               <span className="block text-[10px] font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase">{new Date(evt.event_date).toLocaleString('default', {month:'short'})}</span>
                               <span className="block text-xl font-black text-brand-dark leading-none">{new Date(evt.event_date).getDate()}</span>
                            </div>
                            <div className="flex-1">
                               <p className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-800 line-clamp-1">{evt.title}</p>
                               <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 flex items-center gap-1 mt-1"><Clock size={10}/> {new Date(evt.event_date).toLocaleTimeString([],{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'})}</p>
                               {zoomLink ? (
                                 <a href={zoomLink} target="_blank" className="text-[10px] bg-green-50 text-green-700 px-2 py-1 rounded mt-2 inline-flex items-center gap-1 font-bold hover:bg-green-100 transition">
                                   <ExternalLink size={10}/> Join Zoom
                                 </a>
                               ) : (
                                 <span className="text-[10px] bg-gray-50 text-gray-400 px-2 py-1 rounded mt-2 inline-block">Link TBA</span>
                               )}
                            </div>
                         </div>
                       )
                    })}
                 </div>
              ) : (
                 <div className="text-center py-6">
                    <p className="text-sm text-gray-400 mb-3">{t('dash_no_live')}</p>
                    <Link href="/lezioni-live" className="text-xs btn btn-plum-gradient px-4 py-2 rounded-lg">Book Class</Link>
                 </div>
              )}
           </div>
           <div className="bg-gradient-to-br from-brand-dark to-[#2a3b55] rounded-2xl p-6 text-white relative overflow-hidden shadow-lg">
              <div className="relative z-10">
                 <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                        <div className="bg-white/20 p-2 rounded-lg backdrop-blur-sm"><Award size={20}/></div>
                        <h3 className="font-bold">{t('dash_league')}</h3>
                    </div>
                    <Link href="/leaderboard" className="text-xs font-bold bg-white/10 px-2 py-1 rounded hover:bg-white/20 transition">View</Link>
                 </div>
                 <p className="text-gray-300 text-sm mb-1 font-medium">Level {level} • Silver League</p>
                 <div className="w-full bg-black/30 rounded-full h-2 mb-4 overflow-hidden border border-white/10">
                    <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-yellow-400 to-orange-500 h-2 rounded-full" style={{width: `${(points % 1000) / 10}%`}}></div>
                 </div>
              </div>
              <div className="absolute -bottom-6 -right-6 text-white/5 rotate-12"><Award size={140}/></div>
           </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/app/(app)/downloads/page.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { useLanguage } from '@/context/LanguageContext'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { Download, FileText, Loader2, ExternalLink } from 'lucide-react'
import { extractTag, cleanDescription } from '@/lib/helpers'

export default function MyDownloads() {
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const { t } = useLanguage()
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [downloads, setDownloads] = useState([])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (user) fetchDownloads()
  }, [user])

  const fetchDownloads = async () => {
    try {
      const { data: purchases } = await supabase.from('purchases').select('product_id').eq('user_id', user.id).eq('status', 'completed')
      if (purchases && purchases.length > 0) {
        const ids = purchases.map(p => p.product_id)
        const { data: products } = await supabase.from('products').select('*').in('id', ids).eq('type', 'ebook')
        setDownloads(products || [])
      }
    } catch (e) { console.error(e) } finally { setLoading(false) }
  }

  const trackAndDownload = async (url, productId) => {
    await supabase.from('downloads_log').insert([{ user_id: user.id, product_id: productId }])
    window.open(url, '_blank')
  }

  if (loading) return <div className="flex justify-center p-10"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-brand-purple" /></div>

  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">{t('dl_title')}</h1>
      <p className="text-gray-500 mb-8">{t('dl_desc')}</p>

      {downloads.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="bg-white p-12 rounded-2xl border border-dashed border-gray-300 text-center">
          <p className="text-gray-500 mb-4">{t('dl_empty')}</p>
          <a href="/store" className="text-brand-purple font-bold hover:underline">{t('nav_store')}</a>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid gap-4">
          {downloads.map(item => {
            const downloadUrl = extractTag(item.description, 'DOWNLOAD_URL');
            
            return (
              <div key={item.id} className="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 flex flex-col md:flex-row md:items-center justify-between gap-4">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                  <div className="w-14 h-14 bg-blue-50 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center text-brand-blue flex-shrink-0">
                    <FileText size={28} />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <h3 className="font-bold text-brand-dark text-lg">{item.title}</h3>
                    <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 line-clamp-1">{cleanDescription(item.description)}</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
                
                {downloadUrl ? (
                  <button 
                    onClick={() => trackAndDownload(downloadUrl, item.id)}
                    className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-brand-purple text-white px-6 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold hover:opacity-90 transition shadow-md hover:shadow-lg"
                  >
                    <Download size={18} /> {t('dl_btn')}
                  </button>
                ) : (
                  <button disabled className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-gray-100 text-gray-400 px-6 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold cursor-not-allowed">
                    <ExternalLink size={18} /> Link unavailable
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>
            )
          })}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

"use client";
import { useRouter, usePathname } from 'next/navigation'

import { useState } from 'react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { LayoutDashboard, BookOpen, Calendar, LogOut, User, ShieldCheck, ShoppingBag, Settings, Download, Menu, X } from 'lucide-react'

export default function AppLayout({ children }) {
  const { signOut, user, profile, isAdmin } = useAuth()
  const pathname = usePathname()
  const router = useRouter()
  const [mobileOpen, setMobileOpen] = useState(false)

  const handleLogout = async () => {
    await signOut()
    router.push('/login')
  }

  const NavItem = ({ href, icon: Icon, label }) => {
    const isActive = pathname === href
    return (
      <Link 
        href={href} 
        onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}
        className={`flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 rounded-xl transition-all ${
          isActive ? 'bg-brand-purple text-white shadow-md' : 'text-gray-600 hover:bg-purple-50 hover:text-brand-purple'
        }`}
      >
        <Icon size={20} />
        <span className="font-medium">{label}</span>
      </Link>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen bg-[#F8F9FC]">
      
      <div className="md:hidden fixed top-0 left-0 w-full bg-white z-30 px-4 py-3 shadow-sm flex justify-between items-center">
        <span className="font-bold text-brand-purple text-lg">Annyeong LMS</span>
        <button onClick={() => setMobileOpen(!mobileOpen)} className="text-gray-700">
           {mobileOpen ? <X size={24}/> : <Menu size={24}/>}
        </button>
      </div>

      {mobileOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-20 md:hidden" onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}></div>
      )}

      <aside className={`fixed top-0 left-0 h-full w-64 bg-white border-r border-gray-200 z-30 transform transition-transform duration-300 ease-in-out md:translate-x-0 ${mobileOpen ? 'translate-x-0' : '-translate-x-full'}`}>
        <div className="p-6 hidden md:block">
          <span className="text-xl font-bold bg-clip-text text-transparent bg-gradient-to-r from-brand-blue to-brand-purple">
            Annyeong LMS
          </span>
        </div>
        
        <div className="md:hidden p-6 border-b border-gray-100 flex items-center justify-between">
           <span className="font-bold text-brand-dark">Menu</span>
           <button onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}><X size={20}/></button>
        </div>

        <nav className="flex-1 px-4 space-y-2 mt-4 overflow-y-auto">
          <NavItem href="/dashboard" icon={LayoutDashboard} label="Dashboard" />
          <NavItem href="/corsi" icon={BookOpen} label="All Courses" />
          <NavItem href="/lezioni-live" icon={Calendar} label="Live Lessons" />
          <NavItem href="/store" icon={ShoppingBag} label="Store & Ebooks" />
          <NavItem href="/downloads" icon={Download} label="My Downloads" />
          <NavItem href="/profile" icon={Settings} label="Profile" />
          
          {isAdmin && (
            <div className="pt-4 mt-4 border-t border-gray-100">
               <p className="px-4 text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase mb-2">Admin Area</p>
               <NavItem href="/admin" icon={ShieldCheck} label="Management" />
            </div>
          )}
        </nav>

        <div className="p-4 border-t border-gray-100">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 mb-2">
            <div className="w-8 h-8 bg-gray-200 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-gray-500 overflow-hidden">
              {profile?.avatar_url ? (
                <img src={profile.avatar_url} alt="Profile" className="w-full h-full object-cover"/>
              ) : (
                <User size={16} />
              )}
            </div>
            <div className="text-sm overflow-hidden">
              <p className="font-bold text-gray-700 truncate">{profile?.full_name || 'Student'}</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 truncate">{user?.email}</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button onClick={handleLogout} className="w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-2 text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50 rounded-lg transition text-sm font-medium">
            <LogOut size={18} /> Logout
          </button>
        </div>
      </aside>

      <div className="flex-1 md:ml-64 pt-16 md:pt-0">
        <main className="p-4 md:p-8 max-w-6xl mx-auto">
          {children}
        </main>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { Loader2, Trophy, Medal } from 'lucide-react'

export default function Leaderboard() {
  const [users, setUsers] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchLeaderboard = async () => {
      // In a real app, you'd join with a 'user_stats' table.
      // For now, let's mock it or use profiles if they had XP columns.
      // Assuming we added 'xp' to user_stats table in previous steps.
      try {
        const { data } = await supabase.from('user_stats').select('*, profiles(full_name, avatar_url)').order('xp', { ascending: false }).limit(20)
        setUsers(data || [])
      } catch (e) { console.error(e) }
      setLoading(false)
    }
    fetchLeaderboard()
  }, [])

  if (loading) return <div className="flex justify-center p-20"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-brand-purple"/></div>

  return (
    <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto pb-20">
      <div className="text-center mb-10">
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2 flex items-center justify-center gap-3">
          <Trophy className="text-yellow-500" size={32} /> Hall of Fame
        </h1>
        <p className="text-gray-500">Top learners this month. Keep studying to rise up!</p>
      </div>

      <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 overflow-hidden">
        {users.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="p-10 text-center text-gray-400">No stats available yet. Start learning!</div>
        ) : (
          users.map((user, idx) => (
            <div key={user.user_id} className={`flex items-center gap-4 p-4 md:p-6 border-b border-gray-50 last:border-0 hover:bg-gray-50 transition ${idx < 3 ? 'bg-yellow-50/30' : ''}`}>
              <div className="w-8 text-center font-bold text-gray-400">
                {idx === 0 ? <Medal className="text-yellow-500 mx-auto"/> : idx === 1 ? <Medal className="text-gray-400 mx-auto"/> : idx === 2 ? <Medal className="text-orange-400 mx-auto"/> : `#${idx + 1}`}
              </div>
              <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-gray-200 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                <img src={user.profiles?.avatar_url || `https://placehold.co/100?text=${user.profiles?.full_name?.[0]}`} className="w-full h-full object-cover"/>
              </div>
              <div className="flex-1">
                <p className="font-bold text-brand-dark">{user.profiles?.full_name || 'Anonymous'}</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">{user.streak} Day Streak 🔥</p>
              </div>
              <div className="text-right">
                <p className="font-black text-brand-purple text-xl">{user.xp} XP</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

PATH: ./src/app/(app)/lezioni-live/page.jsx
