"use client";
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { Calendar, Loader2, Users, Video, CheckCircle, AlertTriangle } from 'lucide-react'
import { usePayment } from '@/context/PaymentContext'
import { useLanguage } from '@/context/LanguageContext'
import { extractTag, cleanDescription } from '@/lib/helpers'

export default function LiveLessons() {
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const { t } = useLanguage()
  const { initiateCheckout } = usePayment()
  const [lessons, setLessons] = useState([])
  const [purchasedIds, setPurchasedIds] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchData()
  }, [user])

  const fetchData = async () => {
    try {
      const { data: products, error } = await supabase
        .from('products')
        .select('*')
        .eq('type', 'standalone')
        .order('event_date', { ascending: true, nullsLast: true })
      
      if (error) throw error
      setLessons(products || [])

      if (user) {
        const { data: purchases } = await supabase.from('purchases').select('product_id').eq('user_id', user.id).eq('status', 'completed')
        if (purchases) setPurchasedIds(purchases.map(p => p.product_id))
      }
    } catch (error) { console.error(error) } finally { setLoading(false) }
  }

  if (loading) return <div className="flex justify-center p-10"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-brand-purple" /></div>

  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">{t('live_schedule')}</h1>
      <p className="text-gray-500 mb-8">{t('live_desc')}</p>
      
      <div className="space-y-4">
        {lessons.length === 0 && <p className="text-gray-400 italic">{t('dash_no_live')}</p>}

        {lessons.map(lesson => {
          const isPurchased = purchasedIds.includes(lesson.id)
          const zoomLink = extractTag(lesson.description, 'ZOOM') || "https://zoom.us/join"
          const eventDate = lesson.event_date ? new Date(lesson.event_date) : null
          const isExpired = eventDate && eventDate < new Date()
          
          return (
            <div key={lesson.id} className={`bg-white rounded-2xl p-8 border transition relative ${isPurchased ? 'border-green-200 bg-green-50/30' : 'border-gray-100 shadow-sm hover:shadow-md'} ${isExpired ? 'opacity-60 grayscale' : ''}`}>
              
              {isExpired && (
                <div className="absolute top-4 right-4 bg-gray-200 text-gray-500 px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1">
                  <AlertTriangle size={12} /> {t('live_expired')}
                </div>
              )}

              <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-6 items-center">
                <div className={`p-4 rounded-2xl flex-shrink-0 w-full md:w-24 text-center ${isPurchased ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-600' : 'bg-purple-50 text-brand-purple'}`}>
                  {eventDate ? (
                    <>
                      <span className="block text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-widest">{eventDate.toLocaleString('default', { month: 'short' })}</span>
                      <span className="block text-3xl font-black leading-none my-1">{eventDate.getDate()}</span>
                      <span className="block text-xs font-bold">{eventDate.toLocaleTimeString([], {hour: '2-digit', minute:'2-digit'})}</span>
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <div className="flex flex-col items-center py-2">
                      <Calendar size={32} />
                      <span className="text-xs font-bold mt-2">{t('live_tba')}</span>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
                
                <div className="flex-1 text-center md:text-left">
                  <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">{lesson.title}</h3>
                  <p className="text-gray-600 mb-3">{cleanDescription(lesson.description)}</p>
                  
                  <div className="flex justify-center md:justify-start gap-4 text-sm font-medium text-gray-500">
                    <span className="flex items-center gap-1"><Users size={16}/> Small Group</span>
                    {isPurchased && <span className="flex items-center gap-1 text-green-600 font-bold bg-white px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md shadow-sm border border-green-100"><CheckCircle size={14}/> {t('live_confirmed')}</span>}
                  </div>
                </div>
                
                <div className="text-center min-w-[180px]">
                  {isPurchased ? (
                    <div className="space-y-2">
                      <a 
                        href={isExpired ? '#' : zoomLink} 
                        target={isExpired ? '' : "_blank"} 
                        className={`block w-full px-5 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-lg ${isExpired ? 'bg-gray-300 cursor-not-allowed text-gray-500' : 'bg-green-600 text-white hover:bg-green-700 shadow-green-200'}`}
                      >
                        <Video size={18} /> {isExpired ? t('live_expired') : t('live_join')}
                      </a>
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <div className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-purple mb-3">{lesson.price}</div>
                      <button 
                        onClick={() => initiateCheckout(lesson)} 
                        disabled={isExpired}
                        className="w-full bg-brand-blue text-white px-4 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold hover:opacity-90 transition shadow-md hover:shadow-lg disabled:bg-gray-300 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
                      >
                        {isExpired ? t('live_closed') : t('live_book')}
                      </button>
                    </>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/app/(app)/messages/page.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { Loader2, MessageCircle, User } from 'lucide-react'
import ChatWindow from '@/components/ui/ChatWindow'

export default function Inbox() {
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const [conversations, setConversations] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [activeChat, setActiveChat] = useState(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    if(user) fetchConversations()
  }, [user])

  const fetchConversations = async () => {
    // This is a simplified logic. In a real app, you'd perform a complex join or have a separate 'conversations' table.
    // Here we fetch all messages and group them by the OTHER user.
    const { data } = await supabase.from('direct_messages').select('*').or(`sender_id.eq.${user.id},receiver_id.eq.${user.id}`)
    
    if (data) {
      const grouped = {}
      data.forEach(msg => {
        const otherId = msg.sender_id === user.id ? msg.receiver_id : msg.sender_id
        if (!grouped[otherId]) grouped[otherId] = { id: otherId, lastMsg: msg, count: 0 }
        grouped[otherId].lastMsg = new Date(msg.created_at) > new Date(grouped[otherId].lastMsg.created_at) ? msg : grouped[otherId].lastMsg
        grouped[otherId].count++
      })
      
      // Fetch names for these IDs
      const userIds = Object.keys(grouped)
      if (userIds.length > 0) {
        // Try profiles first, then tutors
        const { data: profiles } = await supabase.from('profiles').select('id, full_name, avatar_url').in('id', userIds)
        const { data: tutors } = await supabase.from('tutors').select('id, name, image_url').in('id', userIds)
        
        const map = {}
        profiles?.forEach(p => map[p.id] = { name: p.full_name, avatar: p.avatar_url })
        tutors?.forEach(t => map[t.id] = { name: t.name, avatar: t.image_url }) // Tutors overwrite profiles if duplicate

        setConversations(Object.values(grouped).map(c => ({
          ...c,
          name: map[c.id]?.name || 'Unknown User',
          avatar: map[c.id]?.avatar
        })).sort((a,b) => new Date(b.lastMsg.created_at) - new Date(a.lastMsg.created_at)))
      }
    }
    setLoading(false)
  }

  if (loading) return <div className="flex justify-center p-20"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-brand-purple"/></div>

  return (
    <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto pb-20 relative">
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-8 flex items-center gap-3"><MessageCircle className="text-blue-500"/> Messages</h1>

      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 overflow-hidden">
        {conversations.length === 0 ? (
          <div className="p-12 text-center text-gray-400">
            <p>No messages yet.</p>
            <p className="text-sm mt-2">Visit a Tutor profile to start a conversation!</p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="divide-y divide-gray-100">
            {conversations.map(convo => (
              <div 
                key={convo.id} 
                onClick={() => setActiveChat({ id: convo.id, name: convo.name })}
                className="p-6 flex items-center gap-4 hover:bg-gray-50 cursor-pointer transition"
              >
                <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-gray-200 rounded-full overflow-hidden flex-shrink-0">
                  {convo.avatar ? <img src={convo.avatar} className="w-full h-full object-cover" /> : <div className="w-full h-full flex items-center justify-center"><User size={20} className="text-gray-400"/></div>}
                </div>
                <div className="flex-1">
                  <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-1">
                    <h4 className="font-bold text-gray-800">{convo.name}</h4>
                    <span className="text-xs text-gray-400">{new Date(convo.lastMsg.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 line-clamp-1">
                    {convo.lastMsg.sender_id === user.id ? 'You: ' : ''}{convo.lastMsg.content}
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {activeChat && <ChatWindow partnerId={activeChat.id} partnerName={activeChat.name} onClose={() => setActiveChat(null)} />}
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/app/(app)/notebook/page.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { Loader2, StickyNote, BookOpen, Trash2, Edit, Save, X } from 'lucide-react'
import Link from 'next/link'

export default function Notebook() {
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [editingId, setEditingId] = useState(null)
  const [editContent, setEditContent] = useState('')

  useEffect(() => {
    if (user) fetchNotes()
  }, [user])

  const fetchNotes = async () => {
    try {
      const { data } = await supabase.from('user_notes').select('*, lessons(title, course_id, products(title))').eq('user_id', user.id).order('updated_at', { ascending: false })
      setNotes(data || [])
    } catch (e) { console.error(e) } 
    finally { setLoading(false) }
  }

  const handleDelete = async (id) => {
    if (!confirm("Delete note?")) return
    await supabase.from('user_notes').delete().eq('id', id)
    setNotes(prev => prev.filter(n => n.id !== id))
  }

  const startEdit = (note) => {
    setEditingId(note.id)
    setEditContent(note.content)
  }

  const saveEdit = async (id) => {
    await supabase.from('user_notes').update({ content: editContent, updated_at: new Date() }).eq('id', id)
    setNotes(prev => prev.map(n => n.id === id ? { ...n, content: editContent, updated_at: new Date().toISOString() } : n))
    setEditingId(null)
  }

  if (loading) return <div className="flex justify-center p-20"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-brand-purple"/></div>

  return (
    <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto pb-20">
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2 flex items-center gap-3"><StickyNote className="text-yellow-500"/> My Notebook</h1>
      <p className="text-gray-500 mb-8">All your class notes in one place.</p>

      {notes.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="text-center py-20 bg-white rounded-3xl border border-dashed border-gray-300">
          <p className="text-gray-400">No notes found. Take notes during lessons!</p>
          <Link href="/corsi" className="btn btn-plum-gradient px-6 py-2 mt-4 text-sm">Go to Courses</Link>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid gap-6">
          {notes.map(note => (
            <div key={note.id} className="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 hover:shadow-md transition">
              <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-4 border-b border-gray-50 pb-3">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <div className="bg-purple-50 p-2 rounded-lg text-brand-purple"><BookOpen size={18}/></div>
                  <div>
                    <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800">{note.lessons?.title}</h3>
                    <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">{note.lessons?.products?.title}</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 mr-2">{new Date(note.updated_at).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
                  {editingId === note.id ? (
                    <>
                      <button onClick={() => saveEdit(note.id)} className="text-green-600 hover:bg-green-50 p-2 rounded"><Save size={16}/></button>
                      <button onClick={() => setEditingId(null)} className="text-gray-400 hover:bg-gray-100 p-2 rounded"><X size={16}/></button>
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <button onClick={() => startEdit(note)} className="text-blue-500 hover:bg-blue-50 p-2 rounded"><Edit size={16}/></button>
                      <button onClick={() => handleDelete(note.id)} className="text-gray-300 hover:text-red-500 p-2 rounded"><Trash2 size={16}/></button>
                    </>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
              
              {editingId === note.id ? (
                <textarea 
                  className="w-full p-3 border border-yellow-200 bg-yellow-50 rounded-xl outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-yellow-300 font-handwriting" 
                  rows="4"
                  value={editContent}
                  onChange={e => setEditContent(e.target.value)}
                />
              ) : (
                <div className="bg-yellow-50 p-4 rounded-xl text-gray-700 text-sm whitespace-pre-wrap font-medium leading-relaxed font-handwriting">
                  {note.content}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/app/(app)/profile/page.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { useLanguage } from '@/context/LanguageContext'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { User, Save, Loader2, Camera, Lock, FileText, Download, Award, MapPin, Trash2, AlertTriangle } from 'lucide-react'
import { generateFilePath } from '@/lib/helpers'
import { generateInvoice } from '@/utils/invoiceGenerator'

export default function Profile() {
  const { user, refreshProfile, signOut } = useAuth()
  const { t } = useLanguage()
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const [passLoading, setPassLoading] = useState(false)
  
  // Profile State
  const [fullName, setFullName] = useState('')
  const [avatarUrl, setAvatarUrl] = useState('')
  const [billing, setBilling] = useState({ address: '', city: '', zip: '', country: '' })
  
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false)
  const [orders, setOrders] = useState([])
  const [certificates, setCertificates] = useState([])

  // Password State
  const [newPassword, setNewPassword] = useState('')
  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState('')

  useEffect(() => {
    if (user) {
      getProfile()
      getOrders()
      getCertificates()
    }
  }, [user])

  const getProfile = async () => {
    const { data } = await supabase.from('profiles').select('*').eq('id', user.id).single()
    if (data) { 
      setFullName(data.full_name || '')
      setAvatarUrl(data.avatar_url || '')
      setBilling(data.billing_address || { address: '', city: '', zip: '', country: '' })
    }
  }

  const getOrders = async () => {
    const { data } = await supabase.from('purchases').select('id, created_at, products(title, price)').eq('user_id', user.id).order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    setOrders(data || [])
  }

  const getCertificates = async () => {
    const { data: courses } = await supabase.from('products').select('id, title').eq('type', 'course')
    const { data: progress } = await supabase.from('lesson_progress').select('course_id').eq('user_id', user.id)
    const { data: lessons } = await supabase.from('lessons').select('id, course_id')
    
    if(!courses || !progress || !lessons) return

    const completed = courses.filter(c => {
      const courseLessons = lessons.filter(l => l.course_id === c.id)
      const userProgress = progress.filter(p => p.course_id === c.id)
      return courseLessons.length > 0 && courseLessons.length === userProgress.length
    })
    setCertificates(completed)
  }

  const updateProfile = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setLoading(true)
    await supabase.from('profiles').upsert({ 
      id: user.id, 
      full_name: fullName, 
      avatar_url: avatarUrl, 
      billing_address: billing,
      updated_at: new Date() 
    })
    await refreshProfile()
    setLoading(false)
    alert('Profile saved successfully!')
  }

  const handlePasswordChange = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (newPassword !== confirmPassword) return alert("Passwords do not match")
    setPassLoading(true)
    const { error } = await supabase.auth.updateUser({ password: newPassword })
    setPassLoading(false)
    if (error) alert("Error: " + error.message)
    else {
      alert("Password updated successfully")
      setNewPassword('')
      setConfirmPassword('')
    }
  }

  const handleAvatarUpload = async (event) => {
    setUploading(true)
    const file = event.target.files[0]
    const path = generateFilePath('avatars', file)
    await supabase.storage.from('avatars').upload(path, file)
    const { data } = supabase.storage.from('avatars').getPublicUrl(path)
    setAvatarUrl(data.publicUrl)
    setUploading(false)
  }

  const handleDeleteAccount = async () => {
    const confirmed = window.confirm("Are you sure? This action cannot be undone. Your account will be queued for deletion.")
    if (confirmed) {
      await supabase.from('deletion_requests').insert([{ user_id: user.id, reason: 'User requested' }])
      alert("Account deletion request submitted. You will be logged out.")
      await signOut()
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto pb-20">
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">{t('prof_title')}</h1>
      <p className="text-gray-500 mb-8">{t('prof_desc')}</p>

      <div className="grid gap-8">
        <div className="bg-white p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 relative overflow-hidden">
          <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 w-full h-2 bg-gradient-to-r from-brand-blue to-brand-purple"></div>
          <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center gap-8 mb-8">
            <div className="relative group">
              <div className="w-32 h-32 bg-purple-100 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden border-4 border-white shadow-lg">
                 {avatarUrl ? <img src={avatarUrl} alt="Avatar" className="w-full h-full object-cover" /> : <User size={48} className="text-brand-purple opacity-50" />}
                 {uploading && <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/50 flex items-center justify-center"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-white"/></div>}
              </div>
              <label className="absolute bottom-0 right-0 bg-brand-dark text-white p-2 rounded-full cursor-pointer hover:bg-brand-purple transition shadow-md">
                 <Camera size={18} />
                 <input type="file" accept="image/*" className="hidden" onChange={handleAvatarUpload} disabled={uploading} />
              </label>
            </div>
            <div className="text-center md:text-left">
              <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 uppercase tracking-wider font-bold mb-1">{t('prof_student')}</p>
              <p className="text-xl font-bold text-brand-dark">{fullName || "Unnamed User"}</p>
              <p className="text-gray-400">{user?.email}</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          
          <form onSubmit={updateProfile} className="space-y-6 border-t border-gray-100 pt-6">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-2">{t('auth_fullname')}</label>
              <input type="text" value={fullName} onChange={(e) => setFullName(e.target.value)} className="input-field" />
            </div>

            <div className="bg-gray-50 p-6 rounded-xl border border-gray-200">
              <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-700 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2"><MapPin size={18}/> Billing Address</h3>
              <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                <div className="md:col-span-2"><input placeholder="Address Line" className="input-field bg-white" value={billing.address} onChange={e => setBilling({...billing, address: e.target.value})} /></div>
                <input placeholder="City" className="input-field bg-white" value={billing.city} onChange={e => setBilling({...billing, city: e.target.value})} />
                <input placeholder="ZIP Code" className="input-field bg-white" value={billing.zip} onChange={e => setBilling({...billing, zip: e.target.value})} />
                <input placeholder="Country" className="input-field bg-white md:col-span-2" value={billing.country} onChange={e => setBilling({...billing, country: e.target.value})} />
              </div>
            </div>

            <button disabled={loading} type="submit" className="w-full md:w-auto flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-brand-purple text-white px-8 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold hover:opacity-90 transition shadow-lg">
              {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" size={20} /> : <><Save size={20} /> {t('prof_save')}</>}
            </button>
          </form>
        </div>

        {/* Change Password */}
        <div className="bg-white p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100">
          <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-brand-dark mb-6 flex items-center gap-2"><Lock size={20}/> Security</h3>
          <form onSubmit={handlePasswordChange} className="space-y-4 max-w-md">
            <input type="password" placeholder="New Password" className="input-field" value={newPassword} onChange={e => setNewPassword(e.target.value)} />
            <input type="password" placeholder="Confirm Password" className="input-field" value={confirmPassword} onChange={e => setConfirmPassword(e.target.value)} />
            <button disabled={passLoading} className="btn bg-gray-900 text-white py-2 px-6 rounded-lg text-sm">
              {passLoading ? 'Updating...' : 'Update Password'}
            </button>
          </form>
        </div>

        {/* Certificates & Orders */}
        <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-8">
          <div className="bg-white p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 h-fit">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-6">
              <div className="bg-yellow-50 p-2 rounded-lg text-yellow-600"><Award size={20} /></div>
              <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-brand-dark">My Certificates</h3>
            </div>
            {certificates.length > 0 ? (
              <div className="space-y-4">
                {certificates.map(cert => (
                  <div key={cert.id} className="flex justify-between items-center p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-xl">
                    <p className="font-bold text-gray-800 text-sm">{cert.title}</p>
                    <a href={`/corsi/${cert.id}`} className="text-brand-purple hover:underline text-xs font-bold">Download</a>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : <p className="text-gray-400 italic text-sm">No certificates yet.</p>}
          </div>

          <div className="bg-white p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 h-fit">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-6">
              <div className="bg-blue-50 p-2 rounded-lg text-brand-blue"><FileText size={20} /></div>
              <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-brand-dark">{t('prof_orders')}</h3>
            </div>
            {orders.length > 0 ? (
              <div className="space-y-4 max-h-[300px] overflow-y-auto">
                {orders.map(order => (
                  <div key={order.id} className="flex justify-between items-center p-3 bg-gray-50 rounded-xl">
                    <div>
                      <p className="font-bold text-gray-800 text-sm">{order.products?.title}</p>
                      <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">{new Date(order.created_at).toLocaleDateString()} • {order.products?.price}</p>
                    </div>
                    <button onClick={() => generateInvoice(order, {full_name: fullName, billing_address: billing})} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-brand-purple transition"><Download size={18} /></button>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : <p className="text-gray-400 italic text-sm">{t('prof_no_orders')}</p>}
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Danger Zone */}
        <div className="bg-red-50 p-8 rounded-2xl border border-red-100 flex items-center justify-between">
          <div>
            <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-red-700 flex items-center gap-2"><AlertTriangle size={20}/> Danger Zone</h3>
            <p className="text-sm text-red-600">Permanently delete your account and all data.</p>
          </div>
          <button onClick={handleDeleteAccount} className="bg-white text-red-600 border border-red-200 px-6 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm hover:bg-red-50 transition flex items-center gap-2">
            <Trash2 size={16}/> Delete Account
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
      <style>{`
        .input-field { width: 100%; padding: 0.75rem; border: 1px solid #e5e7eb; border-radius: 0.5rem; outline: none; font-size: 0.9rem; transition: all 0.2s; }
        .input-field:focus { border-color: #6a11cb; ring: 2px solid #6a11cb33; }
      `}</style>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/app/(app)/store/page.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { useCart } from '@/context/CartContext'
import { useLanguage } from '@/context/LanguageContext'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { ShoppingBag, Loader2, Download, Check, Plus, Search, Tag } from 'lucide-react'
import { cleanDescription } from '@/lib/helpers'
import WishlistButton from '@/components/ui/WishlistButton'
import ShareButton from '@/components/ui/ShareButton'
import SaleCountdown from '@/components/ui/SaleCountdown'

export default function Store() {
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const { t } = useLanguage()
  const { addToCart } = useCart()
  
  const [products, setProducts] = useState([])
  const [purchasedIds, setPurchasedIds] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState('all')
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('')

  useEffect(() => { fetchData() }, [user])

  const fetchData = async () => {
    try {
      const { data } = await supabase.from('products').select('*').in('type', ['ebook', 'merch', 'bundle']).order('created_at', {ascending: false})
      setProducts(data || [])
      if (user) {
        const { data: purchases } = await supabase.from('purchases').select('product_id').eq('user_id', user.id).eq('status', 'completed')
        if (purchases) setPurchasedIds(purchases.map(p => p.product_id))
      }
    } catch (error) { console.error(error) } finally { setLoading(false) }
  }

  const filteredProducts = products.filter(p => {
    const matchesCat = filter === 'all' || p.type === filter
    const matchesSearch = p.title.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase())
    return matchesCat && matchesSearch
  })

  if (loading) return <div className="flex justify-center p-10"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-brand-purple" /></div>

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-end mb-8 gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">{t('store_title')}</h1>
          <p className="text-gray-500">{t('store_desc')}</p>
        </div>
        
        <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row gap-3 w-full md:w-auto">
           <div className="relative">
              <Search className="absolute left-3 top-2.5 text-gray-400" size={18}/>
              <input 
                placeholder="Search items..." 
                className="pl-10 pr-4 py-2 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl outline-none focus:border-brand-purple w-full"
                value={search}
                onChange={e => setSearch(e.target.value)}
              />
           </div>
           <div className="flex gap-2">
              <button onClick={() => setFilter('all')} className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold transition ${filter === 'all' ? 'bg-brand-dark text-white' : 'bg-white border border-gray-200 text-gray-600'}`}>All</button>
              <button onClick={() => setFilter('ebook')} className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold transition ${filter === 'ebook' ? 'bg-brand-dark text-white' : 'bg-white border border-gray-200 text-gray-600'}`}>Ebooks</button>
              <button onClick={() => setFilter('merch')} className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold transition ${filter === 'merch' ? 'bg-brand-dark text-white' : 'bg-white border border-gray-200 text-gray-600'}`}>Merch</button>
           </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid md:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
        {filteredProducts.map(product => {
          const isOwned = purchasedIds.includes(product.id)
          const isEbook = product.type === 'ebook'
          const isOnSale = !!product.sale_price
          
          return (
            <div key={product.id} className="bg-white rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border border-gray-100 shadow-sm hover:shadow-lg transition group flex flex-col relative">
              <div className="h-56 overflow-hidden relative bg-gray-50 flex items-center justify-center">
                <img src={product.image_url || 'https://placehold.co/400x400'} alt={product.title} className={`h-full w-full object-cover transition duration-500 ${!isOwned && 'group-hover:scale-105'}`} />
                
                <div className="absolute top-4 right-4 flex flex-col gap-2 items-end">
                   <div className="bg-white/90 backdrop-blur px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold text-brand-purple shadow-sm uppercase">{product.type}</div>
                   {isOnSale && (
                     <div className="bg-red-500 text-white px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold shadow-md flex items-center gap-1">
                        <Tag size={10}/> SALE
                     </div>
                   )}
                </div>

                {product.sale_ends_at && isOnSale && <SaleCountdown endDate={product.sale_ends_at} />}

                <div className="absolute top-4 left-4 flex gap-2">
                  <WishlistButton productId={product.id} />
                  <ShareButton title={product.title} text={`Check out ${product.title}`} url={`${window.location.origin}/store?item=${product.id}`} />
                </div>
                {isOwned && isEbook && <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/40 flex items-center justify-center backdrop-blur-[2px]"><div className="bg-green-500 text-white w-12 h-12 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center shadow-lg"><Check size={24} strokeWidth={3} /></div></div>}
              </div>
              <div className="p-6 flex-1 flex flex-col">
                <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">{product.title}</h3>
                <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mb-4 line-clamp-2 flex-1">{cleanDescription(product.description)}</p>
                <div className="mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-gray-50 flex items-center justify-between">
                  {isOwned && isEbook ? (
                    <Link href="/downloads" className="w-full flex items-center justify-center gap-2 bg-green-50 text-green-700 border border-green-200 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold hover:bg-green-100 transition"><Download size={16} /> {t('store_download')}</Link>
                  ) : (
                    <>
                      <div className="flex flex-col">
                        {isOnSale && <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 line-through">{product.price}</span>}
                        <span className={`text-xl font-bold ${isOnSale ? 'text-red-500' : 'text-brand-purple'}`}>{isOnSale ? product.sale_price : product.price}</span>
                      </div>
                      <button onClick={() => addToCart({ ...product, price: product.sale_price || product.price })} className="flex items-center gap-2 bg-brand-dark text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold hover:bg-gray-800 transition shadow-md">
                        <Plus size={16} /> Add to Cart
                      </button>
                    </>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/app/(app)/subscription/page.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { Loader2, CreditCard, CheckCircle, XCircle, Calendar } from 'lucide-react'
import confetti from 'canvas-confetti'

export default function SubscriptionPage() {
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const [sub, setSub] = useState(null)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (user) fetchSub()
  }, [user])

  const fetchSub = async () => {
    const { data } = await supabase.from('subscriptions').select('*').eq('user_id', user.id).single()
    setSub(data)
    setLoading(false)
  }

  const handleSubscribe = async () => {
    if (!confirm("Start Premium Subscription? (Mock Payment)")) return
    setLoading(true)
    
    // Mock creating a subscription
    const nextMonth = new Date()
    nextMonth.setMonth(nextMonth.getMonth() + 1)
    
    await supabase.from('subscriptions').upsert({
      user_id: user.id,
      status: 'active',
      plan_name: 'Premium Monthly',
      current_period_end: nextMonth
    }, { onConflict: 'user_id' })
    
    confetti({ particleCount: 150, spread: 70, origin: { y: 0.6 } })
    fetchSub()
  }

  const handleCancel = async () => {
    if(!confirm("Are you sure you want to cancel?")) return
    setLoading(true)
    await supabase.from('subscriptions').update({ status: 'cancelled' }).eq('user_id', user.id)
    fetchSub()
  }

  if (loading) return <div className="flex justify-center p-20"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-brand-purple"/></div>

  return (
    <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto pb-20">
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2 flex items-center gap-3">
        <CreditCard className="text-brand-blue"/> Membership
      </h1>
      <p className="text-gray-500 mb-8">Manage your Premium access.</p>

      {sub && sub.status === 'active' ? (
        <div className="bg-white p-8 rounded-3xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100">
          <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-6">
            <div>
              <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-brand-dark">{sub.plan_name}</h3>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mt-2">
                 <span className="bg-green-100 text-green-700 px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1"><CheckCircle size={14}/> Active</span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="text-right">
               <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 uppercase font-bold">Renews On</p>
               <p className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800">{sub.current_period_end ? new Date(sub.current_period_end).toLocaleDateString() : 'Lifetime'}</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <hr className="border-gray-100 my-6"/>

          <div className="space-y-3 mb-8">
             <p className="flex items-center gap-3 text-gray-600"><CheckCircle size={18} className="text-brand-purple"/> Access to ALL video courses</p>
             <p className="flex items-center gap-3 text-gray-600"><CheckCircle size={18} className="text-brand-purple"/> Monthly Live Q&A</p>
             <p className="flex items-center gap-3 text-gray-600"><CheckCircle size={18} className="text-brand-purple"/> 10% Store Discount</p>
          </div>

          <button onClick={handleCancel} className="text-red-500 font-bold hover:bg-red-50 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg transition text-sm">
            Cancel Subscription
          </button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="bg-gradient-to-br from-brand-purple to-brand-blue p-10 rounded-3xl text-white text-center shadow-xl">
          <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-4">Upgrade to Premium 👑</h2>
          <p className="text-white/90 mb-8 max-w-md mx-auto">Unlock unlimited access to all courses, live sessions, and exclusive content for one simple price.</p>
          
          <div className="bg-white/10 backdrop-blur-md p-6 rounded-2xl max-w-xs mx-auto border border-white/20 mb-8">
             <p className="text-4xl font-black">9.900 Ft<span className="text-sm font-normal text-white/70">/mo</span></p>
          </div>

          <button 
            onClick={handleSubscribe} 
            className="bg-white text-brand-purple px-8 py-3 rounded-full font-bold shadow-lg hover:scale-105 transition"
          >
            Start Free Trial
          </button>
          {sub && sub.status === 'cancelled' && <p className="text-xs text-white/50 mt-4">Your previous subscription was cancelled.</p>}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/app/(app)/wishlist/page.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { useCart } from '@/context/CartContext'
import { Loader2, Heart, Trash2, ShoppingCart } from 'lucide-react'

export default function Wishlist() {
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const { addToCart } = useCart()
  const [items, setItems] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)

  useEffect(() => {
    if(user) fetchWishlist()
  }, [user])

  const fetchWishlist = async () => {
    try {
      const { data } = await supabase.from('wishlist').select('product_id, products(*)').eq('user_id', user.id)
      setItems(data?.map(i => i.products) || [])
    } catch (e) { console.error(e) } 
    finally { setLoading(false) }
  }

  const removeWishlist = async (productId) => {
    await supabase.from('wishlist').delete().eq('user_id', user.id).eq('product_id', productId)
    setItems(prev => prev.filter(i => i.id !== productId))
  }

  if (loading) return <div className="flex justify-center p-20"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-brand-purple"/></div>

  return (
    <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto pb-20">
      <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2 flex items-center gap-3"><Heart className="text-red-500" fill="currentColor"/> My Wishlist</h1>
      <p className="text-gray-500 mb-8">Save items for later.</p>

      {items.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="text-center py-20 bg-white rounded-3xl border border-dashed border-gray-300">
          <p className="text-gray-400">Your wishlist is empty.</p>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
          {items.map(item => (
            <div key={item.id} className="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 flex flex-col justify-between">
              <div className="flex gap-4 mb-4">
                <img src={item.image_url || 'https://placehold.co/100'} className="w-20 h-20 rounded-xl object-cover bg-gray-100"/>
                <div>
                  <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800">{item.title}</h3>
                  <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 uppercase mt-1">{item.type}</p>
                  <p className="text-brand-purple font-bold text-lg mt-2">{item.price}</p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <button onClick={() => addToCart(item)} className="flex-1 bg-brand-dark text-white py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm flex items-center justify-center gap-2 hover:bg-gray-800">
                  <ShoppingCart size={16}/> Add to Cart
                </button>
                <button onClick={() => removeWishlist(item.id)} className="p-2 bg-red-50 text-red-500 rounded-lg hover:bg-red-100">
                  <Trash2 size={18}/>
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/app/blog/page.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import Link from 'next/link'
import Navbar from '@/components/sections/Navbar'
import Footer from '@/components/sections/Footer'
import { Loader2, Calendar, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react'

export default function BlogList() {
  const [posts, setPosts] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [page, setPage] = useState(0)
  const [total, setTotal] = useState(0)
  const LIMIT = 6

  useEffect(() => { fetchPosts() }, [page])

  const fetchPosts = async () => {
    setLoading(true)
    // Get count
    const { count } = await supabase.from('posts').select('*', { count: 'exact', head: true }).eq('published', true)
    setTotal(count || 0)

    // Get Data
    const from = page * LIMIT
    const to = from + LIMIT - 1
    const { data } = await supabase
      .from('posts')
      .select('*')
      .eq('published', true)
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
      .range(from, to)
    
    setPosts(data || [])
    setLoading(false)
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-[#F8F9FC]">
      <Navbar />
      <main className="flex-grow pt-32 pb-20 px-4">
        <div className="max-w-6xl mx-auto">
          <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">Annyeong Blog ✍️</h1>
          <p className="text-gray-600 mb-12">Latest news, culture, and learning tips.</p>

          {loading ? <div className="py-20 flex justify-center"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-brand-purple" size={32}/></div> : (
            <>
              <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8 mb-12">
                {posts.map(post => (
                  <Link href={`/blog/${post.slug}`} key={post.id} className="bg-white rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-sm hover:shadow-lg transition border border-gray-100 group">
                    <div className="h-48 bg-gray-200 overflow-hidden">
                      <img src={post.image_url || 'https://placehold.co/600x400'} alt={post.title} className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-105 transition duration-500" />
                    </div>
                    <div className="p-6">
                      <div className="flex gap-2 mb-3">
                        {post.tags?.map(t => <span key={t} className="text-xs font-bold text-brand-purple bg-purple-50 px-2 py-1 rounded-md">{t}</span>)}
                      </div>
                      <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2 group-hover:text-brand-purple transition">{post.title}</h2>
                      <p className="text-gray-500 text-sm line-clamp-3 mb-4">{post.excerpt}</p>
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-xs text-gray-400 border-t border-gray-50 pt-4">
                        <span className="flex items-center gap-1"><Calendar size={12}/> {new Date(post.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </Link>
                ))}
              </div>

              {/* Pagination Controls */}
              {total > LIMIT && (
                <div className="flex justify-center items-center gap-4">
                  <button 
                    disabled={page === 0} 
                    onClick={() => setPage(p => p - 1)} 
                    className="p-3 bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-full hover:bg-gray-50 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transition"
                  >
                    <ChevronLeft size={20} />
                  </button>
                  <span className="font-bold text-gray-600">Page {page + 1} of {Math.ceil(total / LIMIT)}</span>
                  <button 
                    disabled={(page + 1) * LIMIT >= total} 
                    onClick={() => setPage(p => p + 1)} 
                    className="p-3 bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-full hover:bg-gray-50 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transition"
                  >
                    <ChevronRight size={20} />
                  </button>
                </div>
              )}
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/app/blog/[slug]/page.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase' 
import Navbar from '@/components/sections/Navbar'
import Footer from '@/components/sections/Footer'
import { ArrowLeft, Calendar, MessageCircle, Send, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import ShareButton from '@/components/ui/ShareButton'

// Dynamic Metadata
export async function generateMetadata({ params }) {
  const { slug } = params
  return {
    title: `Blog Post | Annyeong Academy`, 
    description: 'Learn Korean culture and language tips.',
  }
}

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { useParams } from 'next/navigation'
import { Skeleton } from '@/components/ui/Skeletons'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'

export default function BlogPost() {
  const { slug } = useParams()
  const { user, profile } = useAuth()
  const [post, setPost] = useState(null)
  const [comments, setComments] = useState([])
  const [newComment, setNewComment] = useState('')
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    if(slug) {
      fetchPost()
      fetchComments()
    }
  }, [slug])

  const fetchPost = async () => {
    const { data } = await supabase.from('posts').select('*').eq('slug', slug).single()
    setPost(data)
    setLoading(false)
  }

  const fetchComments = async () => {
    const { data } = await supabase.from('comments').select('*, profiles(full_name, avatar_url)').eq('post_slug', slug).order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    setComments(data || [])
  }

  const handlePostComment = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (!user) return alert("Please login to comment")
    if (!newComment.trim()) return
    setSubmitting(true)
    await supabase.from('comments').insert([{ post_slug: slug, user_id: user.id, content: newComment }])
    setNewComment('')
    fetchComments()
    setSubmitting(false)
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-[#F8F9FC]">
        <Navbar />
        <main className="flex-grow pt-32 pb-20 px-4">
          <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto space-y-8">
            <Skeleton className="h-96 w-full rounded-3xl" />
            <div className="space-y-4">
                <Skeleton className="h-10 w-3/4" />
                <Skeleton className="h-4 w-full" />
                <Skeleton className="h-4 w-full" />
                <Skeleton className="h-4 w-2/3" />
            </div>
          </div>
        </main>
        <Footer />
      </div>
    )
  }

  if (!post) return <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center">Post not found</div>

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-[#F8F9FC]">
      <Navbar />
      <main className="flex-grow pt-32 pb-20 px-4">
        <article className="max-w-3xl mx-auto bg-white rounded-3xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 overflow-hidden animate-fade-in">
          <div className="h-64 md:h-96 bg-gray-200">
            <img src={post.image_url || 'https://placehold.co/800x400'} alt={post.title} className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
          </div>
          <div className="p-8 md:p-12">
            <Link href="/blog" className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-gray-400 hover:text-brand-purple text-sm mb-6 font-bold"><ArrowLeft size={16}/> Back to Blog</Link>
            <div className="flex gap-2 mb-4">
               {post.tags?.map(t => <span key={t} className="text-xs font-bold text-brand-blue bg-blue-50 px-2 py-1 rounded-md uppercase tracking-wider">{t}</span>)}
            </div>
            <h1 className="text-3xl md:text-4xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-6 leading-tight">{post.title}</h1>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 text-sm text-gray-500 mb-8 border-b border-gray-100 pb-8">
              <span className="flex items-center gap-2"><Calendar size={16}/> {new Date(post.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
              <ShareButton title={post.title} text={post.excerpt} />
            </div>
            <div className="prose prose-lg text-gray-600 max-w-none">
              {post.content.split('\n').map((p, i) => <p key={i} className="mb-4">{p}</p>)}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-gray-50 p-8 md:p-12 border-t border-gray-100">
            <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-6 flex items-center gap-2"><MessageCircle /> Comments ({comments.length})</h3>
            {user ? (
              <form onSubmit={handlePostComment} className="mb-10">
                <div className="flex gap-4">
                  <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-white rounded-full overflow-hidden flex-shrink-0 border border-gray-200">
                    <img src={profile?.avatar_url || `https://placehold.co/100?text=${profile?.full_name?.[0]}`} className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex-1">
                    <textarea className="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border border-gray-200 outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-purple bg-white text-sm" rows="3" placeholder="Share your thoughts..." value={newComment} onChange={e => setNewComment(e.target.value)}></textarea>
                    <button disabled={submitting} className="mt-2 bg-brand-purple text-white px-6 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm hover:opacity-90 transition flex items-center gap-2">
                      {submitting ? <Loader2 size={16} className="animate-spin"/> : <><Send size={16}/> Post Comment</>}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </form>
            ) : (
              <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl text-center border border-gray-200 mb-10">
                <p className="text-gray-500 mb-4">Please log in to join the conversation.</p>
                <Link href="/login" className="text-brand-purple font-bold hover:underline">Login Now</Link>
              </div>
            )}
            <div className="space-y-6">
              {comments.map(comment => (
                <div key={comment.id} className="flex gap-4">
                  <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-gray-200 rounded-full overflow-hidden flex-shrink-0">
                    <img src={comment.profiles?.avatar_url || `https://placehold.co/100?text=${comment.profiles?.full_name?.[0] || 'U'}`} className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
                      <span className="font-bold text-gray-800 text-sm">{comment.profiles?.full_name || 'User'}</span>
                      <span className="text-xs text-gray-400">• {new Date(comment.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-gray-600 text-sm leading-relaxed">{comment.content}</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </article>
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/app/cart/page.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import Navbar from '@/components/sections/Navbar'
import Footer from '@/components/sections/Footer'
import CouponInput from '@/components/ui/CouponInput'
import { Trash2, ShoppingBag, ArrowRight, CreditCard, Lock, Plus, Sparkles } from 'lucide-react'
import { useCart } from '@/context/CartContext'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'

export default function CartPage() {
  const { cart, addToCart, removeFromCart, clearCart, cartTotal } = useCart()
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const router = useRouter()
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const [discount, setDiscount] = useState(0)
  const [upsellItem, setUpsellItem] = useState(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchUpsell = async () => {
      // Logic: Check if any item in cart has a linked upsell product ID
      const cartIds = cart.map(i => i.id)
      const triggeringItem = cart.find(i => i.upsell_product_id)
      
      let data = null
      
      if (triggeringItem) {
        // Fetch specific linked upsell
        const { data: linked } = await supabase.from('products').select('*').eq('id', triggeringItem.upsell_product_id).single()
        data = linked
      }
      
      // Fallback: Random ebook if specific upsell isn't in cart
      if (!data || cartIds.includes(data.id)) {
         const { data: random } = await supabase
          .from('products')
          .select('*')
          .eq('type', 'ebook')
          .not('id', 'in', `(${cartIds.join(',')})`)
          .limit(1)
          .maybeSingle()
         data = random
      }

      if (data && !cartIds.includes(data.id)) setUpsellItem(data)
      else setUpsellItem(null)
    }
    if (cart.length > 0) fetchUpsell()
    else setUpsellItem(null)
  }, [cart])
  
  const discountAmount = Math.round((cartTotal * discount) / 100)
  const finalTotal = cartTotal - discountAmount

  const handleCheckout = async () => {
    if (!user) {
      alert("Please login to checkout")
      router.push('/login')
      return
    }
    setLoading(true)
    
    setTimeout(async () => {
      try {
        const subscriptions = cart.filter(item => item.type === 'subscription')
        const oneTimePurchases = cart.filter(item => item.type !== 'subscription')

        for (const sub of subscriptions) {
          const nextMonth = new Date()
          nextMonth.setMonth(nextMonth.getMonth() + 1)
          await supabase.from('subscriptions').upsert({
            user_id: user.id,
            status: 'active',
            plan_name: sub.title,
            current_period_end: nextMonth
          }, { onConflict: 'user_id' })
        }

        if (oneTimePurchases.length > 0) {
          const purchases = oneTimePurchases.map(item => ({ 
            user_id: user.id, 
            product_id: item.id, 
            status: 'completed' 
          }))
          await supabase.from('purchases').insert(purchases)
        }
        
        alert("Purchase successful! Redirecting...")
        clearCart()
        router.push('/dashboard')
      } catch (err) { alert(err.message) } 
      finally { setLoading(false) }
    }, 2000)
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-[#F8F9FC]">
      <Navbar />
      <main className="flex-grow pt-32 pb-20 px-4">
        <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto">
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-8 flex items-center gap-3"><ShoppingBag className="text-brand-purple"/> Your Cart</h1>

          {cart.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="text-center py-20 bg-white rounded-3xl border border-dashed border-gray-300">
              <ShoppingBag size={48} className="mx-auto text-gray-300 mb-4" />
              <p className="text-gray-500 mb-6">Your cart is empty.</p>
              <a href="/corsi" className="btn btn-plum-gradient px-8 py-3">Browse Courses</a>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="grid md:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
              <div className="md:col-span-2 space-y-4">
                {cart.map((item, idx) => (
                  <div key={`${item.id}-${idx}`} className="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm flex justify-between items-center border border-gray-100">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                      <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-gray-100 rounded-lg overflow-hidden">
                        <img src={item.image_url || 'https://placehold.co/100'} className="w-full h-full object-cover"/>
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <h4 className="font-bold text-gray-800 text-lg">{item.title}</h4>
                        <p className="text-xs font-bold uppercase text-brand-blue bg-blue-50 px-2 py-1 rounded-md inline-block mt-1">{item.type}</p>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-6">
                      <span className="font-bold text-brand-dark text-lg">{item.price}</span>
                      <button onClick={() => removeFromCart(item.id)} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-red-500 transition"><Trash2 size={20}/></button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}

                {upsellItem && (
                  <div className="bg-orange-50 border border-orange-100 p-4 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-between mt-4 animate-fade-in">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                      <div className="bg-white p-2 rounded-lg shadow-sm text-orange-500"><Sparkles size={24}/></div>
                      <div>
                        <p className="font-bold text-orange-800">Add {upsellItem.title}?</p>
                        <p className="text-xs text-orange-600">Accelerate your learning for just {upsellItem.price}.</p>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <button onClick={() => addToCart(upsellItem)} className="bg-orange-500 text-white p-2 rounded-lg hover:bg-orange-600 transition shadow-md"><Plus size={20}/></button>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>

              <div className="bg-white p-8 rounded-3xl shadow-lg border border-gray-100 h-fit sticky top-24">
                <h3 className="font-bold text-xl text-gray-800 mb-6 border-b border-gray-100 pb-4">Order Summary</h3>
                <div className="space-y-4 mb-6">
                  <div className="flex justify-between text-gray-600"><span>Subtotal</span><span>{cartTotal} Ft</span></div>
                  {discount > 0 && <div className="flex justify-between text-green-600 font-bold"><span>Discount ({discount}%)</span><span>-{discountAmount} Ft</span></div>}
                  <CouponInput onApply={setDiscount} />
                </div>
                <div className="border-t border-gray-100 pt-4 flex justify-between text-2xl font-black text-brand-dark mb-8"><span>Total</span><span>{finalTotal} Ft</span></div>
                <button onClick={handleCheckout} disabled={loading} className="w-full btn btn-plum-gradient py-4 rounded-xl font-bold shadow-xl flex items-center justify-center gap-2 mb-4">
                  {loading ? "Processing..." : <>Checkout <ArrowRight size={20}/></>}
                </button>
                <p className="text-center text-xs text-gray-400 flex items-center justify-center gap-1"><Lock size={12}/> Secure Payment via Stripe</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  )
}

PATH: ./src/app/contact/page.jsx

"use client";
import { useState } from 'react'
import Navbar from '@/components/sections/Navbar'
import Footer from '@/components/sections/Footer'
import { Mail, Phone, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useLanguage } from '@/context/LanguageContext'

export default function Contact() {
  const { t } = useLanguage()
  const [sent, setSent] = useState(false)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({ name: '', email: '', message: '' })

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setLoading(true)
    try {
      await supabase.from('contact_messages').insert([formData])
      setSent(true)
    } catch (error) {
      alert("Error sending message")
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-[#F8F9FC]">
      <Navbar />
      <main className="flex-grow pt-32 pb-20 px-4">
        <div className="max-w-6xl mx-auto grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-12">
          
          <div>
            <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-6">{t('contact_title')}</h1>
            <p className="text-gray-600 mb-8">{t('contact_desc')}</p>
            
            <div className="space-y-6">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-white rounded-full shadow-sm flex items-center justify-center text-brand-purple"><Mail /></div>
                <div>
                  <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">Email</p>
                  <p className="font-bold text-brand-dark">info@annyeong-academy.com</p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-white rounded-full shadow-sm flex items-center justify-center text-brand-blue"><Phone /></div>
                <div>
                  <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">Phone</p>
                  <p className="font-bold text-brand-dark">+36 30 123 4567</p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-white p-8 rounded-3xl shadow-lg">
            {sent ? (
              <div className="text-center py-12">
                <div className="text-5xl mb-4">✅</div>
                <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-brand-dark">{t('contact_sent')}</h3>
                <p className="text-gray-500">{t('contact_reply')}</p>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">{t('contact_name')}</label>
                  <input 
                    required 
                    type="text" 
                    className="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-purple outline-none"
                    value={formData.name}
                    onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, name: e.target.value})}
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">{t('auth_email')}</label>
                  <input 
                    required 
                    type="email" 
                    className="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-purple outline-none"
                    value={formData.email}
                    onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, email: e.target.value})}
                  />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">{t('contact_msg')}</label>
                  <textarea 
                    required 
                    rows="4" 
                    className="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-purple outline-none"
                    value={formData.message}
                    onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, message: e.target.value})}
                  ></textarea>
                </div>
                <button 
                  disabled={loading}
                  type="submit" 
                  className="w-full bg-brand-dark text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl hover:opacity-90 transition flex justify-center"
                >
                  {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" /> : t('btn_send')}
                </button>
              </form>
            )}
          </div>

        </div>
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  )
}

PATH: ./src/app/error.jsx
