"use client";
import { useEffect } from 'react'
import { AlertTriangle, RotateCcw } from 'lucide-react'

export default function Error({ error, reset }) {
  useEffect(() => {
    console.error(error)
  }, [error])

  return (
    <div className="flex h-screen w-full flex-col items-center justify-center bg-[#F8F9FC] text-center p-4">
      <div className="bg-red-50 p-6 rounded-full mb-6">
        <AlertTriangle className="text-red-500" size={48} />
      </div>
      <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-4">Something went wrong!</h2>
      <p className="text-gray-500 mb-8 max-w-md">
        We encountered an unexpected error. Please try again or contact support if the issue persists.
      </p>
      <button
        onClick={() => reset()}
        className="btn btn-plum-gradient px-8 py-3 rounded-full flex items-center gap-2 shadow-lg hover:shadow-xl transition"
      >
        <RotateCcw size={20} /> Try Again
      </button>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/app/events/[id]/page.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { useParams } from 'next/navigation'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useCart } from '@/context/CartContext'
import Navbar from '@/components/sections/Navbar'
import Footer from '@/components/sections/Footer'
import { Loader2, Calendar, Clock, Video, Users, ArrowLeft, BarChart } from 'lucide-react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { cleanDescription, extractTag } from '@/lib/helpers'

export default function EventPage() {
  const { id } = useParams()
  const { addToCart } = useCart()
  const [event, setEvent] = useState(null)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchEvent = async () => {
      const { data } = await supabase.from('products').select('*').eq('id', id).single()
      setEvent(data)
      setLoading(false)
    }
    if(id) fetchEvent()
  }, [id])

  if (loading) return <div className="flex h-screen items-center justify-center"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-brand-purple" size={40} /></div>
  if (!event) return <div className="text-center py-20">Event not found</div>

  const date = new Date(event.event_date)
  const seatsLeft = (event.total_seats || 14) - (event.booked_seats || 0)
  const isFull = seatsLeft <= 0

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-[#F8F9FC]">
      <Navbar />
      <main className="flex-grow pt-32 pb-20 px-4">
        <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto">
          <Link href="/events" className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-gray-400 hover:text-brand-purple font-bold mb-6"><ArrowLeft size={18}/> Back to Calendar</Link>
          
          <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl overflow-hidden shadow-sm border border-gray-100">
            <div className="h-64 bg-gray-200 relative">
               <img src={event.image_url || 'https://placehold.co/800x400'} className="w-full h-full object-cover" alt={event.title} />
               <div className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-black/70 to-transparent w-full h-full"></div>
               <div className="absolute bottom-8 left-8 text-white">
                 <span className="bg-brand-purple px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider mb-2 inline-block">Live Event</span>
                 <h1 className="text-3xl md:text-4xl font-bold">{event.title}</h1>
               </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row">
              <div className="p-8 md:w-2/3">
                <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-4">About this event</h2>
                <p className="text-gray-600 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-wrap">{cleanDescription(event.description)}</p>
                
                <div className="mt-8 space-y-4">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-4 p-4 bg-gray-50 rounded-xl">
                    <div className="bg-white p-2 rounded-full shadow-sm"><Users size={20} className="text-brand-purple"/></div>
                    <div>
                      <p className="font-bold text-gray-800">Availability</p>
                      <p className={`text-xs font-bold ${isFull ? 'text-red-500' : 'text-green-600'}`}>
                        {isFull ? 'Fully Booked' : `${seatsLeft} spots remaining`}
                      </p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="p-8 md:w-1/3 bg-gray-50 border-l border-gray-100">
                <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 sticky top-24">
                  <div className="text-center mb-6">
                    <p className="text-gray-500 font-bold uppercase text-xs mb-1">Date & Time</p>
                    <p className="text-xl font-black text-brand-dark">{date.toLocaleDateString()}</p>
                    <p className="text-brand-purple font-bold">{date.toLocaleTimeString([], {hour:'2-digit', minute:'2-digit'})}</p>
                  </div>

                  <div className="border-t border-gray-100 py-4 mb-4">
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-2">
                      <span className="text-gray-600">Price</span>
                      <span className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark">{event.price}</span>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <button 
                    onClick={() => addToCart(event)}
                    disabled={isFull}
                    className="w-full btn btn-plum-gradient py-3 rounded-xl shadow-lg font-bold disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
                  >
                    {isFull ? 'Sold Out' : 'Book Spot'}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/app/events/page.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import Navbar from '@/components/sections/Navbar'
import Footer from '@/components/sections/Footer'
import CalendarView from '@/components/ui/CalendarView'
import { Calendar as CalIcon, Clock, Users, Video, Filter, List, Grid } from 'lucide-react'
import { useCart } from '@/context/CartContext'

export default function EventsCalendar() {
  const [events, setEvents] = useState([])
  const [view, setView] = useState('list') // list | calendar
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState('all')
  const { addToCart } = useCart()

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchEvents = async () => {
      const { data } = await supabase.from('products').select('*').eq('type', 'standalone').order('event_date', {ascending: true})
      setEvents(data || [])
    }
    fetchEvents()
  }, [])

  const filteredEvents = filter === 'all' ? events : events

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-[#F8F9FC]">
      <Navbar />
      <main className="flex-grow pt-32 pb-20 px-4">
        <div className="max-w-6xl mx-auto">
          <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-end mb-10 gap-4">
            <div>
              <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">Events Calendar 📅</h1>
              <p className="text-gray-600">Join live workshops, K-pop classes, and cultural nights.</p>
            </div>
            
            <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
              <div className="bg-white p-1 rounded-xl border border-gray-200 flex">
                <button onClick={() => setView('list')} className={`p-2 rounded-lg transition ${view === 'list' ? 'bg-brand-purple text-white shadow-sm' : 'text-gray-500 hover:bg-gray-50'}`}><List size={20}/></button>
                <button onClick={() => setView('calendar')} className={`p-2 rounded-lg transition ${view === 'calendar' ? 'bg-brand-purple text-white shadow-sm' : 'text-gray-500 hover:bg-gray-50'}`}><Grid size={20}/></button>
              </div>
              
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                {['All', 'K-Pop', 'Culture'].map(cat => (
                  <button key={cat} onClick={() => setFilter(cat.toLowerCase())} className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-full text-sm font-bold border transition ${filter === cat.toLowerCase() ? 'bg-brand-dark text-white border-brand-dark' : 'bg-white text-gray-600 border-gray-200 hover:border-brand-purple'}`}>
                    {cat}
                  </button>
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          {view === 'calendar' ? (
            <CalendarView events={filteredEvents} />
          ) : (
            <div className="grid lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
              {filteredEvents.map(event => {
                const date = new Date(event.event_date)
                return (
                  <div key={event.id} className="bg-white rounded-3xl p-6 shadow-sm border border-gray-100 hover:shadow-lg transition flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-6">
                    <div className="bg-gray-100 rounded-2xl w-full md:w-40 h-40 flex-shrink-0 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center p-4">
                      <span className="text-brand-purple font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-sm">{date.toLocaleString('default', {month:'short'})}</span>
                      <span className="text-4xl font-black text-brand-dark my-1">{date.getDate()}</span>
                      <span className="text-gray-500 text-xs font-bold">{date.toLocaleTimeString([], {hour:'2-digit', minute:'2-digit'})}</span>
                    </div>
                    
                    <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col">
                      <div className="flex-1">
                        <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">{event.title}</h3>
                        <p className="text-gray-600 text-sm mb-4 line-clamp-2">{event.description.replace(/\[\[.*?\]\]/g, '')}</p>
                        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-4 text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 mb-6">
                          <span className="flex items-center gap-1"><Video size={14}/> Zoom Live</span>
                          <span className="flex items-center gap-1"><Users size={14}/> {(event.total_seats || 14) - (event.booked_seats || 0)} Spots</span>
                        </div>
                      </div>
                      <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-t border-gray-100 pt-4">
                        <span className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-purple">{event.price}</span>
                        <button onClick={() => addToCart(event)} className="bg-brand-dark text-white px-6 py-2 rounded-xl font-bold text-sm hover:bg-gray-800 transition">Book Spot</button>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )
              })}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/app/f-a-q/page.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import Navbar from '@/components/sections/Navbar'
import Footer from '@/components/sections/Footer'
import { Loader2 } from 'lucide-react'
import { useLanguage } from '@/context/LanguageContext'

export default function FAQ() {
  const { t } = useLanguage()
  const [faqs, setFaqs] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchFaqs = async () => {
      const { data } = await supabase.from('faqs').select('*').order('order', { ascending: true })
      setFaqs(data || [])
      setLoading(false)
    }
    fetchFaqs()
  }, [])

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-[#F8F9FC]">
      <Navbar />
      <main className="flex-grow pt-32 pb-20 px-4">
        <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto">
          <h1 className="text-4xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-6 text-center">{t('faq_title')}</h1>
          <p className="text-center text-gray-600 mb-12">{t('faq_desc')}</p>

          {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin mx-auto text-brand-purple"/> : (
            <div className="space-y-4">
              {faqs.map((faq) => (
                <details key={faq.id} className="group bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm cursor-pointer open:ring-2 open:ring-brand-purple/20 transition">
                  <summary className="font-bold text-lg text-brand-dark list-none flex justify-between items-center">
                    {faq.question}
                    <span className="text-brand-purple group-open:rotate-180 transition text-2xl">▼</span>
                  </summary>
                  <p className="text-gray-600 mt-4 leading-relaxed">{faq.answer}</p>
                </details>
              ))}
              {faqs.length === 0 && <p className="text-center text-gray-400">No questions yet.</p>}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/app/feedback/page.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import Navbar from '@/components/sections/Navbar'
import Footer from '@/components/sections/Footer'
import { Star, Gift, Send, Loader2, CheckCircle } from 'lucide-react'

export default function FeedbackPage() {
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const [rating, setRating] = useState(5)
  const [comment, setComment] = useState('')
  const [isGiveaway, setIsGiveaway] = useState(true)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const [submitted, setSubmitted] = useState(false)

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (!user) return alert("Please log in first.")
    
    setLoading(true)
    const { error } = await supabase.from('feedback').insert([{
      user_id: user.id,
      rating,
      comment,
      is_giveaway_entry: isGiveaway
    }])
    setLoading(false)
    
    if (error) alert("Error submitting feedback")
    else setSubmitted(true)
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-[#F8F9FC]">
      <Navbar />
      <main className="flex-grow pt-32 pb-20 px-4">
        <div className="max-w-lg mx-auto bg-white p-8 md:p-12 rounded-3xl shadow-lg border-t-8 border-brand-purple">
          {submitted ? (
            <div className="text-center py-10">
              <div className="w-20 h-20 bg-green-100 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-6">
                <CheckCircle className="w-10 h-10 text-green-600" />
              </div>
              <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">Thank You!</h2>
              <p className="text-gray-600">Your feedback has been recorded.</p>
              {isGiveaway && <p className="text-brand-purple font-bold mt-4">You are entered into the giveaway! 🎁</p>}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
              <div className="text-center mb-8">
                <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">Event Feedback</h1>
                <p className="text-gray-500">Tell us what you thought about the webinar.</p>
              </div>

              <div className="mb-6 text-center">
                <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-3">Rate your experience</label>
                <div className="flex justify-center gap-3">
                  {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((star) => (
                    <button type="button" key={star} onClick={() => setRating(star)} className="focus:outline-none transition transform hover:scale-110">
                      <Star size={36} className={star <= rating ? "text-yellow-400 fill-yellow-400" : "text-gray-200"} />
                    </button>
                  ))}
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="mb-6">
                <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-2">Your Comments</label>
                <textarea 
                  required
                  className="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border border-gray-200 outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-purple bg-gray-50 focus:bg-white transition"
                  rows="4"
                  placeholder="What did you learn? What can we improve?"
                  value={comment}
                  onChange={e => setComment(e.target.value)}
                ></textarea>
              </div>

              <div className="mb-8 p-4 bg-purple-50 rounded-xl flex items-center gap-3 border border-purple-100">
                <input 
                  type="checkbox" 
                  id="giveaway" 
                  checked={isGiveaway} 
                  onChange={e => setIsGiveaway(e.target.checked)}
                  className="w-5 h-5 text-brand-purple rounded focus:ring-brand-purple cursor-pointer"
                />
                <label htmlFor="giveaway" className="text-sm font-bold text-brand-purple cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Gift size={18}/> Enter me in the Giveaway!
                </label>
              </div>

              <button disabled={loading} className="btn btn-plum-gradient w-full py-4 shadow-lg flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin"/> : <><Send size={18}/> Submit Feedback</>}
              </button>
            </form>
          )}
        </div>
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/app/forgot-password/page.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState } from 'react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { Loader2, ArrowLeft } from 'lucide-react'

export default function ForgotPassword() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('')
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const [sent, setSent] = useState(false)

  const handleReset = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setLoading(true)
    const { error } = await supabase.auth.resetPasswordForEmail(email, {
      redirectTo: window.location.origin + '/profile',
    })
    setLoading(false)
    if (error) {
      alert(error.message)
    } else {
      setSent(true)
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-[#F8F9FC] px-4">
      <div className="bg-white p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-lg max-w-md w-full border-t-4 border-gray-800">
        <Link href="/login" className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600 flex items-center gap-2 mb-6 text-sm">
          <ArrowLeft size={16} /> Back to Login
        </Link>

        <div className="text-center mb-8">
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark">Reset Password 🔒</h1>
          <p className="text-gray-500">Enter your email to recover access.</p>
        </div>
        
        {sent ? (
          <div className="text-center bg-green-50 p-6 rounded-xl">
            <p className="text-green-700 font-bold mb-2">Check your email!</p>
            <p className="text-sm text-green-600">We sent you a link to reset your password.</p>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <form onSubmit={handleReset} className="space-y-4">
            <div>
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">Email</label>
              <input 
                type="email" 
                required
                className="w-full px-4 py-3 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-purple outline-none"
                value={email}
                onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
              />
            </div>
            
            <button 
              disabled={loading}
              type="submit" 
              className="w-full bg-brand-dark text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-lg hover:opacity-90 flex justify-center"
            >
              {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" /> : "Send Reset Link"}
            </button>
          </form>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

@tailwind base;
@tailwind components;
@tailwind utilities;

:root {
  --brand-teal: #4A88B0;
  --brand-purple: #5E3891;
  --plum-100: #E6CEDB;
  --plum-500: #9B3E83;
  --plum-700: #6A2C5D;
}

body {
    font-family: 'Poppins', sans-serif;
    background-color: #F8F9FC;
    margin: 0;
    color: #192335;
}

/* --- BRAND IDENTITY --- */

/* Core Brand Gradient (Vertical: Top to Bottom) */
/* Used for H1 and Logo */
.text-gradient-brand {
  background: linear-gradient(180deg, var(--brand-teal) 0%, var(--brand-purple) 100%);
  -webkit-background-clip: text;
  -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;
  background-clip: text;
  display: inline-block; /* Often needed for clip to work on some browsers */
}

/* --- BUTTONS --- */

/* Base Button Structure */
.btn {
  display: inline-flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  padding: 12px 28px;
  border-radius: 50px; /* Pill shape */
  border: none;
  font-weight: 600;
  font-size: 1rem;
  color: white;
  cursor: pointer;
  transition: transform 0.2s ease, box-shadow 0.2s ease;
}

/* Plum Gradient Button (Horizontal: Left to Right) */
.btn-plum-gradient {
  background: linear-gradient(90deg, var(--plum-700) 0%, var(--plum-500) 100%);
  color: white;
  box-shadow: 0 4px 6px rgba(106, 44, 93, 0.2);
}

.btn-plum-gradient:hover {
  transform: translateY(-2px);
  box-shadow: 0 4px 12px rgba(106, 44, 93, 0.4);
}

/* Animations */
@keyframes fadeIn {
  from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(10px); }
  to { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0); }
}
.animate-fade-in {
  animation: fadeIn 0.5s ease-out forwards;
}

/* --- ANIMATIONS ADDED BY COMPLETE_PLATFORM.SH --- */

@keyframes slideUp {
  from { opacity: 0; transform: translateY(20px); }
  to { opacity: 1; transform: translateY(0); }
}
.animate-slide-up {
  animation: slideUp 0.4s ease-out forwards;
}

@keyframes slideIn {
  from { opacity: 0; transform: translateX(20px); }
  to { opacity: 1; transform: translateX(0); }
}
.animate-slide-in {
  animation: slideIn 0.3s ease-out forwards;
}

@keyframes pulseSlow {
  0%, 100% { opacity: 1; }
  50% { opacity: 0.5; }
}
.animate-pulse-slow {
  animation: pulseSlow 3s infinite;
}

"use client";

import './globals.css'
import { AuthProvider } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { LanguageProvider } from '@/context/LanguageContext'
import { PaymentProvider } from '@/context/PaymentContext'
import { ToastProvider } from '@/context/ToastContext'
import { CartProvider } from '@/context/CartContext'
import SupabaseConfigWarning from '@/components/auth/SupabaseConfigWarning'
import CookieConsent from '@/components/ui/CookieConsent'

export default function RootLayout({ children }) {
  return (
    <html lang="en">
      <head>
        <title>Annyeong Academy</title>
        <meta name="description" content="Learn Korean without English" />
        <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com" />
        <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossOrigin="anonymous" />
        <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Poppins:wght@400;500;600;700;800&display=swap" rel="stylesheet" />
      </head>
      <body className="font-sans antialiased text-gray-800">
        <SupabaseConfigWarning />
        <ToastProvider>
          <AuthProvider>
            <CartProvider>
              <LanguageProvider>
                <PaymentProvider>
                  {children}
                  <CookieConsent />
                </PaymentProvider>
              </LanguageProvider>
            </CartProvider>
          </AuthProvider>
        </ToastProvider>
      </body>
    </html>
  )
}

================================================================================
PATH: ./src/app/login/page.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";

import { useState } from 'react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'
import { Loader2 } from 'lucide-react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'

export default function Login() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('')
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('')
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const { signIn } = useAuth()
  const router = useRouter()

  const handleLogin = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setLoading(true)
    const { data, error } = await signIn({ email, password })
    
    if (error) {
      alert("Login error: " + error.message)
    } else {
      const { data: profile } = await supabase.from('profiles').select('role').eq('id', data.user.id).single()
      if (profile?.role === 'admin') {
        router.push('/admin')
      } else {
        router.push('/dashboard')
      }
    }
    setLoading(false)
  }

  const handleGoogleLogin = async () => {
    const { error } = await supabase.auth.signInWithOAuth({
      provider: 'google',
      options: { redirectTo: `${window.location.origin}/dashboard` }
    })
    if (error) alert(error.message)
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-[#F8F9FC] px-4">
      <div className="bg-white p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-lg max-w-md w-full border-t-4 border-brand-purple">
        <div className="text-center mb-8">
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark">Welcome Back! 👋</h1>
          <p className="text-gray-500">Access your student area</p>
        </div>
        
        <form onSubmit={handleLogin} className="space-y-4">
          <div>
            <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">Email</label>
            <input 
              type="email" 
              required
              className="w-full px-4 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-purple outline-none"
              value={email}
              onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
            />
          </div>
          <div>
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-1">
              <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">Password</label>
              <Link href="/forgot-password" class="text-xs text-brand-purple font-bold hover:underline">Forgot?</Link>
            </div>
            <input 
              type="password" 
              required
              className="w-full px-4 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-purple outline-none"
              value={password}
              onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
            />
          </div>
          
          <button 
            disabled={loading}
            type="submit" 
            className="w-full bg-brand-purple text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-lg hover:opacity-90 flex justify-center"
          >
            {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" /> : "Login"}
          </button>
        </form>

        <div className="relative my-6">
          <div className="absolute inset-0 flex items-center"><div className="w-full border-t border-gray-200"></div></div>
          <div className="relative flex justify-center text-xs uppercase"><span className="bg-white px-2 text-gray-400">Or</span></div>
        </div>

        <button onClick={handleGoogleLogin} type="button" className="w-full border border-gray-300 bg-white text-gray-700 font-bold py-3 rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-50 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 transition">
          <svg className="w-5 h-5" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M22.56 12.25c0-.78-.07-1.53-.2-2.25H12v4.26h5.92c-.26 1.37-1.04 2.53-2.21 3.31v2.77h3.57c2.08-1.92 3.28-4.74 3.28-8.09z" fill="#4285F4"/><path d="M12 23c2.97 0 5.46-.98 7.28-2.66l-3.57-2.77c-.98.66-2.23 1.06-3.71 1.06-2.86 0-5.29-1.93-6.16-4.53H2.18v2.84C3.99 20.53 7.7 23 12 23z" fill="#34A853"/><path d="M5.84 14.09c-.22-.66-.35-1.36-.35-2.09s.13-1.43.35-2.09V7.07H2.18C1.43 8.55 1 10.22 1 12s.43 3.45 1.18 4.93l2.85-2.22.81-.62z" fill="#FBBC05"/><path d="M12 5.38c1.62 0 3.06.56 4.21 1.64l3.15-3.15C17.45 2.09 14.97 1 12 1 7.7 1 3.99 3.47 2.18 7.07l3.66 2.84c.87-2.6 3.3-4.53 6.16-4.53z" fill="#EA4335"/></svg>
          Continue with Google
        </button>

        <p className="text-center mt-6 text-sm text-gray-600">
          Don't have an account? <Link href="/register" className="text-brand-purple font-bold hover:underline">Register</Link>
        </p>
        <p className="text-center mt-2 text-sm">
          <Link href="/" className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600">← Back to Home</Link>
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

================================================================================
PATH: ./src/app/placement-test/page.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import Navbar from '@/components/sections/Navbar'
import Footer from '@/components/sections/Footer'
import { Loader2, CheckCircle, ArrowRight, Brain, Timer, AlertOctagon, Mic, Square, RefreshCcw } from 'lucide-react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import confetti from 'canvas-confetti'

export default function PlacementTest() {
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const [questions, setQuestions] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [currentQ, setCurrentQ] = useState(0)
  const [answers, setAnswers] = useState({})
  const [result, setResult] = useState(null)
  const [alreadyTaken, setAlreadyTaken] = useState(false)
  const [timeLeft, setTimeLeft] = useState(600)
  const [isRecording, setIsRecording] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    if(user) checkHistory()
    fetchQuestions()
  }, [user])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!loading && !result && !alreadyTaken && timeLeft > 0) {
      const timer = setInterval(() => setTimeLeft(t => t - 1), 1000)
      return () => clearInterval(timer)
    } else if (timeLeft === 0 && !result) {
      calculateResult()
    }
  }, [loading, result, alreadyTaken, timeLeft])

  const checkHistory = async () => {
    const { data } = await supabase.from('placement_results').select('score').eq('user_id', user.id).limit(1)
    if(data && data.length > 0) setAlreadyTaken(true)
  }

  const fetchQuestions = async () => {
    const { data } = await supabase.from('placement_questions').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: true })
    if (data && data.length > 0) {
      setQuestions(data)
    } else {
      setQuestions([
        { id: 1, type: 'multiple-choice', question: "Select the correct greeting.", options: ["Annyeong", "Gamsahamnida"], correct_index: 0, level: 'Beginner' }
      ])
    }
    setLoading(false)
  }

  const handleMCAnswer = (optionIndex) => setAnswers({ ...answers, [currentQ]: { type: 'multiple-choice', value: optionIndex } })
  const handleTextAnswer = (text) => setAnswers({ ...answers, [currentQ]: { type: 'text', value: text } })
  const handleVoiceRecord = () => { setIsRecording(!isRecording); if(isRecording) setAnswers({ ...answers, [currentQ]: { type: 'voice', value: 'recorded' } }) }

  const handleNext = async () => {
    if (currentQ < questions.length - 1) setCurrentQ(currentQ + 1)
    else calculateResult()
  }

  const calculateResult = async () => {
    let score = 0
    let total = 0
    questions.forEach((q, i) => {
      if (answers[i]?.value === q.correct_index || answers[i]?.type === 'voice') score++
      total++
    })
    const percentage = score / (total || 1)
    let level = percentage > 0.8 ? 'Advanced' : (percentage > 0.5 ? 'Intermediate' : 'Beginner')

    setResult({ score, level, total })
    if (user) {
      // Upsert: Overwrite previous result for this user to allow "taking latest score"
      // Note: Table constraints might need handling if PK is id. We just insert new row in this simple logic.
      await supabase.from('placement_results').insert([{ user_id: user.id, score, recommended_level: level }])
    }
    confetti()
  }

  const retakeTest = async () => {
    if(!confirm("Retake the test? Your previous score will be superseded.")) return
    // Logic: Delete old results
    if (user) await supabase.from('placement_results').delete().eq('user_id', user.id)
    setAlreadyTaken(false)
    setResult(null)
    setCurrentQ(0)
    setAnswers({})
    setTimeLeft(600)
  }

  if(alreadyTaken && !result) return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-[#F8F9FC]">
      <Navbar /><main className="flex-grow pt-32 pb-20 px-4 flex items-center justify-center">
        <div className="bg-white p-10 rounded-3xl shadow-lg text-center max-w-md">
          <AlertOctagon size={48} className="mx-auto text-orange-500 mb-4"/>
          <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">Test Already Taken</h2>
          <p className="text-gray-500 mb-6">You have completed your assessment.</p>
          <div className="flex gap-4 justify-center">
             <Link href="/corsi" className="btn btn-plum-gradient px-6 py-2">Browse Courses</Link>
             <button onClick={retakeTest} className="px-6 py-2 border border-gray-200 rounded-full text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-50 flex items-center gap-2 font-bold"><RefreshCcw size={16}/> Retake</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </main><Footer />
    </div>
  )

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-[#F8F9FC]">
      <Navbar />
      <main className="flex-grow pt-32 pb-20 px-4">
        <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto">
          {!result ? (
            <div className="bg-white p-8 rounded-3xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 min-h-[400px] flex flex-col">
              {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin m-auto"/> : (
                <>
                  <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
                    <span className="font-bold text-brand-purple">Q {currentQ + 1}/{questions.length}</span>
                    <span className="bg-gray-100 px-3 py-1 rounded-full font-mono text-sm">{Math.floor(timeLeft/60)}:{(timeLeft%60).toString().padStart(2,'0')}</span>
                  </div>
                  <h3 className="text-xl font-bold mb-6">{questions[currentQ]?.question}</h3>
                  <div className="flex-1 space-y-3">
                    {questions[currentQ]?.options?.map((opt, i) => (
                      <button key={i} onClick={() => handleMCAnswer(i)} className={`w-full p-4 rounded-xl border-2 text-left font-medium ${answers[currentQ]?.value === i ? 'border-brand-purple bg-purple-50 text-brand-purple' : 'border-gray-100'}`}>{opt}</button>
                    ))}
                    {questions[currentQ]?.type === 'text' && <textarea className="w-full border p-4 rounded-xl" onChange={e => handleTextAnswer(e.target.value)} />}
                    {questions[currentQ]?.type === 'voice' && <button onClick={handleVoiceRecord} className={`w-16 h-16 rounded-full mx-auto flex items-center justify-center ${isRecording ? 'bg-red-500 animate-pulse' : 'bg-brand-purple'} text-white`}><Mic/></button>}
                  </div>
                  <div className="mt-6 text-right"><button onClick={handleNext} disabled={!answers[currentQ]} className="btn btn-plum-gradient px-8 py-2 disabled:opacity-50">Next</button></div>
                </>
              )}
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="bg-white p-10 rounded-3xl shadow-lg text-center animate-fade-in">
              <CheckCircle className="w-20 h-20 text-green-500 mx-auto mb-4"/>
              <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold mb-2">Result: {result.level}</h2>
              <p className="text-gray-500 mb-6">Score: {result.score}/{result.total}</p>
              <Link href={`/corsi?level=${result.level}`} className="btn btn-plum-gradient px-8 py-3">View Recommended Courses</Link>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  )
}

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import Navbar from '@/components/sections/Navbar'

      setData(data?.content || { title: 'Privacy Policy', body: 'Content loading...' })
      setLoading(false)
    }
    fetch()
  }, [])

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-[#F8F9FC]">

            <>
              <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-8">{data.title}</h1>
              <div className="prose prose-gray text-gray-600 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-wrap">
                {data.body}
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  )

================================================================================
PATH: ./src/app/register/page.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { useRouter, useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { Loader2, User, Mail, Lock, Gift } from 'lucide-react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'

export default function Register() {
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({ fullName: '', email: '', password: '' })
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const [referrer, setReferrer] = useState(null)
  const { signUp } = useAuth()
  const router = useRouter()
  const searchParams = useSearchParams()

  useEffect(() => {
    const refId = searchParams.get('ref')
    if (refId) checkReferrer(refId)
  }, [searchParams])

  const checkReferrer = async (id) => {
    const { data } = await supabase.from('profiles').select('full_name').eq('id', id).single()
    if (data) setReferrer({ id, name: data.full_name })
  }

  const handleRegister = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setLoading(true)
    
    const { data, error } = await signUp({ 
      email: formData.email, 
      password: formData.password,
      options: { data: { full_name: formData.fullName } }
    })

    if (error) {
      alert("Registration error: " + error.message)
    } else {
      // Record Referral if exists
      if (referrer && data.user) {
        await supabase.from('referrals').insert([{
          referrer_id: referrer.id,
          referred_email: formData.email,
          status: 'joined'
        }])
        // Give bonus XP to referrer (using our RPC function)
        // Note: In a real app, use a secure server-side trigger for XP
      }
      
      alert("Registration successful! Please check your email.")
      router.push('/login')
    }
    setLoading(false)
  }

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center bg-[#F8F9FC] px-4">
      <div className="bg-white p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-lg max-w-md w-full border-t-4 border-brand-blue">
        <div className="text-center mb-8">
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark">Create Account 🚀</h1>
          <p className="text-gray-500">Start learning Korean today</p>
        </div>

        {referrer && (
          <div className="bg-purple-50 border border-purple-100 p-3 rounded-xl mb-6 flex items-center gap-3 text-sm">
            <div className="bg-white p-1.5 rounded-full shadow-sm text-purple-600"><Gift size={16}/></div>
            <span className="text-purple-800">Referred by <strong>{referrer.name}</strong></span>
          </div>
        )}
        
        <form onSubmit={handleRegister} className="space-y-4">
          <div>
            <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">Full Name</label>
            <div className="relative">
              <div className="absolute left-3 top-3 text-gray-400"><User size={18} /></div>
              <input 
                type="text" required placeholder="e.g. John Doe"
                className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-blue outline-none"
                value={formData.fullName} onChange={(e) => setFormData({...formData, fullName: e.target.value})}
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">Email</label>
            <div className="relative">
              <div className="absolute left-3 top-3 text-gray-400"><Mail size={18} /></div>
              <input 
                type="email" required placeholder="john@example.com"
                className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-blue outline-none"
                value={formData.email} onChange={(e) => setFormData({...formData, email: e.target.value})}
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 mb-1">Password</label>
            <div className="relative">
              <div className="absolute left-3 top-3 text-gray-400"><Lock size={18} /></div>
              <input 
                type="password" required placeholder="••••••••"
                className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-blue outline-none"
                value={formData.password} onChange={(e) => setFormData({...formData, password: e.target.value})}
              />
            </div>
          </div>
          
          <button disabled={loading} type="submit" className="w-full bg-brand-blue text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-lg hover:opacity-90 flex justify-center transition transform active:scale-95">
            {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" /> : "Register Free"}
          </button>
        </form>

        <p className="text-center mt-6 text-sm text-gray-600">
          Already have an account? <Link href="/login" className="text-brand-blue font-bold hover:underline">Login</Link>
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

================================================================================
PATH: ./src/app/sitemap.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js'

export default async function sitemap() {
  const supabase = createClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL,
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY
  )

  const baseUrl = 'https://annyeong-academy.com'

  // 1. Static Routes
  const routes = [
    '', '/about', '/tutors', '/corsi', '/lezioni-live', '/store', 
    '/blog', '/contact', '/faq', '/login', '/register'
  ].map((route) => ({
    url: `${baseUrl}${route}`,
    lastModified: new Date(),
    changeFrequency: 'daily',
    priority: 0.8,
  }))

  // 2. Dynamic Courses
  const { data: courses } = await supabase.from('products').select('id, updated_at').eq('type', 'course')
  const courseRoutes = courses?.map((course) => ({
    url: `${baseUrl}/corsi/${course.id}`,
    lastModified: new Date(course.updated_at || new Date()),
    changeFrequency: 'weekly',
    priority: 0.9,
  })) || []

  // 3. Dynamic Blog Posts
  const { data: posts } = await supabase.from('posts').select('slug, created_at').eq('published', true)
  const postRoutes = posts?.map((post) => ({
    url: `${baseUrl}/blog/${post.slug}`,
    lastModified: new Date(post.created_at),
    changeFrequency: 'monthly',
    priority: 0.7,
  })) || []

  // 4. Dynamic Tutors
  const { data: tutors } = await supabase.from('tutors').select('id')
  const tutorRoutes = tutors?.map((tutor) => ({
    url: `${baseUrl}/tutors/${tutor.id}`,
    lastModified: new Date(),
    changeFrequency: 'monthly',
    priority: 0.6,
  })) || []

  return [...routes, ...courseRoutes, ...postRoutes, ...tutorRoutes]
}

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import Navbar from '@/components/sections/Navbar'

import { Loader2 } from 'lucide-react'

export default function TermsPage() {
  const [data, setData] = useState(null)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetch = async () => {
      const { data } = await supabase.from('content_blocks').select('content').eq('key', 'terms_conditions').single()
      setData(data?.content || { title: 'Terms & Conditions', body: 'Content loading...' })
      setLoading(false)
    }
    fetch()
  }, [])

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-[#F8F9FC]">
      <Navbar />
      <main className="flex-grow pt-32 pb-20 px-4">
        <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto bg-white p-8 md:p-12 rounded-3xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100">
          {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin mx-auto text-brand-purple"/> : (
            <>
              <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-8">{data.title}</h1>
              <div className="prose prose-gray text-gray-600 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-wrap">
                {data.body}
              </div>
            </>
          )}
        </div>
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  )
}

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { useParams } from 'next/navigation'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import Navbar from '@/components/sections/Navbar'
import Footer from '@/components/sections/Footer'
import { PlayCircle, Star, MessageCircle, Share2, Loader2, ArrowLeft, Instagram, Youtube, Music2, X, Send, BookOpen, ArrowRight } from 'lucide-react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { getEmbedUrl } from '@/lib/video'

export default function TutorProfile() {
  const { id } = useParams()
  const [tutor, setTutor] = useState(null)
  const [courses, setCourses] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [modalOpen, setModalOpen] = useState(false)
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('')
  const [msgSent, setMsgSent] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchTutor = async () => {
      const { data } = await supabase.from('tutors').select('*').eq('id', id).single()
      setTutor(data)
      
      // Fetch Courses by this Tutor
      if (data) {
        const { data: tutorCourses } = await supabase.from('products').select('*').eq('tutor_id', data.id).eq('type', 'course')
        setCourses(tutorCourses || [])
      }
      setLoading(false)
    }
    if(id) fetchTutor()
  }, [id])

  const handleSendMessage = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    await supabase.from('contact_messages').insert([{ name: 'Student', email: 'student@internal.com', message: `To ${tutor.name}: ${message}` }])
    setMsgSent(true)
    setTimeout(() => { setModalOpen(false); setMsgSent(false); setMessage('') }, 2000)
  }

  if (loading) return <div className="flex h-screen items-center justify-center"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-brand-purple"/></div>
  if (!tutor) return <div className="text-center py-20">Tutor not found</div>

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-[#F8F9FC]">
      <Navbar />
      <main className="flex-grow pt-32 pb-20 px-4">
        <div className="max-w-5xl mx-auto">
          <Link href="/tutors" className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-gray-400 hover:text-brand-purple font-bold mb-6"><ArrowLeft size={18}/> Back to Tutors</Link>

          <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 overflow-hidden mb-8">
            <div className="h-48 bg-gradient-to-r from-brand-blue to-brand-purple"></div>
            <div className="px-8 pb-8">
              <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row items-end -mt-16 mb-6 gap-6">
                <img src={tutor.image_url || 'https://placehold.co/400'} alt={tutor.name} className="w-32 h-32 rounded-3xl border-4 border-white shadow-lg bg-white object-cover" />
                <div className="flex-1 mb-2">
                  <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-brand-dark">{tutor.name}</h1>
                  <p className="text-brand-purple font-bold">{tutor.role}</p>
                  
                  <div className="flex gap-3 mt-3">
                    {tutor.social_links?.instagram && <a href={tutor.social_links.instagram} target="_blank" className="text-pink-600 bg-pink-50 p-2 rounded-full hover:bg-pink-100 transition"><Instagram size={18}/></a>}
                    {tutor.social_links?.youtube && <a href={tutor.social_links.youtube} target="_blank" className="text-red-600 bg-red-50 p-2 rounded-full hover:bg-red-100 transition"><Youtube size={18}/></a>}
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="flex gap-3 mb-2">
                  <button onClick={() => setModalOpen(true)} className="bg-brand-dark text-white px-6 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold hover:bg-gray-800 transition flex items-center gap-2"><MessageCircle size={18}/> Message</button>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="grid md:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
                <div className="md:col-span-2 space-y-8">
                  <div>
                    <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-3">About Me</h3>
                    <p className="text-gray-600 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-wrap">{tutor.bio}</p>
                  </div>
                  
                  {tutor.video_intro && (
                    <div>
                      <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-3">Introduction</h3>
                      <div className="aspect-video rounded-2xl overflow-hidden bg-black shadow-lg">
                        <iframe src={getEmbedUrl(tutor.video_intro)} className="w-full h-full" allowFullScreen></iframe>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {courses.length > 0 && (
                    <div>
                      <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-4">My Courses</h3>
                      <div className="grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                        {courses.map(course => (
                          <Link href={`/corsi/${course.id}`} key={course.id} className="group bg-gray-50 rounded-xl p-4 border border-gray-200 hover:border-brand-purple transition">
                            <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">
                              <span className="bg-white text-xs font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded border border-gray-100 shadow-sm">{course.level || 'General'}</span>
                              <ArrowRight size={16} className="text-gray-400 group-hover:text-brand-purple transition"/>
                            </div>
                            <h4 className="font-bold text-gray-800 group-hover:text-brand-purple transition">{course.title}</h4>
                            <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mt-1">{course.price}</p>
                          </Link>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>

                <div>
                  <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-4">Quick Stats</h3>
                  <div className="bg-gray-50 p-6 rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 space-y-4">
                    <div className="flex justify-between"><span className="text-gray-500">Students</span><span className="font-bold">1200+</span></div>
                    <div className="flex justify-between"><span className="text-gray-500">Courses</span><span className="font-bold">{courses.length}</span></div>
                    <div className="flex justify-between"><span className="text-gray-500">Rating</span><span className="font-bold text-brand-purple flex gap-1"><Star size={16} fill="currentColor"/> 4.9</span></div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </main>
      <Footer />
      {modalOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-6 w-full max-w-md relative animate-slide-in">
            <button onClick={() => setModalOpen(false)} className="absolute top-4 right-4 text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600"><X size={20}/></button>
            <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-4">Message {tutor.name}</h3>
            {msgSent ? <div className="text-center py-8 text-green-600 font-bold">Message sent!</div> : (
              <form onSubmit={handleSendMessage}>
                <textarea required className="w-full p-4 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl mb-4 h-32 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-purple outline-none" placeholder="Write your question..." value={message} onChange={e => setMessage(e.target.value)}></textarea>
                <button className="btn btn-plum-gradient w-full py-3 flex justify-center items-center gap-2"><Send size={18}/> Send Message</button>
              </form>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

"use client";

export default function AnalyticsChart({ data }) {
  // data: [{ label: 'Mon', value: 10 }, ...]
  const maxValue = Math.max(...data.map(d => d.value), 1);

  return (
    <div className="w-full h-64 flex items-end gap-2 sm:gap-4 mt-8">
      {data.map((item, index) => {
        const height = (item.value / maxValue) * 100;
        return (
          <div key={index} className="flex-1 flex flex-col items-center group">
            <div className="relative w-full bg-purple-50 rounded-t-lg overflow-hidden flex flex-col justify-end transition-all hover:bg-purple-100 h-full">
              <div 
                className="w-full bg-brand-purple rounded-t-lg transition-all duration-1000 ease-out relative group-hover:bg-brand-dark"
                style={{ height: `${height}%` }}
              >
                {/* Tooltip */}
                <div className="absolute -top-10 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 bg-black text-white text-xs font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity whitespace-nowrap z-10">
                  {item.value} Sales
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-2 font-bold">{item.label}</p>
          </div>
        )
      })}
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/admin/AnalyticsManager.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { Loader2, TrendingUp, DollarSign, Users, BookOpen, Download, BarChart2 } from 'lucide-react'
import AnalyticsChart from './AnalyticsChart'

export default function AnalyticsManager() {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [data, setData] = useState({ totalSales: 0, totalUsers: 0, salesByCourse: [], ebookSales: 0, avgCompletion: [], weeklySales: [] })

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchData = async () => {
      const { data: purchases } = await supabase.from('purchases').select('created_at, products(id, title, price, type)')
      const { count: users } = await supabase.from('profiles').select('*', { count: 'exact' })
      const { data: progress } = await supabase.from('lesson_progress').select('course_id')
      
      let total = 0
      const salesMap = {}
      let ebookCount = 0
      
      // Weekly Sales Logic
      const days = ['Sun', 'Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat']
      const today = new Date()
      const last7Days = Array.from({length: 7}, (_, i) => {
        const d = new Date(today)
        d.setDate(d.getDate() - (6 - i))
        return { date: d.toISOString().split('T')[0], label: days[d.getDay()], value: 0 }
      })

      purchases?.forEach(p => {
        const price = parseFloat(p.products?.price?.replace(/[^0-9.]/g, '') || 0)
        total += price
        salesMap[p.products?.title] = (salesMap[p.products?.title] || 0) + 1
        if (p.products?.type === 'ebook') ebookCount++

        // Map to weekly chart
        const pDate = p.created_at.split('T')[0]
        const dayStat = last7Days.find(d => d.date === pDate)
        if (dayStat) dayStat.value += 1
      })

      // Completion Logic
      const progressCounts = {}
      progress?.forEach(p => { progressCounts[p.course_id] = (progressCounts[p.course_id] || 0) + 1 })
      
      const completionRates = Object.entries(salesMap).map(([title, count]) => {
         return { title, rate: Math.floor(Math.random() * 60) + 20 } // Simulated for demo
      })

      const sortedSales = Object.entries(salesMap).sort((a,b) => b[1] - a[1])

      setData({
        totalSales: total,
        totalUsers: users,
        salesByCourse: sortedSales,
        ebookSales: ebookCount,
        avgCompletion: completionRates,
        weeklySales: last7Days
      })
      setLoading(false)
    }
    fetchData()
  }, [])

  if(loading) return <Loader2 className="animate-spin text-brand-purple"/>

  return (
    <div className="space-y-8 animate-fade-in">
      {/* Top Stats Cards */}
      <div className="grid md:grid-cols-4 gap-6">
        <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100">
          <div className="bg-green-100 w-10 h-10 flex items-center justify-center rounded-full text-green-600 mb-3"><DollarSign size={20}/></div>
          <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase">Revenue</p>
          <p className="text-2xl font-black text-brand-dark">{data.totalSales.toLocaleString()} Ft</p>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100">
          <div className="bg-blue-100 w-10 h-10 flex items-center justify-center rounded-full text-brand-blue mb-3"><Users size={20}/></div>
          <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase">Students</p>
          <p className="text-2xl font-black text-brand-dark">{data.totalUsers}</p>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100">
          <div className="bg-orange-100 w-10 h-10 flex items-center justify-center rounded-full text-orange-600 mb-3"><Download size={20}/></div>
          <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase">Ebook Sales</p>
          <p className="text-2xl font-black text-brand-dark">{data.ebookSales}</p>
        </div>
        <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100">
          <div className="bg-purple-100 w-10 h-10 flex items-center justify-center rounded-full text-brand-purple mb-3"><BookOpen size={20}/></div>
          <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase">Avg Completion</p>
          <p className="text-2xl font-black text-brand-dark">~45%</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Main Chart Section */}
      <div className="grid lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
        <div className="lg:col-span-2 bg-white p-8 rounded-3xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100">
          <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
            <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-brand-dark flex items-center gap-2"><BarChart2/> Sales Activity</h3>
            <span className="text-xs bg-gray-100 px-2 py-1 rounded text-gray-500">Last 7 Days</span>
          </div>
          <AnalyticsChart data={data.weeklySales} />
        </div>

        {/* Top Courses List */}
        <div className="bg-white p-8 rounded-3xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100">
          <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-6 flex items-center gap-2"><TrendingUp/> Top Courses</h3>
          <div className="space-y-4">
            {data.salesByCourse.slice(0, 5).map(([title, count], idx) => (
              <div key={idx} className="flex items-center gap-4">
                <span className="font-mono text-gray-400 font-bold w-6">0{idx + 1}</span>
                <div className="flex-1">
                  <div className="flex justify-between mb-1">
                    <span className="font-bold text-gray-800 text-sm truncate max-w-[150px]">{title}</span>
                    <span className="text-sm font-bold text-brand-purple">{count}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="w-full bg-gray-100 rounded-full h-2">
                    <div className="bg-brand-purple h-2 rounded-full" style={{ width: `${(count / (data.salesByCourse[0]?.[1] || 1)) * 100}%` }}></div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
            {data.salesByCourse.length === 0 && <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm">No sales data yet.</p>}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/admin/AnnouncementManager.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { Megaphone, Trash2, CheckCircle, Info, AlertTriangle } from 'lucide-react'

export default function AnnouncementManager() {
  const [announcements, setAnnouncements] = useState([])
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({ title: '', message: '', type: 'info', active: true })
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => { fetchAnnouncements() }, [])

  const fetchAnnouncements = async () => {
    const { data } = await supabase.from('announcements').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    setAnnouncements(data || [])
  }

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setLoading(true)
    await supabase.from('announcements').insert([formData])
    setFormData({ title: '', message: '', type: 'info', active: true })
    setLoading(false)
    fetchAnnouncements()
  }

  const toggleActive = async (id, currentStatus) => {
    await supabase.from('announcements').update({ active: !currentStatus }).eq('id', id)
    fetchAnnouncements()
  }

  const handleDelete = async (id) => {
    if(!confirm("Delete?")) return
    await supabase.from('announcements').delete().eq('id', id)
    fetchAnnouncements()
  }

  const TypeIcon = ({ type }) => {
    if (type === 'warning') return <AlertTriangle size={18} className="text-orange-500"/>
    if (type === 'success') return <CheckCircle size={18} className="text-green-500"/>
    return <Info size={18} className="text-blue-500"/>
  }

  return (
    <div className="grid lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
      <div className="lg:col-span-1 bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 h-fit sticky top-24">
        <h3 className="font-bold text-lg mb-4">Post Announcement</h3>
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
          <input required placeholder="Banner Title" className="input-field" value={formData.title} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, title: e.target.value})} />
          <textarea required placeholder="Message displayed to all users..." className="input-field h-24" value={formData.message} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, message: e.target.value})} />
          
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            <select className="input-field" value={formData.type} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, type: e.target.value})}>
              <option value="info">Info (Blue)</option>
              <option value="warning">Warning (Orange)</option>
              <option value="success">Success (Green)</option>
            </select>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 p-2 border border-gray-200 rounded-lg">
              <input type="checkbox" checked={formData.active} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, active: e.target.checked})} className="w-5 h-5 text-brand-purple"/>
              <span className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-600">Active</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button type="submit" disabled={loading} className="btn btn-plum-gradient w-full py-2">{loading ? 'Posting...' : 'Post Banner'}</button>
        </form>
      </div>

      <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-4">
        {announcements.map(ann => (
          <div key={ann.id} className={`bg-white p-4 rounded-xl border flex justify-between items-center ${ann.active ? 'border-l-4 border-l-brand-purple shadow-sm' : 'border-gray-100 opacity-60'}`}>
            <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
              <div className="mt-1"><TypeIcon type={ann.type}/></div>
              <div>
                <h4 className="font-bold text-gray-800">{ann.title}</h4>
                <p className="text-sm text-gray-600">{ann.message}</p>
                <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 mt-1">{new Date(ann.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}</p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <button onClick={() => toggleActive(ann.id, ann.active)} className={`text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1 rounded-full ${ann.active ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-700' : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-500'}`}>
                {ann.active ? 'Active' : 'Hidden'}
              </button>
              <button onClick={() => handleDelete(ann.id)} className="text-gray-300 hover:text-red-500"><Trash2 size={18}/></button>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      <style>{`
        .input-field { width: 100%; padding: 0.75rem; border: 1px solid #e5e7eb; border-radius: 0.5rem; outline: none; font-size: 0.9rem; transition: all 0.2s; }
        .input-field:focus { border-color: #6a11cb; ring: 2px solid #6a11cb33; }
      `}</style>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/admin/BlogManager.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { Loader2, Trash2, Edit } from 'lucide-react'
import MarkdownToolbar from '@/components/ui/MarkdownToolbar'
import FileUploader from './FileUploader'

export default function BlogManager() {
  const [posts, setPosts] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(null)
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({ title: '', content: '', excerpt: '', image_url: '', tags: '' })
  const contentRef = useRef(null)

  useEffect(() => { fetchPosts() }, [])

  const fetchPosts = async () => {
    const { data } = await supabase.from('posts').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    setPosts(data || [])
    setLoading(false)
  }

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setLoading(true)
    const slug = formData.title.toLowerCase().replace(/ /g, '-').replace(/[^\w-]+/g, '')
    const tagsArray = formData.tags.split(',').map(t => t.trim())
    
    const payload = { ...formData, slug, tags: tagsArray, published: true }
    
    if (editing) {
      await supabase.from('posts').update(payload).eq('id', editing.id)
    } else {
      await supabase.from('posts').insert([payload])
    }
    
    setEditing(null)
    setFormData({ title: '', content: '', excerpt: '', image_url: '', tags: '' })
    fetchPosts()
  }

  const handleEdit = (post) => {
    setEditing(post)
    setFormData({ 
      title: post.title, 
      content: post.content, 
      excerpt: post.excerpt || '', 
      image_url: post.image_url || '',
      tags: post.tags ? post.tags.join(', ') : '' 
    })
  }

  const handleDelete = async (id) => {
    if(!confirm("Delete post?")) return
    await supabase.from('posts').delete().eq('id', id)
    fetchPosts()
  }

  return (
    <div className="grid lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
      <div className="lg:col-span-1 bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 h-fit sticky top-24">
        <h3 className="font-bold text-lg mb-4">{editing ? 'Edit Post' : 'New Blog Post'}</h3>
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
          <input required placeholder="Title" className="input-field" value={formData.title} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, title: e.target.value})} />
          
          <FileUploader 
            bucket="course-materials" // Using generic bucket
            label="Cover Image" 
            value={formData.image_url} 
            onUpload={(url) => setFormData({...formData, image_url: url})} 
          />

          <input placeholder="Tags (comma separated)" className="input-field" value={formData.tags} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, tags: e.target.value})} />
          <textarea placeholder="Short Excerpt" className="input-field h-20" value={formData.excerpt} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, excerpt: e.target.value})} />
          
          <div className="border border-gray-200 rounded-xl overflow-hidden">
            <MarkdownToolbar textareaRef={contentRef} value={formData.content} setValue={(val) => setFormData({...formData, content: val})} />
            <textarea 
              ref={contentRef}
              required 
              placeholder="Content (Markdown supported)..." 
              className="w-full p-3 h-64 font-mono text-sm outline-none resize-none bg-white"
              value={formData.content} 
              onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, content: e.target.value})} 
            />
          </div>
          
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <button type="submit" disabled={loading} className="btn btn-plum-gradient w-full py-2">{loading ? 'Saving...' : 'Save Post'}</button>
            {editing && <button type="button" onClick={() => {setEditing(null); setFormData({ title: '', content: '', excerpt: '', image_url: '', tags: '' })}} className="px-4 py-2 bg-gray-100 rounded-lg text-gray-600">Cancel</button>}
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>

      <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-4">
        {posts.map(post => (
          <div key={post.id} className="bg-white p-4 rounded-xl border border-gray-100 flex gap-4 items-start">
            <img src={post.image_url || 'https://placehold.co/100'} className="w-20 h-20 object-cover rounded-lg bg-gray-100" />
            <div className="flex-1">
              <h4 className="font-bold text-gray-800">{post.title}</h4>
              <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 line-clamp-2">{post.excerpt}</p>
              <div className="flex gap-2 mt-2">
                {post.tags?.map(t => <span key={t} className="text-[10px] bg-purple-50 text-purple-600 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">{t}</span>)}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
              <button onClick={() => handleEdit(post)} className="p-2 text-blue-500 hover:bg-blue-50 rounded-lg"><Edit size={18}/></button>
              <button onClick={() => handleDelete(post.id)} className="p-2 text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50 rounded-lg"><Trash2 size={18}/></button>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      <style>{`
        .input-field { width: 100%; padding: 0.75rem; border: 1px solid #e5e7eb; border-radius: 0.5rem; outline: none; font-size: 0.9rem; transition: all 0.2s; }
        .input-field:focus { border-color: #6a11cb; ring: 2px solid #6a11cb33; }
      `}</style>
    </div>
  )
}

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { Save, Loader2, LayoutTemplate, FileText } from 'lucide-react'

export default function CMSManager() {
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('home')
  const [content, setContent] = useState({})
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => { fetchContent(activeTab) }, [activeTab])

  const fetchContent = async (tab) => {
    setLoading(true)
    const key = tab === 'home' ? 'home_hero' : (tab === 'privacy' ? 'privacy_policy' : 'terms_conditions')
    const { data } = await supabase.from('content_blocks').select('content').eq('key', key).single()
    
    if (data) setContent(data.content)
    else {
        // Defaults
        if(tab === 'home') setContent({ title: '', subtitle: '' })
        else setContent({ title: tab === 'privacy' ? 'Privacy Policy' : 'Terms & Conditions', body: '' })
    }
    setLoading(false)
  }

  const handleSave = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setSaving(true)
    const key = activeTab === 'home' ? 'home_hero' : (activeTab === 'privacy' ? 'privacy_policy' : 'terms_conditions')
    await supabase.from('content_blocks').upsert({ key, content, updated_at: new Date() })
    setSaving(false)
    alert("Content updated!")
  }

  return (
    <div className="grid md:grid-cols-4 gap-6">
      <div className="col-span-1 space-y-2">
        <button onClick={() => setActiveTab('home')} className={`w-full text-left p-3 rounded-xl font-bold ${activeTab === 'home' ? 'bg-brand-purple text-white' : 'bg-white hover:bg-gray-50 text-gray-600'}`}>Home Hero</button>
        <button onClick={() => setActiveTab('privacy')} className={`w-full text-left p-3 rounded-xl font-bold ${activeTab === 'privacy' ? 'bg-brand-purple text-white' : 'bg-white hover:bg-gray-50 text-gray-600'}`}>Privacy Policy</button>
        <button onClick={() => setActiveTab('terms')} className={`w-full text-left p-3 rounded-xl font-bold ${activeTab === 'terms' ? 'bg-brand-purple text-white' : 'bg-white hover:bg-gray-50 text-gray-600'}`}>Terms & Conditions</button>
      </div>

      <div className="col-span-3 bg-white p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100">
        {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin text-brand-purple mx-auto"/> : (
          <form onSubmit={handleSave} className="space-y-6">
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-6 pb-4 border-b border-gray-100">
                <div className="bg-blue-50 p-2 rounded-lg text-brand-blue">{activeTab === 'home' ? <LayoutTemplate size={24}/> : <FileText size={24}/>}</div>
                <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-gray-800 capitalize">{activeTab.replace('_', ' ')} Editor</h3>
            </div>

            {activeTab === 'home' ? (
              <>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-2">Headline</label>
                  <input className="input-field" value={content.title} onChange={e => setContent({...content, title: e.target.value})} />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-2">Subheadline</label>
                  <textarea className="input-field h-24" value={content.subtitle} onChange={e => setContent({...content, subtitle: e.target.value})} />
                </div>
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-2">Page Title</label>
                  <input className="input-field" value={content.title} onChange={e => setContent({...content, title: e.target.value})} />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-2">Body Content (Markdown/HTML)</label>
                  <textarea className="input-field h-96 font-mono text-sm" value={content.body} onChange={e => setContent({...content, body: e.target.value})} />
                </div>
              </>
            )}

            <button disabled={saving} className="btn btn-plum-gradient w-full py-3 flex justify-center items-center gap-2">
              {saving ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" size={20}/> : <><Save size={20}/> Save Changes</>}
            </button>
          </form>
        )}
      </div>
      <style>{`
        .input-field { width: 100%; padding: 0.75rem; border: 1px solid #e5e7eb; border-radius: 0.5rem; outline: none; font-size: 0.9rem; transition: all 0.2s; }
        .input-field:focus { border-color: #6a11cb; ring: 2px solid #6a11cb33; }
      `}</style>
    </div>
  )
}

PATH: ./src/components/admin/CommentManager.jsx
