"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { Loader2, Trash2, MessageSquare, FileText, MonitorPlay } from 'lucide-react'

export default function CommentManager() {
  const [comments, setComments] = useState([])
  const [discussions, setDiscussions] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState('blog')

  useEffect(() => { fetchAll() }, [])

  const fetchAll = async () => {
    setLoading(true)
    const { data: blogComments } = await supabase.from('comments').select('*, profiles(full_name, email), posts(title)').order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    const { data: lessonChats } = await supabase.from('lesson_discussions').select('*, profiles(full_name, email), lessons(title)').order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    
    setComments(blogComments || [])
    setDiscussions(lessonChats || [])
    setLoading(false)
  }

  const handleDelete = async (table, id) => {
    if (!confirm("Permanently delete this comment?")) return
    await supabase.from(table).delete().eq('id', id)
    fetchAll()
  }

  const List = ({ data, type }) => (
    <div className="space-y-4">
      {data.length === 0 && <p className="text-gray-400 text-center py-8">No comments found.</p>}
      {data.map(item => (
        <div key={item.id} className="bg-white p-4 rounded-xl border border-gray-100 flex gap-4 hover:shadow-sm transition">
          <div className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 ${type === 'blog' ? 'bg-orange-100 text-orange-600' : 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-600'}`}>
            {type === 'blog' ? <FileText size={18}/> : <MonitorPlay size={18}/>}
          </div>
          <div className="flex-1">
            <div className="flex justify-between items-start">
              <div>
                <p className="font-bold text-gray-800 text-sm">{item.profiles?.full_name || 'Unknown'}</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">{item.profiles?.email}</p>
              </div>
              <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 bg-gray-50 px-2 py-1 rounded">
                {new Date(item.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}
              </span>
            </div>
            
            <div className="mt-2 bg-gray-50 p-2 rounded-lg text-sm text-gray-700">
              {item.content}
            </div>
            
            <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-2 flex items-center gap-1">
              On: <span className="font-bold text-gray-600">{type === 'blog' ? item.posts?.title : item.lessons?.title}</span>
            </p>
          </div>
          <button 
            onClick={() => handleDelete(type === 'blog' ? 'comments' : 'lesson_discussions', item.id)}
            className="text-gray-300 hover:text-red-500 self-center p-2"
            title="Delete Comment"
          >
            <Trash2 size={18}/>
          </button>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  )

  return (
    <div className="space-y-6 animate-fade-in">
      <div className="flex gap-4 border-b border-gray-200 pb-1">
        <button 
          onClick={() => setActiveTab('blog')}
          className={`pb-2 px-4 font-bold text-sm transition ${activeTab === 'blog' ? 'text-brand-purple border-b-2 border-brand-purple' : 'text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-700'}`}
        >
          Blog Comments ({comments.length})
        </button>
        <button 
          onClick={() => setActiveTab('lesson')}
          className={`pb-2 px-4 font-bold text-sm transition ${activeTab === 'lesson' ? 'text-brand-purple border-b-2 border-brand-purple' : 'text-gray-500 hover:text-gray-700'}`}
        >
          Lesson Discussions ({discussions.length})
        </button>
      </div>

      {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin mx-auto text-brand-purple"/> : (
        activeTab === 'blog' ? <List data={comments} type="blog"/> : <List data={discussions} type="lesson"/>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

PATH: ./src/components/admin/CouponManager.jsx

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { Loader2, Trash2, Tag, Plus, CheckCircle, XCircle } from 'lucide-react'

export default function CouponManager() {
  const [coupons, setCoupons] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({ code: '', discount_percent: '' })

  useEffect(() => { fetchCoupons() }, [])

  const fetchCoupons = async () => {
    const { data } = await supabase.from('coupons').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    setCoupons(data || [])
    setLoading(false)
  }

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (!formData.code || !formData.discount_percent) return
    setLoading(true)
    
    const payload = {
      code: formData.code.toUpperCase().trim(),
      discount_percent: parseInt(formData.discount_percent),
      active: true
    }

    const { error } = await supabase.from('coupons').insert([payload])
    if (error) alert("Error creating coupon: " + error.message)
    
    setFormData({ code: '', discount_percent: '' })
    fetchCoupons()
    setLoading(false)
  }

  const toggleActive = async (code, currentStatus) => {
    await supabase.from('coupons').update({ active: !currentStatus }).eq('code', code)
    fetchCoupons()
  }

  const handleDelete = async (code) => {
    if (!confirm("Delete this coupon?")) return
    await supabase.from('coupons').delete().eq('code', code)
    fetchCoupons()
  }

  return (
    <div className="grid lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8 animate-fade-in">
      {/* Creation Form */}
      <div className="lg:col-span-1 bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 h-fit sticky top-24">
        <h3 className="font-bold text-lg mb-4 flex items-center gap-2"><Tag size={20}/> Create Coupon</h3>
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase">Coupon Code</label>
            <input 
              required 
              className="input-field mt-1 uppercase font-mono" 
              placeholder="e.g. SUMMER20" 
              value={formData.code} 
              onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, code: e.target.value})} 
            />
          </div>
          
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase">Discount Percentage</label>
            <div className="relative mt-1">
              <input 
                required 
                type="number" 
                min="1" 
                max="100" 
                className="input-field pr-8" 
                placeholder="20" 
                value={formData.discount_percent} 
                onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, discount_percent: e.target.value})} 
              />
              <span className="absolute right-3 top-2.5 text-gray-400 font-bold">%</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <button disabled={loading} className="btn btn-plum-gradient w-full py-2 flex justify-center items-center gap-2">
            {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" size={16}/> : <><Plus size={16}/> Create Code</>}
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>

      {/* List */}
      <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-4">
        {coupons.length === 0 && <div className="text-center p-8 text-gray-400 border border-dashed rounded-xl">No active coupons.</div>}
        
        {coupons.map((coupon) => (
          <div key={coupon.code} className={`bg-white p-5 rounded-xl border flex items-center justify-between transition ${coupon.active ? 'border-green-100 shadow-sm' : 'border-gray-100 opacity-60 bg-gray-50'}`}>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
              <div className={`w-12 h-12 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-xl ${coupon.active ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-600' : 'bg-gray-200 text-gray-500'}`}>
                %
              </div>
              <div>
                <h4 className="font-mono font-bold text-lg text-brand-dark tracking-wide">{coupon.code}</h4>
                <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">{coupon.discount_percent}% Discount • {new Date(coupon.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}</p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <button 
                onClick={() => toggleActive(coupon.code, coupon.active)}
                className={`flex items-center gap-1 px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold transition ${coupon.active ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-700 hover:bg-green-200' : 'bg-gray-200 text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-300'}`}
              >
                {coupon.active ? <CheckCircle size={14}/> : <XCircle size={14}/>}
                {coupon.active ? 'Active' : 'Inactive'}
              </button>
              <button onClick={() => handleDelete(coupon.code)} className="p-2 text-gray-300 hover:text-red-500 transition rounded-lg hover:bg-red-50">
                <Trash2 size={18}/>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      <style>{`
        .input-field { width: 100%; padding: 0.75rem; border: 1px solid #e5e7eb; border-radius: 0.5rem; outline: none; font-size: 0.9rem; transition: all 0.2s; }
        .input-field:focus { border-color: #6a11cb; ring: 2px solid #6a11cb33; }
      `}</style>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/admin/CourseManager.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { Loader2, Trash2, Edit, Video, HelpCircle, Plus, Folder, GripVertical, Save } from 'lucide-react'
import SnippetHelper from './SnippetHelper'
import FileUploader from './FileUploader'

export default function CourseManager() {
  const [courses, setCourses] = useState([])
  const [lessons, setLessons] = useState([])
  const [selectedCourse, setSelectedCourse] = useState("")
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(null)
  
  // Drag & Drop State
  const dragItem = useRef(null)
  const dragOverItem = useRef(null)
  const [isDragging, setIsDragging] = useState(false)
  const [moduleGroups, setModuleGroups] = useState({})

  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({ 
    title: '', video_url: '', description: '', quiz_data: '', type: 'video', release_date: '', module: 'General', pdf_url: ''
  })

  useEffect(() => { fetchData() }, [])

  const fetchData = async () => {
    setLoading(true)
    const { data: c } = await supabase.from('products').select('*').eq('type', 'course')
    setCourses(c || [])
    if (c && c.length > 0 && !selectedCourse) setSelectedCourse(c[0].id)
    if (selectedCourse) {
      const { data: l } = await supabase.from('lessons').select('*').eq('course_id', selectedCourse).order('position', { ascending: true })
      setLessons(l || [])
    }
    setLoading(false)
  }

  useEffect(() => { if(selectedCourse) fetchData() }, [selectedCourse])

  // GROUPING LOGIC
  useEffect(() => {
    const grouped = lessons.reduce((acc, lesson) => {
      const mod = lesson.module || 'General'
      if (!acc[mod]) acc[mod] = []
      acc[mod].push(lesson)
      return acc
    }, {})
    setModuleGroups(grouped)
  }, [lessons])

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (!selectedCourse) return alert("Select a course first")
    setLoading(true)
    
    let parsedQuiz = null
    if (formData.quiz_data) {
      try { parsedQuiz = JSON.parse(formData.quiz_data) } catch { alert("Invalid Quiz JSON"); setLoading(false); return; }
    }

    // Determine position: End of list
    const position = lessons.length

    const payload = { 
      course_id: selectedCourse, 
      title: formData.title, 
      type: formData.type,
      video_url: formData.video_url,
      pdf_url: formData.pdf_url,
      description: formData.description, 
      quiz_data: parsedQuiz,
      release_date: formData.release_date || new Date().toISOString(),
      module: formData.module || 'General',
      position: editing ? editing.position : position
    }

    if (editing) await supabase.from('lessons').update(payload).eq('id', editing.id)
    else await supabase.from('lessons').insert([payload])

    setEditing(null)
    setFormData({ title: '', video_url: '', pdf_url: '', description: '', quiz_data: '', type: 'video', release_date: '', module: 'General' })
    fetchData()
  }

  const handleEdit = (lesson) => {
    setEditing(lesson)
    setFormData({
      title: lesson.title, 
      video_url: lesson.video_url || '', 
      pdf_url: lesson.pdf_url || '',
      description: lesson.description || '',
      type: lesson.type || 'video', 
      quiz_data: lesson.quiz_data ? JSON.stringify(lesson.quiz_data, null, 2) : '',
      release_date: lesson.release_date ? new Date(lesson.release_date).toISOString().slice(0,16) : '',
      module: lesson.module || 'General'
    })
  }

  const handleDelete = async (id) => {
    if(!confirm("Delete lesson?")) return
    await supabase.from('lessons').delete().eq('id', id)
    fetchData()
  }

  const insertSnippet = (text) => setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, description: prev.description + text }))
  const insertQuizTemplate = (json) => setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, quiz_data: json }))

  // DRAG AND DROP HANDLERS
  const onDragStart = (e, index, moduleName) => {
    dragItem.current = { index, module: moduleName }
    setIsDragging(true)
  }

  const onDragEnter = (e, index, moduleName) => {
    dragOverItem.current = { index, module: moduleName }
  }

  const onDragEnd = async () => {
    if (!dragItem.current || !dragOverItem.current) {
        setIsDragging(false)
        return
    }

    const srcMod = dragItem.current.module
    const destMod = dragOverItem.current.module
    
    // Convert Grouped structure to Flat List, perform swap, then re-group.
    const newGroups = { ...moduleGroups }
    newGroups[srcMod] = [...newGroups[srcMod]] // Copy
    const itemToMove = newGroups[srcMod].splice(dragItem.current.index, 1)[0] // Remove
    
    // Update module if changed
    if (srcMod !== destMod) {
        itemToMove.module = destMod
    }
    
    if(!newGroups[destMod]) newGroups[destMod] = []
    const targetGroup = srcMod === destMod ? newGroups[srcMod] : [...newGroups[destMod]]
    
    if(srcMod !== destMod) newGroups[destMod] = targetGroup
    
    targetGroup.splice(dragOverItem.current.index, 0, itemToMove)
    
    setModuleGroups(newGroups)
    setIsDragging(false)
    dragItem.current = null
    dragOverItem.current = null
  }

  const saveOrder = async () => {
    setLoading(true)
    // Flatten groups to array with updated indices
    let flatList = []
    Object.keys(moduleGroups).forEach(mod => {
        const items = moduleGroups[mod]
        items.forEach((item, idx) => {
            flatList.push({ ...item, module: mod, position: flatList.length })
        })
    })

    // Batch update
    const updates = flatList.map(l => ({
        id: l.id,
        course_id: l.course_id,
        title: l.title,
        module: l.module,
        position: l.position
    }))

    const { error } = await supabase.from('lessons').upsert(updates, { onConflict: 'id' })
    if(error) alert("Error saving order: " + error.message)
    else alert("Order saved!")
    
    fetchData() // Refresh
  }

  const interactiveQuizExample = JSON.stringify([
    {
      "timestamp": 10,
      "question": "What phrase was just used?",
      "options": ["Hello", "Goodbye"],
      "correct": 0
    }
  ], null, 2);

  return (
    <div className="grid lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
      <div className="lg:col-span-1 bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 h-fit sticky top-24">
        <div className="mb-6">
          <label className="block text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 mb-2">Select Course</label>
          <select className="input-field" value={selectedCourse} onChange={(e) => setSelectedCourse(e.target.value)}>
            {courses.map(c => <option key={c.id} value={c.id}>{c.title}</option>)}
          </select>
        </div>

        <h3 className="font-bold text-lg mb-4 border-t border-gray-100 pt-4">{editing ? 'Edit Lesson' : 'Add Lesson'}</h3>
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
          <div>
            <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase mb-1">Module</label>
            <input list="modules" className="input-field" value={formData.module} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, module: e.target.value})} placeholder="e.g. Introduction" />
            <datalist id="modules">{Object.keys(moduleGroups).map(m => <option key={m} value={m} />)}</datalist>
          </div>
          <input required placeholder="Lesson Title" className="input-field" value={formData.title} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, title: e.target.value})} />
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
            <select className="input-field" value={formData.type} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, type: e.target.value})}><option value="video">Video</option><option value="audio">Audio</option></select>
            <input type="datetime-local" className="input-field" value={formData.release_date} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, release_date: e.target.value})} />
          </div>
          
          <FileUploader 
            bucket="course-materials" 
            label="Lesson Media (Video/Audio)" 
            accept="video/*,audio/*"
            value={formData.video_url} 
            onUpload={(url) => setFormData({...formData, video_url: url})} 
          />

          <FileUploader 
            bucket="course-materials" 
            label="PDF Handout (Optional)" 
            accept="application/pdf"
            value={formData.pdf_url} 
            onUpload={(url) => setFormData({...formData, pdf_url: url})} 
          />
          
          <div>
             <SnippetHelper onInsert={insertSnippet} onInsertQuiz={insertQuizTemplate} />
             <div className="flex gap-2 mb-2">
                <button type="button" onClick={() => insertQuizTemplate(interactiveQuizExample)} className="text-xs bg-orange-100 text-orange-700 px-3 py-1 rounded-full border border-orange-200 hover:bg-orange-200">
                    + Interactive Video Quiz
                </button>
             </div>
             <textarea placeholder="Description / Vocabulary" className="input-field h-24" value={formData.description} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, description: e.target.value})} />
          </div>
          
          <textarea placeholder='Quiz JSON' className="input-field h-24 font-mono text-xs" value={formData.quiz_data} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, quiz_data: e.target.value})} />

          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <button type="submit" disabled={loading} className="btn btn-plum-gradient w-full py-2">{loading ? 'Saving...' : 'Save Lesson'}</button>
            {editing && <button type="button" onClick={() => {setEditing(null); setFormData({ title: '', video_url: '', pdf_url: '', description: '', quiz_data: '', type: 'video', release_date: '', module: 'General' })}} className="px-4 py-2 bg-gray-100 rounded-lg text-gray-600">Cancel</button>}
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>

      <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-6">
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
            <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-brand-dark">Curriculum Builder</h2>
            <button onClick={saveOrder} disabled={loading} className="btn bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-700 px-4 py-2 text-sm flex items-center gap-2 shadow-sm">
               {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" size={16}/> : <Save size={16}/>} Save Order
            </button>
        </div>

        {Object.entries(moduleGroups).map(([moduleName, moduleLessons]) => (
          <div key={moduleName} className="bg-white p-4 rounded-2xl border border-gray-100">
            <h4 className="font-bold text-brand-dark flex items-center gap-2 mb-4 px-2 bg-gray-50 p-2 rounded-lg"><Folder size={18} className="text-brand-purple"/> {moduleName}</h4>
            <div className="space-y-2">
              {moduleLessons.map((l, index) => (
                <div 
                    key={l.id} 
                    draggable
                    onDragStart={(e) => onDragStart(e, index, moduleName)}
                    onDragEnter={(e) => onDragEnter(e, index, moduleName)}
                    onDragEnd={onDragEnd}
                    onDragOver={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
                    className="bg-white p-3 rounded-xl flex gap-4 items-center group hover:shadow-md transition border border-gray-200 cursor-move active:bg-purple-50 active:border-brand-purple"
                >
                  <GripVertical size={20} className="text-gray-300"/>
                  <div className="w-8 h-8 bg-gray-100 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-brand-purple shrink-0 shadow-sm">{l.type === 'audio' ? <Plus size={16}/> : <Video size={16}/>}</div>
                  <div className="flex-1">
                    <h5 className="font-bold text-gray-700 text-sm">{l.title}</h5>
                    <div className="flex gap-2 mt-1 text-[10px]">
                      {l.quiz_data && <span className="bg-orange-100 text-orange-600 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded flex items-center gap-1"><HelpCircle size={8}/> Quiz</span>}
                      {l.pdf_url && <span className="bg-green-100 text-green-600 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded flex items-center gap-1">PDF</span>}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex gap-1">
                    <button onClick={() => handleEdit(l)} className="p-2 text-gray-400 hover:text-blue-500"><Edit size={16}/></button>
                    <button onClick={() => handleDelete(l.id)} className="p-2 text-gray-400 hover:text-red-500"><Trash2 size={16}/></button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      <style>{`
        .input-field { width: 100%; padding: 0.75rem; border: 1px solid #e5e7eb; border-radius: 0.5rem; outline: none; font-size: 0.9rem; transition: all 0.2s; }
        .input-field:focus { border-color: #6a11cb; ring: 2px solid #6a11cb33; }
      `}</style>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/admin/EventManager.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { Loader2, Trash2, Edit, Calendar, Video, Users, X, Download, PlusCircle } from 'lucide-react'
import { downloadCSV } from '@/utils/csvExport'
import { useEdgeFunction } from '@/hooks/useEdgeFunction'

export default function EventManager() {
  const [events, setEvents] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(null)
  
  // Hooks
  const { invoke } = useEdgeFunction()
  const [genZoomLoading, setGenZoomLoading] = useState(false)

  // Roster State
  const [viewingRoster, setViewingRoster] = useState(null)
  const [rosterData, setRosterData] = useState([])
  const [rosterLoading, setRosterLoading] = useState(false)

  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({ 
    title: '', price: '', description: '', image_url: '', 
    event_date: '', type: 'standalone', total_seats: 15
  })

  useEffect(() => { fetchEvents() }, [])

  const fetchEvents = async () => {
    const { data } = await supabase.from('products').select('*').eq('type', 'standalone').order('event_date', { ascending: true })
    setEvents(data || [])
    setLoading(false)
  }

  const fetchRoster = async (eventId) => {
    setRosterLoading(true)
    const { data } = await supabase.from('purchases')
      .select('created_at, profiles(full_name, email)')
      .eq('product_id', eventId)
      .eq('status', 'completed')
    setRosterData(data || [])
    setRosterLoading(false)
  }

  const handleViewRoster = (evt) => {
    setViewingRoster(evt)
    fetchRoster(evt.id)
  }

  const handleGenerateZoom = async () => {
    if (!formData.title || !formData.event_date) return alert("Please enter title and date first")
    setGenZoomLoading(true)
    
    // Call Edge Function
    const { data, fallback } = await invoke('create-zoom', {
      topic: formData.title,
      start_time: formData.event_date
    })

    const link = fallback ? `https://zoom.us/j/${Math.floor(Math.random()*1000000000)}?pwd=mock` : data.join_url
    
    // Append to description
    setFormData(prev => ({ ...prev, description: prev.description + `\n\n[[ZOOM::${link}]]` }))
    setGenZoomLoading(false)
  }

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setLoading(true)
    const id = editing ? editing.id : `evt-${Date.now()}`
    
    if (editing) {
      await supabase.from('products').update(formData).eq('id', id)
    } else {
      await supabase.from('products').insert([{ ...formData, id }])
    }
    
    setEditing(null)
    setFormData({ title: '', price: '', description: '', image_url: '', event_date: '', type: 'standalone', total_seats: 15 })
    fetchEvents()
  }

  const handleEdit = (evt) => {
    setEditing(evt)
    setFormData({ 
      title: evt.title, price: evt.price, description: evt.description, 
      image_url: evt.image_url, event_date: new Date(evt.event_date).toISOString().slice(0,16), type: 'standalone',
      total_seats: evt.total_seats || 15
    })
  }

  const handleDelete = async (id) => {
    if(!confirm("Delete event?")) return
    await supabase.from('products').delete().eq('id', id)
    fetchEvents()
  }

  return (
    <div className="grid lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8 relative">
      
      {viewingRoster && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl w-full max-w-lg shadow-2xl overflow-hidden animate-fade-in flex flex-col max-h-[80vh]">
            <div className="p-4 border-b border-gray-100 flex justify-between items-center bg-gray-50">
              <div>
                <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800">{viewingRoster.title}</h3>
                <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">Attendee List</p>
              </div>
              <button onClick={() => setViewingRoster(null)}><X size={20} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600"/></button>
            </div>
            
            <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4">
              {rosterLoading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin mx-auto text-brand-purple"/> : (
                <div className="space-y-2">
                  {rosterData.length === 0 && <p className="text-center text-gray-400 py-4">No bookings yet.</p>}
                  {rosterData.map((record, idx) => (
                    <div key={idx} className="flex justify-between items-center p-3 border border-gray-100 rounded-xl hover:bg-gray-50">
                      <div>
                        <p className="font-bold text-gray-800 text-sm">{record.profiles?.full_name || 'Unknown'}</p>
                        <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">{record.profiles?.email}</p>
                      </div>
                      <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-400">{new Date(record.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
                    </div>
                  ))}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className="p-4 border-t border-gray-100 bg-gray-50 text-right">
               <button onClick={() => downloadCSV(rosterData.map(r => ({ Name: r.profiles?.full_name, Email: r.profiles?.email, Date: r.created_at })), 'roster')} className="text-xs font-bold text-brand-purple flex items-center gap-1 ml-auto hover:underline">
                 <Download size={14}/> Export CSV
               </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="lg:col-span-1 bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 h-fit sticky top-24">
        <h3 className="font-bold text-lg mb-4">{editing ? 'Edit Event' : 'Create Event'}</h3>
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
          <input required placeholder="Event Title" className="input-field" value={formData.title} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, title: e.target.value})} />
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
            <input required placeholder="Price" className="input-field" value={formData.price} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, price: e.target.value})} />
            <input required type="number" placeholder="Seats" className="input-field" value={formData.total_seats} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, total_seats: e.target.value})} />
          </div>
          <input required type="datetime-local" className="input-field" value={formData.event_date} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, event_date: e.target.value})} />
          <input placeholder="Cover Image URL" className="input-field" value={formData.image_url} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, image_url: e.target.value})} />
          
          <div className="relative">
             <textarea required placeholder="Description (include [[ZOOM::link]] tag)" className="input-field h-32" value={formData.description} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, description: e.target.value})} />
             <button type="button" onClick={handleGenerateZoom} disabled={genZoomLoading} className="absolute bottom-2 right-2 text-xs bg-blue-50 text-blue-600 px-2 py-1 rounded border border-blue-100 flex items-center gap-1 hover:bg-blue-100">
               {genZoomLoading ? <Loader2 size={12} className="animate-spin"/> : <Video size={12}/>} Gen Zoom Link
             </button>
          </div>
          
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <button type="submit" disabled={loading} className="btn btn-plum-gradient w-full py-2">{loading ? 'Saving...' : 'Save Event'}</button>
            {editing && <button type="button" onClick={() => {setEditing(null); setFormData({ title: '', price: '', description: '', image_url: '', event_date: '', type: 'standalone', total_seats: 15 })}} className="px-4 py-2 bg-gray-100 rounded-lg text-gray-600">Cancel</button>}
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>

      <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-4">
        {events.map(evt => {
          const booked = evt.booked_seats || 0
          const total = evt.total_seats || 15
          return (
            <div key={evt.id} className="bg-white p-4 rounded-xl border border-gray-100 flex gap-4 items-center">
              <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-gray-100 rounded-lg flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center p-1 shrink-0">
                 <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-gray-500">{new Date(evt.event_date).toLocaleString('default', {month:'short'})}</span>
                 <span className="text-xl font-bold text-brand-dark">{new Date(evt.event_date).getDate()}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex-1">
                <h4 className="font-bold text-gray-800">{evt.title}</h4>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3 text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1">
                   <span>{new Date(evt.event_date).toLocaleTimeString([], {hour:'2-digit', minute:'2-digit'})}</span>
                   <span className="flex items-center gap-1 font-bold text-brand-purple bg-purple-50 px-2 py-0.5 rounded"><Users size={12}/> {booked}/{total} Booked</span>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
                <button onClick={() => handleViewRoster(evt)} className="px-3 py-1 bg-blue-50 text-blue-600 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold hover:bg-blue-100">Roster</button>
                <div className="flex gap-1 justify-end">
                  <button onClick={() => handleEdit(evt)} className="p-2 text-gray-400 hover:text-blue-500"><Edit size={16}/></button>
                  <button onClick={() => handleDelete(evt.id)} className="p-2 text-gray-400 hover:text-red-500"><Trash2 size={16}/></button>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          )
        })}
      </div>
      <style>{`
        .input-field { width: 100%; padding: 0.75rem; border: 1px solid #e5e7eb; border-radius: 0.5rem; outline: none; font-size: 0.9rem; transition: all 0.2s; }
        .input-field:focus { border-color: #6a11cb; ring: 2px solid #6a11cb33; }
      `}</style>
    </div>
  )
}

PATH: ./src/components/admin/FAQManager.jsx

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { Loader2, Trash2, Edit, Plus, HelpCircle } from 'lucide-react'

export default function FAQManager() {
  const [faqs, setFaqs] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(null)
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({ question: '', answer: '', order: 0 })

  useEffect(() => { fetchFaqs() }, [])

  const fetchFaqs = async () => {
    const { data } = await supabase.from('faqs').select('*').order('order', { ascending: true })
    setFaqs(data || [])
    setLoading(false)
  }

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setLoading(true)
    if (editing) {
      await supabase.from('faqs').update(formData).eq('id', editing.id)
    } else {
      await supabase.from('faqs').insert([formData])
    }
    setEditing(null)
    setFormData({ question: '', answer: '', order: 0 })
    fetchFaqs()
  }

  const handleEdit = (item) => {
    setEditing(item)
    setFormData({ question: item.question, answer: item.answer, order: item.order })
  }

  const handleDelete = async (id) => {
    if(!confirm("Delete FAQ?")) return
    await supabase.from('faqs').delete().eq('id', id)
    fetchFaqs()
  }

  return (
    <div className="grid lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
      <div className="lg:col-span-1 bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 h-fit sticky top-24">
        <h3 className="font-bold text-lg mb-4">{editing ? 'Edit FAQ' : 'Add FAQ'}</h3>
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
          <input required placeholder="Question" className="input-field" value={formData.question} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, question: e.target.value})} />
          <textarea required placeholder="Answer" className="input-field h-32" value={formData.answer} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, answer: e.target.value})} />
          <input type="number" placeholder="Sort Order" className="input-field" value={formData.order} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, order: e.target.value})} />
          
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <button type="submit" disabled={loading} className="btn btn-plum-gradient w-full py-2">{loading ? 'Saving...' : 'Save'}</button>
            {editing && <button type="button" onClick={() => {setEditing(null); setFormData({ question: '', answer: '', order: 0 })}} className="px-4 py-2 bg-gray-100 rounded-lg text-gray-600">Cancel</button>}
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>

      <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-3">
        {faqs.map(faq => (
          <div key={faq.id} className="bg-white p-4 rounded-xl border border-gray-100 flex gap-4">
            <div className="bg-gray-50 h-8 w-8 flex items-center justify-center rounded-full text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 shrink-0">{faq.order}</div>
            <div className="flex-1">
              <h4 className="font-bold text-gray-800">{faq.question}</h4>
              <p className="text-sm text-gray-600 mt-1">{faq.answer}</p>
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
              <button onClick={() => handleEdit(faq)} className="text-blue-500 hover:bg-blue-50 p-1 rounded"><Edit size={16}/></button>
              <button onClick={() => handleDelete(faq.id)} className="text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50 p-1 rounded"><Trash2 size={16}/></button>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      <style>{`
        .input-field { width: 100%; padding: 0.75rem; border: 1px solid #e5e7eb; border-radius: 0.5rem; outline: none; font-size: 0.9rem; transition: all 0.2s; }
        .input-field:focus { border-color: #6a11cb; ring: 2px solid #6a11cb33; }
      `}</style>
    </div>
  )
}

PATH: ./src/components/admin/FeedbackViewer.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { Loader2, Star, MessageSquare } from 'lucide-react'

export default function FeedbackViewer() {
  const [feedbacks, setFeedbacks] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetch = async () => {
      const { data } = await supabase.from('feedback').select('*, profiles(full_name, email)').order('created_at', { ascending: false })
      setFeedbacks(data || [])
      setLoading(false)
    }
    fetch()
  }, [])

  if (loading) return <Loader2 className="animate-spin text-brand-purple" />

  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 overflow-hidden">
      <div className="p-6 border-b border-gray-100 font-bold text-gray-700 flex items-center gap-2">
        <MessageSquare size={20}/> Event Feedback ({feedbacks.length})
      </div>
      <div className="divide-y divide-gray-100">
        {feedbacks.map(item => (
          <div key={item.id} className="p-6 hover:bg-gray-50 transition">
            <div className="flex justify-between mb-2">
              <div>
                <span className="font-bold text-gray-800">{item.profiles?.full_name || 'Anonymous'}</span>
                <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 ml-2">{item.profiles?.email}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex text-yellow-400 text-xs">
                {[...Array(5)].map((_, i) => <Star key={i} size={12} fill={i < item.rating ? "currentColor" : "none"} />)}
              </div>
            </div>
            <p className="text-sm text-gray-600 bg-gray-50 p-3 rounded-lg border border-gray-100">{item.comment}</p>
            <div className="mt-2 flex justify-between items-center">
               <span className="text-[10px] text-gray-400">{new Date(item.created_at).toLocaleString()}</span>
               {item.is_giveaway_entry && <span className="text-[10px] bg-purple-100 text-purple-700 px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full font-bold">Giveaway Entrant</span>}
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
        {feedbacks.length === 0 && <div className="p-8 text-center text-gray-400">No feedback yet.</div>}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/admin/FileUploader.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { Upload, Loader2, X, Check } from 'lucide-react'
import { generateFilePath } from '@/lib/helpers'

export default function FileUploader({ bucket, onUpload, value, accept = "image/*", label = "Upload File" }) {
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false)

  const handleUpload = async (e) => {
    try {
      const file = e.target.files[0]
      if (!file) return
      setUploading(true)

      const path = generateFilePath(bucket, file)
      const { error } = await supabase.storage.from(bucket).upload(path, file)
      
      if (error) throw error

      // Get Public URL
      const { data } = supabase.storage.from(bucket).getPublicUrl(path)
      onUpload(data.publicUrl)
    } catch (error) {
      alert("Upload failed: " + error.message)
    } finally {
      setUploading(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="mb-4">
      <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase mb-1">{label}</label>
      <div className="flex gap-2 items-center">
        <input 
          type="text" 
          value={value} 
          readOnly
          placeholder="File URL will appear here"
          className="input-field bg-gray-50 text-gray-500 flex-1"
        />
        <label className="cursor-pointer bg-brand-purple text-white p-2.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-brand-dark transition shadow-sm flex-shrink-0">
          {uploading ? <Loader2 size={20} className="animate-spin"/> : <Upload size={20}/>}
          <input 
            type="file" 
            className="hidden" 
            accept={accept}
            onChange={handleUpload}
            disabled={uploading}
          />
        </label>
        {value && <button type="button" onClick={() => onUpload('')} className="p-2.5 bg-red-50 text-red-500 rounded-lg hover:bg-red-100"><X size={20}/></button>}
      </div>
      {value && accept.startsWith('image') && (
        <div className="mt-2 w-20 h-20 bg-gray-100 rounded-lg overflow-hidden border border-gray-200 relative">
          <img src={value} className="w-full h-full object-cover" alt="Preview"/>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/admin/GiveawayManager.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { Gift, Loader2, RefreshCw, Trophy } from 'lucide-react'
import confetti from 'canvas-confetti'

export default function GiveawayManager() {
  const [entries, setEntries] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [winner, setWinner] = useState(null)
  const [isPicking, setIsPicking] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => { fetchEntries() }, [])

  const fetchEntries = async () => {
    const { data } = await supabase.from('feedback').select('*, profiles(full_name, email)').eq('is_giveaway_entry', true)
    setEntries(data || [])
    setLoading(false)
  }

  const pickWinner = () => {
    if (entries.length === 0) return alert("No entries yet!")
    setIsPicking(true)
    setWinner(null)
    
    // Animation effect
    let count = 0
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      setWinner(entries[Math.floor(Math.random() * entries.length)])
      count++
      if (count > 20) {
        clearInterval(interval)
        const finalWinner = entries[Math.floor(Math.random() * entries.length)]
        setWinner(finalWinner)
        setIsPicking(false)
        confetti({ particleCount: 150, spread: 70, origin: { y: 0.6 } })
      }
    }, 100)
  }

  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 p-8 text-center max-w-2xl mx-auto">
      <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-purple-100 text-brand-purple rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-6">
        <Gift size={32}/>
      </div>
      <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">Giveaway Randomizer</h2>
      <p className="text-gray-500 mb-8">{entries.length} valid entries found in the database.</p>

      {winner && (
        <div className={`p-6 rounded-2xl border-2 mb-8 transition-all ${isPicking ? 'border-gray-200 bg-gray-50' : 'border-green-500 bg-green-50 scale-105 shadow-xl'}`}>
          <p className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase mb-2">{isPicking ? 'Picking...' : 'The Winner is'}</p>
          <h3 className="text-3xl font-black text-brand-dark mb-1">{winner.profiles?.full_name || 'Unknown'}</h3>
          <p className="text-brand-purple font-mono">{winner.profiles?.email}</p>
          {!isPicking && (
            <div className="mt-4 inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-green-700 font-bold bg-green-100 px-4 py-2 rounded-full text-sm">
              <Trophy size={16}/> Selected Randomly
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      )}

      <button 
        onClick={pickWinner} 
        disabled={loading || isPicking || entries.length === 0}
        className="btn btn-plum-gradient px-12 py-4 text-lg shadow-lg hover:shadow-xl transform hover:-translate-y-1 transition disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed"
      >
        {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin"/> : (isPicking ? 'Rolling...' : 'Pick a Winner!')}
      </button>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/admin/HomeworkManager.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { Loader2, FileText, CheckCircle, Download, Save, ExternalLink } from 'lucide-react'

export default function HomeworkManager() {
  const [submissions, setSubmissions] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [gradingId, setGradingId] = useState(null)
  const [gradeData, setGradeData] = useState({ grade: '', feedback: '' })

  useEffect(() => { fetchSubmissions() }, [])

  const fetchSubmissions = async () => {
    const { data } = await supabase.from('homework_submissions')
      .select('*, profiles(full_name, email), lessons(title)')
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    setSubmissions(data || [])
    setLoading(false)
  }

  const getDownloadLink = async (path) => {
    // Attempt signed URL first (for private buckets)
    const { data, error } = await supabase.storage.from('homework').createSignedUrl(path, 60)
    if (error || !data) {
      // Fallback to public URL
      const { data: publicData } = supabase.storage.from('homework').getPublicUrl(path)
      return publicData.publicUrl
    }
    return data.signedUrl
  }

  const handleDownload = async (path) => {
    const url = await getDownloadLink(path)
    window.open(url, '_blank')
  }

  const handleGrade = async (id) => {
    await supabase.from('homework_submissions').update({ 
      status: 'graded', 
      grade: gradeData.grade, 
      feedback: gradeData.feedback 
    }).eq('id', id)
    
    setGradingId(null)
    setGradeData({ grade: '', feedback: '' })
    fetchSubmissions()
  }

  const openGrading = (sub) => {
    setGradingId(sub.id)
    setGradeData({ grade: sub.grade || '', feedback: sub.feedback || '' })
  }

  if (loading) return <Loader2 className="animate-spin text-brand-purple" />

  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 overflow-hidden">
      <div className="p-6 border-b border-gray-100 font-bold text-gray-700 flex items-center gap-2">
        <FileText size={20}/> Homework Submissions
      </div>
      
      <div className="divide-y divide-gray-100">
        {submissions.map(sub => (
          <div key={sub.id} className="p-6 hover:bg-gray-50 transition">
            <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-4">
              <div>
                <p className="font-bold text-gray-800">{sub.lessons?.title}</p>
                <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">Student: {sub.profiles?.full_name} ({sub.profiles?.email})</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1">{new Date(sub.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}</p>
              </div>
              <div className="flex flex-col items-end gap-2">
                 {sub.status === 'pending' ? (
                   <span className="bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-700 px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold">Pending Review</span>
                 ) : (
                   <span className="bg-green-100 text-green-700 px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold">Graded: {sub.grade}</span>
                 )}
                 <button onClick={() => handleDownload(sub.file_url)} className="text-brand-blue text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1 hover:underline cursor-pointer">
                   <Download size={12}/> Download File
                 </button>
              </div>
            </div>

            {gradingId === sub.id ? (
              <div className="bg-gray-50 p-4 rounded-xl border border-gray-200 mt-4 animate-fade-in">
                <div className="grid grid-cols-4 gap-4 mb-3">
                  <div className="col-span-1">
                    <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 mb-1">Grade</label>
                    <input className="w-full p-2 rounded border" placeholder="e.g. A, 95/100" value={gradeData.grade} onChange={e => setGradeData({...gradeData, grade: e.target.value})}/>
                  </div>
                  <div className="col-span-3">
                    <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 mb-1">Feedback</label>
                    <input className="w-full p-2 rounded border" placeholder="Great work!" value={gradeData.feedback} onChange={e => setGradeData({...gradeData, feedback: e.target.value})}/>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
                  <button onClick={() => setGradingId(null)} className="px-3 py-1 text-sm text-gray-500">Cancel</button>
                  <button onClick={() => handleGrade(sub.id)} className="bg-brand-purple text-white px-4 py-1 rounded text-sm font-bold flex items-center gap-1">
                    <Save size={14}/> Save Grade
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <button onClick={() => openGrading(sub)} className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-500 hover:text-brand-purple mt-2 underline">
                {sub.status === 'pending' ? 'Grade Now' : 'Edit Grade'}
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        ))}
        {submissions.length === 0 && <div className="p-8 text-center text-gray-400">No submissions yet.</div>}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/admin/LeadsManager.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { Trash2, Mail, Users, AlertTriangle, Download, Send, X, Megaphone } from 'lucide-react'
import { downloadCSV } from '@/utils/csvExport'

export default function LeadsManager() {
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const [leads, setLeads] = useState([])
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState([])
  const [view, setView] = useState('leads')
  const [replyingTo, setReplyingTo] = useState(null)
  const [replyBody, setReplyBody] = useState('')
  const [broadcasting, setBroadcasting] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => { fetchData() }, [])

  const fetchData = async () => {
    const { data: l } = await supabase.from('webinar_leads').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    setLeads(l || [])
    
    const { data: m } = await supabase.from('contact_messages').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    setMessages(m || [])
  }

  const handleDelete = async (table, id) => {
    if(!confirm("Delete this record?")) return
    await supabase.from(table).delete().eq('id', id)
    fetchData()
  }

  const handleSendReply = async () => {
    if(!replyBody) return
    await supabase.from('admin_email_logs').insert([{
      admin_id: user.id,
      recipient_email: replyingTo.email,
      subject: `Re: ${replyingTo.message ? 'Your Message' : 'Webinar Info'}`,
      body: replyBody
    }])
    alert(`Reply sent to ${replyingTo.email}`)
    setReplyingTo(null)
    setReplyBody('')
  }

  const handleBroadcast = async () => {
    if(!replyBody) return
    const confirmed = confirm(`Send this email to ALL ${leads.length} leads?`)
    if(!confirmed) return

    // Log broadcast
    await supabase.from('admin_email_logs').insert([{
      admin_id: user.id,
      recipient_email: 'ALL_LEADS',
      subject: 'Broadcast: Webinar Update',
      body: replyBody
    }])
    
    alert(`Broadcast queued for ${leads.length} recipients.`)
    setBroadcasting(false)
    setReplyBody('')
  }

  return (
    <div className="grid lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8 relative">
      {(replyingTo || broadcasting) && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-6 w-full max-w-lg shadow-2xl animate-fade-in border-t-8 border-brand-purple">
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4 border-b pb-4">
              <h3 className="font-bold text-lg flex items-center gap-2">
                {broadcasting ? <><Megaphone size={20}/> Broadcast to All</> : `Reply to ${replyingTo.name || replyingTo.full_name}`}
              </h3>
              <button onClick={() => {setReplyingTo(null); setBroadcasting(false)}}><X className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600"/></button>
            </div>
            
            {broadcasting && <div className="bg-yellow-50 p-3 rounded-lg mb-4 text-sm text-yellow-800 font-bold">⚠️ You are about to email {leads.length} people.</div>}
            
            {!broadcasting && (
              <div className="bg-gray-50 p-3 rounded-lg mb-4 text-xs text-gray-500 italic max-h-24 overflow-y-auto">
                "{replyingTo.message || 'Webinar Registration'}"
              </div>
            )}
            
            <textarea 
              className="w-full p-4 border rounded-xl h-40 mb-4 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-purple outline-none"
              placeholder={broadcasting ? "Write your newsletter/update here..." : "Type your email reply here..."}
              value={replyBody}
              onChange={e => setReplyBody(e.target.value)}
            />
            <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
              <button onClick={() => {setReplyingTo(null); setBroadcasting(false)}} className="px-4 py-2 text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-100 rounded-lg">Cancel</button>
              <button onClick={broadcasting ? handleBroadcast : handleSendReply} className="btn btn-plum-gradient px-6 py-2 flex items-center gap-2">
                <Send size={16}/> {broadcasting ? 'Send Broadcast' : 'Send Email'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="lg:col-span-1 space-y-2">
        <button onClick={() => setView('leads')} className={`w-full p-4 rounded-xl text-left flex items-center gap-3 transition ${view === 'leads' ? 'bg-brand-purple text-white shadow-md' : 'bg-white text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-50'}`}>
          <Users size={20}/>
          <div className="flex-1"><p className="font-bold">Webinar Leads</p><p className="text-xs opacity-80">{leads.length} Registered</p></div>
        </button>
        <button onClick={() => setView('messages')} className={`w-full p-4 rounded-xl text-left flex items-center gap-3 transition ${view === 'messages' ? 'bg-brand-blue text-white shadow-md' : 'bg-white text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-50'}`}>
          <Mail size={20}/>
          <div className="flex-1"><p className="font-bold">Messages & Reports</p><p className="text-xs opacity-80">{messages.length} Inbox</p></div>
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className="lg:col-span-2">
        {view === 'leads' ? (
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 overflow-hidden">
            <div className="p-4 border-b border-gray-100 font-bold text-gray-700 flex justify-between items-center">
              <span>Webinar Registrations</span>
              <div className="flex gap-2">
                <button onClick={() => setBroadcasting(true)} className="text-xs flex items-center gap-1 bg-purple-50 text-brand-purple px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-purple-100 font-bold"><Megaphone size={14}/> Broadcast</button>
                <button onClick={() => downloadCSV(leads, 'webinar_leads')} className="text-xs flex items-center gap-1 bg-green-50 text-green-700 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-lg hover:bg-green-100 font-bold"><Download size={14}/> Export</button>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="max-h-[500px] overflow-y-auto">
              {leads.map(lead => (
                <div key={lead.id} className="p-4 border-b border-gray-50 flex justify-between items-center hover:bg-gray-50">
                  <div><p className="font-bold text-gray-800">{lead.full_name}</p><p className="text-xs text-gray-500">{lead.email}</p></div>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                    <button onClick={() => setReplyingTo(lead)} className="text-blue-500 hover:bg-blue-50 p-2 rounded-lg"><Mail size={16}/></button>
                    <button onClick={() => handleDelete('webinar_leads', lead.id)} className="text-gray-300 hover:text-red-500"><Trash2 size={16}/></button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            {messages.map(msg => (
              <div key={msg.id} className={`bg-white p-5 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border ${msg.email === 'system@report.com' ? 'border-l-4 border-l-red-500' : 'border-gray-100'}`}>
                <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    {msg.email === 'system@report.com' && <AlertTriangle size={16} className="text-red-500"/>}
                    <span className="font-bold text-gray-800">{msg.name}</span>
                    <span className="text-xs text-gray-400">• {new Date(msg.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex gap-2">
                    <button onClick={() => setReplyingTo(msg)} className="text-blue-500 hover:bg-blue-50 p-1.5 rounded"><Mail size={16}/></button>
                    <button onClick={() => handleDelete('contact_messages', msg.id)} className="text-gray-300 hover:text-red-500 p-1.5 rounded"><Trash2 size={16}/></button>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mb-2">{msg.email}</p>
                <div className="bg-gray-50 p-3 rounded-lg text-sm text-gray-700 leading-relaxed whitespace-pre-wrap">{msg.message}</div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/admin/OrderManager.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { Loader2, Search, DollarSign, RefreshCcw, XCircle, CheckCircle } from 'lucide-react'

export default function OrderManager() {
  const [orders, setOrders] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState('')

  useEffect(() => { fetchOrders() }, [])

  const fetchOrders = async () => {
    const { data } = await supabase
      .from('purchases')
      .select('*, profiles(full_name, email), products(title, price, type)')
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    setOrders(data || [])
    setLoading(false)
  }

  const handleRefund = async (id) => {
    if (!confirm("Are you sure you want to refund and revoke access?")) return
    // In real Stripe app, call API here.
    await supabase.from('purchases').update({ status: 'refunded' }).eq('id', id)
    fetchOrders()
  }

  const filteredOrders = orders.filter(o => 
    o.id.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase()) ||
    o.profiles?.email?.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase()) ||
    o.products?.title?.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase())
  )

  if (loading) return <Loader2 className="animate-spin text-brand-purple" />

  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 overflow-hidden">
      <div className="p-6 border-b border-gray-100 flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-center gap-4">
        <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800 flex items-center gap-2"><DollarSign size={20}/> Transaction History</h3>
        <div className="relative w-full md:w-64">
          <Search className="absolute left-3 top-2.5 text-gray-400" size={18} />
          <input 
            type="text" 
            placeholder="Search order ID, email..." 
            className="pl-10 pr-4 py-2 w-full border border-gray-200 rounded-lg outline-none focus:border-brand-purple"
            value={searchTerm}
            onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)}
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="overflow-x-auto">
        <table className="w-full text-left text-sm">
          <thead className="bg-gray-50 text-gray-500 font-bold uppercase text-xs">
            <tr>
              <th className="p-4">Date</th>
              <th className="p-4">Customer</th>
              <th className="p-4">Product</th>
              <th className="p-4">Amount</th>
              <th className="p-4">Status</th>
              <th className="p-4 text-right">Actions</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody className="divide-y divide-gray-100">
            {filteredOrders.map(order => (
              <tr key={order.id} className="hover:bg-gray-50 transition">
                <td className="p-4 font-mono text-gray-500 text-xs">{new Date(order.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}</td>
                <td className="p-4">
                  <p className="font-bold text-gray-800">{order.profiles?.full_name || 'Guest'}</p>
                  <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">{order.profiles?.email}</p>
                </td>
                <td className="p-4">
                  <span className="font-medium text-brand-purple">{order.products?.title}</span>
                  <span className="block text-xs text-gray-400 uppercase">{order.products?.type}</span>
                </td>
                <td className="p-4 font-bold">{order.products?.price}</td>
                <td className="p-4">
                  {order.status === 'completed' ? (
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 rounded-full bg-green-100 text-green-700 text-xs font-bold">
                      <CheckCircle size={12}/> Paid
                    </span>
                  ) : (
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 px-2 py-1 rounded-full bg-red-100 text-red-700 text-xs font-bold">
                      <XCircle size={12}/> Refunded
                    </span>
                  )}
                </td>
                <td className="p-4 text-right">
                  {order.status === 'completed' && (
                    <button 
                      onClick={() => handleRefund(order.id)}
                      className="text-gray-400 hover:text-red-500 flex items-center gap-1 ml-auto text-xs font-bold"
                      title="Refund & Revoke"
                    >
                      <RefreshCcw size={14}/> Refund
                    </button>
                  )}
                </td>
              </tr>
            ))}
          </tbody>
        </table>
        {filteredOrders.length === 0 && <div className="p-8 text-center text-gray-400">No transactions found.</div>}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

PATH: ./src/components/admin/ReviewManager.jsx

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { Loader2, Trash2, Star, MessageSquare } from 'lucide-react'

export default function ReviewManager() {
  const [reviews, setReviews] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)

  useEffect(() => { fetchReviews() }, [])

  const fetchReviews = async () => {
    const { data } = await supabase.from('reviews').select('*, profiles(full_name, email), products(title)').order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    setReviews(data || [])
    setLoading(false)
  }

  const handleDelete = async (id) => {
    if(!confirm("Delete this review?")) return
    await supabase.from('reviews').delete().eq('id', id)
    fetchReviews()
  }

  if (loading) return <Loader2 className="animate-spin text-brand-purple" />

  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 overflow-hidden">
      <div className="p-6 border-b border-gray-100 font-bold text-gray-700 flex items-center gap-2">
        <MessageSquare size={20}/> All Reviews ({reviews.length})
      </div>
      <div className="divide-y divide-gray-100">
        {reviews.map(review => (
          <div key={review.id} className="p-6 hover:bg-gray-50 transition flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-4">
            <div className="flex-1">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
                <span className="font-bold text-gray-800">{review.profiles?.full_name || 'Anonymous'}</span>
                <span className="text-xs text-gray-400">on {review.products?.title}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex text-yellow-400 text-xs mb-2">
                {[...Array(5)].map((_, i) => <Star key={i} size={12} fill={i < review.rating ? "currentColor" : "none"} />)}
              </div>
              <p className="text-sm text-gray-600 bg-gray-50 p-3 rounded-lg border border-gray-100">{review.comment}</p>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center">
              <button onClick={() => handleDelete(review.id)} className="text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50 p-2 rounded-lg flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-bold">
                <Trash2 size={16}/> Delete
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
        {reviews.length === 0 && <div className="p-8 text-center text-gray-400">No reviews yet.</div>}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

"use client";
import { useState } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { Database, RefreshCcw, AlertTriangle, Loader2, ArrowRight } from 'lucide-react'

export default function SeedManager() {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)

  const handleMigration = async () => {
    setLoading(true)
    // Since we don't have direct SQL access from client without RPC, we alert.
    alert("Please run this SQL in your Supabase Dashboard -> SQL Editor:\n\nALTER TABLE public.lessons ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS position integer DEFAULT 0;")
    setLoading(false)
  }

  // Client-side seed script for demonstration
  const handleSeed = async () => {
    if (!confirm("WARNING: This will insert dummy data. Continue?")) return
    setLoading(true)
    
    try {
      await supabase.from('tutors').insert([{
        id: crypto.randomUUID(),
        name: 'Kim Min-ji',
        role: 'Head Teacher',
        bio: 'Expert in Korean linguistics.',
        image_url: 'https://placehold.co/400',
        specialties: ['Grammar', 'Exam Prep'],
        is_featured: true
      }])

      const courseId = 'course-seed-' + Date.now()
      await supabase.from('products').insert([{
        id: courseId,
        title: 'Korean 101 (Seed)',
        description: 'Generated course.',
        price: '5000 Ft',
        type: 'course',
        level: 'Beginner',
        image_url: 'https://placehold.co/400'
      }])

      await supabase.from('lessons').insert([{
        course_id: courseId,
        title: 'Lesson 1: Hangeul',
        type: 'video',
        video_url: 'https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=s5aobqyEaMQ',
        description: 'Learn the basics. [[VOCAB::A|First Vowel]]',
        module: 'Introduction',
        position: 0
      }])

      alert("Database Seeded Successfully!")
    } catch (e) {
      alert("Error: " + e.message)
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }

  const handleReset = async () => {
    alert("Resetting requires SQL access for safety. Please use Supabase Dashboard.")
  }

  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-8 shadow-sm border border-gray-100 max-w-xl mx-auto text-center">
      <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-blue-100 text-brand-blue rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-6">
        <Database size={32}/>
      </div>
      <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">Database Tools</h2>
      
      <div className="bg-yellow-50 p-4 rounded-xl border border-yellow-100 text-left mb-6 text-sm">
        <strong>Required Migration:</strong> For Drag & Drop to work, the lessons table needs a 'position' column.
      </div>

      <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
        <button onClick={handleMigration} disabled={loading} className="btn bg-brand-dark text-white py-3 px-6 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center gap-2 mx-auto w-full">
            {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin"/> : <><Database size={20}/> Show SQL Migration</>}
        </button>
        
        <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 mt-2">
            <button onClick={handleSeed} disabled={loading} className="btn bg-green-600 hover:bg-green-700 text-white py-3 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
            <RefreshCcw size={18}/> Seed Data
            </button>
            <button onClick={handleReset} className="btn bg-red-50 text-red-600 hover:bg-red-100 py-3 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center gap-2 border border-red-100">
            <AlertTriangle size={18}/> Reset DB
            </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

PATH: ./src/components/admin/SettingsManager.jsx
