"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { Loader2, Save, AlertTriangle, Globe } from 'lucide-react'

export default function SettingsManager() {
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false)
  const [maintenance, setMaintenance] = useState({ enabled: false, message: '' })
  const [metadata, setMetadata] = useState({ title: '', description: '' })

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchSettings()
  }, [])

  const fetchSettings = async () => {
    const { data: m } = await supabase.from('system_settings').select('value').eq('key', 'maintenance_mode').single()
    if (m) setMaintenance(m.value)
    
    const { data: s } = await supabase.from('system_settings').select('value').eq('key', 'site_metadata').single()
    if (s) setMetadata(s.value)
    
    setLoading(false)
  }

  const handleSave = async () => {
    setSaving(true)
    await supabase.from('system_settings').upsert({ key: 'maintenance_mode', value: maintenance, updated_at: new Date() })
    await supabase.from('system_settings').upsert({ key: 'site_metadata', value: metadata, updated_at: new Date() })
    setSaving(false)
    alert("System settings updated!")
  }

  if (loading) return <Loader2 className="animate-spin text-brand-purple" />

  return (
    <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-8">
      
      {/* Maintenance Mode Card */}
      <div className={`p-6 rounded-2xl border-2 transition-all ${maintenance.enabled ? 'bg-red-50 border-red-200' : 'bg-white border-gray-100'}`}>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-4">
          <div className={`p-2 rounded-full ${maintenance.enabled ? 'bg-red-100 text-red-600' : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-500'}`}>
            <AlertTriangle size={24} />
          </div>
          <div>
            <h3 className="font-bold text-lg text-gray-800">Maintenance Mode</h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">Lock the site for non-admins</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="space-y-4">
          <label className="flex items-center gap-3 cursor-pointer bg-white p-3 rounded-xl border border-gray-200">
             <div className={`w-12 h-6 rounded-full p-1 transition-colors ${maintenance.enabled ? 'bg-red-500' : 'bg-gray-300'}`}>
               <div className={`bg-white w-4 h-4 rounded-full shadow-md transform transition-transform ${maintenance.enabled ? 'translate-x-6' : 'translate-x-0'}`}></div>
             </div>
             <input type="checkbox" className="hidden" checked={maintenance.enabled} onChange={e => setMaintenance({...maintenance, enabled: e.target.checked})} />
             <span className="font-bold text-sm">{maintenance.enabled ? 'Enabled (Site Locked)' : 'Disabled (Site Live)'}</span>
          </label>

          <textarea 
            className="w-full p-3 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl text-sm outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-red-200"
            placeholder="Maintenance Message..."
            value={maintenance.message}
            onChange={e => setMaintenance({...maintenance, message: e.target.value})}
          />
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* SEO Settings Card */}
      <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 shadow-sm">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-4">
          <div className="p-2 rounded-full bg-blue-50 text-brand-blue"><Globe size={24} /></div>
          <h3 className="font-bold text-lg text-gray-800">Site Metadata</h3>
        </div>
        <div className="space-y-4">
          <div>
             <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase mb-1">Site Title</label>
             <input className="w-full p-2 border border-gray-200 rounded-lg outline-none" value={metadata.title} onChange={e => setMetadata({...metadata, title: e.target.value})} />
          </div>
          <div>
             <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase mb-1">Description</label>
             <textarea className="w-full p-2 border border-gray-200 rounded-lg outline-none h-24" value={metadata.description} onChange={e => setMetadata({...metadata, description: e.target.value})} />
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="md:col-span-2 text-right">
        <button onClick={handleSave} disabled={saving} className="btn btn-plum-gradient px-8 py-3 flex items-center gap-2 ml-auto">
           {saving ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin"/> : <><Save size={18}/> Save System Settings</>}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/admin/ShopManager.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { Loader2, Trash2, Edit, ShoppingBag, FileText, Tag, Star, CreditCard, Clock } from 'lucide-react'
import FileUploader from './FileUploader'

export default function ShopManager() {
  const [products, setProducts] = useState([])
  const [tutors, setTutors] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(null)
  
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({ 
    title: '', price: '', sale_price: '', sale_ends_at: '', description: '', 
    image_url: '', type: 'ebook', is_featured: false,
    tutor_id: '', upsell_product_id: '', instruction_language: 'English'
  })

  useEffect(() => { 
    const init = async () => {
        await fetchProducts() 
        const { data } = await supabase.from('tutors').select('id, name')
        setTutors(data || [])
        setLoading(false)
    }
    init()
  }, [])

  const fetchProducts = async () => {
    const { data } = await supabase.from('products').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    setProducts(data || [])
  }

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setLoading(true)
    const id = editing ? editing.id : `prod-${Date.now()}`
    
    const payload = { ...formData }
    if (payload.sale_price === '') { payload.sale_price = null; payload.sale_ends_at = null; }
    if (payload.sale_ends_at === '') payload.sale_ends_at = null
    if (payload.tutor_id === '') payload.tutor_id = null
    if (payload.upsell_product_id === '') payload.upsell_product_id = null

    if (editing) {
      await supabase.from('products').update(payload).eq('id', id)
    } else {
      await supabase.from('products').insert([{ ...payload, id }])
    }
    
    setEditing(null)
    resetForm()
    fetchProducts()
    setLoading(false)
  }

  const resetForm = () => {
    setFormData({ 
      title: '', price: '', sale_price: '', sale_ends_at: '', description: '', 
      image_url: '', type: 'ebook', is_featured: false, 
      tutor_id: '', upsell_product_id: '', instruction_language: 'English' 
    })
  }

  const handleEdit = (prod) => {
    setEditing(prod)
    setFormData({ 
      title: prod.title, 
      price: prod.price, 
      sale_price: prod.sale_price || '',
      sale_ends_at: prod.sale_ends_at ? new Date(prod.sale_ends_at).toISOString().slice(0,16) : '',
      description: prod.description || '', 
      image_url: prod.image_url || '', 
      type: prod.type,
      is_featured: prod.is_featured || false,
      tutor_id: prod.tutor_id || '',
      upsell_product_id: prod.upsell_product_id || '',
      instruction_language: prod.instruction_language || 'English'
    })
  }

  const handleDelete = async (id) => {
    if(!confirm("Delete product?")) return
    await supabase.from('products').delete().eq('id', id)
    fetchProducts()
  }

  return (
    <div className="grid lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
      <div className="lg:col-span-1 bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 h-fit sticky top-24">
        <h3 className="font-bold text-lg mb-4">{editing ? 'Edit Product' : 'Add Product'}</h3>
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
          <input required placeholder="Product Title" className="input-field" value={formData.title} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, title: e.target.value})} />
          
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
            <div>
                <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase">Price</label>
                <input required placeholder="5000 Ft" className="input-field" value={formData.price} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, price: e.target.value})} />
            </div>
            <div>
                <label className="text-xs font-bold text-red-500 uppercase">Sale Price</label>
                <input placeholder="Optional" className="input-field border-red-100 text-red-600" value={formData.sale_price} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, sale_price: e.target.value})} />
            </div>
          </div>

          {formData.sale_price && (
            <div>
                <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase">Sale Ends At</label>
                <input type="datetime-local" className="input-field" value={formData.sale_ends_at} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, sale_ends_at: e.target.value})} />
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="flex gap-2 items-center border border-gray-200 p-2 rounded-lg bg-gray-50">
             <input type="checkbox" id="feat" className="w-5 h-5 text-brand-purple" checked={formData.is_featured} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, is_featured: e.target.checked})} />
             <label htmlFor="feat" className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-700 cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-1"><Star size={14} className="text-yellow-500"/> Feature on Homepage</label>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
             <select className="input-field" value={formData.type} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, type: e.target.value})}>
                <option value="ebook">Ebook</option>
                <option value="merch">Merch</option>
                <option value="course">Course</option>
                <option value="subscription">Subscription</option>
                <option value="standalone">Live Event</option>
             </select>
             <select className="input-field" value={formData.instruction_language} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, instruction_language: e.target.value})}>
                <option value="English">Taught in English</option>
                <option value="Hungarian">Taught in Hungarian</option>
                <option value="Korean">Taught in Korean</option>
             </select>
          </div>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
             <select className="input-field" value={formData.tutor_id} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, tutor_id: e.target.value})}>
                <option value="">No Tutor</option>
                {tutors.map(t => <option key={t.id} value={t.id}>{t.name}</option>)}
             </select>
             <select className="input-field" value={formData.upsell_product_id} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, upsell_product_id: e.target.value})}>
                <option value="">No Upsell</option>
                {products.filter(p => p.type === 'ebook' && p.id !== editing?.id).map(p => <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>{p.title}</option>)}
             </select>
          </div>
          
          <FileUploader 
            bucket="course-materials"
            label="Product Image" 
            value={formData.image_url} 
            onUpload={(url) => setFormData({...formData, image_url: url})} 
          />

          <textarea required placeholder="Description" className="input-field h-32" value={formData.description} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, description: e.target.value})} />
          
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <button type="submit" disabled={loading} className="btn btn-plum-gradient w-full py-2">{loading ? 'Saving...' : 'Save Product'}</button>
            {editing && <button type="button" onClick={() => {setEditing(null); resetForm()}} className="px-4 py-2 bg-gray-100 rounded-lg text-gray-600">Cancel</button>}
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>

      <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-4">
        {products.map(p => (
          <div key={p.id} className={`bg-white p-4 rounded-xl border flex gap-4 items-center ${p.is_featured ? 'border-yellow-300 shadow-sm' : 'border-gray-100'}`}>
            <img src={p.image_url || 'https://placehold.co/100'} className="w-12 h-12 object-cover rounded-lg bg-gray-100" />
            <div className="flex-1">
              <h4 className="font-bold text-gray-800">{p.title}</h4>
              <div className="flex gap-2 text-xs items-center">
                 <span className="uppercase bg-gray-100 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-gray-500">{p.type}</span>
                 {p.sale_price ? (
                    <span className="text-red-500 font-bold flex items-center gap-1"><Tag size={10}/> {p.sale_price}</span>
                 ) : (
                    <span className="font-bold">{p.price}</span>
                 )}
                 {p.sale_ends_at && new Date(p.sale_ends_at) > new Date() && <span className="text-orange-500 flex items-center gap-1"><Clock size={10}/> Timer Active</span>}
              </div>
            </div>
            <button onClick={() => handleEdit(p)} className="p-2 text-blue-500 hover:bg-blue-50 rounded-lg"><Edit size={18}/></button>
            <button onClick={() => handleDelete(p.id)} className="p-2 text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50 rounded-lg"><Trash2 size={18}/></button>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      <style>{`
        .input-field { width: 100%; padding: 0.75rem; border: 1px solid #e5e7eb; border-radius: 0.5rem; outline: none; font-size: 0.9rem; transition: all 0.2s; }
        .input-field:focus { border-color: #6a11cb; ring: 2px solid #6a11cb33; }
      `}</style>
    </div>
  )
}

"use client";
import { Code, Link, Type, HelpCircle } from 'lucide-react'

export default function SnippetHelper({ onInsert, onInsertQuiz }) {
  const exampleQuiz = JSON.stringify([
    {
      "question": "What is the capital of Korea?",
      "options": ["Busan", "Seoul", "Incheon", "Jeju"],
      "correct": 1
    }
  ], null, 2);

  return (
    <div className="flex gap-2 mb-2 overflow-x-auto pb-2">
      <button type="button" onClick={() => onInsert('[[VOCAB::Term|Definition]]')} className="text-xs bg-purple-50 text-purple-700 px-3 py-1 rounded-full border border-purple-100 hover:bg-purple-100 flex items-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap">
        <Type size={12}/> + Vocab
      </button>
      <button type="button" onClick={() => onInsert('[[ZOOM::https://zoom.us/j/123]]')} className="text-xs bg-blue-50 text-blue-700 px-3 py-1 rounded-full border border-blue-100 hover:bg-blue-100 flex items-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap">
        <Link size={12}/> + Zoom Link
      </button>
      <button type="button" onClick={() => onInsert('[[DOWNLOAD_URL::https://link.com]]')} className="text-xs bg-green-50 text-green-700 px-3 py-1 rounded-full border border-green-100 hover:bg-green-100 flex items-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap">
        <Code size={12}/> + Download
      </button>
      {onInsertQuiz && (
        <button type="button" onClick={() => onInsertQuiz(exampleQuiz)} className="text-xs bg-orange-50 text-orange-700 px-3 py-1 rounded-full border border-orange-100 hover:bg-orange-100 flex items-center gap-1 whitespace-nowrap">
          <HelpCircle size={12}/> + Quiz Template
        </button>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/admin/StudentManager.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { Loader2, Download, Bell, Send, X, PlusCircle, Trash2 } from 'lucide-react'

export default function StudentManager() {
  const [users, setUsers] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState('')
  
  // Detail & Action State
  const [viewingUser, setViewingUser] = useState(null)
  const [userDetails, setUserDetails] = useState(null)
  const [notifModalOpen, setNotifModalOpen] = useState(false)
  const [grantModalOpen, setGrantModalOpen] = useState(false)
  const [notifData, setNotifData] = useState({ title: '', message: '' })
  const [sendingNotif, setSendingNotif] = useState(false)
  
  // Granting
  const [availableProducts, setAvailableProducts] = useState([])
  const [selectedProduct, setSelectedProduct] = useState('')

  useEffect(() => { fetchData() }, [])

  const fetchData = async () => {
    const { data: u } = await supabase.from('profiles').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    setUsers(u || [])
    setLoading(false)
  }

  const fetchUserDetails = async (id) => {
    const { data: stats } = await supabase.from('user_stats').select('*').eq('user_id', id).single()
    const { data: progress } = await supabase.from('lesson_progress').select('count', { count: 'exact' }).eq('user_id', id)
    const { data: quizzes } = await supabase.from('quiz_results').select('score, passed, created_at').eq('user_id', id)
    const { data: purchases } = await supabase.from('purchases').select('id, created_at, products(title)').eq('user_id', id)
    
    setUserDetails({ 
        stats, 
        lessonsCompleted: progress?.count || 0, 
        quizzes: quizzes || [],
        purchases: purchases || []
    })
  }

  const handleViewUser = (user) => {
    setViewingUser(user)
    fetchUserDetails(user.id)
  }

  const handleSendNotification = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if(!notifData.title || !notifData.message) return
    setSendingNotif(true)
    try {
      await supabase.from('notifications').insert([{
        user_id: viewingUser.id,
        title: notifData.title,
        message: notifData.message,
        is_read: false
      }])
      alert("Notification sent!")
      setNotifModalOpen(false)
      setNotifData({ title: '', message: '' })
    } catch (e) { alert("Error sending notification") } finally { setSendingNotif(false) }
  }

  const openGrantModal = async () => {
    const { data } = await supabase.from('products').select('id, title, type')
    setAvailableProducts(data || [])
    setGrantModalOpen(true)
  }

  const handleGrantAccess = async () => {
    if (!selectedProduct) return
    await supabase.from('purchases').insert([{
        user_id: viewingUser.id,
        product_id: selectedProduct,
        status: 'completed'
    }])
    alert("Access Granted!")
    setGrantModalOpen(false)
    fetchUserDetails(viewingUser.id) // Refresh
  }

  const handleRevokeAccess = async (purchaseId) => {
    if(!confirm("Revoke access to this product?")) return
    await supabase.from('purchases').delete().eq('id', purchaseId)
    fetchUserDetails(viewingUser.id)
  }

  const filteredUsers = users.filter(u => u.full_name?.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase()))

  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8 relative">
      
      {/* Notification Modal */}
      {notifModalOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-6 w-full max-w-md shadow-2xl animate-fade-in">
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
                <h3 className="font-bold text-lg flex items-center gap-2"><Bell size={20}/> Notify {viewingUser?.full_name}</h3>
                <button onClick={() => setNotifModalOpen(false)}><X size={20} className="text-gray-400"/></button>
            </div>
            <form onSubmit={handleSendNotification} className="space-y-4">
                <input required placeholder="Title" className="input-field" value={notifData.title} onChange={e => setNotifData({...notifData, title: e.target.value})} />
                <textarea required placeholder="Message" className="input-field h-24" value={notifData.message} onChange={e => setNotifData({...notifData, message: e.target.value})} />
                <button disabled={sendingNotif} className="btn btn-plum-gradient w-full py-2 flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                    {sendingNotif ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" size={18}/> : <><Send size={18}/> Send Now</>}
                </button>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Grant Modal */}
      {grantModalOpen && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-6 w-full max-w-md shadow-2xl animate-fade-in">
            <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-4">
                <h3 className="font-bold text-lg">Grant Product Access</h3>
                <button onClick={() => setGrantModalOpen(false)}><X size={20} className="text-gray-400"/></button>
            </div>
            <div className="space-y-4">
                <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">Manually enroll <strong>{viewingUser?.full_name}</strong> in a course or product.</p>
                <select className="input-field" value={selectedProduct} onChange={e => setSelectedProduct(e.target.value)}>
                    <option value="">Select Product...</option>
                    {availableProducts.map(p => <option key={p.id} value={p.id}>{p.type.toUpperCase()}: {p.title}</option>)}
                </select>
                <button onClick={handleGrantAccess} className="btn bg-green-600 w-full py-2 flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                    Grant Access
                </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className={`lg:col-span-${viewingUser ? '1' : '3'} bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 overflow-hidden h-[600px] flex flex-col`}>
        <div className="p-4 border-b border-gray-100 flex gap-2">
          <input placeholder="Search students..." className="w-full p-2 border rounded-lg text-sm outline-none focus:border-brand-purple" value={searchTerm} onChange={e => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)}/>
        </div>
        <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto">
          {filteredUsers.map(user => (
            <div key={user.id} onClick={() => handleViewUser(user)} className={`p-4 border-b hover:bg-gray-50 cursor-pointer flex items-center gap-3 ${viewingUser?.id === user.id ? 'bg-purple-50' : ''}`}>
              <div className="w-8 h-8 bg-gray-200 rounded-full overflow-hidden"><img src={user.avatar_url || 'https://placehold.co/50'} className="w-full h-full object-cover"/></div>
              <div className="flex-1">
                 <p className="font-bold text-sm text-gray-800">{user.full_name}</p>
                 <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">{user.email}</p>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      </div>

      {viewingUser && (
        <div className="lg:col-span-2 bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 p-8 relative max-h-[600px] overflow-y-auto">
           <button onClick={() => setViewingUser(null)} className="absolute top-4 right-4 text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600">✕</button>
           
           <div className="flex items-center gap-4 mb-8">
             <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-gray-200 rounded-full overflow-hidden"><img src={viewingUser.avatar_url || 'https://placehold.co/100'} className="w-full h-full object-cover"/></div>
             <div>
               <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark">{viewingUser.full_name}</h2>
               <p className="text-gray-500">{viewingUser.email}</p>
               <span className="text-xs bg-blue-100 text-blue-700 px-2 py-1 rounded mt-1 inline-block capitalize">{viewingUser.role}</span>
             </div>
             <div className="ml-auto flex gap-2">
                <button onClick={() => setNotifModalOpen(true)} className="bg-gray-100 p-2 rounded-xl text-gray-600 hover:bg-brand-purple hover:text-white transition" title="Send Notification">
                    <Bell size={20}/>
                </button>
             </div>
           </div>

           {userDetails ? (
             <div className="space-y-6">
                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
                    <div className="bg-gray-50 p-4 rounded-xl border border-gray-100">
                        <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 uppercase font-bold">XP & Streak</p>
                        <p className="text-xl font-black text-brand-purple">{userDetails.stats?.xp || 0} XP / {userDetails.stats?.streak || 0} Days</p>
                    </div>
                    <div className="bg-gray-50 p-4 rounded-xl border border-gray-100">
                        <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 uppercase font-bold">Progress</p>
                        <p className="text-xl font-black text-brand-blue">{userDetails.lessonsCompleted} Lessons Done</p>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div>
                    <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-2">
                        <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800">Enrollments</h3>
                        <button onClick={openGrantModal} className="text-xs font-bold text-brand-purple flex items-center gap-1 hover:bg-purple-50 px-2 py-1 rounded"><PlusCircle size={14}/> Grant Access</button>
                    </div>
                    <div className="space-y-2">
                        {userDetails.purchases.length === 0 && <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm">No active enrollments.</p>}
                        {userDetails.purchases.map((p, i) => (
                        <div key={i} className="flex justify-between text-sm p-2 bg-blue-50 rounded-lg group">
                            <span className="text-blue-800 font-medium">{p.products?.title}</span>
                            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                                <span className="text-xs text-blue-500">{new Date(p.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
                                <button onClick={() => handleRevokeAccess(p.id)} className="text-red-400 hover:text-red-600 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition" title="Revoke"><Trash2 size={14}/></button>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        ))}
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div>
                    <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800 mb-2">Quiz Activity</h3>
                    <div className="space-y-2">
                        {userDetails.quizzes.slice(0, 5).map((q, i) => (
                        <div key={i} className="flex justify-between text-sm p-2 bg-gray-50 rounded-lg">
                            <span className="text-gray-600">{new Date(q.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
                            <span className="text-green-600 font-bold">{q.score} pts - {q.passed ? 'Passed' : 'Failed'}</span>
                        </div>
                        ))}
                        {userDetails.quizzes.length === 0 && <p className="text-gray-400 text-sm">No quiz data.</p>}
                    </div>
                </div>
             </div>
           ) : <Loader2 className="animate-spin mx-auto"/>}
        </div>
      )}
      <style>{`
        .input-field { width: 100%; padding: 0.75rem; border: 1px solid #e5e7eb; border-radius: 0.5rem; outline: none; font-size: 0.9rem; transition: all 0.2s; }
        .input-field:focus { border-color: #6a11cb; ring: 2px solid #6a11cb33; }
      `}</style>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/admin/TestManager.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { Loader2, Trash2, Plus, Brain, Save, Mic, AlignLeft, List } from 'lucide-react'

export default function TestManager() {
  const [questions, setQuestions] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  
  // Form State
  const [qText, setQText] = useState('')
  const [type, setType] = useState('multiple-choice')
  const [options, setOptions] = useState(['', '', '', ''])
  const [correctIdx, setCorrectIdx] = useState(0)
  const [level, setLevel] = useState('beginner')

  useEffect(() => { fetchQuestions() }, [])

  const fetchQuestions = async () => {
    const { data } = await supabase.from('placement_questions').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: true })
    setQuestions(data || [])
    setLoading(false)
  }

  const handleOptionChange = (idx, val) => {
    const newOpts = [...options]
    newOpts[idx] = val
    setOptions(newOpts)
  }

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setLoading(true)
    
    // For non-choice types, we might not use options/correctIdx the same way,
    // but we'll keep the structure consistent or clear it.
    const finalOptions = (type === 'multiple-choice' || type === 'gap-fill') ? options : []
    const finalCorrect = (type === 'multiple-choice' || type === 'gap-fill') ? parseInt(correctIdx) : 0

    const payload = {
      question: qText,
      type: type,
      options: finalOptions,
      correct_index: finalCorrect,
      level
    }
    
    await supabase.from('placement_questions').insert([payload])
    
    // Reset Form
    setQText('')
    setOptions(['', '', '', ''])
    setCorrectIdx(0)
    setType('multiple-choice')
    fetchQuestions()
    setLoading(false)
  }

  const handleDelete = async (id) => {
    if(!confirm("Delete question?")) return
    await supabase.from('placement_questions').delete().eq('id', id)
    fetchQuestions()
  }

  return (
    <div className="grid lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
      <div className="lg:col-span-1 bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 h-fit sticky top-24">
        <h3 className="font-bold text-lg mb-4 flex items-center gap-2"><Brain size={20}/> Add Question</h3>
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase">Question Type</label>
            <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 mt-1">
              <button type="button" onClick={() => setType('multiple-choice')} className={`p-2 text-xs font-bold rounded border ${type === 'multiple-choice' ? 'bg-brand-purple text-white border-brand-purple' : 'bg-white text-gray-600'}`}>Multiple Choice</button>
              <button type="button" onClick={() => setType('gap-fill')} className={`p-2 text-xs font-bold rounded border ${type === 'gap-fill' ? 'bg-brand-purple text-white border-brand-purple' : 'bg-white text-gray-600'}`}>Gap Fill</button>
              <button type="button" onClick={() => setType('voice')} className={`p-2 text-xs font-bold rounded border ${type === 'voice' ? 'bg-brand-purple text-white border-brand-purple' : 'bg-white text-gray-600'}`}>Voice Rec</button>
              <button type="button" onClick={() => setType('essay')} className={`p-2 text-xs font-bold rounded border ${type === 'essay' ? 'bg-brand-purple text-white border-brand-purple' : 'bg-white text-gray-600'}`}>Essay</button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase">Question Text</label>
            <input required className="input-field mt-1" value={qText} onChange={e => setQText(e.target.value)} placeholder={type === 'gap-fill' ? "I am ____ student." : "Enter question prompt..."} />
          </div>
          
          {(type === 'multiple-choice' || type === 'gap-fill') && (
            <div>
              <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase">Options</label>
              <div className="space-y-2 mt-1">
                {options.map((opt, i) => (
                  <div key={i} className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <input 
                      type="radio" 
                      name="correct" 
                      checked={correctIdx === i} 
                      onChange={() => setCorrectIdx(i)}
                      className="accent-brand-purple"
                    />
                    <input 
                      required 
                      className="input-field py-1 text-sm" 
                      placeholder={`Option ${i+1}`}
                      value={opt}
                      onChange={e => handleOptionChange(i, e.target.value)}
                    />
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
              <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 mt-1">* Select the radio button for the correct answer</p>
            </div>
          )}

          <div>
            <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase">Difficulty</label>
            <select className="input-field mt-1" value={level} onChange={e => setLevel(e.target.value)}>
              <option value="beginner">Beginner</option>
              <option value="intermediate">Intermediate</option>
              <option value="advanced">Advanced</option>
            </select>
          </div>

          <button disabled={loading} className="btn btn-plum-gradient w-full py-2 flex justify-center items-center gap-2">
            {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" size={16}/> : <><Plus size={16}/> Add Question</>}
          </button>
        </form>
      </div>

      <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-4">
        {questions.length === 0 && <div className="text-center p-8 text-gray-400 border border-dashed rounded-xl">No questions yet.</div>}
        {questions.map((q, i) => (
          <div key={q.id} className="bg-white p-5 rounded-xl border border-gray-100 relative group">
            <button onClick={() => handleDelete(q.id)} className="absolute top-4 right-4 text-gray-300 hover:text-red-500"><Trash2 size={16}/></button>
            <div className="flex gap-2 mb-2">
               <span className={`text-[10px] uppercase font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded inline-block ${q.level === 'beginner' ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-700' : 'bg-orange-100 text-orange-700'}`}>{q.level}</span>
               <span className="text-[10px] uppercase font-bold px-2 py-1 rounded inline-block bg-gray-100 text-gray-600">{q.type}</span>
            </div>
            <h4 className="font-bold text-gray-800 mb-3 flex items-start gap-2">
               {q.type === 'voice' ? <Mic size={18} className="text-brand-purple mt-0.5"/> : (q.type === 'essay' ? <AlignLeft size={18} className="text-brand-purple mt-0.5"/> : <List size={18} className="text-brand-purple mt-0.5"/>)}
               <span><span className="text-gray-400 mr-2">#{i+1}</span>{q.question}</span>
            </h4>
            
            {(q.type === 'multiple-choice' || q.type === 'gap-fill') && (
              <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-sm">
                {q.options.map((opt, idx) => (
                  <div key={idx} className={`p-2 rounded border ${idx === q.correct_index ? 'bg-green-50 border-green-200 text-green-800 font-bold' : 'bg-gray-50 border-gray-100 text-gray-600'}`}>
                    {opt}
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      <style>{`
        .input-field { width: 100%; padding: 0.5rem; border: 1px solid #e5e7eb; border-radius: 0.5rem; outline: none; font-size: 0.9rem; transition: all 0.2s; }
        .input-field:focus { border-color: #6a11cb; ring: 2px solid #6a11cb33; }
      `}</style>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/admin/TutorManager.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { Loader2, Trash2, Edit, Plus, Instagram, Youtube, Music2 } from 'lucide-react'
import FileUploader from './FileUploader'

export default function TutorManager() {
  const [tutors, setTutors] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [editing, setEditing] = useState(null)
  
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({ 
    id: '', name: '', role: '', bio: '', image_url: '', video_intro: '', specialties: '',
    instagram: '', tiktok: '', youtube: ''
  })

  useEffect(() => { fetchTutors() }, [])

  const fetchTutors = async () => {
    const { data } = await supabase.from('tutors').select('*')
    setTutors(data || [])
    setLoading(false)
  }

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setLoading(true)
    
    const specs = formData.specialties.split(',').map(s => s.trim())
    const social_links = {
      instagram: formData.instagram,
      tiktok: formData.tiktok,
      youtube: formData.youtube
    }

    const payload = { 
      name: formData.name, 
      role: formData.role, 
      bio: formData.bio, 
      image_url: formData.image_url, 
      video_intro: formData.video_intro, 
      specialties: specs,
      social_links: social_links
    }
    
    if (editing) {
      await supabase.from('tutors').update(payload).eq('id', editing.id)
    } else {
      const id = crypto.randomUUID()
      await supabase.from('tutors').insert([{ ...payload, id }])
    }
    
    setEditing(null)
    setFormData({ id: '', name: '', role: '', bio: '', image_url: '', video_intro: '', specialties: '', instagram: '', tiktok: '', youtube: '' })
    fetchTutors()
  }

  const handleEdit = (t) => {
    setEditing(t)
    setFormData({ 
      id: t.id, name: t.name, role: t.role, bio: t.bio, 
      image_url: t.image_url || '', video_intro: t.video_intro || '', 
      specialties: t.specialties ? t.specialties.join(', ') : '',
      instagram: t.social_links?.instagram || '',
      tiktok: t.social_links?.tiktok || '',
      youtube: t.social_links?.youtube || ''
    })
  }

  const handleDelete = async (id) => {
    if(!confirm("Delete tutor?")) return
    await supabase.from('tutors').delete().eq('id', id)
    fetchTutors()
  }

  return (
    <div className="grid lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
      <div className="lg:col-span-1 bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 h-fit sticky top-24">
        <h3 className="font-bold text-lg mb-4">{editing ? 'Edit Tutor' : 'Add Tutor'}</h3>
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
          <input required placeholder="Full Name" className="input-field" value={formData.name} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, name: e.target.value})} />
          <input required placeholder="Role (e.g. Head Teacher)" className="input-field" value={formData.role} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, role: e.target.value})} />
          
          <FileUploader 
            bucket="avatars" 
            label="Profile Photo" 
            value={formData.image_url} 
            onUpload={(url) => setFormData({...formData, image_url: url})} 
          />

          <input placeholder="Video Intro URL (YouTube)" className="input-field" value={formData.video_intro} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, video_intro: e.target.value})} />
          <input placeholder="Specialties (comma separated)" className="input-field" value={formData.specialties} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, specialties: e.target.value})} />
          <textarea required placeholder="Bio" className="input-field h-32" value={formData.bio} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, bio: e.target.value})} />
          
          <div className="space-y-2 pt-2 border-t border-gray-100">
            <p className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase">Social Links</p>
            <div className="relative"><Instagram size={16} className="absolute left-3 top-3 text-gray-400"/><input placeholder="Instagram URL" className="input-field pl-9" value={formData.instagram} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, instagram: e.target.value})} /></div>
            <div className="relative"><Music2 size={16} className="absolute left-3 top-3 text-gray-400"/><input placeholder="TikTok URL" className="input-field pl-9" value={formData.tiktok} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, tiktok: e.target.value})} /></div>
            <div className="relative"><Youtube size={16} className="absolute left-3 top-3 text-gray-400"/><input placeholder="YouTube URL" className="input-field pl-9" value={formData.youtube} onChange={e => setFormData({...formData, youtube: e.target.value})} /></div>
          </div>

          <div className="flex gap-2 pt-2">
            <button type="submit" disabled={loading} className="btn btn-plum-gradient w-full py-2">{loading ? 'Saving...' : 'Save Tutor'}</button>
            {editing && <button type="button" onClick={() => {setEditing(null); setFormData({ id: '', name: '', role: '', bio: '', image_url: '', video_intro: '', specialties: '', instagram: '', tiktok: '', youtube: '' })}} className="px-4 py-2 bg-gray-100 rounded-lg text-gray-600">Cancel</button>}
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>

      <div className="lg:col-span-2 space-y-4">
        {tutors.map(t => (
          <div key={t.id} className="bg-white p-4 rounded-xl border border-gray-100 flex gap-4 items-start">
            <img src={t.image_url || 'https://placehold.co/100'} className="w-16 h-16 object-cover rounded-full bg-gray-100" />
            <div className="flex-1">
              <h4 className="font-bold text-gray-800">{t.name}</h4>
              <p className="text-xs font-bold text-brand-purple uppercase">{t.role}</p>
              <div className="flex gap-2 mt-2">
                {t.social_links?.instagram && <Instagram size={14} className="text-gray-400"/>}
                {t.social_links?.tiktok && <Music2 size={14} className="text-gray-400"/>}
                {t.social_links?.youtube && <Youtube size={14} className="text-gray-400"/>}
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
              <button onClick={() => handleEdit(t)} className="p-2 text-blue-500 hover:bg-blue-50 rounded-lg"><Edit size={18}/></button>
              <button onClick={() => handleDelete(t.id)} className="p-2 text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50 rounded-lg"><Trash2 size={18}/></button>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      <style>{`
        .input-field { width: 100%; padding: 0.75rem; border: 1px solid #e5e7eb; border-radius: 0.5rem; outline: none; font-size: 0.9rem; transition: all 0.2s; }
        .input-field:focus { border-color: #6a11cb; ring: 2px solid #6a11cb33; }
      `}</style>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/auth/AdminRoute.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { Loader2 } from 'lucide-react'

export default function AdminRoute() {
  const { user, isAdmin, loading } = useAuth()

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="h-screen flex items-center justify-center">
        <Loader2 className="animate-spin text-brand-purple" size={40} />
      </div>
    )
  }

  if (!user || !isAdmin) {
    return <Navigate href="/dashboard" replace />
  }

  return <Outlet />
}

"use client";
import { AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react'

export default function SupabaseConfigWarning() {
  const isConfigured = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL && process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY

  if (isConfigured) return null

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-gray-900 bg-opacity-90 z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-8 max-w-lg text-center shadow-2xl">
        <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-red-100 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-6">
          <AlertCircle className="w-8 h-8 text-red-600" />
        </div>
        <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-900 mb-4">Missing Configuration</h2>
        <p className="text-gray-600 mb-6">
          The application cannot connect to the database. 
          <br/>Please create a <code className="bg-gray-100 px-2 py-1 rounded font-mono text-sm">.env.local</code> file in the root directory.
        </p>
        <div className="bg-gray-50 p-4 rounded-xl text-left text-sm font-mono overflow-x-auto">
          <p className="text-gray-500 mb-2"># .env.local</p>
          <p>VITE_SUPABASE_URL=https://your-project.supabase.co</p>
          <p>VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY=your-anon-key</p>
        </div>
        <button 
          onClick={() => window.location.reload()}
          className="mt-8 w-full bg-brand-dark text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl hover:bg-gray-800 transition"
        >
          Retry Connection
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/course/AudioVisualizer.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';

export default function AudioVisualizer({ isRecording }) {
  const canvasRef = useRef(null);
  const animationRef = useRef(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isRecording) {
      if (animationRef.current) cancelAnimationFrame(animationRef.current);
      return;
    }

    const canvas = canvasRef.current;
    const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
    
    // Animation variables
    let bars = 5;
    let step = 0;

    const animate = () => {
      ctx.clearRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      ctx.fillStyle = '#5E3891'; // Brand Purple

      for (let i = 0; i < bars; i++) {
        // Create a mock waveform effect using sine
        const height = 10 + Math.random() * 20 + Math.sin(step + i) * 10;
        const x = i * 12 + 6;
        const y = (canvas.height - height) / 2;
        
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.roundRect(x, y, 6, height, 3);
        ctx.fill();
      }

      step += 0.2;
      animationRef.current = requestAnimationFrame(animate);
    };

    animate();

    return () => {
      if (animationRef.current) cancelAnimationFrame(animationRef.current);
    };
  }, [isRecording]);

  return <canvas ref={canvasRef} width={70} height={40} className="mx-auto" />;
}
