================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/course/CoursePlayer.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react'
import { PlayCircle, CheckCircle, Circle, Download, HelpCircle, AlertTriangle, ExternalLink, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, FileDown, Lock, Music, Clock, Menu, X, Gamepad2, Layers, ChevronDown, ChevronUp } from 'lucide-react'
import { cleanDescription } from '@/lib/helpers'
import confetti from 'canvas-confetti'
import { useGamification } from '@/hooks/useGamification'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import Certificate from './Certificate'
import LessonNotes from './LessonNotes'
import QuizTaker from './QuizTaker'
import CustomAudioPlayer from './CustomAudioPlayer'
import InteractiveVideoPlayer from './InteractiveVideoPlayer'
import VocabularyDrill from './VocabularyDrill'
import MatchingGame from './MatchingGame'
import FlashcardViewer from './FlashcardViewer'
import ReviewSection from './ReviewSection'
import ReportModal from '../ui/ReportModal'
import LessonDiscussion from './LessonDiscussion'
import HomeworkUploader from './HomeworkUploader'
import html2canvas from 'html2canvas'
import jsPDF from 'jspdf'

export default function CoursePlayer({ course, lessons, user }) {
  const [activeLessonIndex, setActiveLessonIndex] = useState(0)
  const [completedLessonIds, setCompletedLessonIds] = useState([])
  const [passedQuizLessonIds, setPassedQuizLessonIds] = useState([]) 
  const [viewMode, setViewMode] = useState('content')
  const [showReport, setShowReport] = useState(false)
  const [showFlashcards, setShowFlashcards] = useState(false)
  const [generatingPdf, setGeneratingPdf] = useState(false)
  const [sidebarOpen, setSidebarOpen] = useState(false)
  const [openModules, setOpenModules] = useState({}) 
  
  const certificateRef = useRef()
  const { addXP } = useGamification(user.id)
  const [gamePairs, setGamePairs] = useState([])
  const [vocabTerms, setVocabTerms] = useState([])

  // Group Lessons
  const groupedLessons = lessons.reduce((acc, lesson, index) => {
    const mod = lesson.module || 'General'
    if (!acc[mod]) acc[mod] = []
    acc[mod].push({ ...lesson, globalIndex: index })
    return acc
  }, {})

  useEffect(() => {
    const initPlayer = async () => {
      const { data: progress } = await supabase.from('lesson_progress').select('lesson_id, last_accessed').eq('user_id', user.id).eq('course_id', course.id)
      const { data: quizResults } = await supabase.from('quiz_results').select('lesson_id, passed').eq('user_id', user.id).eq('course_id', course.id).eq('passed', true)
      
      if(quizResults) setPassedQuizLessonIds(quizResults.map(q => q.lesson_id))

      if(progress) {
        const completed = progress.map(p => p.lesson_id)
        setCompletedLessonIds(completed)
        
        const lastAccessedEntry = progress.sort((a,b) => new Date(b.last_accessed) - new Date(a.last_accessed))[0]
        if (lastAccessedEntry) {
            const index = lessons.findIndex(l => l.id === lastAccessedEntry.lesson_id)
            if (index !== -1) {
                setActiveLessonIndex(index)
                const mod = lessons[index].module || 'General'
                setOpenModules(prev => ({ ...prev, [mod]: true }))
            }
        } else {
            const firstMod = lessons[0]?.module || 'General'
            setOpenModules({ [firstMod]: true })
        }
      }
    }
    initPlayer()
  }, [course.id, user.id])

  const activeLesson = lessons[activeLessonIndex]
  
  useEffect(() => {
    setViewMode('content')
    setSidebarOpen(false)
    if (activeLesson?.description) {
      const regex = /\[\[VOCAB::(.*?)\|(.*?)\]\]/g
      const matches = []
      const terms = []
      let match
      let idCounter = 1
      while ((match = regex.exec(activeLesson.description)) !== null) {
        matches.push({ id: idCounter++, text: match[1], match: match[2] })
        terms.push({ term: match[1], definition: match[2] })
      }
      setGamePairs(matches)
      setVocabTerms(terms)
    } else {
      setGamePairs([])
      setVocabTerms([])
    }
    
    if(activeLesson) {
        supabase.from('lesson_progress').upsert(
            { user_id: user.id, lesson_id: activeLesson.id, course_id: course.id, last_accessed: new Date() }, 
            { onConflict: 'user_id, lesson_id' }
        )
        setOpenModules(prev => ({ ...prev, [activeLesson.module || 'General']: true }))
    }
  }, [activeLessonIndex, activeLesson])

  const isLessonCompleted = completedLessonIds.includes(activeLesson?.id)
  const progressPercent = Math.round((completedLessonIds.length / lessons.length) * 100)

  const previousLesson = activeLessonIndex > 0 ? lessons[activeLessonIndex - 1] : null
  const isPrereqLocked = previousLesson && !completedLessonIds.includes(previousLesson.id)
  const isDateLocked = activeLesson?.release_date && new Date(activeLesson.release_date) > new Date()
  const isLocked = isPrereqLocked || isDateLocked

  const hasQuiz = activeLesson?.quiz_data && activeLesson.quiz_data.length > 0
  const isQuizPassed = passedQuizLessonIds.includes(activeLesson?.id)
  const canMarkComplete = !hasQuiz || isQuizPassed

  const toggleCompletion = async () => {
    if (!activeLesson) return
    const lessonId = activeLesson.id
    
    if (!canMarkComplete && !isLessonCompleted) {
      alert("You must pass the quiz before completing this lesson.")
      const isInteractive = activeLesson.quiz_data.some(q => q.timestamp)
      if(!isInteractive) setViewMode('quiz')
      return
    }

    const isComplete = completedLessonIds.includes(lessonId)
    if (isComplete) {
      await supabase.from('lesson_progress').delete().eq('user_id', user.id).eq('lesson_id', lessonId)
      setCompletedLessonIds(prev => prev.filter(id => id !== lessonId))
    } else {
      await supabase.from('lesson_progress').insert([{ user_id: user.id, lesson_id: lessonId, course_id: course.id }])
      setCompletedLessonIds(prev => [...prev, lessonId])
      addXP(50)
      if (completedLessonIds.length + 1 === lessons.length) confetti({ particleCount: 150, spread: 80, origin: { y: 0.6 } })
    }
  }

  const handleQuizPass = (score) => {
    if(!passedQuizLessonIds.includes(activeLesson.id)) {
      setPassedQuizLessonIds(prev => [...prev, activeLesson.id])
    }
  }

  const downloadCertificate = async () => {
    if (!certificateRef.current) return
    setGeneratingPdf(true)
    try {
      const canvas = await html2canvas(certificateRef.current, { scale: 2 })
      const imgData = canvas.toDataURL('image/png')
      const pdf = new jsPDF('landscape', 'mm', 'a4')
      pdf.addImage(imgData, 'PNG', 0, 0, 297, 210)
      pdf.save(`Certificate_${course.title.replace(/\s+/g, '_')}.pdf`)
    } catch (err) { alert("Error generating certificate") } 
    finally { setGeneratingPdf(false) }
  }

  const toggleModule = (modName) => setOpenModules(prev => ({ ...prev, [modName]: !prev[modName] }))

  const interactiveQuizData = activeLesson?.quiz_data?.filter(q => q.timestamp) || []
  const examQuizData = activeLesson?.quiz_data?.filter(q => !q.timestamp) || []

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col lg:flex-row gap-6 min-h-screen relative">
      <Certificate ref={certificateRef} studentName={user.email} courseTitle={course.title} date={new Date().toLocaleDateString()} />
      <ReportModal isOpen={showReport} onClose={() => setShowReport(false)} contextData={`Course: ${course.title}, Lesson: ${activeLesson?.title}`} />
      
      {showFlashcards && <FlashcardViewer terms={vocabTerms} onClose={() => setShowFlashcards(false)} />}

      <div className="lg:hidden mb-4">
        <button onClick={() => setSidebarOpen(!sidebarOpen)} className="w-full bg-brand-dark text-white p-4 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-between font-bold shadow-lg">
          <span>{activeLesson?.title || "Select Lesson"}</span>
          {sidebarOpen ? <X/> : <Menu/>}
        </button>
      </div>

      <div className={`flex-1 flex flex-col ${sidebarOpen ? 'hidden lg:flex' : 'flex'}`}>
        {isLocked ? (
          <div className="flex-1 bg-white rounded-3xl p-12 shadow-sm border border-gray-100 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center min-h-[400px]">
            <div className="w-20 h-20 bg-gray-100 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mb-6">
              {isDateLocked ? <Clock size={32} className="text-blue-400" /> : <Lock size={32} className="text-gray-400" />}
            </div>
            <h2 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-800 mb-2">{isDateLocked ? "Coming Soon" : "Lesson Locked"}</h2>
            <p className="text-gray-500 mb-6">{isDateLocked ? `Unlocks on ${new Date(activeLesson.release_date).toLocaleDateString()}` : "Complete the previous lesson to unlock."}</p>
            {!isDateLocked && <button onClick={() => setActiveLessonIndex(activeLessonIndex - 1)} className="btn btn-plum-gradient px-6 py-2 rounded-xl text-sm">Go to Previous Lesson</button>}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <>
            <div className="flex gap-2 mb-4 bg-white p-1 rounded-xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 w-fit flex-wrap">
              <button onClick={() => setViewMode('content')} className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm transition ${viewMode === 'content' ? 'bg-brand-purple text-white shadow-sm' : 'text-gray-500 hover:bg-gray-50'}`}>Content</button>
              {examQuizData.length > 0 && <button onClick={() => setViewMode('quiz')} className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm transition flex items-center gap-2 ${viewMode === 'quiz' ? 'bg-brand-purple text-white shadow-sm' : 'text-gray-500 hover:bg-gray-50'}`}><HelpCircle size={14}/> Quiz</button>}
              {gamePairs.length >= 2 && <button onClick={() => setViewMode('game')} className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm transition flex items-center gap-2 ${viewMode === 'game' ? 'bg-brand-purple text-white shadow-sm' : 'text-gray-500 hover:bg-gray-50'}`}><Gamepad2 size={14}/> Game</button>}
            </div>

            {viewMode === 'quiz' && <QuizTaker quizData={examQuizData} onComplete={handleQuizPass} lessonId={activeLesson.id} courseId={course.id} />}
            {viewMode === 'game' && <MatchingGame pairs={gamePairs} onComplete={() => addXP(20)} />}

            {viewMode === 'content' && (
              <>
                <div className="mb-6">
                  {activeLesson?.type === 'audio' ? 
                    <CustomAudioPlayer src={activeLesson.video_url} title={activeLesson.title} /> : 
                    <InteractiveVideoPlayer 
                      src={activeLesson.video_url} 
                      title={activeLesson.title} 
                      quizData={interactiveQuizData}
                      onQuizPass={handleQuizPass}
                    />
                  }
                </div>
                
                <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 shadow-sm mb-8">
                  <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-4">
                    <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark">{activeLesson?.title}</h1>
                    <button 
                      onClick={toggleCompletion} 
                      disabled={!canMarkComplete && !isLessonCompleted}
                      className={`flex-shrink-0 flex items-center gap-2 px-4 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold transition ${isLessonCompleted ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-700' : (!canMarkComplete ? 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-400 cursor-not-allowed' : 'bg-gray-100 text-gray-500 hover:bg-gray-200')}`}
                      title={!canMarkComplete ? "Pass Quiz First" : ""}
                    >
                       {isLessonCompleted ? <CheckCircle size={18}/> : (canMarkComplete ? <Circle size={18}/> : <Lock size={18}/>)} 
                       {isLessonCompleted ? "Completed" : (canMarkComplete ? "Mark Complete" : "Pass Quiz First")}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                  <VocabularyDrill description={activeLesson?.description} />
                  <HomeworkUploader lessonId={activeLesson?.id} />
                  
                  <div className="flex gap-3 my-6">
                     {vocabTerms.length > 0 && <button onClick={() => setShowFlashcards(true)} className="flex gap-2 items-center text-sm font-bold text-blue-600 bg-blue-50 px-3 py-1 rounded hover:bg-blue-100"><Layers size={14}/> Flashcards</button>}
                     {activeLesson?.pdf_url && <a href={activeLesson.pdf_url} target="_blank" className="flex gap-2 items-center text-sm font-bold text-purple-600 bg-purple-50 px-3 py-1 rounded hover:bg-purple-100"><FileDown size={14}/> PDF</a>}
                     {activeLesson?.quizlet_url && <a href={activeLesson.quizlet_url} target="_blank" className="flex gap-2 items-center text-sm font-bold text-blue-600 bg-blue-50 px-3 py-1 rounded hover:bg-blue-100"><ExternalLink size={14}/> Quizlet</a>}
                  </div>
                  <div className="prose prose-sm text-gray-600 whitespace-pre-wrap">{cleanDescription(activeLesson?.description)}</div>
                  
                  <div className="flex justify-between items-center pt-6 mt-6 border-t border-gray-100">
                     <button disabled={activeLessonIndex === 0} onClick={() => setActiveLessonIndex(i => i - 1)} className="text-gray-500 font-bold flex items-center gap-1 disabled:opacity-30 hover:text-brand-dark transition"><ChevronLeft/> Previous</button>
                     <button onClick={() => setShowReport(true)} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-red-500 text-xs flex items-center gap-1 transition"><AlertTriangle size={12}/> Report</button>
                     <button disabled={activeLessonIndex === lessons.length - 1} onClick={() => setActiveLessonIndex(i => i + 1)} className="text-brand-purple font-bold flex items-center gap-1 disabled:opacity-30 hover:underline transition">Next <ChevronRight/></button>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <LessonDiscussion lessonId={activeLesson?.id} />
                <ReviewSection productId={course.id} />
              </>
            )}
          </>
        )}
      </div>

      <div className={`w-full lg:w-80 flex flex-col gap-6 h-fit lg:sticky lg:top-24 ${sidebarOpen ? 'block' : 'hidden lg:flex'}`}>
         <div className="h-64 flex-shrink-0"><LessonNotes lessonId={activeLesson?.id} userId={user.id} /></div>
         <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-200 overflow-hidden flex flex-col shadow-sm max-h-[calc(100vh-200px)]">
            <div className="p-5 border-b border-gray-100 bg-gray-50/50">
              <h3 className="font-bold text-brand-dark text-lg mb-2">Curriculum</h3>
              <div className="w-full bg-gray-200 rounded-full h-2 mb-2"><div className="bg-green-500 h-2 rounded-full transition-all" style={{ width: `${progressPercent}%` }}></div></div>
              <div className="flex justify-between text-xs text-gray-500"><span>{progressPercent}% Done</span>{progressPercent === 100 && <button onClick={downloadCertificate} disabled={generatingPdf} className="flex items-center gap-1 text-brand-purple font-bold hover:underline">{generatingPdf ? '...' : <><Download size={10}/> Certificate</>}</button>}</div>
            </div>
            <div className="overflow-y-auto flex-1">
              {Object.entries(groupedLessons).map(([moduleName, modLessons]) => (
                <div key={moduleName} className="border-b border-gray-50 last:border-0">
                  <button onClick={() => toggleModule(moduleName)} className="w-full px-4 py-3 flex justify-between items-center bg-gray-50 hover:bg-gray-100 transition">
                    <span className="font-bold text-gray-700 text-sm">{moduleName}</span>
                    {openModules[moduleName] ? <ChevronUp size={16} className="text-gray-400"/> : <ChevronDown size={16} className="text-gray-400"/>}
                  </button>
                  {openModules[moduleName] && (
                    <div className="bg-white">
                      {modLessons.map((l) => {
                        const idx = l.globalIndex
                        const prev = idx > 0 ? lessons[idx-1] : null
                        const lockedPrereq = prev && !completedLessonIds.includes(prev.id)
                        const lockedDate = l.release_date && new Date(l.release_date) > new Date()
                        const locked = lockedPrereq || lockedDate

                        return (
                          <button key={l.id} disabled={locked} onClick={() => setActiveLessonIndex(idx)} className={`w-full text-left p-3 pl-6 border-b border-gray-50 hover:bg-gray-50 flex items-center gap-3 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:bg-white ${activeLessonIndex === idx ? 'bg-purple-50 border-l-4 border-l-brand-purple pl-5' : ''}`}>
                            <div className="text-gray-400">
                              {locked ? <Lock size={14}/> : (completedLessonIds.includes(l.id) ? <CheckCircle size={14} className="text-green-500"/> : (l.type === 'audio' ? <Music size={14}/> : <PlayCircle size={14}/>))}
                            </div>
                            <div className="text-xs font-medium text-gray-700 truncate flex-1">{l.title}</div>
                          </button>
                        )
                      })}
                    </div>
                  )}
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
         </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/course/CustomAudioPlayer.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react'
import { Play, Pause, RotateCcw, MoreHorizontal } from 'lucide-react'

export default function CustomAudioPlayer({ src, title }) {
  const audioRef = useRef(null)
  const [isPlaying, setIsPlaying] = useState(false)
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState(0)
  const [duration, setDuration] = useState(0)
  const [speed, setSpeed] = useState(1)

  useEffect(() => {
    const audio = audioRef.current
    if(!audio) return

    const updateProgress = () => setProgress((audio.currentTime / audio.duration) * 100)
    const setAudioDuration = () => setDuration(audio.duration)
    const onEnd = () => setIsPlaying(false)

    audio.addEventListener('timeupdate', updateProgress)
    audio.addEventListener('loadedmetadata', setAudioDuration)
    audio.addEventListener('ended', onEnd)

    return () => {
      audio.removeEventListener('timeupdate', updateProgress)
      audio.removeEventListener('loadedmetadata', setAudioDuration)
      audio.removeEventListener('ended', onEnd)
    }
  }, [src])

  const togglePlay = () => {
    if (isPlaying) audioRef.current.pause()
    else audioRef.current.play()
    setIsPlaying(!isPlaying)
  }

  const changeSpeed = () => {
    const speeds = [0.8, 1, 1.25, 1.5]
    const nextIdx = (speeds.indexOf(speed) + 1) % speeds.length
    const nextSpeed = speeds[nextIdx]
    audioRef.current.playbackRate = nextSpeed
    setSpeed(nextSpeed)
  }

  const handleSeek = (e) => {
    const width = e.target.clientWidth
    const clickX = e.nativeEvent.offsetX
    const duration = audioRef.current.duration
    audioRef.current.currentTime = (clickX / width) * duration
  }

  const formatTime = (time) => {
    if(isNaN(time)) return "0:00"
    const min = Math.floor(time / 60)
    const sec = Math.floor(time % 60)
    return `${min}:${sec < 10 ? '0'+sec : sec}`
  }

  return (
    <div className="bg-brand-dark text-white rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-lg">
      <audio ref={audioRef} src={src} />
      
      <div className="flex items-center gap-4 mb-4">
        <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-brand-purple rounded-full flex items-center justify-center flex-shrink-0 animate-pulse-slow">
          <div className="w-4 h-4 bg-white rounded-full"></div>
        </div>
        <div>
          <h3 className="font-bold text-lg leading-tight">{title}</h3>
          <p className="text-xs text-gray-400">Audio Lesson</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mb-4 cursor-pointer group py-2" onClick={handleSeek}>
        <div className="w-full bg-gray-700 rounded-full h-2 relative">
          <div 
            className="bg-gradient-to-r from-brand-blue to-brand-purple h-2 rounded-full relative" 
            style={{ width: `${progress}%` }}
          >
            <div className="w-4 h-4 bg-white rounded-full absolute right-0 top-1/2 transform -translate-y-1/2 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition shadow-md"></div>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="flex justify-between text-xs text-gray-400 mt-1 font-mono">
          <span>{formatTime(audioRef.current?.currentTime)}</span>
          <span>{formatTime(duration)}</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <button 
          onClick={changeSpeed}
          className="text-xs font-bold bg-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-600 px-3 py-1 rounded-lg transition w-16"
        >
          {speed}x
        </button>

        <div className="flex items-center gap-6">
          <button onClick={() => { audioRef.current.currentTime -= 10 }} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-white transition">
            <RotateCcw size={20} />
          </button>
          <button 
            onClick={togglePlay}
            className="w-14 h-14 bg-white text-brand-dark rounded-full flex items-center justify-center hover:scale-105 transition shadow-lg shadow-white/10"
          >
            {isPlaying ? <Pause size={24} fill="currentColor" /> : <Play size={24} fill="currentColor" className="ml-1" />}
          </button>
          <button onClick={() => { audioRef.current.currentTime += 10 }} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-white transition transform scale-x-[-1]">
            <RotateCcw size={20} />
          </button>
        </div>

        <div className="w-16"></div> {/* Spacer for centering */}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/course/FlashcardViewer.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState } from 'react'
import { RotateCw, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight, X } from 'lucide-react'

export default function FlashcardViewer({ terms = [], onClose }) {
  const [index, setIndex] = useState(0)
  const [flipped, setFlipped] = useState(false)

  if (!terms || terms.length === 0) return null

  const handleNext = () => {
    setFlipped(false)
    setTimeout(() => setIndex((prev) => (prev + 1) % terms.length), 150)
  }

  const handlePrev = () => {
    setFlipped(false)
    setTimeout(() => setIndex((prev) => (prev - 1 + terms.length) % terms.length), 150)
  }

  const currentCard = terms[index]

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/80 z-[70] flex items-center justify-center p-4 animate-fade-in">
      <div className="relative w-full max-w-lg">
        <button onClick={onClose} className="absolute -top-12 right-0 text-white hover:text-gray-300">
          <X size={32} />
        </button>
        
        <div className="perspective-1000 w-full h-80 cursor-pointer" onClick={() => setFlipped(!flipped)}>
          <div className={`relative w-full h-full text-center transition-transform duration-500 transform-style-3d ${flipped ? 'rotate-y-180' : ''}`}>
            
            {/* Front */}
            <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-white rounded-3xl flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-8 backface-hidden shadow-2xl">
              <span className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-widest mb-4">Term</span>
              <h3 className="text-4xl font-black text-brand-dark">{currentCard.term}</h3>
              <p className="text-sm text-gray-400 mt-8 animate-pulse">Tap to flip</p>
            </div>

            {/* Back */}
            <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-brand-purple rounded-3xl flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-8 backface-hidden rotate-y-180 text-white shadow-2xl">
              <span className="text-xs font-bold text-white/60 uppercase tracking-widest mb-4">Definition</span>
              <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold">{currentCard.definition}</h3>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mt-8 text-white">
          <button onClick={handlePrev} className="p-2 hover:bg-white/10 rounded-full transition"><ChevronLeft size={32}/></button>
          <span className="font-bold font-mono">{index + 1} / {terms.length}</span>
          <button onClick={handleNext} className="p-2 hover:bg-white/10 rounded-full transition"><ChevronRight size={32}/></button>
        </div>
      </div>
      
      <style jsx>{`
        .perspective-1000 { perspective: 1000px; }
        .transform-style-3d { transform-style: preserve-3d; }
        .backface-hidden { backface-visibility: hidden; }
        .rotate-y-180 { transform: rotateY(180deg); }
      `}</style>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/course/HomeworkUploader.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { Upload, FileText, CheckCircle, Loader2, Clock } from 'lucide-react'
import { generateFilePath } from '@/lib/helpers'

export default function HomeworkUploader({ lessonId }) {
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const [submission, setSubmission] = useState(null)
  const [uploading, setUploading] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (lessonId) fetchSubmission()
  }, [lessonId])

  const fetchSubmission = async () => {
    const { data } = await supabase.from('homework_submissions').select('*').eq('lesson_id', lessonId).eq('user_id', user.id).single()
    setSubmission(data)
  }

  const handleUpload = async (e) => {
    const file = e.target.files[0]
    if (!file) return
    setUploading(true)
    try {
      const path = generateFilePath('homework', file)
      await supabase.storage.from('homework').upload(path, file)
      const { data } = supabase.storage.from('homework').getPublicUrl(path) // Note: Buckets are private, ideally use createSignedUrl for stricter security
      
      const payload = { lesson_id: lessonId, user_id: user.id, file_url: path, status: 'pending' }
      
      if (submission) {
        await supabase.from('homework_submissions').update(payload).eq('id', submission.id)
      } else {
        await supabase.from('homework_submissions').insert([payload])
      }
      fetchSubmission()
    } catch (error) {
      alert("Upload failed")
    } finally {
      setUploading(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="bg-blue-50 rounded-2xl border border-blue-100 p-6 mt-6">
      <h3 className="font-bold text-blue-900 mb-4 flex items-center gap-2"><FileText size={20}/> Homework Assignment</h3>
      
      {submission ? (
        <div className="bg-white rounded-xl p-4 shadow-sm">
          <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-2">
            <p className="text-sm font-bold text-gray-700">Your Submission</p>
            {submission.status === 'pending' ? (
              <span className="text-xs bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-700 px-2 py-1 rounded font-bold flex items-center gap-1"><Clock size={12}/> Pending Review</span>
            ) : (
              <span className="text-xs bg-green-100 text-green-700 px-2 py-1 rounded font-bold flex items-center gap-1"><CheckCircle size={12}/> Graded: {submission.grade}</span>
            )}
          </div>
          <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 truncate mb-4">{submission.file_url}</p>
          
          {submission.feedback && (
            <div className="bg-green-50 p-3 rounded-lg border border-green-100 text-sm text-green-800">
              <strong>Teacher Feedback:</strong> {submission.feedback}
            </div>
          )}
          
          <div className="mt-4 text-right">
             <label className="text-xs font-bold text-blue-600 cursor-pointer hover:underline">
               Upload New Version
               <input type="file" className="hidden" onChange={handleUpload} disabled={uploading} />
             </label>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="text-center border-2 border-dashed border-blue-200 rounded-xl p-6 bg-white/50">
           <p className="text-sm text-blue-800 mb-4">Upload your assignment for feedback.</p>
           <label className="btn btn-plum-gradient px-6 py-2 rounded-xl text-sm font-bold cursor-pointer inline-flex items-center gap-2">
             {uploading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin"/> : <Upload size={16}/>} Upload File
             <input type="file" className="hidden" onChange={handleUpload} disabled={uploading} />
           </label>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/course/InteractiveVideoPlayer.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react'
import { Play, Pause, RotateCcw, Volume2, VolumeX, Maximize } from 'lucide-react'
import QuizTaker from './QuizTaker'

export default function InteractiveVideoPlayer({ src, title, quizData = [], onQuizPass }) {
  const videoRef = useRef(null)
  const [isPlaying, setIsPlaying] = useState(false)
  const [progress, setProgress] = useState(0)
  const [isMuted, setIsMuted] = useState(false)
  const [activeQuiz, setActiveQuiz] = useState(null)
  const [triggeredQuizzes, setTriggeredQuizzes] = useState([]) 

  useEffect(() => {
    const video = videoRef.current
    if (!video) return

    const handleTimeUpdate = () => {
      const current = video.currentTime
      setProgress((current / video.duration) * 100)

      if (quizData && quizData.length > 0) {
        quizData.forEach((quiz, index) => {
          if (
            quiz.timestamp && 
            !triggeredQuizzes.includes(index) && 
            current >= quiz.timestamp && 
            current < quiz.timestamp + 1
          ) {
            video.pause()
            setIsPlaying(false)
            setActiveQuiz({ ...quiz, index })
            setTriggeredQuizzes(prev => [...prev, index])
          }
        })
      }
    }

    video.addEventListener('timeupdate', handleTimeUpdate)
    return () => video.removeEventListener('timeupdate', handleTimeUpdate)
  }, [quizData, triggeredQuizzes])

  const togglePlay = () => {
    if (videoRef.current.paused) {
      videoRef.current.play()
      setIsPlaying(true)
    } else {
      videoRef.current.pause()
      setIsPlaying(false)
    }
  }

  const handleSeek = (e) => {
    const width = e.target.clientWidth
    const clickX = e.nativeEvent.offsetX
    const duration = videoRef.current.duration
    videoRef.current.currentTime = (clickX / width) * duration
  }

  const handleQuizComplete = (score) => {
    setActiveQuiz(null)
    videoRef.current.play()
    setIsPlaying(true)
    if(onQuizPass) onQuizPass(score)
  }

  // Simple check for native support
  const isNative = src && (src.includes('supabase') || src.endsWith('.mp4') || src.endsWith('.webm'))

  if (!isNative) {
    return (
      <div className="relative aspect-video bg-black rounded-2xl overflow-hidden">
        <iframe src={src} className="w-full h-full" allowFullScreen title={title} />
        {quizData.some(q => q.timestamp) && (
          <div className="absolute top-4 right-4 bg-black/70 text-white text-xs px-3 py-1 rounded-full backdrop-blur-md">
            Interactive quizzes available on native video only
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="relative group bg-black rounded-2xl overflow-hidden shadow-2xl">
      <video 
        ref={videoRef} 
        src={src} 
        className="w-full h-full"
        onClick={togglePlay}
        onEnded={() => setIsPlaying(false)}
      />

      {/* Custom Controls Overlay */}
      <div className="absolute bottom-0 left-0 right-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-black/80 to-transparent p-4 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition-opacity duration-300">
        
        {/* Progress Bar */}
        <div className="w-full h-2 bg-gray-600/50 rounded-full mb-4 cursor-pointer hover:h-3 transition-all" onClick={handleSeek}>
          <div className="h-full bg-brand-purple rounded-full relative" style={{ width: `${progress}%` }}>
            <div className="absolute right-0 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 w-4 h-4 bg-white rounded-full shadow-md scale-0 group-hover:scale-100 transition-transform"></div>
          </div>
          {/* Quiz Markers */}
          {quizData.map((q, i) => q.timestamp && (
            <div 
              key={i} 
              className="absolute top-0 w-1 h-full bg-yellow-400 z-10 pointer-events-none" 
              style={{ left: `${(q.timestamp / videoRef.current?.duration) * 100}%` }}
              title="Quiz Checkpoint"
            ></div>
          ))}
        </div>

        <div className="flex items-center justify-between text-white">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
            <button onClick={togglePlay} className="hover:text-brand-purple transition">
              {isPlaying ? <Pause size={24} fill="currentColor"/> : <Play size={24} fill="currentColor"/>}
            </button>
            <button onClick={() => { videoRef.current.currentTime -= 10 }} className="hover:text-brand-purple transition"><RotateCcw size={20}/></button>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 group/vol">
              <button onClick={() => { videoRef.current.muted = !isMuted; setIsMuted(!isMuted) }}>
                {isMuted ? <VolumeX size={20}/> : <Volume2 size={20}/>}
              </button>
            </div>
            <span className="text-xs font-mono">{videoRef.current ? new Date(videoRef.current.currentTime * 1000).toISOString().substr(14, 5) : "00:00"} / {videoRef.current ? new Date(videoRef.current.duration * 1000).toISOString().substr(14, 5) : "00:00"}</span>
          </div>
          <button onClick={() => videoRef.current.requestFullscreen()}><Maximize size={20}/></button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* QUIZ OVERLAY */}
      {activeQuiz && (
        <div className="absolute inset-0 bg-black/90 backdrop-blur-sm z-50 flex items-center justify-center p-4 md:p-10 animate-fade-in">
          <div className="w-full max-w-2xl">
            <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl overflow-hidden shadow-2xl border-4 border-brand-purple">
              <div className="bg-brand-purple text-white p-4 text-center font-bold">
                Video Paused • Checkpoint
              </div>
              <div className="p-6">
                <QuizTaker 
                  quizData={[activeQuiz]} 
                  onComplete={handleQuizComplete} 
                />
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

PATH: ./src/components/course/LessonDiscussion.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { Loader2, Send, MessageSquare } from 'lucide-react'

export default function LessonDiscussion({ lessonId }) {
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const [comments, setComments] = useState([])
  const [newComment, setNewComment] = useState('')
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (lessonId) fetchComments()
  }, [lessonId])

  const fetchComments = async () => {
    const { data } = await supabase.from('lesson_discussions')
      .select('*, profiles(full_name, avatar_url)')
      .eq('lesson_id', lessonId)
      .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    setComments(data || [])
  }

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (!newComment.trim()) return
    setSubmitting(true)
    await supabase.from('lesson_discussions').insert([{ lesson_id: lessonId, user_id: user.id, content: newComment }])
    setNewComment('')
    setSubmitting(false)
    fetchComments()
  }

  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-100 p-6 mt-8">
      <h3 className="font-bold text-lg text-brand-dark mb-6 flex items-center gap-2"><MessageSquare size={20}/> Class Discussion</h3>
      
      <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="flex gap-4 mb-8">
        <input 
          className="flex-1 bg-gray-50 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl px-4 py-3 outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-purple"
          placeholder="Ask a question..."
          value={newComment}
          onChange={e => setNewComment(e.target.value)}
        />
        <button disabled={submitting} className="bg-brand-purple text-white p-3 rounded-xl hover:bg-brand-dark transition disabled:opacity-50">
          {submitting ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin"/> : <Send size={20}/>}
        </button>
      </form>

      <div className="space-y-6">
        {comments.length === 0 && <p className="text-gray-400 text-center text-sm">No questions yet. Be the first!</p>}
        {comments.map(c => (
          <div key={c.id} className="flex gap-4">
            <img src={c.profiles?.avatar_url || 'https://placehold.co/50'} className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full object-cover bg-gray-200"/>
            <div>
              <div className="flex items-baseline gap-2">
                <p className="font-bold text-sm text-gray-800">{c.profiles?.full_name || 'Student'}</p>
                <span className="text-xs text-gray-400">{new Date(c.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-gray-600 text-sm mt-1">{c.content}</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

PATH: ./src/components/course/LessonNotes.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { Save, StickyNote, Loader2, Cloud, CloudOff } from 'lucide-react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useLanguage } from '@/context/LanguageContext'

export default function LessonNotes({ lessonId, userId }) {
  const { t } = useLanguage()
  const [note, setNote] = useState('')
  const [status, setStatus] = useState('idle')

  useEffect(() => { fetchNote() }, [lessonId])

  const fetchNote = async () => {
    try {
      const { data } = await supabase.from('user_notes').select('content').eq('user_id', userId).eq('lesson_id', lessonId).single()
      if (data) setNote(data.content)
      else setNote('')
    } catch (error) {}
  }

  const saveNote = async (content) => {
    setStatus('saving')
    try {
      await supabase.from('user_notes').upsert({ user_id: userId, lesson_id: lessonId, content, updated_at: new Date() }, { onConflict: 'user_id, lesson_id' })
      setStatus('saved')
      setTimeout(() => setStatus('idle'), 2000)
    } catch (error) { setStatus('error') }
  }

  const handleManualSave = () => saveNote(note)

  return (
    <div className="bg-yellow-50 border border-yellow-100 rounded-2xl p-4 h-full flex flex-col relative">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-2">
        <h4 className="text-yellow-800 font-bold flex items-center gap-2"><StickyNote size={18} /> {t('notes_title')}</h4>
        <div className="text-xs font-medium">
          {status === 'saving' && <span className="text-yellow-600 flex items-center gap-1"><Loader2 size={12} className="animate-spin"/> {t('notes_saving')}</span>}
          {status === 'saved' && <span className="text-green-600 flex items-center gap-1"><Cloud size={12}/> {t('notes_saved')}</span>}
        </div>
      </div>
      <textarea 
        className="flex-1 w-full bg-transparent border-none outline-none resize-none text-gray-700 text-sm leading-relaxed font-medium placeholder-yellow-800/30 focus:ring-0"
        placeholder={t('notes_placeholder')}
        value={note}
        onChange={(e) => setNote(e.target.value)}
        onBlur={handleManualSave}
      ></textarea>
      <button onClick={handleManualSave} className="mt-2 w-full bg-yellow-200 text-yellow-900 text-sm font-bold py-2 rounded-xl hover:bg-yellow-300 transition flex justify-center items-center gap-2">
        <Save size={16} /> {t('notes_save')}
      </button>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/course/MatchingGame.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { RefreshCw, Check, X } from 'lucide-react'
import confetti from 'canvas-confetti'

export default function MatchingGame({ pairs = [], onComplete }) {
  // pairs: [{ id: 1, text: 'Hello', match: 'Annyeong' }, ...]
  const [leftItems, setLeftItems] = useState([])
  const [rightItems, setRightItems] = useState([])
  const [selectedLeft, setSelectedLeft] = useState(null)
  const [selectedRight, setSelectedRight] = useState(null)
  const [matches, setMatches] = useState([]) // Array of IDs
  const [shake, setShake] = useState(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (pairs.length > 0) {
      setLeftItems(pairs.map(p => ({ id: p.id, text: p.text })).sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5))
      setRightItems(pairs.map(p => ({ id: p.id, text: p.match })).sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5))
    }
  }, [pairs])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedLeft && selectedRight) {
      if (selectedLeft === selectedRight) {
        // Match!
        setMatches(prev => [...prev, selectedLeft])
        setSelectedLeft(null)
        setSelectedRight(null)
      } else {
        // No match
        setShake({ left: selectedLeft, right: selectedRight })
        setTimeout(() => {
          setSelectedLeft(null)
          setSelectedRight(null)
          setShake(null)
        }, 500)
      }
    }
  }, [selectedLeft, selectedRight])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (pairs.length > 0 && matches.length === pairs.length) {
      confetti({ particleCount: 100, spread: 70, origin: { y: 0.6 } })
      if(onComplete) onComplete()
    }
  }, [matches])

  if (pairs.length === 0) return null

  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-6 border border-gray-200">
      <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-brand-dark mb-6 text-center">Match the pairs!</h3>
      <div className="flex gap-8 justify-between">
        <div className="flex-1 space-y-3">
          {leftItems.map(item => {
            const isMatched = matches.includes(item.id)
            const isSelected = selectedLeft === item.id
            const isShaking = shake?.left === item.id
            return (
              <button 
                key={item.id}
                disabled={isMatched}
                onClick={() => setSelectedLeft(item.id)}
                className={`w-full py-4 px-6 rounded-xl font-bold transition transform ${isMatched ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-700 opacity-50 scale-95' : isSelected ? 'bg-brand-purple text-white scale-105 shadow-md' : 'bg-gray-50 text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-100'} ${isShaking ? 'animate-shake bg-red-100 text-red-600' : ''}`}
              >
                {item.text}
              </button>
            )
          })}
        </div>
        <div className="flex-1 space-y-3">
          {rightItems.map(item => {
            const isMatched = matches.includes(item.id)
            const isSelected = selectedRight === item.id
            const isShaking = shake?.right === item.id
            return (
              <button 
                key={item.id}
                disabled={isMatched}
                onClick={() => setSelectedRight(item.id)}
                className={`w-full py-4 px-6 rounded-xl font-bold transition transform ${isMatched ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-700 opacity-50 scale-95' : isSelected ? 'bg-brand-purple text-white scale-105 shadow-md' : 'bg-gray-50 text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-100'} ${isShaking ? 'animate-shake bg-red-100 text-red-600' : ''}`}
              >
                {item.text}
              </button>
            )
          })}
        </div>
      </div>
      <style jsx>{`
        @keyframes shake {
          0%, 100% { transform: translateX(0); }
          25% { transform: translateX(-5px); }
          75% { transform: translateX(5px); }
        }
        .animate-shake { animation: shake 0.3s ease-in-out; }
      `}</style>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/course/QuizTaker.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { CheckCircle, XCircle, RefreshCw, Trophy, Mic, Type, ArrowRight, Save, Loader2, AlertTriangle, Square } from 'lucide-react'
import confetti from 'canvas-confetti'
import { useLanguage } from '@/context/LanguageContext'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { useAudioRecorder } from '@/hooks/useAudioRecorder'
import AudioVisualizer from './AudioVisualizer'

export default function QuizTaker({ quizData, onComplete, lessonId, courseId }) {
  const { t } = useLanguage()
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const [currentQuestion, setCurrentQuestion] = useState(0)
  const [score, setScore] = useState(0)
  const [showScore, setShowScore] = useState(false)
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false)
  const [attempts, setAttempts] = useState(0)
  const [maxAttempts] = useState(3)
  
  const [selectedOption, setSelectedOption] = useState(null)
  const [textAnswer, setTextAnswer] = useState('')
  const [isAnswered, setIsAnswered] = useState(false)
  const [feedback, setFeedback] = useState(null)
  
  // Audio Hooks
  const { isRecording, audioBlob, startRecording, stopRecording } = useAudioRecorder()
  const [processingAudio, setProcessingAudio] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (user && lessonId) checkAttempts()
  }, [user, lessonId])

  const checkAttempts = async () => {
    const { count } = await supabase.from('quiz_results')
      .select('*', { count: 'exact', head: true })
      .eq('user_id', user.id)
      .eq('lesson_id', lessonId)
    setAttempts(count || 0)
  }

  if (!quizData || quizData.length === 0) return null

  if (attempts >= maxAttempts && !showScore) {
    return (
      <div className="bg-red-50 p-8 rounded-2xl text-center border border-red-100">
        <AlertTriangle className="mx-auto text-red-500 mb-4" size={40}/>
        <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-red-800">Max Attempts Reached</h3>
        <p className="text-red-600 mt-2">You have taken this quiz {attempts} times. Please contact your tutor or wait 24 hours to retake.</p>
      </div>
    )
  }

  const question = quizData[currentQuestion]
  const questionType = question.type || 'multiple-choice' 

  const handleMultipleChoice = (optionIndex) => {
    setSelectedOption(optionIndex)
    setIsAnswered(true)
    if (optionIndex === question.correct) {
      setScore(score + 1)
      setFeedback('correct')
      confetti({ particleCount: 30, spread: 50, origin: { y: 0.7 } })
    } else {
      setFeedback('incorrect')
    }
  }

  const handleTextSubmit = (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setIsAnswered(true)
    const isCorrect = textAnswer.trim().toLowerCase() === question.correctAnswer?.toLowerCase()
    if (isCorrect) {
      setScore(score + 1)
      setFeedback('correct')
      confetti({ particleCount: 30, spread: 50, origin: { y: 0.7 } })
    } else {
      setFeedback('incorrect')
    }
  }

  const toggleRecording = async () => {
    if (isRecording) {
      stopRecording()
      // Simulate Processing AI Analysis
      setProcessingAudio(true)
      setTimeout(() => {
        setProcessingAudio(false)
        setIsAnswered(true)
        // In a real app, send audioBlob to backend -> Whisper API -> Text -> Grade
        // Here we simulate a "Good" result
        setScore(score + 1)
        setFeedback('correct')
        confetti({ particleCount: 30, spread: 50, origin: { y: 0.7 } })
      }, 1500)
    } else {
      await startRecording()
    }
  }

  const handleNext = () => {
    if (currentQuestion < quizData.length - 1) {
      setCurrentQuestion(currentQuestion + 1)
      setSelectedOption(null)
      setTextAnswer('')
      setIsAnswered(false)
      setFeedback(null)
    } else {
      finishQuiz()
    }
  }

  const finishQuiz = async () => {
    setShowScore(true)
    const passed = (score / quizData.length) >= 0.7
    
    if (user) {
      setSaving(true)
      try {
        await supabase.from('quiz_results').insert([{
          user_id: user.id,
          course_id: courseId,
          lesson_id: lessonId,
          score: score,
          total_questions: quizData.length,
          passed: passed
        }])
        await checkAttempts()
      } catch (err) {
        console.error("Failed to save quiz result", err)
      } finally {
        setSaving(false)
      }
    }

    if (onComplete && passed) onComplete(score)
  }

  const restart = () => {
    if (attempts >= maxAttempts) return
    setCurrentQuestion(0)
    setScore(0)
    setShowScore(false)
    setSelectedOption(null)
    setTextAnswer('')
    setIsAnswered(false)
    setFeedback(null)
  }

  if (showScore) {
    const passed = score / quizData.length >= 0.7
    return (
      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-8 text-center border-2 border-gray-100 animate-fade-in">
        <div className="w-20 h-20 mx-auto bg-gray-50 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mb-4">
          <Trophy size={40} className={passed ? "text-yellow-500" : "text-gray-400"} />
        </div>
        <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">{t('quiz_completed')}</h3>
        <p className="text-gray-500 mb-2">{t('quiz_scored')} {score} / {quizData.length}</p>
        <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mb-6">Attempts: {attempts} / {maxAttempts}</p>
        
        {saving && <p className="text-xs text-brand-purple flex items-center justify-center gap-1 mb-4"><Loader2 className="animate-spin" size={12}/> Saving Result...</p>}

        {!passed && attempts < maxAttempts && (
          <button onClick={restart} className="bg-brand-purple text-white px-6 py-2 rounded-xl font-bold hover:opacity-90 transition flex items-center gap-2 mx-auto">
            <RefreshCw size={18} /> {t('quiz_retry')}
          </button>
        )}
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl border border-gray-200 p-6 md:p-8 shadow-sm">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-6">
        <span className="text-xs font-bold text-brand-purple uppercase tracking-wider">
          Question {currentQuestion + 1} / {quizData.length}
        </span>
        <span className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-400">{t('quiz_score')}: {score}</span>
      </div>

      <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-6">{question.question}</h3>

      <div className="mb-8 min-h-[150px]">
        {questionType === 'multiple-choice' && (
          <div className="space-y-3">
            {question.options?.map((option, index) => {
              let btnClass = "w-full p-4 text-left rounded-xl border-2 transition font-medium "
              if (isAnswered) {
                if (index === question.correct) btnClass += "border-green-500 bg-green-50 text-green-700"
                else if (index === selectedOption) btnClass += "border-red-500 bg-red-50 text-red-700"
                else btnClass += "border-gray-100 text-gray-400 opacity-50"
              } else {
                btnClass += "border-gray-100 hover:border-brand-purple hover:bg-purple-50 text-gray-700"
              }
              return (
                <button key={index} disabled={isAnswered} onClick={() => handleMultipleChoice(index)} className={btnClass}>
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                    <span>{option}</span>
                    {isAnswered && index === question.correct && <CheckCircle size={20} className="text-green-500"/>}
                    {isAnswered && index === selectedOption && index !== question.correct && <XCircle size={20} className="text-red-500"/>}
                  </div>
                </button>
              )
            })}
          </div>
        )}

        {questionType === 'text' && (
          <form onSubmit={handleTextSubmit} className="space-y-4">
            <div className="relative">
              <Type className="absolute left-3 top-3.5 text-gray-400" size={18} />
              <input 
                type="text" disabled={isAnswered} placeholder="Type your answer here..."
                className={`w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-3 rounded-xl border-2 outline-none transition ${isAnswered ? (feedback === 'correct' ? 'border-green-500 bg-green-50' : 'border-red-500 bg-red-50') : 'border-gray-200 focus:border-brand-purple'}`}
                value={textAnswer} onChange={(e) => setTextAnswer(e.target.value)}
              />
            </div>
            {!isAnswered && <button type="submit" className="bg-brand-dark text-white px-6 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm">Check Answer</button>}
            {isAnswered && feedback === 'incorrect' && <p className="text-red-500 text-sm font-bold">Correct answer: {question.correctAnswer}</p>}
          </form>
        )}

        {questionType === 'voice' && (
          <div className="flex flex-col items-center justify-center py-8">
            {isRecording && <div className="mb-4"><AudioVisualizer isRecording={isRecording} /></div>}
            
            <button disabled={isAnswered || processingAudio} onClick={toggleRecording} className={`w-20 h-20 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center transition-all shadow-lg ${isRecording ? 'bg-red-500 animate-pulse scale-110' : (isAnswered ? 'bg-green-500' : 'bg-brand-purple hover:scale-105')}`}>
              {processingAudio ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin text-white"/> : (isRecording ? <Square size={24} className="text-white fill-current"/> : <Mic size={32} className="text-white" />)}
            </button>
            
            <p className="mt-4 text-gray-500 font-medium">
                {processingAudio ? "Analyzing pronunciation..." : (isRecording ? "Listening..." : (isAnswered ? "Great pronunciation!" : "Tap to Speak"))}
            </p>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {isAnswered && (
        <div className="flex justify-end pt-6 border-t border-gray-100">
          <button onClick={handleNext} className="bg-brand-dark text-white px-8 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold hover:opacity-90 transition flex items-center gap-2">
            {currentQuestion === quizData.length - 1 ? t('quiz_finish') : t('quiz_next')} <ArrowRight size={18}/>
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useCart } from '@/context/CartContext'
import { ShoppingBag, Plus, ArrowRight } from 'lucide-react'
import Link from 'next/link'

export default function RelatedProducts({ currentCourseId }) {
  const [products, setProducts] = useState([])
  const { addToCart } = useCart()

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchRelated = async () => {
      const { data } = await supabase
        .from('products')
        .select('*')
        .neq('id', currentCourseId)
        .in('type', ['ebook', 'merch'])
        .limit(2)
      setProducts(data || [])
    }
    fetchRelated()
  }, [currentCourseId])

  if (products.length === 0) return null

  return (
    <div className="mt-12 animate-fade-in">
      <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-6 flex items-center gap-2">
        <ShoppingBag className="text-brand-purple"/> Recommended Materials
      </h3>
      
      <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
        {products.map(product => (
          <div key={product.id} className="bg-white p-4 rounded-xl border border-gray-100 shadow-sm flex items-center gap-4 hover:shadow-md transition">
            <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-gray-100 rounded-lg overflow-hidden flex-shrink-0">
              <img src={product.image_url || 'https://placehold.co/100'} alt={product.title} className="w-full h-full object-cover"/>
            </div>
            <div className="flex-1">
              <h4 className="font-bold text-gray-800 line-clamp-1">{product.title}</h4>
              <p className="text-sm text-brand-purple font-bold">{product.price}</p>
            </div>
            <button 
              onClick={() => addToCart(product)}
              className="bg-gray-900 text-white p-2 rounded-lg hover:bg-gray-700 transition"
              title="Add to Cart"
            >
              <Plus size={20}/>
            </button>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
      
      <div className="text-right mt-4">
        <Link href="/store" className="text-sm font-bold text-brand-blue hover:underline inline-flex items-center gap-1">
          Visit Store <ArrowRight size={14}/>
        </Link>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/course/ReviewSection.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { Star, Loader2, Trash2 } from 'lucide-react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { useToast } from '@/context/ToastContext'
import { useLanguage } from '@/context/LanguageContext'

export default function ReviewSection({ productId }) {
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const { t } = useLanguage()
  const { addToast } = useToast()
  const [reviews, setReviews] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [rating, setRating] = useState(5)
  const [comment, setComment] = useState('')
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false)
  const [hasReviewed, setHasReviewed] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => { fetchReviews() }, [productId])

  const fetchReviews = async () => {
    try {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.from('reviews').select('*, profiles(full_name, avatar_url)').eq('product_id', productId).order('created_at', { ascending: false })
      if (error) throw error
      setReviews(data)
      if (user) setHasReviewed(data.some(r => r.user_id === user.id))
    } catch (error) { console.error(error) } finally { setLoading(false) }
  }

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (!comment.trim()) return
    setSubmitting(true)
    try {
      const { error } = await supabase.from('reviews').insert([{ user_id: user.id, product_id: productId, rating, comment }])
      if (error) throw error
      addToast("Review submitted!", "success")
      setComment('')
      setHasReviewed(true)
      fetchReviews()
    } catch (error) { addToast(error.message, "error") } finally { setSubmitting(false) }
  }

  const handleDelete = async (id) => {
    if (!window.confirm("Delete your review?")) return
    await supabase.from('reviews').delete().eq('id', id)
    setHasReviewed(false)
    fetchReviews()
  }

  const avgRating = reviews.length ? (reviews.reduce((acc, r) => acc + r.rating, 0) / reviews.length).toFixed(1) : 0

  if (loading) return <div className="py-10"><Loader2 className="animate-spin mx-auto text-brand-purple"/></div>

  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-6 md:p-8 border border-gray-100 shadow-sm mt-8">
      <div className="flex items-center gap-4 mb-8">
        <div className="bg-yellow-50 p-4 rounded-2xl text-center min-w-[100px]">
          <span className="block text-3xl font-black text-brand-dark">{avgRating}</span>
          <div className="flex justify-center text-yellow-400 text-sm">{[...Array(5)].map((_, i) => <Star key={i} size={12} fill={i < Math.round(avgRating) ? "currentColor" : "none"} />)}</div>
          <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 font-bold mt-1">{reviews.length} Reviews</span>
        </div>
        <div>
          <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-brand-dark">{t('review_title')}</h3>
          <p className="text-gray-500">{t('review_subtitle')}</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      {!hasReviewed && user && (
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="mb-8 bg-gray-50 p-6 rounded-xl border border-gray-200">
          <h4 className="font-bold text-gray-700 mb-4">{t('review_write')}</h4>
          <div className="flex gap-2 mb-4">
            {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map(star => (
              <button type="button" key={star} onClick={() => setRating(star)} className="focus:outline-none transition transform hover:scale-110">
                <Star size={24} className={star <= rating ? "text-yellow-400 fill-yellow-400" : "text-gray-300"} />
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
          <textarea 
            className="w-full p-3 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-purple text-sm"
            placeholder={t('review_placeholder')} rows="3" value={comment} onChange={e => setComment(e.target.value)}
          />
          <button disabled={submitting} className="mt-3 bg-brand-purple text-white px-6 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm hover:opacity-90 transition">
            {submitting ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" /> : t('review_submit')}
          </button>
        </form>
      )}

      <div className="space-y-6">
        {reviews.length === 0 && <p className="text-gray-400 text-center italic">{t('review_no_reviews')}</p>}
        {reviews.map(review => (
          <div key={review.id} className="border-b border-gray-50 pb-6 last:border-0 last:pb-0">
            <div className="flex justify-between items-start mb-2">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <img src={review.profiles?.avatar_url || 'https://placehold.co/50'} className="w-10 h-10 rounded-full object-cover bg-gray-200" />
                <div>
                  <p className="font-bold text-gray-800 text-sm">{review.profiles?.full_name || 'Student'}</p>
                  <div className="flex text-yellow-400 text-xs">{[...Array(5)].map((_, i) => <Star key={i} size={10} fill={i < review.rating ? "currentColor" : "none"} />)}</div>
                </div>
              </div>
              {user?.id === review.user_id && <button onClick={() => handleDelete(review.id)} className="text-gray-300 hover:text-red-500 transition"><Trash2 size={16}/></button>}
            </div>
            <p className="text-gray-600 text-sm leading-relaxed mt-2">{review.comment}</p>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/course/VocabularyDrill.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { RefreshCw, Check, X } from 'lucide-react'
import { useLanguage } from '@/context/LanguageContext'

export default function VocabularyDrill({ description }) {
  const { t } = useLanguage()
  const [vocab, setVocab] = useState([])
  const [currentIndex, setCurrentIndex] = useState(0)
  const [showTranslation, setShowTranslation] = useState(false)
  const [finished, setFinished] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    const regex = /\[\[VOCAB::(.*?)\|(.*?)\]\]/g
    const matches = []
    let match
    while ((match = regex.exec(description)) !== null) {
      matches.push({ term: match[1], definition: match[2] })
    }
    setVocab(matches)
    setCurrentIndex(0)
    setFinished(false)
    setShowTranslation(false)
  }, [description])

  const handleNext = () => {
    setShowTranslation(false)
    if (currentIndex < vocab.length - 1) {
      setCurrentIndex(currentIndex + 1)
    } else {
      setFinished(true)
    }
  }

  const reset = () => {
    setCurrentIndex(0)
    setFinished(false)
    setShowTranslation(false)
  }

  if (vocab.length === 0) return null

  if (finished) {
    return (
      <div className="bg-gradient-to-br from-purple-500 to-blue-500 rounded-2xl p-6 text-center text-white shadow-lg mb-6">
        <div className="text-4xl mb-2">🎉</div>
        <h3 className="font-bold text-xl mb-4">{t('vocab_complete')}</h3>
        <button onClick={reset} className="bg-white text-brand-purple px-6 py-2 rounded-full font-bold hover:bg-gray-100 transition flex items-center gap-2 mx-auto">
          <RefreshCw size={16} /> {t('vocab_retry')}
        </button>
      </div>
    )
  }

  const currentCard = vocab[currentIndex]

  return (
    <div className="bg-white border-2 border-gray-100 rounded-2xl p-6 mb-6 shadow-sm relative overflow-hidden">
      <div className="absolute top-0 left-0 w-full h-1 bg-gray-100">
        <div className="h-1 bg-brand-purple transition-all duration-300" style={{ width: `${((currentIndex + 1) / vocab.length) * 100}%` }}></div>
      </div>
      
      <div className="flex justify-between items-center mb-8 text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wider">
        <span>{t('vocab_title')}</span>
        <span>{currentIndex + 1} / {vocab.length}</span>
      </div>

      <div className="h-32 flex flex-col items-center justify-center cursor-pointer mb-6 perspective-1000" onClick={() => setShowTranslation(!showTranslation)}>
        <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">{currentCard.term}</h2>
        <p className={`text-xl text-brand-blue font-medium transition-all duration-300 ${showTranslation ? 'opacity-100 translate-y-0' : 'opacity-0 translate-y-4'}`}>
          {currentCard.definition}
        </p>
        {!showTranslation && <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 mt-4 animate-bounce">{t('vocab_reveal')}</p>}
      </div>

      {showTranslation && (
        <div className="flex gap-3">
          <button onClick={handleNext} className="flex-1 bg-red-50 text-red-600 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold hover:bg-red-100 transition flex justify-center items-center gap-2">
            <X size={18} /> {t('vocab_hard')}
          </button>
          <button onClick={handleNext} className="flex-1 bg-green-50 text-green-600 py-3 rounded-xl font-bold hover:bg-green-100 transition flex justify-center items-center gap-2">
            <Check size={18} /> {t('vocab_easy')}
          </button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

PATH: ./src/components/dashboard/ReferralCard.jsx

"use client";
import { useState } from 'react'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { Gift, Copy, Check, Users } from 'lucide-react'

export default function ReferralCard() {
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false)

  const referralLink = typeof window !== 'undefined' ? `${window.location.origin}/register?ref=${user?.id}` : ''

  const copyToClipboard = () => {
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(referralLink)
    setCopied(true)
    setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000)
  }

  return (
    <div className="bg-gradient-to-r from-purple-600 to-indigo-600 rounded-2xl p-6 text-white shadow-lg relative overflow-hidden animate-slide-up">
      <div className="absolute top-0 right-0 p-4 opacity-10">
        <Gift size={120} />
      </div>
      
      <div className="relative z-10">
        <h3 className="font-bold text-xl mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
          <Users size={20}/> Invite Friends
        </h3>
        <p className="text-purple-100 text-sm mb-6 max-w-xs">
          Share the love of learning! For every friend who joins, you both get <span className="font-bold text-yellow-300">100 XP</span> boost.
        </p>

        <div className="bg-white/10 backdrop-blur-md p-1 rounded-xl flex items-center border border-white/20">
          <input 
            readOnly 
            value={referralLink} 
            className="bg-transparent border-none outline-none text-xs text-white px-3 flex-1 font-mono truncate"
          />
          <button 
            onClick={copyToClipboard}
            className="bg-white text-purple-600 p-2 rounded-lg hover:bg-purple-50 transition flex-shrink-0"
          >
            {copied ? <Check size={16}/> : <Copy size={16}/>}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

PATH: ./src/components/layout/AppLayout.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useRouter, usePathname } from 'next/navigation'
import { useState } from 'react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { useLanguage } from '@/context/LanguageContext'
import { LayoutDashboard, BookOpen, Calendar, LogOut, User, ShieldCheck, ShoppingBag, Settings, Download, Menu, X, Trophy, StickyNote, PenTool, MessageSquare, CreditCard } from 'lucide-react'
import AnnouncementBanner from '../ui/AnnouncementBanner'

export default function AppLayout() {
  const { signOut, user, profile, isAdmin } = useAuth()
  const { t } = useLanguage()
  const pathname = usePathname()
  const router = useRouter()
  const [mobileOpen, setMobileOpen] = useState(false)

  const handleLogout = async () => {
    await signOut()
    router.push('/login')
  }

  const NavItem = ({ href, icon: Icon, label }) => {
    const isActive = pathname === href
    return (
      <Link 
        href={href} 
        onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}
        className={`flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 rounded-xl transition-all ${
          isActive ? 'bg-brand-purple text-white shadow-md' : 'text-gray-600 hover:bg-purple-50 hover:text-brand-purple'
        }`}
      >
        <Icon size={20} />
        <span className="font-medium">{label}</span>
      </Link>
    )
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen bg-[#F8F9FC]">
      <div className="md:hidden fixed top-0 left-0 w-full bg-white z-30 px-4 py-3 shadow-sm flex justify-between items-center">
        <span className="font-bold text-brand-purple text-lg">Annyeong LMS</span>
        <button onClick={() => setMobileOpen(!mobileOpen)} className="text-gray-700"><X size={24} className={!mobileOpen && "hidden"}/><Menu size={24} className={mobileOpen && "hidden"}/></button>
      </div>

      {mobileOpen && <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-20 md:hidden" onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}></div>}

      <aside className={`fixed top-0 left-0 h-full w-64 bg-white border-r border-gray-200 z-30 transform transition-transform duration-300 ease-in-out md:translate-x-0 ${mobileOpen ? 'translate-x-0' : '-translate-x-full'}`}>
        <div className="p-6 hidden md:block">
          <span className="text-xl font-bold bg-clip-text text-transparent bg-gradient-to-r from-brand-blue to-brand-purple">Annyeong LMS</span>
        </div>
        
        <div className="md:hidden p-6 border-b border-gray-100 flex items-center justify-between">
           <span className="font-bold text-brand-dark">Menu</span>
           <button onClick={() => setMobileOpen(false)}><X size={20}/></button>
        </div>

        <nav className="flex-1 px-4 space-y-2 mt-4 overflow-y-auto">
          <NavItem href="/dashboard" icon={LayoutDashboard} label={t('nav_dashboard')} />
          <NavItem href="/corsi" icon={BookOpen} label={t('nav_courses')} />

               <p className="px-4 text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase mb-2">{t('nav_admin')}</p>
               <NavItem href="/admin" icon={ShieldCheck} label={t('nav_management')} />
            </div>
          )}
        </nav>

        <div className="p-4 border-t border-gray-100">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 mb-2">
            <div className="w-8 h-8 bg-gray-200 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-gray-500 overflow-hidden">
              {profile?.avatar_url ? <img src={profile.avatar_url} alt="Profile" className="w-full h-full object-cover"/> : <User size={16} />}
            </div>
            <div className="text-sm overflow-hidden">
              <p className="font-bold text-gray-700 truncate">{profile?.full_name || 'Student'}</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-gray-400 truncate">{user?.email}</p>
            </div>
          </div>
          <button onClick={handleLogout} className="w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-2 text-red-500 hover:bg-red-50 rounded-lg transition text-sm font-medium">
            <LogOut size={18} /> {t('nav_logout')}
          </button>
        </div>
      </aside>

      <div className="flex-1 md:ml-64 pt-16 md:pt-0 flex flex-col min-h-screen">
        <AnnouncementBanner />
        <main className="p-4 md:p-8 max-w-6xl mx-auto w-full flex-1">
          {/* Outlet is implicit */}
        </main>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/payment/PaymentModal.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { X, CreditCard, Lock, CheckCircle, Loader2, ShieldCheck, ExternalLink } from 'lucide-react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { useToast } from '@/context/ToastContext'
import { useLanguage } from '@/context/LanguageContext'
import { useEdgeFunction } from '@/hooks/useEdgeFunction'

export default function PaymentModal({ isOpen, onClose, item }) {
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const { t } = useLanguage()
  const { addToast } = useToast()
  const { invoke } = useEdgeFunction()
  
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const [step, setStep] = useState('form') 

  useEffect(() => { if (isOpen) setStep('form') }, [isOpen])

  if (!isOpen || !item) return null

  const handlePayment = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setLoading(true)
    
    // Call Edge Function for Checkout Session
    const { data, fallback } = await invoke('create-checkout', {
      items: [item],
      userId: user.id
    })

    if (!fallback && data?.sessionId) {
        // Redirect to Real Stripe (Mocked via link here if no stripe-js installed in frontend logic)
        // Ideally: stripe.redirectToCheckout({ sessionId: data.sessionId })
        // For this demo structure, we stick to simulation fallback if no stripe lib
        console.log("Stripe Session:", data.sessionId)
    }

    // Fallback Simulation (Or if Edge Function sends success signal directly for demo)
    setTimeout(async () => {
      setStep('processing')
      setTimeout(async () => {
        try {
          const { error } = await supabase.from('purchases').insert([{ user_id: user.id, product_id: item.id, status: 'completed' }])
          if (error) throw error
          setStep('success')
          addToast('Purchase successful!', 'success')
          setTimeout(() => window.location.reload(), 2000)
        } catch (error) {
          setLoading(false)
          setStep('form')
          addToast(error.message, 'error')
        }
      }, 1500)
    }, 1000)
  }

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-brand-dark/80 backdrop-blur-sm z-[60] flex items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-2xl max-w-md w-full overflow-hidden relative animate-slide-in">
        <div className="bg-gray-50 p-4 flex justify-between items-center border-b border-gray-100">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-brand-dark font-bold"><ShieldCheck className="text-green-500" size={20} /> {t('pay_secure')}</div>
          <button onClick={onClose} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600"><X size={20}/></button>
        </div>

        <div className="p-6">
          {step === 'form' && (
            <form onSubmit={handlePayment}>
              <div className="flex gap-4 mb-6">
                <img src={item.image_url || 'https://placehold.co/100'} className="w-20 h-20 rounded-lg object-cover bg-gray-100" />
                <div>
                  <h3 className="font-bold text-gray-800 leading-tight">{item.title}</h3>
                  <p className="text-brand-purple font-bold text-xl">{item.price}</p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="space-y-4 mb-6">
                <div className="bg-blue-50 border border-blue-100 p-3 rounded-lg text-xs text-blue-700 mb-4">
                   <strong>Demo Mode:</strong> No actual charge will be made.
                </div>
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase mb-1">{t('pay_card')}</label>
                  <div className="relative">
                    <input type="text" placeholder="4242 4242 4242 4242" className="w-full pl-10 pr-4 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg outline-none focus:border-brand-purple font-mono" readOnly value="4242 4242 4242 4242" />
                    <CreditCard className="absolute left-3 top-2.5 text-gray-400" size={18} />
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className="flex gap-4">
                  <div className="flex-1">
                    <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase mb-1">{t('pay_expiry')}</label>
                    <input type="text" placeholder="MM/YY" className="w-full px-4 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg outline-none focus:border-brand-purple font-mono" readOnly value="12/28" />
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex-1">
                    <label className="block text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase mb-1">{t('pay_cvc')}</label>
                    <input type="text" placeholder="123" className="w-full px-4 py-2 border border-gray-300 rounded-lg outline-none focus:border-brand-purple font-mono" readOnly value="123" />
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>

              <button disabled={loading} type="submit" className="w-full bg-brand-dark text-white font-bold py-3 rounded-xl hover:bg-gray-800 transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-lg">
                {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" /> : <><Lock size={16} /> {t('pay_btn')} {item.price}</>}
              </button>
            </form>
          )}

          {step === 'processing' && (
            <div className="py-10 text-center">
              <Loader2 className="w-16 h-16 text-brand-purple animate-spin mx-auto mb-4" />
              <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800">{t('pay_processing')}</h3>
            </div>
          )}

          {step === 'success' && (
            <div className="py-10 text-center">
              <div className="w-16 h-16 bg-green-100 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-4 animate-bounce">
                <CheckCircle className="w-8 h-8 text-green-600" />
              </div>
              <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-gray-800">{t('pay_success')}</h3>
              <p className="text-gray-500">{t('pay_redirect')}</p>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
