================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/sections/Countdown.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import Section from '../ui/Section'

export default function Countdown() {
  const [timeLeft, setTimeLeft] = useState({ days: 0, hours: 0, minutes: 0, seconds: 0 });

  useEffect(() => {
    const currentYear = new Date().getFullYear();
    const targetDate = new Date(`December 14, ${currentYear} 11:00:00`).getTime();

    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      const now = new Date().getTime();
      const distance = targetDate - now;

      if (distance < 0) {
        clearInterval(interval);
      } else {
        setTimeLeft({
          days: Math.floor(distance / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24)),
          hours: Math.floor((distance % (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24)) / (1000 * 60 * 60)),
          minutes: Math.floor((distance % (1000 * 60 * 60)) / (1000 * 60)),
          seconds: Math.floor((distance % (1000 * 60)) / 1000)
        });
      }
    }, 1000);

    return () => clearInterval(interval);
  }, []);

  const scrollToForm = () => {
    document.getElementById('registration').scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
  };

  return (
    <section className="bg-brand-dark text-white py-8 px-4 text-center border-t border-white/10">
      <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center justify-center gap-6">
        <div>
          <p className="text-sm font-bold uppercase tracking-widest text-brand-teal mb-1">Don't miss out!</p>
          <p className="text-xl font-medium">The trial class starts in:</p>
        </div>
        
        <div className="flex gap-4 text-center">
          {[
            { label: 'Days', value: timeLeft.days },
            { label: 'Hours', value: timeLeft.hours },
            { label: 'Mins', value: timeLeft.minutes },
            { label: 'Secs', value: timeLeft.seconds }
          ].map((item, idx) => (
            <div key={idx} className="bg-white/10 rounded-lg p-3 min-w-[70px] backdrop-blur-sm">
              <span className="block text-2xl font-bold font-mono">{item.value}</span>
              <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 uppercase">{item.label}</span>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>

        <button 
          onClick={scrollToForm}
          className="btn btn-plum-gradient"
        >
          Reserve my spot now!
        </button>
      </div>
    </section>
  )
}

================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/sections/FeaturedCourses.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import Link from 'next/link'
import Section from '../ui/Section'
import { Video, ArrowRight, Sparkles } from 'lucide-react'

export default function FeaturedCourses() {
  const [courses, setCourses] = useState([])

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchCourses = async () => {
      // Fetch products marked as featured
      const { data } = await supabase
        .from('products')
        .select('*')
        .eq('type', 'course')
        .eq('is_featured', true)
        .limit(3)
      
      // Fallback: If no featured courses, just get the latest 3
      if (!data || data.length === 0) {
         const { data: fallback } = await supabase.from('products').select('*').eq('type', 'course').limit(3)
         setCourses(fallback || [])
      } else {
         setCourses(data)
      }
    }
    fetchCourses()
  }, [])

  if (courses.length === 0) return null

  return (
    <Section className="bg-[#F8F9FC]">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-end mb-12">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-3xl md:text-4xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2 flex items-center gap-2">
            <Sparkles className="text-yellow-500"/> Popular Courses
          </h2>
          <p className="text-gray-600">Hand-picked curriculum to start your journey.</p>
        </div>
        <Link href="/corsi" className="hidden md:flex items-center gap-2 font-bold text-brand-purple hover:underline">
          View All <ArrowRight size={18}/>
        </Link>
      </div>

      <div className="grid md:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
        {courses.map(course => (
          <Link href={`/corsi/${course.id}`} key={course.id} className="group bg-white rounded-3xl p-4 shadow-sm hover:shadow-xl transition duration-300 border border-transparent hover:border-brand-purple/20">
            <div className="h-48 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl relative bg-gray-100 mb-4">
              <img src={course.image_url || 'https://placehold.co/400x300'} className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-105 transition duration-500" alt={course.title} />
              <div className="absolute top-3 right-3 bg-white/90 backdrop-blur px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold text-brand-dark flex items-center gap-1">
                <Video size={12} className="text-brand-purple" /> COURSE
              </div>
              {course.sale_price && (
                <div className="absolute top-3 left-3 bg-red-500 text-white px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold shadow-md animate-pulse">
                  SALE
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
            <div className="px-2 pb-2">
              <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2 line-clamp-1">{course.title}</h3>
              <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-sm">
                <span className="text-gray-500 bg-gray-100 px-2 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold">{course.level || 'Beginner'}</span>
                <div className="text-right">
                  {course.sale_price ? (
                    <>
                      <span className="text-xs text-gray-400 line-through mr-2">{course.price}</span>
                      <span className="font-bold text-red-500 text-lg">{course.sale_price}</span>
                    </>
                  ) : (
                    <span className="font-bold text-brand-purple text-lg">{course.price}</span>
                  )}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </Link>
        ))}
      </div>
    </Section>
  )
}

PATH: ./src/components/sections/Footer.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { Facebook, Instagram, Youtube, Music2, Mail, Check } from 'lucide-react'
import { useLanguage } from '@/context/LanguageContext'

export default function Footer() {
  const { t } = useLanguage()
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('')
  const [subscribed, setSubscribed] = useState(false)

  const handleSubscribe = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (!email) return
    await supabase.from('subscribers').insert([{ email }])
    setSubscribed(true)
    setEmail('')
  }

  return (
    <footer className="bg-brand-dark text-gray-400 py-12">
      <div className="max-w-6xl mx-auto px-4">
        
        <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 gap-12 mb-12 border-b border-gray-800 pb-12">
          <div className="text-center md:text-left">
            <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gradient-brand mb-4">Annyeong Academy</h3>
            <p className="text-sm max-w-sm mx-auto md:mx-0">
              Breaking language barriers with technology and culture. Learn Korean directly from Seoul.
            </p>
            <div className="flex justify-center md:justify-start gap-6 mt-6">
              <a href="#" className="hover:text-white transition transform hover:scale-110"><Facebook size={24} /></a>
              <a href="#" className="hover:text-white transition transform hover:scale-110"><Instagram size={24} /></a>
              <a href="#" className="hover:text-white transition transform hover:scale-110"><Music2 size={24} /></a>
              <a href="#" className="hover:text-white transition transform hover:scale-110"><Youtube size={24} /></a>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="text-center md:text-right">
            <h4 className="text-white font-bold mb-4">Stay Updated</h4>
            {subscribed ? (
              <div className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-green-400 font-bold bg-green-900/30 px-4 py-2 rounded-full">
                <Check size={18}/> Subscribed!
              </div>
            ) : (
              <form onSubmit={handleSubscribe} className="flex gap-2 max-w-sm mx-auto md:ml-auto">
                <input 
                  type="email" 
                  placeholder="Your email address" 
                  className="flex-1 bg-gray-800 border-none rounded-lg px-4 py-2 text-white outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-purple"
                  value={email}
                  onChange={e => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                />
                <button className="bg-brand-purple text-white p-2 rounded-lg hover:bg-purple-600 transition"><Mail size={20}/></button>
              </form>
            )}
            <p className="text-xs mt-3 opacity-50">Join our newsletter for weekly tips.</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-x-8 gap-y-4 mb-8 text-sm">
          <Link href="/about" className="hover:text-white transition">{t('About Us')}</Link>
          <Link href="/tutors" className="hover:text-white transition">{t('Teachers')}</Link>
          <Link href="/faq" className="hover:text-white transition">{t('FAQ')}</Link>
          <Link href="/contact" className="hover:text-white transition">{t('Contact')}</Link>
          <span className="text-gray-600">|</span>
          <Link href="/privacy" className="hover:text-white transition">{t('Privacy')}</Link>
          <Link href="/terms" className="hover:text-white transition">{t('Terms')}</Link>
          <Link href="/impressum" className="hover:text-white transition">Impressum</Link>
          <Link href="/giveaway-rules" className="hover:text-white transition">Giveaway Rules</Link>
        </div>
        <p className="text-xs opacity-50 text-center">&copy; 2025 Annyeong Academy. All rights reserved.</p>
      </div>
    </footer>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/sections/LatestBlog.jsx

"use client";
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import Link from 'next/link'
import Section from '../ui/Section'
import { ArrowRight } from 'lucide-react'

export default function LatestBlog() {
  const [posts, setPosts] = useState([])

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchPosts = async () => {
      const { data } = await supabase.from('posts').select('*').eq('published', true).order('created_at', {ascending: false}).limit(3)
      setPosts(data || [])
    }
    fetchPosts()
  }, [])

  if(posts.length === 0) return null

  return (
    <Section className="bg-white">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-end mb-12">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-3xl md:text-4xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">Latest from the Blog</h2>
          <p className="text-gray-600">Culture, tips, and academy news.</p>
        </div>
        <Link href="/blog" className="hidden md:flex items-center gap-2 font-bold text-brand-purple hover:underline">View All <ArrowRight size={18}/></Link>
      </div>

      <div className="grid md:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
        {posts.map(post => (
          <Link href={`/blog/${post.slug}`} key={post.id} className="group cursor-pointer">
            <div className="rounded-2xl overflow-hidden mb-4 bg-gray-100 h-48">
              <img src={post.image_url || 'https://placehold.co/600x400'} className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-105 transition duration-500" alt={post.title} />
            </div>
            <h3 className="font-bold text-xl text-brand-dark mb-2 group-hover:text-brand-purple transition">{post.title}</h3>
            <p className="text-gray-600 text-sm line-clamp-2">{post.excerpt}</p>
          </Link>
        ))}
      </div>
      
      <div className="mt-8 text-center md:hidden">
        <Link href="/blog" className="btn btn-plum-gradient px-8 py-3">View All Posts</Link>
      </div>
    </Section>
  )
}

================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/sections/Navbar.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useRouter, usePathname } from 'next/navigation'
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { Menu, X, LogIn, LayoutDashboard, ShoppingCart, Search } from 'lucide-react'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { useCart } from '@/context/CartContext'
import NotificationBell from '@/components/ui/NotificationBell'
import GlobalSearch from '@/components/ui/GlobalSearch'
import LanguageSwitcher from '@/components/ui/LanguageSwitcher' // NEW

export default function Navbar() {
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const { cart } = useCart()
  const [scrolled, setScrolled] = useState(false)
  const [mobileMenuOpen, setMobileMenuOpen] = useState(false)
  const [searchOpen, setSearchOpen] = useState(false)
  const pathname = usePathname()
  const router = useRouter()

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleScroll = () => setScrolled(window.scrollY > 50)
    window.addEventListener('scroll', handleScroll)
    
    const handleKeydown = (e) => {
      if ((e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey) && e.key === 'k') {
        e.preventDefault()
        setSearchOpen(true)
      }
      if (e.key === 'Escape') setSearchOpen(false)
    }
    window.addEventListener('keydown', handleKeydown)

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('scroll', handleScroll)
      window.removeEventListener('keydown', handleKeydown)
    }
  }, [])

  const scrollToForm = () => {
    setMobileMenuOpen(false)
    if (pathname !== '/') {
      router.push('/')
      setTimeout(() => {
        const form = document.getElementById('registration')
        if (form) form.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' })
      }, 500)
    } else {
      const form = document.getElementById('registration')
      if (form) form.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' })
    }
  }

  return (
    <>
      <nav className={`fixed top-0 left-0 w-full z-50 transition-all duration-300 ${scrolled ? 'bg-white/90 backdrop-blur-md shadow-sm py-3' : 'bg-transparent py-5'}`}>
        <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto px-6 flex justify-between items-center">
          
          <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
            <Link href="/" className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="text-2xl font-bold text-gradient-brand">
                Annyeong Academy
              </span>
            </Link>
            {/* Desktop Lang Switcher */}
            <div className="hidden md:block"><LanguageSwitcher /></div>
          </div>

          <div className="hidden md:flex items-center gap-6">
            <Link href="/about" className="text-gray-600 hover:text-brand-purple font-medium transition">About</Link>
            <Link href="/corsi" className="text-gray-600 hover:text-brand-purple font-medium transition">Courses</Link>
            <Link href="/tutors" className="text-gray-600 hover:text-brand-purple font-medium transition">Teachers</Link>
            
            <button onClick={() => setSearchOpen(true)} className="p-2 text-gray-500 hover:text-brand-purple transition">
              <Search size={20} />
            </button>

            <div className="flex items-center gap-4 border-l border-gray-300 pl-6">
              {user && <NotificationBell />}
              
              <Link href="/cart" className="relative text-gray-600 hover:text-brand-purple transition">
                <ShoppingCart size={22} />
                {cart.length > 0 && (
                  <span className="absolute -top-2 -right-2 bg-red-500 text-white text-[10px] font-bold w-5 h-5 flex items-center justify-center rounded-full">
                    {cart.length}
                  </span>
                )}
              </Link>

              {user ? (
                <Link href="/dashboard" className="flex items-center gap-2 text-brand-dark font-bold hover:text-brand-purple transition">
                  <div className="bg-brand-purple text-white p-1 rounded-md"><LayoutDashboard size={16} /></div>
                  Dash
                </Link>
              ) : (
                <Link href="/login" className="flex items-center gap-2 text-brand-dark font-bold hover:text-brand-purple transition">
                  <LogIn size={18} /> Login
                </Link>
              )}
              
              <button 
                onClick={scrollToForm}
                className="btn btn-plum-gradient px-5 py-2 text-sm"
              >
                Join Free
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="md:hidden flex items-center gap-4">
            <LanguageSwitcher />
            <button onClick={() => setSearchOpen(true)} className="text-gray-700">
              <Search size={24} />
            </button>
            <button className="text-gray-700" onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(!mobileMenuOpen)}>
              {mobileMenuOpen ? <X size={28} /> : <Menu size={28} />}
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        {mobileMenuOpen && (
          <div className="md:hidden absolute top-full left-0 w-full bg-white border-t border-gray-100 shadow-xl p-6 flex flex-col gap-4 animate-slide-in">
            <Link href="/about" className="text-lg font-medium text-gray-700" onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)}>About Us</Link>
            <Link href="/corsi" className="text-lg font-medium text-gray-700" onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)}>Courses</Link>
            <Link href="/tutors" className="text-lg font-medium text-gray-700" onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)}>Teachers</Link>
            <Link href="/blog" className="text-lg font-medium text-gray-700" onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)}>Blog</Link>
            <Link href="/cart" className="text-lg font-medium text-gray-700 flex items-center gap-2" onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)}>
              <ShoppingCart size={20}/> Cart ({cart.length})
            </Link>
            
            {user ? (
               <Link href="/dashboard" className="text-lg font-bold text-brand-purple flex items-center gap-2" onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)}>
                  <LayoutDashboard size={20} /> Dashboard
               </Link>
            ) : (
               <Link href="/login" className="text-lg font-bold text-brand-dark flex items-center gap-2" onClick={() => setMobileMenuOpen(false)}>
                  <LogIn size={20} /> Login
               </Link>
            )}

            <button 
              onClick={scrollToForm}
              className="btn btn-plum-gradient w-full"
            >
              Book Webinar
            </button>
          </div>
        )}
      </nav>
      {searchOpen && <GlobalSearch onClose={() => setSearchOpen(false)} />}
    </>
  )
}

================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/sections/StickyBar.jsx

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'

export default function StickyBar() {
  const [isVisible, setIsVisible] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleScroll = () => {
      if (window.scrollY > 100) {
        setIsVisible(true);
      } else {
        setIsVisible(false);
      }
    };

    window.addEventListener('scroll', handleScroll);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('scroll', handleScroll);
  }, []);

  const scrollToForm = () => {
    document.getElementById('registration').scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth' });
  };

  if (!isVisible) return null;

  return (
    <div className="md:hidden fixed bottom-0 left-0 w-full bg-white border-t border-gray-200 p-4 shadow-[0_-4px_10px_rgba(0,0,0,0.1)] z-50 flex items-center justify-between animate-slide-up">
      <div className="text-sm font-bold text-brand-dark">
        Free Korean Trial + <br/> AI Translation
      </div>
      <button 
        onClick={scrollToForm}
        className="btn btn-plum-gradient px-6 py-2 text-sm"
      >
        Sign Up
      </button>
    </div>
  )
}

PATH: ./src/components/sections/Teachers.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import Section from '../ui/Section'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { Loader2 } from 'lucide-react'

export default function Teachers() {
  const [tutors, setTutors] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchTutors = async () => {
      const { data } = await supabase.from('tutors').select('*').eq('is_featured', true).limit(3)
      setTutors(data || [])
      setLoading(false)
    }
    fetchTutors()
  }, [])

  return (
    <Section className="bg-[#F8F9FC]">
      <div className="text-center mb-16">
        <h2 className="text-3xl md:text-4xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-4">Who will you learn from?</h2>
        <p className="text-gray-600 max-w-2xl mx-auto">
          Native teachers who currently live in Korea. They deliver the living language of today.
        </p>
      </div>

      {loading ? (
        <div className="flex justify-center"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-brand-purple" /></div>
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-8">
          {tutors.map(tutor => (
            <Link href={`/tutors/${tutor.id}`} key={tutor.id} className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow-lg max-w-sm text-center overflow-hidden group hover:-translate-y-2 transition duration-300 cursor-pointer w-full md:w-80">
              <div className="h-64 bg-gray-200 relative overflow-hidden">
                 <img src={tutor.image_url || 'https://placehold.co/400'} alt={tutor.name} className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-105 transition" />
              </div>
              <div className="p-8">
                <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-1">{tutor.name}</h3>
                <p className="text-brand-purple font-bold text-sm uppercase tracking-wider mb-4">{tutor.role}</p>
                <p className="text-gray-600 italic text-sm line-clamp-3">
                  {tutor.bio}
                </p>
              </div>
            </Link>
          ))}
          {tutors.length === 0 && <p className="text-gray-400">No featured tutors yet.</p>}
        </div>
      )}
    </Section>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/sections/Testimonials.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import Section from '../ui/Section'
import { Quote, Star, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react'

export default function Testimonials() {
  const [reviews, setReviews] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchReviews = async () => {
      const { data } = await supabase
        .from('reviews')
        .select('*, profiles(full_name, avatar_url), products(title)')
        .eq('rating', 5)
        .limit(3)
        .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
      
      setReviews(data || [])
      setLoading(false)
    }
    fetchReviews()
  }, [])

  const fallbackReviews = [
    {
      id: 'static-1',
      comment: "Finally, I don't need to be fluent in English to learn Korean! This technology is incredible.",
      profiles: { full_name: 'Susie', avatar_url: '' },
      products: { title: 'Korean Foundation' }
    },
    {
      id: 'static-2',
      comment: "I love that the teacher connects live from Seoul. It feels like I'm actually there.",
      profiles: { full_name: 'Esther', avatar_url: '' },
      products: { title: 'Live K-Pop Class' }
    }
  ]

  const displayReviews = reviews.length > 0 ? reviews : fallbackReviews

  return (
    <Section className="bg-[#F8F9FC]">
      <div className="text-center mb-12">
        <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-brand-dark">Student Stories</h2>
        <p className="text-gray-500 mt-2">Hear from our community.</p>
      </div>
      
      {loading ? (
        <div className="flex justify-center"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-brand-purple"/></div>
      ) : (
        <div className="flex flex-wrap justify-center gap-8">
          {displayReviews.map((review, idx) => (
            <div key={review.id} className="bg-white p-8 rounded-2xl shadow-lg max-w-md flex-1 min-w-[300px] relative border border-gray-100 transform hover:-translate-y-1 transition duration-300">
              <Quote className={`w-10 h-10 absolute top-6 left-6 opacity-20 ${idx % 2 === 0 ? 'text-brand-purple' : 'text-brand-blue'}`} />
              
              <div className="relative z-10 pt-4">
                <p className="text-gray-700 italic mb-6 text-lg leading-relaxed">"{review.comment}"</p>
                
                <div className="flex items-center gap-4">
                  <div className={`w-10 h-10 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center text-white font-bold overflow-hidden ${idx % 2 === 0 ? 'bg-brand-purple' : 'bg-brand-blue'}`}>
                    {review.profiles?.avatar_url ? (
                      <img src={review.profiles.avatar_url} className="w-full h-full object-cover"/>
                    ) : (
                      review.profiles?.full_name?.[0] || 'S'
                    )}
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <p className="font-bold text-brand-dark">{review.profiles?.full_name || 'Student'}</p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-gray-500">{review.products?.title || 'Course Student'}</p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="ml-auto flex text-yellow-400">
                    {[...Array(5)].map((_, i) => <Star key={i} size={14} fill="currentColor" />)}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </Section>
  )
}

PATH: ./src/components/sections/UpcomingEvents.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import Link from 'next/link'
import Section from '../ui/Section'
import { Calendar, ArrowRight, Clock } from 'lucide-react'

export default function UpcomingEvents() {
  const [events, setEvents] = useState([])

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchEvents = async () => {
      const { data } = await supabase
        .from('products')
        .select('*')
        .eq('type', 'standalone')
        .gt('event_date', new Date().toISOString())
        .order('event_date', { ascending: true })
        .limit(3)
      setEvents(data || [])
    }
    fetchEvents()
  }, [])

  if (events.length === 0) return null

  return (
    <Section className="bg-white">
      <div className="flex justify-between items-end mb-8">
        <div>
          <h2 className="text-3xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">Upcoming Events</h2>
          <p className="text-gray-600">Join live sessions this week.</p>
        </div>
        <Link href="/events" className="hidden md:flex items-center gap-2 font-bold text-brand-purple hover:underline">View Calendar <ArrowRight size={18}/></Link>
      </div>

      <div className="grid md:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
        {events.map(event => {
          const date = new Date(event.event_date)
          return (
            <Link href={`/events/${event.id}`} key={event.id} className="group border border-gray-100 rounded-2xl p-4 hover:shadow-lg transition bg-white flex flex-col">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-4 mb-4">
                <div className="bg-gray-100 rounded-xl w-16 h-16 flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-center shrink-0">
                  <span className="text-xs font-bold text-brand-purple uppercase">{date.toLocaleString('default', {month:'short'})}</span>
                  <span className="text-2xl font-black text-brand-dark leading-none">{date.getDate()}</span>
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h3 className="font-bold text-brand-dark group-hover:text-brand-purple transition line-clamp-1">{event.title}</h3>
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1">
                    <Clock size={12}/> {date.toLocaleTimeString([], {hour:'2-digit', minute:'2-digit'})}
                  </div>
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="mt-auto pt-3 border-t border-gray-50 flex justify-between items-center text-sm">
                <span className="font-bold text-brand-dark">{event.price}</span>
                <span className="text-brand-purple font-medium flex items-center gap-1">Details <ArrowRight size={14}/></span>
              </div>
            </Link>
          )
        })}
      </div>
      <div className="mt-6 text-center md:hidden">
        <Link href="/events" className="btn btn-plum-gradient px-6 py-2 text-sm">View Calendar</Link>
      </div>
    </Section>
  )
}

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { Megaphone, X } from 'lucide-react'

export default function AnnouncementBanner() {
  const [announcement, setAnnouncement] = useState(null)
  const [visible, setVisible] = useState(true)

  useEffect(() => {
    const fetchAnnouncement = async () => {
      const { data } = await supabase
        .from('announcements')
        .select('*')
        .eq('active', true)
        .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
        .limit(1)
      
      if (data && data.length > 0) setAnnouncement(data[0])
    }
    fetchAnnouncement()
  }, [])

  if (!announcement || !visible) return null

  const colors = {
    info: 'bg-blue-600',
    warning: 'bg-orange-500',
    success: 'bg-green-600'
  }

  return (
    <div className={`${colors[announcement.type] || colors.info} text-white px-4 py-3 relative shadow-md`}>
      <div className="max-w-7xl mx-auto flex items-start md:items-center gap-3 pr-8">
        <Megaphone size={20} className="flex-shrink-0 mt-0.5 md:mt-0" />
        <div>
          <span className="font-bold mr-2">{announcement.title}:</span>
          <span className="text-sm md:text-base opacity-90">{announcement.message}</span>
        </div>
      </div>
      <button 
        onClick={() => setVisible(false)} 
        className="absolute right-2 top-2 p-1 hover:bg-white/20 rounded-full transition"
      >
        <X size={16} />
      </button>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/ui/CalendarView.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import Link from 'next/link'

export default function CalendarView({ events }) {
  const days = ['Sun', 'Mon', 'Tue', 'Wed', 'Thu', 'Fri', 'Sat']
  const today = new Date()
  const currentMonth = today.getMonth()
  const currentYear = today.getFullYear()

  // Simple calendar logic
  const firstDay = new Date(currentYear, currentMonth, 1).getDay()
  const daysInMonth = new Date(currentYear, currentMonth + 1, 0).getDate()
  
  const emptyDays = Array(firstDay).fill(null)
  const monthDays = Array.from({ length: daysInMonth }, (_, i) => i + 1)

  const getEventsForDay = (day) => {
    return events.filter(e => {
      const d = new Date(e.event_date)
      return d.getDate() === day && d.getMonth() === currentMonth && d.getFullYear() === currentYear
    })
  }

  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-3xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 overflow-hidden">
      <div className="p-6 bg-gray-50 border-b border-gray-100 flex justify-between items-center">
        <h2 className="font-bold text-xl text-brand-dark">{today.toLocaleString('default', { month: 'long', year: 'numeric' })}</h2>
      </div>
      
      <div className="grid grid-cols-7 border-b border-gray-100">
        {days.map(d => (
          <div key={d} className="p-4 text-center text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase tracking-wider">{d}</div>
        ))}
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-7 bg-gray-50/30">
        {emptyDays.map((_, i) => <div key={`empty-${i}`} className="min-h-[120px] border-b border-r border-gray-100 bg-gray-50/50"></div>)}
        
        {monthDays.map(day => {
          const dayEvents = getEventsForDay(day)
          const isToday = day === today.getDate()
          
          return (
            <div key={day} className={`min-h-[120px] p-2 border-b border-r border-gray-100 relative group transition hover:bg-white ${isToday ? 'bg-purple-50/30' : ''}`}>
              <span className={`text-sm font-bold mb-2 block w-7 h-7 flex items-center justify-center rounded-full ${isToday ? 'bg-brand-purple text-white' : 'text-gray-500'}`}>{day}</span>
              
              <div className="space-y-1">
                {dayEvents.map(ev => {
                  const seatsLeft = (ev.total_seats || 99) - (ev.booked_seats || 0)
                  const isFull = seatsLeft <= 0
                  
                  return (
                    <Link href={`/events/${ev.id}`} key={ev.id} className={`block text-[10px] p-1.5 rounded border font-bold truncate transition ${isFull ? 'bg-gray-200 text-gray-500 border-gray-300' : 'bg-brand-blue/10 text-brand-blue border-brand-blue/20 hover:bg-brand-blue hover:text-white'}`}>
                      {new Date(ev.event_date).toLocaleTimeString([], {hour:'2-digit', minute:'2-digit'})} {ev.title}
                      {!isFull && <span className="block font-normal text-[9px] opacity-80">{seatsLeft} spots left</span>}
                      {isFull && <span className="block font-normal text-[9px] text-red-500">FULL</span>}
                    </Link>
                  )
                })}
              </div>
            </div>
          )
        })}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/ui/ChatWindow.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect, useRef } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { Send, Loader2, User } from 'lucide-react'

export default function ChatWindow({ partnerId, partnerName, onClose }) {
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const [messages, setMessages] = useState([])
  const [newMessage, setNewMessage] = useState('')
  const [sending, setSending] = useState(false)
  const scrollRef = useRef()

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user || !partnerId) return

    // 1. Fetch initial history
    const fetchHistory = async () => {
      const { data } = await supabase.from('direct_messages')
        .select('*')
        .or(`and(sender_id.eq.${user.id},receiver_id.eq.${partnerId}),and(sender_id.eq.${partnerId},receiver_id.eq.${user.id})`)
        .order('created_at', { ascending: true })
      
      setMessages(data || [])
      scrollToBottom()
    }
    fetchHistory()

    // 2. Subscribe to new messages
    const channel = supabase.channel(`chat:${user.id}-${partnerId}`)
      .on('postgres_changes', { event: 'INSERT', schema: 'public', table: 'direct_messages' }, 
      (payload) => {
        const msg = payload.new
        if ((msg.sender_id === partnerId && msg.receiver_id === user.id) || 
            (msg.sender_id === user.id && msg.receiver_id === partnerId)) {
          setMessages(prev => [...prev, msg])
          scrollToBottom()
        }
      })
      .subscribe()

    return () => supabase.removeChannel(channel)
  }, [user, partnerId])

  const scrollToBottom = () => {
    setTimeout(() => scrollRef.current?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: "smooth" }), 100)
  }

  const handleSend = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (!newMessage.trim()) return
    setSending(true)
    
    await supabase.from('direct_messages').insert([{
      sender_id: user.id,
      receiver_id: partnerId,
      content: newMessage
    }])
    
    setNewMessage('')
    setSending(false)
  }

  return (
    <div className="fixed bottom-4 right-4 w-96 h-[500px] bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-2xl border border-gray-200 flex flex-col z-50 overflow-hidden animate-slide-up">
      {/* Header */}
      <div className="bg-brand-dark text-white p-4 flex justify-between items-center cursor-pointer" onClick={onClose}>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="w-8 h-8 bg-white/20 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center"><User size={16}/></div>
          <span className="font-bold">{partnerName || 'Chat'}</span>
        </div>
        <button className="text-white/50 hover:text-white">✕</button>
      </div>

      {/* Messages */}
      <div className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-4 bg-gray-50 space-y-3">
        {messages.map(msg => {
          const isMe = msg.sender_id === user.id
          return (
            <div key={msg.id} className={`flex ${isMe ? 'justify-end' : 'justify-start'}`}>
              <div className={`max-w-[80%] p-3 rounded-2xl text-sm ${isMe ? 'bg-brand-purple text-white rounded-br-none' : 'bg-white text-gray-700 border border-gray-100 rounded-bl-none shadow-sm'}`}>
                {msg.content}
              </div>
            </div>
          )
        })}
        <div ref={scrollRef}></div>
      </div>

      {/* Input */}
      <form onSubmit={handleSend} className="p-3 bg-white border-t border-gray-100 flex gap-2">
        <input 
          className="flex-1 bg-gray-100 rounded-full px-4 py-2 text-sm outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-purple"
          placeholder="Type a message..."
          value={newMessage}
          onChange={e => setNewMessage(e.target.value)}
        />
        <button disabled={sending} className="bg-brand-purple text-white p-2 rounded-full hover:bg-brand-dark transition">
          {sending ? <Loader2 size={18} className="animate-spin"/> : <Send size={18}/>}
        </button>
      </form>
    </div>
  )
}

PATH: ./src/components/ui/CookieConsent.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { useLanguage } from '@/context/LanguageContext'
import { Cookie } from 'lucide-react'

export default function CookieConsent() {
  const [show, setShow] = useState(false)
  const { t } = useLanguage()

  useEffect(() => {
    const consent = localStorage.getItem('cookie_consent')
    if (!consent) setTimeout(() => setShow(true), 1000)
  }, [])

  const accept = () => {
    localStorage.setItem('cookie_consent', 'true')
    setShow(false)
  }

  if (!show) return null

  return (
    <div className="fixed bottom-6 left-6 right-6 md:left-auto md:right-6 md:w-96 bg-white p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-2xl border border-gray-100 z-[60] animate-slide-up">
      <div className="flex items-start gap-4">
        <div className="bg-orange-100 p-2 rounded-full text-orange-500 shrink-0"><Cookie size={24}/></div>
        <div>
          <h4 className="font-bold text-gray-800 mb-1">Cookies 🍪</h4>
          <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 leading-relaxed mb-4">
            We use cookies to improve your learning experience and analyze traffic. By continuing, you agree to our use of cookies.
          </p>
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <button onClick={accept} className="flex-1 bg-brand-dark text-white py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold hover:bg-gray-800 transition">
              Accept All
            </button>
            <button onClick={accept} className="flex-1 bg-gray-100 text-gray-600 py-2 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold hover:bg-gray-200 transition">
              Essential Only
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/ui/CouponInput.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { Tag, Loader2, Check } from 'lucide-react'

export default function CouponInput({ onApply }) {
  const [code, setCode] = useState('')
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const [status, setStatus] = useState(null) // null, 'valid', 'invalid'

  const handleApply = async () => {
    if (!code) return
    setLoading(true)
    setStatus(null)
    
    try {
      const { data, error } = await supabase.from('coupons').select('*').eq('code', code.toUpperCase()).eq('active', true).single()
      
      if (error || !data) {
        setStatus('invalid')
      } else {
        setStatus('valid')
        onApply(data.discount_percent)
      }
    } catch (e) {
      setStatus('invalid')
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="bg-gray-50 p-4 rounded-xl border border-gray-200">
      <label className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-500 uppercase mb-2 block">Have a coupon?</label>
      <div className="flex gap-2">
        <div className="relative flex-1">
          <Tag className="absolute left-3 top-2.5 text-gray-400" size={16} />
          <input 
            type="text" 
            placeholder="Enter code" 
            className="w-full pl-9 pr-3 py-2 rounded-lg border border-gray-200 text-sm outline-none uppercase font-bold text-gray-700 focus:border-brand-purple"
            value={code}
            onChange={(e) => setCode(e.target.value)}
            disabled={status === 'valid'}
          />
        </div>
        <button 
          onClick={handleApply}
          disabled={loading || status === 'valid'}
          className={`px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold transition ${status === 'valid' ? 'bg-green-100 text-green-700' : 'bg-brand-dark text-white hover:bg-gray-800'}`}
        >
          {loading ? <Loader2 size={16} className="animate-spin"/> : (status === 'valid' ? <Check size={16}/> : 'Apply')}
        </button>
      </div>
      {status === 'invalid' && <p className="text-xs text-red-500 mt-2 font-bold">Invalid or expired code.</p>}
      {status === 'valid' && <p className="text-xs text-green-600 mt-2 font-bold">Coupon applied successfully!</p>}
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/ui/GlobalSearch.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { Search, X, Loader2, BookOpen, User, FileText } from 'lucide-react'
import Link from 'next/link'

export default function GlobalSearch({ onClose }) {
  const [query, setQuery] = useState('')
  const [results, setResults] = useState({ courses: [], tutors: [], posts: [] })
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    const delayDebounce = setTimeout(() => {
      if (query.length > 2) performSearch()
    }, 500)
    return () => clearTimeout(delayDebounce)
  }, [query])

  const performSearch = async () => {
    setLoading(true)
    const { data: c } = await supabase.from('products').select('*').eq('type', 'course').ilike('title', `%${query}%`).limit(3)
    const { data: t } = await supabase.from('tutors').select('*').ilike('name', `%${query}%`).limit(3)
    const { data: p } = await supabase.from('posts').select('*').ilike('title', `%${query}%`).limit(3)
    
    setResults({ courses: c || [], tutors: t || [], posts: p || [] })
    setLoading(false)
  }

  const hasResults = results.courses.length > 0 || results.tutors.length > 0 || results.posts.length > 0

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-brand-dark/80 backdrop-blur-sm z-[100] flex items-start justify-center pt-20 animate-fade-in p-4">
      <div className="bg-white w-full max-w-2xl rounded-2xl shadow-2xl overflow-hidden relative">
        <div className="p-4 border-b border-gray-100 flex items-center gap-3">
          <Search className="text-gray-400" size={24}/>
          <input 
            autoFocus
            className="flex-1 text-lg outline-none placeholder-gray-400"
            placeholder="Search for courses, tutors, or articles..."
            value={query}
            onChange={e => setQuery(e.target.value)}
          />
          <button onClick={onClose} className="p-2 bg-gray-100 rounded-full hover:bg-gray-200"><X size={16}/></button>
        </div>

        <div className="max-h-[60vh] overflow-y-auto bg-gray-50/50 p-4">
          {loading ? (
            <div className="flex justify-center py-8"><Loader2 className="animate-spin text-brand-purple"/></div>
          ) : query.length < 3 ? (
            <p className="text-center text-gray-400 py-8 text-sm">Type at least 3 characters to search.</p>
          ) : !hasResults ? (
            <p className="text-center text-gray-400 py-8 text-sm">No results found for "{query}".</p>
          ) : (
            <div className="space-y-6">
              {results.courses.length > 0 && (
                <div>
                  <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase mb-2 ml-2">Courses</h4>
                  <div className="space-y-2">
                    {results.courses.map(item => (
                      <Link onClick={onClose} href={`/corsi/${item.id}`} key={item.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 bg-white rounded-xl hover:bg-purple-50 transition border border-gray-100 hover:border-purple-100 group">
                        <div className="bg-purple-100 p-2 rounded-lg text-brand-purple group-hover:bg-purple-200"><BookOpen size={18}/></div>
                        <span className="font-bold text-gray-700">{item.title}</span>
                      </Link>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {results.tutors.length > 0 && (
                <div>
                  <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase mb-2 ml-2">Tutors</h4>
                  <div className="space-y-2">
                    {results.tutors.map(item => (
                      <Link onClick={onClose} href={`/tutors/${item.id}`} key={item.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 bg-white rounded-xl hover:bg-blue-50 transition border border-gray-100 hover:border-blue-100 group">
                        <img src={item.image_url} className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full object-cover"/>
                        <span className="font-bold text-gray-700">{item.name}</span>
                      </Link>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              {results.posts.length > 0 && (
                <div>
                  <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-gray-400 uppercase mb-2 ml-2">Blog Posts</h4>
                  <div className="space-y-2">
                    {results.posts.map(item => (
                      <Link onClick={onClose} href={`/blog/${item.slug}`} key={item.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 p-3 bg-white rounded-xl hover:bg-orange-50 transition border border-gray-100 hover:border-orange-100 group">
                        <div className="bg-orange-100 p-2 rounded-lg text-orange-600 group-hover:bg-orange-200"><FileText size={18}/></div>
                        <span className="font-bold text-gray-700">{item.title}</span>
                      </Link>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
        
        <div className="p-3 bg-gray-100 text-center text-xs text-gray-500 border-t border-gray-200">
          Press <kbd className="bg-white px-1 py-0.5 rounded border border-gray-300 font-mono">Esc</kbd> to close
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { useLanguage } from '@/context/LanguageContext'

export default function LegalNotice() {
  const [show, setShow] = useState(false)
  const { t } = useLanguage()

  useEffect(() => {
    const consent = localStorage.getItem('cookie_consent')
    if (!consent) setShow(true)
  }, [])

  const accept = () => {
    localStorage.setItem('cookie_consent', 'true')
    setShow(false)
  }

  if (!show) return null

  return (
    <div className="fixed bottom-0 left-0 w-full bg-white border-t border-gray-200 p-4 shadow-[0_-4px_6px_-1px_rgba(0,0,0,0.1)] z-50 animate-slide-in">
      <div className="max-w-6xl mx-auto flex flex-col md:flex-row items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <p className="text-sm text-gray-600 text-center md:text-left">
          🍪 {t('legal_cookie')} 
          <a href="/privacy" className="underline ml-1 text-brand-purple">{t('footer_privacy')}</a>.
        </p>
        <button onClick={accept} className="bg-brand-dark text-white px-6 py-2 rounded-full font-bold text-sm hover:bg-gray-800 transition whitespace-nowrap">
          {t('legal_accept')}
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

PATH: ./src/components/ui/MarkdownToolbar.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { Bold, Italic, Link, List, Heading, Quote } from 'lucide-react'

export default function MarkdownToolbar({ textareaRef, value, setValue }) {
  const insertText = (before, after = '') => {
    const el = textareaRef.current
    const start = el.selectionStart
    const end = el.selectionEnd
    const text = value
    const newText = text.substring(0, start) + before + text.substring(start, end) + after + text.substring(end)
    setValue(newText)
    el.focus()
    setTimeout(() => { el.setSelectionRange(start + before.length, end + before.length) }, 0)
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex gap-1 p-2 bg-gray-100 border-b border-gray-200 rounded-t-xl">
      <button type="button" onClick={() => insertText('**', '**')} className="p-2 hover:bg-gray-200 rounded text-gray-600" title="Bold"><Bold size={16}/></button>
      <button type="button" onClick={() => insertText('*', '*')} className="p-2 hover:bg-gray-200 rounded text-gray-600" title="Italic"><Italic size={16}/></button>
      <button type="button" onClick={() => insertText('# ')} className="p-2 hover:bg-gray-200 rounded text-gray-600" title="Heading"><Heading size={16}/></button>
      <button type="button" onClick={() => insertText('[', '](url)')} className="p-2 hover:bg-gray-200 rounded text-gray-600" title="Link"><Link size={16}/></button>
      <button type="button" onClick={() => insertText('- ')} className="p-2 hover:bg-gray-200 rounded text-gray-600" title="List"><List size={16}/></button>
      <button type="button" onClick={() => insertText('> ')} className="p-2 hover:bg-gray-200 rounded text-gray-600" title="Quote"><Quote size={16}/></button>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/ui/NotificationBell.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { Bell } from 'lucide-react'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'

export default function NotificationBell() {
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const [notifications, setNotifications] = useState([])
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  const unreadCount = notifications.filter(n => !n.is_read).length

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!user) return
    
    // Initial fetch
    const fetchNotifs = async () => {
      const { data } = await supabase.from('notifications').select('*').eq('user_id', user.id).order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(10)
      setNotifications(data || [])
    }
    fetchNotifs()

    // Real-time subscription
    const channel = supabase
      .channel('notifs')
      .on('postgres_changes', { event: 'INSERT', schema: 'public', table: 'notifications', filter: `user_id=eq.${user.id}` }, 
      (payload) => {
        setNotifications(prev => [payload.new, ...prev])
      })
      .subscribe()

    return () => { supabase.removeChannel(channel) }
  }, [user])

  const markRead = async () => {
    if (unreadCount > 0) {
      await supabase.from('notifications').update({ is_read: true }).eq('user_id', user.id).eq('is_read', false)
      setNotifications(prev => prev.map(n => ({ ...n, is_read: true })))
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="relative">
      <button 
        onClick={() => { setOpen(!open); if(!open) markRead(); }}
        className="relative p-2 text-gray-500 hover:text-brand-purple transition"
      >
        <Bell size={22} />
        {unreadCount > 0 && <span className="absolute top-1 right-1 w-2.5 h-2.5 bg-red-500 rounded-full border-2 border-white"></span>}
      </button>

      {open && (
        <>
          <div className="fixed inset-0 z-40" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}></div>
          <div className="absolute right-0 mt-2 w-80 bg-white rounded-2xl shadow-xl border border-gray-100 z-50 overflow-hidden animate-slide-up origin-top-right">
            <div className="p-4 border-b border-gray-50 font-bold text-gray-700 bg-gray-50/50">Notifications</div>
            <div className="max-h-[300px] overflow-y-auto">
              {notifications.length === 0 ? (
                <div className="p-8 text-center text-gray-400 text-sm">No new notifications.</div>
              ) : (
                notifications.map(n => (
                  <div key={n.id} className={`p-4 border-b border-gray-50 hover:bg-gray-50 transition ${!n.is_read ? 'bg-blue-50/30' : ''}`}>
                    <p className="font-bold text-sm text-gray-800">{n.title}</p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 mt-1">{n.message}</p>
                    <p className="text-[10px] text-gray-400 mt-2">{new Date(n.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}</p>
                  </div>
                ))
              )}
            </div>
          </div>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

"use client";
import { useState } from 'react'
import { AlertTriangle, X, Loader2, Send } from 'lucide-react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useLanguage } from '@/context/LanguageContext'

export default function ReportModal({ isOpen, onClose, contextData }) {
  const { t } = useLanguage()
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('')
  const [sent, setSent] = useState(false)

  if (!isOpen) return null

  const handleSubmit = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setLoading(true)
    
    try {
      await supabase.from('contact_messages').insert([{
        name: 'User Report',
        email: 'system@report.com',
        message: `REPORT [${contextData}]: ${message}`
      }])
      setLoading(false)
      setSent(true)
      setTimeout(() => { setSent(false); setMessage(''); onClose() }, 2000)
    } catch (error) {
      setLoading(false)
      alert("Error sending report")
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="fixed inset-0 bg-black/50 z-[60] flex items-center justify-center p-4">
      <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl p-6 max-w-sm w-full relative">
        <button onClick={onClose} className="absolute top-4 right-4 text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600"><X size={20}/></button>
        
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-4 text-orange-600">
          <AlertTriangle />
          <h3 className="font-bold text-lg">{t('report_title')}</h3>
        </div>

        {sent ? (
          <div className="text-center py-8 text-green-600 font-bold">{t('report_sent')}</div>
        ) : (
          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
            <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mb-4">{t('report_desc')}</p>
            <textarea 
              required
              className="w-full p-3 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-orange-500 text-sm mb-4"
              rows="4"
              placeholder={t('report_placeholder')}
              value={message}
              onChange={e => setMessage(e.target.value)}
            ></textarea>
            <button disabled={loading} className="w-full bg-orange-500 text-white font-bold py-2 rounded-xl hover:bg-orange-600 transition flex justify-center items-center gap-2">
              {loading ? <Loader2 className="animate-spin" size={18}/> : <><Send size={18}/> {t('report_btn')}</>}
            </button>
          </form>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/components/ui/SaleCountdown.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { Clock } from 'lucide-react'

export default function SaleCountdown({ endDate }) {
  const [timeLeft, setTimeLeft] = useState(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!endDate) return
    
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      const now = new Date().getTime()
      const distance = new Date(endDate).getTime() - now
      
      if (distance < 0) {
        clearInterval(interval)
        setTimeLeft(null)
      } else {
        const days = Math.floor(distance / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24))
        const hours = Math.floor((distance % (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24)) / (1000 * 60 * 60))
        const minutes = Math.floor((distance % (1000 * 60 * 60)) / (1000 * 60))
        
        setTimeLeft(`${days}d ${hours}h ${minutes}m`)
      }
    }, 1000)

    return () => clearInterval(interval)
  }, [endDate])

  if (!timeLeft) return null

  return (
    <div className="absolute top-3 left-3 bg-red-600 text-white px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold shadow-md flex items-center gap-1 animate-pulse">
      <Clock size={12} /> Ends in {timeLeft}
    </div>
  )
}

PATH: ./src/components/ui/ShareButton.jsx

"use client";
import { Share2, Check } from 'lucide-react'
import { useState } from 'react'

export default function ShareButton({ title, text, url }) {
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(false)

  const handleShare = async () => {
    const shareData = {
      title: title || 'Annyeong Academy',
      text: text || 'Check this out!',
      url: url || window.location.href
    }

    if (navigator.share) {
      try { await navigator.share(shareData) } catch (e) {}
    } else {
      navigator.clipboard.writeText(shareData.url)
      setCopied(true)
      setTimeout(() => setCopied(false), 2000)
    }
  }

  return (
    <button onClick={handleShare} className="p-2 rounded-full bg-white text-gray-400 hover:text-brand-purple transition shadow-sm hover:shadow-md border border-gray-100">
      {copied ? <Check size={18}/> : <Share2 size={18}/>}
    </button>
  )
}

PATH: ./src/components/ui/WishlistButton.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { Heart, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { useToast } from '@/context/ToastContext'

export default function WishlistButton({ productId }) {
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const { addToast } = useToast()
  const [isWishlisted, setIsWishlisted] = useState(false)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (user && productId) checkStatus()
  }, [user, productId])

  const checkStatus = async () => {
    const { data } = await supabase.from('wishlist').select('product_id').eq('user_id', user.id).eq('product_id', productId).single()
    if (data) setIsWishlisted(true)
  }

  const toggleWishlist = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    e.stopPropagation()
    if (!user) return addToast("Please login to save items", "info")
    
    setLoading(true)
    if (isWishlisted) {
      await supabase.from('wishlist').delete().eq('user_id', user.id).eq('product_id', productId)
      setIsWishlisted(false)
      addToast("Removed from wishlist", "info")
    } else {
      await supabase.from('wishlist').insert([{ user_id: user.id, product_id: productId }])
      setIsWishlisted(true)
      addToast("Added to wishlist!", "success")
    }
    setLoading(false)
  }

  return (
    <button 
      onClick={toggleWishlist}
      disabled={loading}
      className={`p-2 rounded-full transition shadow-sm ${isWishlisted ? 'bg-red-50 text-red-500' : 'bg-white text-gray-400 hover:text-red-500'}`}
    >
      {loading ? <Loader2 size={18} className="animate-spin"/> : <Heart size={18} fill={isWishlisted ? "currentColor" : "none"} />}
    </button>
  )
}

================================================================================
PATH: ./src/context/AuthContext.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";

import { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { createBrowserClient } from '@supabase/ssr'
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation'

const AuthContext = createContext()

export function AuthProvider({ children }) {
  const [user, setUser] = useState(null)
  const [profile, setProfile] = useState(null)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const router = useRouter()
  
  const supabase = createBrowserClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL,
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY
  )

  const fetchProfile = async (userId) => {
    try {
      const { data, error } = await supabase
        .from('profiles')
        .select('*')
        .eq('id', userId)
        .single()
      
      if (!error) setProfile(data)
    } catch (error) {
      console.error('Profile fetch error:', error)
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    const initializeAuth = async () => {
      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession()
      
      if (session?.user) {
        setUser(session.user)
        await fetchProfile(session.user.id)
      }
      setLoading(false)

      const { data: { subscription } } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange(async (event, session) => {
        if (session?.user) {
          setUser(session.user)
          await fetchProfile(session.user.id)
          router.refresh()
        } else {
          setUser(null)
          setProfile(null)
          router.refresh()
        }
      })

      return () => subscription.unsubscribe()
    }

    initializeAuth()
  }, [])

  const value = {
    signUp: (data) => supabase.auth.signUp(data),
    signIn: (data) => supabase.auth.signInWithPassword(data),
    signOut: async () => {
      await supabase.auth.signOut()
      router.push('/login')
    },
    refreshProfile: () => user && fetchProfile(user.id),
    user,
    profile,
    isAdmin: profile?.role === 'admin',
    loading
  }

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider value={value}>
      {!loading && children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  )
}

export const useAuth = () => useContext(AuthContext)


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/context/CartContext.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { createContext, useContext, useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { useToast } from './ToastContext'

const CartContext = createContext()

export function CartProvider({ children }) {
  const [cart, setCart] = useState([])
  const { addToast } = useToast()

  // Load cart from localStorage on mount
  useEffect(() => {
    const savedCart = localStorage.getItem('annyeong_cart')
    if (savedCart) {
      try { setCart(JSON.parse(savedCart)) } catch (e) { console.error(e) }
    }
  }, [])

  // Save cart to localStorage on change
  useEffect(() => {
    localStorage.setItem('annyeong_cart', JSON.stringify(cart))
  }, [cart])

  const addToCart = (product) => {
    setCart((prev) => {
      const exists = prev.find((item) => item.id === product.id)
      if (exists) {
        addToast(`${product.title} is already in cart`, 'info')
        return prev
      }
      addToast(`${product.title} added to cart!`, 'success')
      return [...prev, product]
    })
  }

  const removeFromCart = (productId) => {
    setCart((prev) => prev.filter((item) => item.id !== productId))
  }

  const clearCart = () => setCart([])

  const cartTotal = cart.reduce((total, item) => {
    // Parse price strings like "15.000 Ft" or numbers
    const price = typeof item.price === 'string' 
      ? parseFloat(item.price.replace(/[^0-9.]/g, '')) 
      : item.price
    return total + (isNaN(price) ? 0 : price)
  }, 0)

  return (
    <CartContext.Provider value={{ cart, addToCart, removeFromCart, clearCart, cartTotal }}>
      {children}
    </CartContext.Provider>
  )
}

export const useCart = () => useContext(CartContext)


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/context/LanguageContext.jsx

export function LanguageProvider({ children }) {
  const [lang, setLang] = useState('en')

  useEffect(() => {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem('app_lang')
    if (saved) setLang(saved)
  }, [])

  const toggleLanguage = () => {
    const newLang = lang === 'en' ? 'hu' : 'en'
    setLang(newLang)
    localStorage.setItem('app_lang', newLang)
  }

  const t = (key) => {
    return translations[lang][key] || key
  }

  return (
    <LanguageContext.Provider value={{ lang, toggleLanguage, t }}>
      {children}
    </LanguageContext.Provider>
  )
}

export const useLanguage = () => useContext(LanguageContext)


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/context/PaymentContext.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

import { createContext, useContext, useState } from 'react'
import PaymentModal from '@/components/payment/PaymentModal'
import { useAuth } from './AuthContext'
import { useToast } from './ToastContext'

const PaymentContext = createContext()

export function PaymentProvider({ children }) {
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const { addToast } = useToast()
  const [modalOpen, setModalOpen] = useState(false)
  const [selectedItem, setSelectedItem] = useState(null)

  const initiateCheckout = (item) => {
    if (!user) {
      addToast("Please login to purchase", "error")
      return
    }
    setSelectedItem(item)
    setModalOpen(true)
  }

  return (
    <PaymentContext.Provider value={{ initiateCheckout }}>
      {children}
      <PaymentModal 
        isOpen={modalOpen} 
        onClose={() => setModalOpen(false)} 
        item={selectedItem} 
      />
    </PaymentContext.Provider>
  )
}

export const usePayment = () => useContext(PaymentContext)


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/context/ToastContext.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

import { createContext, useContext, useState, useCallback } from 'react'
import { X, CheckCircle, AlertCircle, Info } from 'lucide-react'

const ToastContext = createContext()

export function ToastProvider({ children }) {
  const [toasts, setToasts] = useState([])

  const addToast = useCallback((message, type = 'info') => {
    const id = Date.now()
    setToasts(prev => [...prev, { id, message, type }])
    setTimeout(() => removeToast(id), 4000)
  }, [])

  const removeToast = useCallback((id) => {
    setToasts(prev => prev.filter(t => t.id !== id))
  }, [])

  return (
    <ToastContext.Provider value={{ addToast }}>
      {children}
      <div className="fixed bottom-4 right-4 z-50 flex flex-col gap-2 pointer-events-none">
        {toasts.map(toast => (
          <div 
            key={toast.id}
            className={`pointer-events-auto flex items-center gap-3 px-4 py-3 rounded-xl shadow-lg border transition-all animate-slide-in min-w-[300px] ${
              toast.type === 'success' ? 'bg-white border-green-100 text-green-800' :
              toast.type === 'error' ? 'bg-white border-red-100 text-red-800' :
              'bg-white border-blue-100 text-brand-dark'
            }`}
          >
            {toast.type === 'success' && <CheckCircle size={20} className="text-green-500" />}
            {toast.type === 'error' && <AlertCircle size={20} className="text-red-500" />}
            {toast.type === 'info' && <Info size={20} className="text-brand-blue" />}
            
            <p className="text-sm font-bold flex-1">{toast.message}</p>
            
            <button onClick={() => removeToast(toast.id)} className="text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600">
              <X size={16} />
            </button>
          </div>
        ))}
      </div>
    </ToastContext.Provider>
  )
}

export const useToast = () => useContext(ToastContext)


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/hooks/useAudioRecorder.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useRef } from "react";

export function useAudioRecorder() {
  const [isRecording, setIsRecording] = useState(false);
  const [audioBlob, setAudioBlob] = useState(null);
  const [permission, setPermission] = useState(false);
  const mediaRecorderRef = useRef(null);
  const chunksRef = useRef([]);

  const getPermission = async () => {
    if ("MediaRecorder" in window) {
      try {
        const streamData = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({
          audio: true,
          video: false,
        });
        setPermission(true);
        return streamData;
      } catch (err) {
        alert("Microphone permission needed to record answers.");
        return null;
      }
    } else {
      alert("The MediaRecorder API is not supported in your browser.");
      return null;
    }
  };

  const startRecording = async () => {
    const stream = await getPermission();
    if (!stream) return;

    setIsRecording(true);
    const mediaRecorder = new MediaRecorder(stream);
    mediaRecorderRef.current = mediaRecorder;
    chunksRef.current = [];

    mediaRecorder.ondataavailable = (event) => {
      if (event.data.size > 0) {
        chunksRef.current.push(event.data);
      }
    };

    mediaRecorder.onstop = () => {
      const blob = new Blob(chunksRef.current, { type: "audio/webm" });
      setAudioBlob(blob);
      
      // Stop all tracks to release microphone
      stream.getTracks().forEach((track) => track.stop());
    };

    mediaRecorder.start();
  };

  const stopRecording = () => {
    setIsRecording(false);
    if (mediaRecorderRef.current && mediaRecorderRef.current.state !== "inactive") {
      mediaRecorderRef.current.stop();
    }
  };

  return { isRecording, audioBlob, startRecording, stopRecording, permission };
}

PATH: ./src/hooks/useEdgeFunction.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'

export function useEdgeFunction() {
  const invoke = async (functionName, body) => {
    try {
      const { data: { session } } = await supabase.auth.getSession()
      if (!session) throw new Error('Not authenticated')

      // Try calling the real Edge Function
      const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke(functionName, {
        body,
        headers: {
          Authorization: `Bearer ${session.access_token}`
        }
      })

      if (error) {
        console.warn(`Edge Function '${functionName}' failed/missing. Using local fallback logic.`, error)
        return { data: null, error, fallback: true }
      }

      return { data, error: null, fallback: false }
    } catch (err) {
      console.error(err)
      return { data: null, error: err, fallback: true }
    }
  }

  return { invoke }
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/hooks/useGamification.jsx
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'

export function useGamification(userId) {
  const [stats, setStats] = useState({ xp: 0, streak: 0, level: 1 })
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!userId) {
      setLoading(false)
      return
    }
    fetchStats()
    
    // Subscribe to realtime changes
    const channel = supabase
      .channel('gamification')
      .on('postgres_changes', { event: 'UPDATE', schema: 'public', table: 'user_stats', filter: `user_id=eq.${userId}` }, 
      (payload) => {
        setStats(prev => ({ ...prev, ...payload.new }))
      })
      .subscribe()

    return () => { supabase.removeChannel(channel) }
  }, [userId])

  const fetchStats = async () => {
    try {
      let { data, error } = await supabase.from('user_stats').select('*').eq('user_id', userId).single()
      
      if (error && error.code === 'PGRST116') {
        // User doesn't exist in stats yet, create default
        const { data: newData } = await supabase.from('user_stats').insert([{ user_id: userId, xp: 0, streak: 1, level: 1 }]).select().single()
        data = newData
      }
      
      if (data) setStats(data)
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Gamification error:", error)
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }

  const addXP = async (amount) => {
    try {
      // Optimistic update
      setStats(prev => ({ ...prev, xp: prev.xp + amount }))
      // Call database function
      await supabase.rpc('increment_xp', { x: amount })
      // Update streak if needed
      await supabase.rpc('update_streak')
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Failed to add XP:", error)
    }
  }

  return { ...stats, loading, addXP }
}

/**
 * Mock Email Service
 * In production, replace console.log with Resend or SendGrid API calls.
 */

export const sendEmail = async ({ to, subject, html }) => {
  console.log(`
  📧 SENDING EMAIL
  To: ${to}
  Subject: ${subject}
  ---------------------
  ${html}
  ---------------------
  `)
  return { success: true }
}

export const sendWelcomeEmail = async (email, name) => {
  return sendEmail({
    to: email,
    subject: "Welcome to Annyeong Academy! 🇰🇷",
    html: `<h1>Annyeong, ${name}!</h1><p>Thanks for joining us. Start your first lesson now.</p>`
  })
}

  return sendEmail({
    to: email,
    subject: `Receipt for Order #${orderId}`,
    html: `<h1>Payment Confirmed</h1><p>We received your payment of ${amount}. Access your content in the dashboard.</p>`
  })
}

PATH: ./src/lib/emailService.js

import { supabase } from './supabase'

/**
 * Attempts to send an email via Supabase Edge Function.
 * Fallbacks to console logging if function fails or is not configured.
 */
export const triggerEmail = async ({ to, subject, html, data }) => {
  try {
    // 1. Log the attempt in database (Audit Trail)
    // Note: Assuming 'admin_email_logs' is accessible, or just rely on console for dev.
    console.log(`[Email Service] Attempting to send to ${to}`)

    // 2. Call Edge Function (if configured)
    const { error } = await supabase.functions.invoke('send-email', {
      body: { to, subject, html, data }
    })

    if (error) throw error
    
    console.log("[Email Service] Success via Edge Function")
    return { success: true }

  } catch (err) {
    console.warn("[Email Service] Edge Function failed/missing. Falling back to Log.", err)
    
    // 3. Fallback: Log to 'contact_messages' as a system log so Admins see it in CRM
    await supabase.from('contact_messages').insert([{
      name: 'System Outbound',
      email: 'system@annyeong.com',
      message: `[MOCK EMAIL SENT]\nTo: ${to}\nSubject: ${subject}\n\n${html.replace(/<[^>]*>?/gm, '')}` // Strip HTML for text log
    }])
    
    return { success: true, mocked: true }
  }
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/lib/helpers.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

/**
 * Extracts a value from a custom tag in the description.
 * Format: [[TAG_NAME::Value]]
 */
export const extractTag = (text, tagName) => {
  if (!text) return null
  const regex = new RegExp(`\\[\\[${tagName}::(.*?)\\]\\]`)
  const match = text.match(regex)
  return match ? match[1] : null
}

/**
 * Removes all custom tags from the description for display purposes.
 */
export const cleanDescription = (text) => {
  if (!text) return ""
  return text.replace(/\[\[.*?\]\]/g, '').trim()
}

/**
 * Generates a random file path for Supabase storage
 */
export const generateFilePath = (folder, file) => {
  const fileExt = file.name.split('.').pop()
  const fileName = `${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 15)}_${Date.now()}.${fileExt}`
  return `${folder}/${fileName}`
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/lib/payment.js

import { supabase } from './supabase'

export const initiateCheckout = async (productId, productTitle) => {
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser()
  if (!user) return alert("You must log in to purchase.")

  const confirm = window.confirm(`STRIPE SIMULATION:\n\nDo you want to purchase '${productTitle}'?\n(Clicking OK will unlock the content)`)
  
  if (confirm) {
    const { error } = await supabase.from('purchases').insert([{ user_id: user.id, product_id: productId, status: 'completed' }])
    if (error) {
      console.error(error)
      alert("Error during simulated purchase.")
    } else {
      alert("Purchase successful! The page will refresh.")
      window.location.reload() 
    }
  }
}
