"use client";
import { createBrowserClient } from '@supabase/ssr'

export const supabase = createBrowserClient(
  process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL,
  process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY
)


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/lib/video.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

export const getEmbedUrl = (url) => {
  if (!url) return null;

  // YouTube (Standard)
  if (url.includes('youtube.com/watch?v=')) {
    return url.replace('watch?v=', 'embed/');
  }
  
  // YouTube (Short)
  if (url.includes('youtu.be/')) {
    return url.replace('youtu.be/', 'youtube.com/embed/');
  }
  
  // Vimeo
  if (url.includes('vimeo.com')) {
    const vimeoId = url.split('.com/')[1];
    if (vimeoId && !url.includes('player.vimeo.com')) {
      return `https://player.vimeo.com/video/${vimeoId}`;
    }
  }

  // Google Drive
  if (url.includes('drive.google.com')) {
    return url.replace('/view', '/preview');
  }

  return url;
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/middleware.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

import { createServerClient } from '@supabase/ssr'
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server'

export async function middleware(request) {
  let response = NextResponse.next({ request: { headers: request.headers } })

  const supabase = createServerClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL,
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY,
    {
      cookies: {
        getAll() { return request.cookies.getAll() },
        setAll(cookiesToSet) {
          cookiesToSet.forEach(({ name, value, options }) => request.cookies.set(name, value))
          response = NextResponse.next({ request: { headers: request.headers } })
          cookiesToSet.forEach(({ name, value, options }) => response.cookies.set(name, value, options))
        },
      },
    }
  )

  // 1. Check Maintenance Mode
  // Note: Fetching from DB in middleware can add latency. For production, consider Edge Config or KV.
  const { data: maintenance } = await supabase.from('system_settings').select('value').eq('key', 'maintenance_mode').single()
  
  const path = request.nextUrl.pathname
  
  // 2. Get User
  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser()
  let role = 'student'
  if (user) {
    const { data: profile } = await supabase.from('profiles').select('role').eq('id', user.id).single()
    role = profile?.role || 'student'
  }

  // 3. Enforce Maintenance Mode (Allow only Admin or Login/Auth pages)
  if (maintenance?.value?.enabled) {
    if (role !== 'admin' && !path.startsWith('/login') && !path.startsWith('/admin') && !path.startsWith('/_next')) {
      return new NextResponse(
        `<html><body style="display:flex;justify-content:center;align-items:center;height:100vh;font-family:sans-serif;text-align:center;background:#F8F9FC;">
           <div>
             <h1 style="color:#192335;margin-bottom:10px;">Maintenance Mode</h1>
             <p style="color:#666;">${maintenance.value.message}</p>
             <p style="font-size:12px;margin-top:20px;"><a href="/login" style="color:#6a11cb">Admin Login</a></p>
           </div>
         </body></html>`,
        { status: 503, headers: { 'content-type': 'text/html' } }
      )
    }
  }

  // 4. Protected Routes
  const protectedPrefixes = ['/dashboard', '/profile', '/corsi', '/store', '/downloads', '/lezioni-live', '/admin']
  const isProtected = protectedPrefixes.some(prefix => path.startsWith(prefix))

  if (isProtected && !user) {
    return NextResponse.redirect(new URL('/login', request.url))
  }

  if (user && (path === '/login' || path === '/register')) {
    return NextResponse.redirect(new URL('/dashboard', request.url))
  }
  
  // 5. Admin Route Guard
  if (path.startsWith('/admin') && role !== 'admin') {
    return NextResponse.redirect(new URL('/dashboard', request.url))
  }

  return response
}

export const config = {
  matcher: [
    '/((?!_next/static|_next/image|favicon.ico|.*\\.(?:svg|png|jpg|jpeg|gif|webp)$).*)',
  ],
}


================================================================================
PATH: ./src/utils/csvExport.js
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

export const downloadCSV = (data, filename) => {
  if (!data || !data.length) {
    alert("No data to export");
    return;
  }

  const separator = ',';
  const keys = Object.keys(data[0]);
  
  const csvContent = [
    keys.join(separator),
    ...data.map(row => keys.map(k => {
      let cell = row[k] === null || row[k] === undefined ? '' : row[k];
      cell = cell instanceof Date ? cell.toLocaleString() : cell.toString().replace(/"/g, '""');
      if (cell.search(/("|,|\n)/g) >= 0) cell = `"${cell}"`;
      return cell;
    }).join(separator))
  ].join('\n');

  const blob = new Blob([csvContent], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8;' });
  const link = document.createElement('a');
  if (link.download !== undefined) {
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    link.setAttribute('href', url);
    link.setAttribute('download', `${filename}.csv`);
    link.style.visibility = 'hidden';
    document.body.appendChild(link);
    link.click();
    document.body.removeChild(link);
  }
}

import jsPDF from 'jspdf'

export const generateInvoice = (order, userDetails) => {
  const doc = new jsPDF()
  
  // Header
  doc.setFontSize(22)
  doc.setTextColor(94, 56, 145) // Brand Purple
  doc.text("INVOICE", 20, 30)
  
  doc.setFontSize(10)
  doc.setTextColor(100)
  doc.text("Annyeong Academy Ltd.", 20, 40)
  doc.text("123 Seoul Street, Budapest", 20, 45)
  doc.text("info@annyeong-academy.com", 20, 50)

  // Details
  doc.setTextColor(0)
  doc.text(`Invoice ID: ${order.id.slice(0,8).toUpperCase()}`, 140, 30)
  doc.text(`Date: ${new Date(order.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}`, 140, 35)

  // Bill To
  doc.setFontSize(12)
  doc.text("Bill To:", 20, 70)
  doc.setFontSize(10)
  doc.text(userDetails.full_name || "Valued Customer", 20, 76)
  
  const billing = userDetails.billing_address || {}
  if(billing.address) {
    doc.text(billing.address, 20, 81)
    doc.text(`${billing.city}, ${billing.zip}`, 20, 86)
    doc.text(billing.country, 20, 91)
  }

  // Line Items
  doc.setLineWidth(0.5)
  doc.line(20, 100, 190, 100)
  
  doc.setFontSize(10)
  doc.text("Item", 20, 108)
  doc.text("Price", 170, 108)
  
  doc.line(20, 112, 190, 112)

  doc.setFontSize(12)
  doc.text(order.products?.title || "Product", 20, 125)
  doc.text(order.products?.price || "0", 170, 125)

  // Total
  doc.line(20, 140, 190, 140)
  doc.setFontSize(14)
  doc.text("Total:", 140, 150)
  doc.text(order.products?.price || "0", 170, 150)

  // Footer
  doc.setFontSize(8)
  doc.setTextColor(150)
  doc.text("Thank you for learning with us!", 20, 280)

  doc.save(`Invoice_${order.id.slice(0,8)}.pdf`)
}

PATH: ./supabase/functions/create-checkout/index.ts
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

import { serve } from "https://deno.land/std@0.168.0/http/server.ts"
import Stripe from "https://esm.sh/stripe@12.4.0"

const stripe = new Stripe(Deno.env.get('STRIPE_SECRET_KEY') ?? '', {
  apiVersion: '2022-11-15',
  httpClient: Stripe.createFetchHttpClient(),
})

serve(async (req) => {
  if (req.method === 'OPTIONS') {
    return new Response('ok', { headers: { 'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*' } })
  }

  try {
    const { items, userId } = await req.json()
    
    // Logic to create line items from DB based on IDs to prevent client-side price tampering
    // For now, mocking valid creation
    
    const session = await stripe.checkout.sessions.create({
      payment_method_types: ['card'],
      line_items: items.map((item: any) => ({
        price_data: {
          currency: 'huf',
          product_data: { name: item.title },
          unit_amount: parseInt(item.price.replace(/\D/g,'')) * 100, // naive parse
        },
        quantity: 1,
      })),
      mode: 'payment',
      success_url: `${req.headers.get('origin')}/dashboard?success=true`,
      cancel_url: `${req.headers.get('origin')}/cart?canceled=true`,
      metadata: { userId },
    })

    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ sessionId: session.id }), {
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    })
  } catch (error) {
    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: error.message }), { status: 400 })
  }
})

PATH: ./supabase/functions/send-email/index.ts
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

// Follow this guide to deploy: https://supabase.com/docs/guides/functions
import { serve } from "https://deno.land/std@0.168.0/http/server.ts"

const RESEND_API_KEY = Deno.env.get('RESEND_API_KEY')

serve(async (req) => {
  if (req.method === 'OPTIONS') {
    return new Response('ok', { headers: { 'Access-Control-Allow-Origin': '*' } })
  }

  try {
    const { record } = await req.json() // The new lead data
    
    // 1. Send Email via Resend (example)
    const res = await fetch('https://api.resend.com/emails', {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        'Authorization': `Bearer ${RESEND_API_KEY}`
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        from: 'Annyeong Academy <info@annyeong-academy.com>',
        to: record.email,
        subject: 'Your Korean Open Day Ticket! 🇰🇷',
        html: `
          <h1>Annyeong, ${record.full_name}!</h1>
          <p>You are registered for the webinar on <strong>${record.webinar_date}</strong>.</p>
          <p>Here is your Zoom Link: <a href="https://zoom.us/j/example">Join Here</a></p>
          <p>See you there!</p>
        `
      })
    })

    const data = await res.json()
    return new Response(JSON.stringify(data), { headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })
  } catch (error) {
    return new Response(JSON.stringify({ error: error.message }), { status: 500 })
  }
})

-- =============================================================================
-- 3. FUNCTIONS & TRIGGERS
-- =============================================================================

-- Setup Profile on Signup
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.handle_new_user() 
RETURNS trigger AS $$
BEGIN
  INSERT INTO public.profiles (id, full_name, role)
  VALUES (new.id, new.raw_user_meta_data->>'full_name', 'student');
  
  INSERT INTO public.user_stats (user_id, xp, streak, level)
  VALUES (new.id, 0, 1, 1);
  
  RETURN new;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER;

-- (Re)Create Trigger safely
DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS on_auth_user_created ON auth.users;
CREATE TRIGGER on_auth_user_created
  AFTER INSERT ON auth.users
  FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE PROCEDURE public.handle_new_user();

-- Increment XP
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION increment_xp(x int)
RETURNS void AS $$
BEGIN
  UPDATE public.user_stats 
  SET xp = xp + x, last_activity = CURRENT_DATE 
  WHERE user_id = auth.uid();
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER;

-- Update Streak
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION update_streak()
RETURNS void AS $$
DECLARE
  last_date date;
BEGIN
  SELECT last_activity INTO last_date FROM public.user_stats WHERE user_id = auth.uid();
  IF last_date = CURRENT_DATE - 1 THEN
    UPDATE public.user_stats SET streak = streak + 1, last_activity = CURRENT_DATE WHERE user_id = auth.uid();
  ELSIF last_date < CURRENT_DATE - 1 THEN
    UPDATE public.user_stats SET streak = 1, last_activity = CURRENT_DATE WHERE user_id = auth.uid();
  END IF;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER;

-- =============================================================================
-- 4. ROW LEVEL SECURITY (RLS) POLICIES
-- =============================================================================

ALTER TABLE profiles ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE tutors ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE products ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE lessons ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE purchases ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE subscriptions ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE lesson_progress ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE user_notes ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE quiz_results ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE homework_submissions ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE user_stats ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- GLOBAL READ POLICIES
CREATE POLICY "Public Read Tutors" ON tutors FOR SELECT USING (true);
CREATE POLICY "Public Read Products" ON products FOR SELECT USING (true);
CREATE POLICY "Public Read Lessons" ON lessons FOR SELECT USING (true);
CREATE POLICY "Public Read Posts" ON posts FOR SELECT USING (published = true);
CREATE POLICY "Public Read Reviews" ON reviews FOR SELECT USING (true);
CREATE POLICY "Public Read FAQs" ON faqs FOR SELECT USING (true);
CREATE POLICY "Public Read Announcements" ON announcements FOR SELECT USING (active = true);
CREATE POLICY "Public Read Profiles" ON profiles FOR SELECT USING (true);
CREATE POLICY "Public Read Placement Qs" ON placement_questions FOR SELECT USING (true);
CREATE POLICY "Public Read Content" ON content_blocks FOR SELECT USING (true);
CREATE POLICY "Public Read Settings" ON system_settings FOR SELECT USING (true);
CREATE POLICY "Public Read Coupons" ON coupons FOR SELECT USING (true);

-- USER SPECIFIC POLICIES
CREATE POLICY "Users Update Own Profile" ON profiles FOR UPDATE USING (auth.uid() = id);
CREATE POLICY "Users Read Own Stats" ON user_stats FOR SELECT USING (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE POLICY "Users Read Own Purchases" ON purchases FOR SELECT USING (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE POLICY "Users Read Own Subscriptions" ON subscriptions FOR SELECT USING (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE POLICY "Users Manage Progress" ON lesson_progress FOR ALL USING (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE POLICY "Users Manage Notes" ON user_notes FOR ALL USING (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE POLICY "Users Manage Wishlist" ON wishlist FOR ALL USING (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE POLICY "Users Read Notifications" ON notifications FOR SELECT USING (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE POLICY "Users Insert Feedback" ON feedback FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE POLICY "Users Insert Messages" ON contact_messages FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (true);
CREATE POLICY "Users Insert Leads" ON webinar_leads FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (true);
CREATE POLICY "Users Insert Subscribers" ON subscribers FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (true);
CREATE POLICY "Users Manage DMs" ON direct_messages FOR ALL USING (auth.uid() = sender_id OR auth.uid() = receiver_id);
CREATE POLICY "Users Manage Comments" ON comments FOR ALL USING (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE POLICY "Users Manage Lesson Chat" ON lesson_discussions FOR ALL USING (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE POLICY "Users Manage Reviews" ON reviews FOR ALL USING (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE POLICY "Users Manage Placement" ON placement_results FOR ALL USING (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE POLICY "Users Manage Referrals" ON referrals FOR ALL USING (auth.uid() = referrer_id);
CREATE POLICY "Users Insert Deletion" ON deletion_requests FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE POLICY "Users Insert Downloads" ON downloads_log FOR INSERT WITH CHECK (auth.uid() = user_id);
CREATE POLICY "Users Manage Homework" ON homework_submissions FOR ALL USING (auth.uid() = user_id);

-- ADMIN POLICIES (Simplified for dev: auth.role() = 'admin' check is better in prod)
CREATE POLICY "Admin Full Access Products" ON products FOR ALL USING (exists (select 1 from profiles where id = auth.uid() and role = 'admin'));
CREATE POLICY "Admin Full Access Lessons" ON lessons FOR ALL USING (exists (select 1 from profiles where id = auth.uid() and role = 'admin'));
CREATE POLICY "Admin Full Access Tutors" ON tutors FOR ALL USING (exists (select 1 from profiles where id = auth.uid() and role = 'admin'));
CREATE POLICY "Admin Full Access Posts" ON posts FOR ALL USING (exists (select 1 from profiles where id = auth.uid() and role = 'admin'));
CREATE POLICY "Admin Full Access Logs" ON admin_email_logs FOR ALL USING (exists (select 1 from profiles where id = auth.uid() and role = 'admin'));
CREATE POLICY "Admin View Leads" ON webinar_leads FOR SELECT USING (exists (select 1 from profiles where id = auth.uid() and role = 'admin'));
CREATE POLICY "Admin View Messages" ON contact_messages FOR SELECT USING (exists (select 1 from profiles where id = auth.uid() and role = 'admin'));
CREATE POLICY "Admin View Deletion" ON deletion_requests FOR SELECT USING (exists (select 1 from profiles where id = auth.uid() and role = 'admin'));
CREATE POLICY "Admin View Downloads" ON downloads_log FOR SELECT USING (exists (select 1 from profiles where id = auth.uid() and role = 'admin'));
CREATE POLICY "Admin View Homework" ON homework_submissions FOR ALL USING (exists (select 1 from profiles where id = auth.uid() and role = 'admin'));

# 🧪 Annyeong Academy - Master Test Plan

**Objective:** Verify that all functional requirements are met and the user experience is smooth before the official launch.
**Environment:** Localhost (`npm run dev`) connected to Supabase.

---

## 🛠️ Phase 0: Prerequisites
Before testing, ensure:
1.  [x] `.env.local` is configured with Supabase URL & Key.
2.  [x] You have run `supabase/MASTER_DB_SETUP.sql` in the Supabase SQL Editor.
3.  [x] Storage buckets (`avatars`, `course-materials`) are created and public.

---

## 1. 📢 Public Funnel (Unauthenticated)
*Goal: Ensure new users can land, read, and register.*

- [x] **Landing Page Load**: Open `http://localhost:5173/`. Ensure no white screen (AdBlocker check).
- [x] **Localization**: Click "HU" in the navbar. Verify text changes to Hungarian. Click "EN" to revert.
- [x] **Webinar Registration**:
    -   Fill out the form at the top.
    -   Leave "GDPR" unchecked -> Verify error message.
    -   Check "GDPR" and submit -> Verify Success Message ("Registration Confirmed").
    -   **Database Check**: Go to Supabase -> `webinar_leads` table. Ensure the row exists.
- [x] **Navigation**: Click links in Navbar (About, Teachers, Contact). Ensure pages load.
- [x] **Contact Form**: Go to `/contact`. Send a message. Verify success UI.

---

## 2. 🔐 Authentication & Security
*Goal: Ensure secure access and role management.*

- [x] **Sign Up**: Go to `/register`. Create a new account (e.g., `student@test.com`).
    -   Verify redirect to Login or Dashboard.
- [x] **Login**: Log out, then log back in.
- [x] **Forgot Password**: Go to `/forgot-password`. Enter email. (Note: Real email sending requires SMTP setup in Supabase, but check if UI feedback works).
- [x] **Protected Routes**:
    -   Log out.
    -   Try to access `http://localhost:5173/dashboard`.
    -   **Expected**: Redirected to `/login`.

---

## 3. 🎓 The Student Experience (LMS)
*Goal: Verify the core value proposition (learning).*

### Dashboard & Catalog
- [x] **Dashboard**: Ensure "Hello, [Name]" appears. Check if Gamification stats (Streak/XP) are visible.
- [x] **Course Catalog**: Go to "All Courses".
    -   **Search**: Type "Korean". List should filter.
    -   **Filter**: Select "Not Owned".
- [x] **Purchase Simulation**:
    -   Click "Enroll" on a course (e.g., "Korean for Beginners").
    -   Fill out the fake credit card form.
    -   **Expected**: Success animation -> Page reload -> Button changes to "Access Course".

### The Course Player
- [x] **Video Playback**: Open a video lesson. Ensure YouTube/Vimeo embed loads.
- [x] **Audio Player**: Open an audio lesson. Test Play/Pause and **Speed Control** (1.2x).
- [x] **Notes**:
    -   Type a note in the sidebar.
    -   Refresh the page.
    -   **Expected**: Note should persist (fetched from DB).
- [x] **Vocabulary Drill**:
    -   Flip a card.
    -   Click "Easy" or "Hard".
    -   Finish the deck -> Verify "Drill Complete" screen.
- [x] **Quiz**:
    -   Take a quiz (if the lesson has one).
    -   Pass the quiz.
    -   **Expected**: Confetti animation 🎉.
- [x] **Progress**:
    -   Click "Mark Complete".
    -   Verify progress bar in sidebar updates.
    -   Verify XP increases in the popup.
- [x] **Report Issue**: Click the warning icon. Send a bug report. Verify it appears in `contact_messages` table.

---

## 4. 🛍️ E-Commerce & Digital Goods
*Goal: Verify non-video product delivery.*

- [x] **Store**: Go to "Store & Ebooks".
- [x] **Buy Ebook**: Purchase "Survival Korean PDF".
- [x] **My Downloads**:
    -   Go to "My Downloads" page.
    -   Click "Download".
    -   **Expected**: It should open the link (or the dummy link provided in seed data).
- [x] **Live Lessons**:
    -   Go to "Live Lessons".
    -   Check if "Date" and "Time" are displayed correctly.
    -   Purchase a spot.
    -   **Expected**: "Book Spot" changes to "Join Zoom".

---

## 5. 🏆 Engagement & Profile
*Goal: Verify retention features.*

- [x] **Leaderboard**: Go to `/leaderboard`. Ensure your user is listed with correct XP.
- [x] **My Notebook**: Go to `/notebook`. Ensure notes taken in Course Player appear here aggregated.
- [x] **Profile Settings**:
    -   Upload an Avatar image.
    -   Change Display Name.
    -   Save.
    -   Refresh page to ensure persistence.
- [x] **Invoice**: In Profile -> Order History, click the Download icon. Verify a PDF is generated.

---

## 6. 🛡️ Admin Panel (CMS)
*Goal: Verify content management.*

**Setup**:
1. Go to Supabase -> Table `profiles`.
2. Find your user row.
3. Change `role` column from `student` to `admin`.
4. Refresh the app.

**Tests**:
- [x] **Access**: Verify "Admin Area" appears in Sidebar.
- [x] **Stats**: Check if the top cards show correct numbers (Users, Sales, etc.).
- [x] **Create Product**:
    -   Go to Products tab.
    -   Create a new Course.
    -   Upload a cover image.
    -   Save.
- [x] **Create Lesson**:
    -   Go to Lessons tab.
    -   Select the course you just made.
    -   Add a video URL.
    -   Add Quiz JSON: `[{"question":"Test?","options":["Yes","No"],"correct":0}]`
    -   Save.
- [x] **Announcements**:
    -   Post a new announcement (e.g., "Maintenance tonight").
    -   Go to Dashboard as a student.
    -   **Expected**: Banner appears at the top of the screen.
- [x] **Manage Users**:
    -   Go to Students tab.
    -   Find a user.
    -   Manually enroll them in a course.

---

## 7. 🐛 Technical & Edge Cases
- [x] **AdBlocker Test**: Enable uBlock Origin / AdBlock Plus. Refresh. **Ensure the page does NOT crash** (White screen test).
- [x] **Mobile View**: Open Chrome DevTools (F12) -> Toggle Device Toolbar (Mobile).
    -   Check Hamburger menu functionality.
    -   Check Video Player responsiveness.
- [x] **404 Page**: Go to `http://localhost:5173/random-page-xyz`. Verify "Page Not Found" design.
- [x] **Profile picture**: Upload a different profile picture

### 3. SECTION: Method (The Solution)

You will pick up the natural rhythm and intonation of the language, learning exactly how native speakers actually talk in Seoul.
*   **💬 Real-Time Translation**
    > Don't worry about getting lost or missing a detail! Every explanation appears instantly on your screen as translated subtitles in real-time. It feels just like watching your favorite series with subs, bridging the gap between you and the teacher so you catch every single nuance and funny moment.

# 1. CREATE SKELETON COMPONENTS
cat << 'EOF' > src/components/ui/Skeletons.jsx
export function Skeleton({ className }) {
  return (
    <div className={`animate-pulse bg-gray-200 rounded-lg ${className}`}></div>
  )
}

export function CourseCardSkeleton() {
  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border border-gray-100 shadow-sm h-full flex flex-col">
      <div className="h-48 bg-gray-200 animate-pulse relative"></div>
      <div className="p-6 flex-1 flex flex-col gap-3">
        <Skeleton className="h-6 w-3/4" />
        <Skeleton className="h-4 w-1/2" />
        <div className="flex-1"></div>
        <div className="flex justify-between items-center mt-4 border-t border-gray-50 pt-4">
          <Skeleton className="h-8 w-24 rounded-lg" />
          <Skeleton className="h-8 w-8 rounded-lg" />
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}

export function BlogCardSkeleton() {
  return (
    <div className="bg-white rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border border-gray-100 shadow-sm h-full flex flex-col">
      <div className="h-48 bg-gray-200 animate-pulse"></div>
      <div className="p-6 flex flex-col gap-3">
        <div className="flex gap-2">
          <Skeleton className="h-5 w-16 rounded-full" />
          <Skeleton className="h-5 w-16 rounded-full" />
        </div>
        <Skeleton className="h-6 w-full" />
        <Skeleton className="h-4 w-full" />
        <Skeleton className="h-4 w-2/3" />
      </div>
    </div>
  )
}
EOF

import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase' 
import Navbar from '@/components/sections/Navbar'
import Footer from '@/components/sections/Footer'
import { ArrowLeft, Calendar, MessageCircle, Send, Loader2 } from 'lucide-react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import ShareButton from '@/components/ui/ShareButton'

// Dynamic Metadata
export async function generateMetadata({ params }) {
  const { slug } = params
  return {
    title: `Blog Post | Annyeong Academy`, 
    description: 'Learn Korean culture and language tips.',
  }
}

"use client";
import { useState, useEffect } from 'react'
import { useParams } from 'next/navigation'
import { Skeleton } from '@/components/ui/Skeletons'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'

export default function BlogPost() {
  const { slug } = useParams()
  const { user, profile } = useAuth()
  const [post, setPost] = useState(null)
  const [comments, setComments] = useState([])
  const [newComment, setNewComment] = useState('')
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    if(slug) {
      fetchPost()
      fetchComments()
    }
  }, [slug])

  const fetchPost = async () => {
    const { data } = await supabase.from('posts').select('*').eq('slug', slug).single()
    setPost(data)
    setLoading(false)
  }

  const fetchComments = async () => {
    const { data } = await supabase.from('comments').select('*, profiles(full_name, avatar_url)').eq('post_slug', slug).order('created_at', { ascending: false })
    setComments(data || [])
  }

  const handlePostComment = async (e) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    if (!user) return alert("Please login to comment")
    if (!newComment.trim()) return
    setSubmitting(true)
    await supabase.from('comments').insert([{ post_slug: slug, user_id: user.id, content: newComment }])
    setNewComment('')
    fetchComments()
    setSubmitting(false)
  }

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-[#F8F9FC]">
        <Navbar />
        <main className="flex-grow pt-32 pb-20 px-4">
          <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto space-y-8">
            <Skeleton className="h-96 w-full rounded-3xl" />
            <div className="space-y-4">
                <Skeleton className="h-10 w-3/4" />
                <Skeleton className="h-4 w-full" />
                <Skeleton className="h-4 w-full" />
                <Skeleton className="h-4 w-2/3" />
            </div>
          </div>
        </main>
        <Footer />
      </div>
    )
  }

  if (!post) return <div className="min-h-screen flex items-center justify-center">Post not found</div>

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col bg-[#F8F9FC]">
      <Navbar />
      <main className="flex-grow pt-32 pb-20 px-4">
        <article className="max-w-3xl mx-auto bg-white rounded-3xl shadow-sm border border-gray-100 overflow-hidden animate-fade-in">
          <div className="h-64 md:h-96 bg-gray-200">
            <img src={post.image_url || 'https://placehold.co/800x400'} alt={post.title} className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
          </div>
          <div className="p-8 md:p-12">
            <Link href="/blog" className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-gray-400 hover:text-brand-purple text-sm mb-6 font-bold"><ArrowLeft size={16}/> Back to Blog</Link>
            <div className="flex gap-2 mb-4">
               {post.tags?.map(t => <span key={t} className="text-xs font-bold text-brand-blue bg-blue-50 px-2 py-1 rounded-md uppercase tracking-wider">{t}</span>)}
            </div>
            <h1 className="text-3xl md:text-4xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-6 leading-tight">{post.title}</h1>
            <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-4 text-sm text-gray-500 mb-8 border-b border-gray-100 pb-8">
              <span className="flex items-center gap-2"><Calendar size={16}/> {new Date(post.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
              <ShareButton title={post.title} text={post.excerpt} />
            </div>
            <div className="prose prose-lg text-gray-600 max-w-none">
              {post.content.split('\n').map((p, i) => <p key={i} className="mb-4">{p}</p>)}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="bg-gray-50 p-8 md:p-12 border-t border-gray-100">
            <h3 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-6 flex items-center gap-2"><MessageCircle /> Comments ({comments.length})</h3>
            {user ? (
              <form onSubmit={handlePostComment} className="mb-10">
                <div className="flex gap-4">
                  <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-white rounded-full overflow-hidden flex-shrink-0 border border-gray-200">
                    <img src={profile?.avatar_url || `https://placehold.co/100?text=${profile?.full_name?.[0]}`} className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
                  </div>
                  <div className="flex-1">
                    <textarea className="w-full p-4 rounded-xl border border-gray-200 outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-brand-purple bg-white text-sm" rows="3" placeholder="Share your thoughts..." value={newComment} onChange={e => setNewComment(e.target.value)}></textarea>
                    <button disabled={submitting} className="mt-2 bg-brand-purple text-white px-6 py-2 rounded-lg font-bold text-sm hover:opacity-90 transition flex items-center gap-2">
                      {submitting ? <Loader2 size={16} className="animate-spin"/> : <><Send size={16}/> Post Comment</>}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>
              </form>
            ) : (
              <div className="bg-white p-6 rounded-xl text-center border border-gray-200 mb-10">
                <p className="text-gray-500 mb-4">Please log in to join the conversation.</p>
                <Link href="/login" className="text-brand-purple font-bold hover:underline">Login Now</Link>
              </div>
            )}
            <div className="space-y-6">
              {comments.map(comment => (
                <div key={comment.id} className="flex gap-4">
                  <div className="w-10 h-10 bg-gray-200 rounded-full overflow-hidden flex-shrink-0">
                    <img src={comment.profiles?.avatar_url || `https://placehold.co/100?text=${comment.profiles?.full_name?.[0] || 'U'}`} className="w-full h-full object-cover" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
                      <span className="font-bold text-gray-800 text-sm">{comment.profiles?.full_name || 'User'}</span>
                      <span className="text-xs text-gray-400">• {new Date(comment.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-gray-600 text-sm leading-relaxed">{comment.content}</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </article>
      </main>
      <Footer />
    </div>
  )
}
EOF

# 3. UPDATE COURSE CATALOG WITH PAGINATION
# Quoting path to handle parentheses safely
cat << 'EOF' > "src/app/(app)/corsi/page.jsx"
"use client";
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { useSearchParams } from 'next/navigation'
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase'
import { useAuth } from '@/context/AuthContext'
import { usePayment } from '@/context/PaymentContext'
import { useLanguage } from '@/context/LanguageContext'
import { Video, Loader2, PlayCircle, ShoppingCart, Search, X, Star, Filter, User, ChevronLeft, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react'
import Link from 'next/link'
import { cleanDescription } from '@/lib/helpers'
import WishlistButton from '@/components/ui/WishlistButton'
import { CourseCardSkeleton } from '@/components/ui/Skeletons'

export default function Courses() {
  const { user } = useAuth()
  const { t } = useLanguage()
  const { initiateCheckout } = usePayment()
  const searchParams = useSearchParams()
  
  const [courses, setCourses] = useState([])
  const [tutors, setTutors] = useState([])
  const [purchasedIds, setPurchasedIds] = useState([])
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true)
  
  // Filters & Pagination
  const [searchTerm, setSearchTerm] = useState('')
  const [ownershipFilter, setOwnershipFilter] = useState('all')
  const [levelFilter, setLevelFilter] = useState('all')
  const [tutorFilter, setTutorFilter] = useState('all')
  const [sortOrder, setSortOrder] = useState('popular')
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1)
  const ITEMS_PER_PAGE = 6

  useEffect(() => {
    const levelParam = searchParams.get('level')
    if (levelParam) setLevelFilter(levelParam)
  }, [searchParams])

  useEffect(() => { fetchData() }, [user])

  const fetchData = async () => {
    try {
      const { data: prods } = await supabase.from('products').select('*, tutors(id, name)').eq('type', 'course')
      setCourses(prods || [])
      
      const { data: tuts } = await supabase.from('tutors').select('id, name')
      setTutors(tuts || [])

      if (user) {
        const { data: purchases } = await supabase.from('purchases').select('product_id').eq('user_id', user.id).eq('status', 'completed')
        if (purchases) setPurchasedIds(purchases.map(p => p.product_id))
      }
    } catch (error) { console.error(error) } finally { setLoading(false) }
  }

  const getPriceVal = (p) => parseFloat(p.replace(/[^0-9.]/g, '') || 0)

  // Client-side filtering because DB is small for demo. 
  const filteredCourses = courses
    .filter(course => {
      const matchesSearch = course.title.toLowerCase().includes(searchTerm.toLowerCase())
      const isOwned = purchasedIds.includes(course.id)
      
      const matchOwn = ownershipFilter === 'all' 
        || (ownershipFilter === 'owned' && isOwned) 
        || (ownershipFilter === 'not_owned' && !isOwned)
      
      const matchLevel = levelFilter === 'all' || course.level?.toLowerCase() === levelFilter.toLowerCase()
      const matchTutor = tutorFilter === 'all' || course.tutor_id === tutorFilter

      return matchesSearch && matchOwn && matchLevel && matchTutor
    })
    .sort((a, b) => {
      if (sortOrder === 'price_asc') return getPriceVal(a.price) - getPriceVal(b.price)
      if (sortOrder === 'price_desc') return getPriceVal(b.price) - getPriceVal(a.price)
      if (sortOrder === 'newest') return new Date(b.created_at || 0) - new Date(a.created_at || 0)
      return 0 
    })

  const totalPages = Math.ceil(filteredCourses.length / ITEMS_PER_PAGE)
  const displayedCourses = filteredCourses.slice((page - 1) * ITEMS_PER_PAGE, page * ITEMS_PER_PAGE)

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="flex flex-col md:flex-row justify-between items-end md:items-center mb-8 gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-dark mb-2">{t('course_catalog')}</h1>
          <p className="text-gray-500">{t('course_desc')}</p>
        </div>
        <div className="relative w-full md:w-64">
          <Search className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 transform -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400" size={18} />
          <input 
            type="text" placeholder={t('course_search')}
            className="w-full pl-10 pr-10 py-2 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl outline-none focus:border-brand-purple transition"
            value={searchTerm} onChange={(e) => setSearchTerm(e.target.value)}
          />
          {searchTerm && <button onClick={() => setSearchTerm('')} className="absolute right-3 top-1/2 transform -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400 hover:text-gray-600"><X size={14}/></button>}
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Advanced Filters Bar */}
      <div className="bg-white p-4 rounded-xl border border-gray-100 shadow-sm mb-8 flex flex-col md:flex-row gap-4 overflow-x-auto">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 min-w-max">
          <Filter size={16} className="text-brand-purple"/> <span className="font-bold text-sm text-gray-700">Filters:</span>
        </div>
        
        <select className="filter-select" value={ownershipFilter} onChange={e => setOwnershipFilter(e.target.value)}>
          <option value="all">All Ownership</option>
          <option value="owned">My Courses</option>
          <option value="not_owned">Available to Buy</option>
        </select>

        <select className="filter-select" value={levelFilter} onChange={e => setLevelFilter(e.target.value)}>
          <option value="all">All Levels</option>
          <option value="Beginner">Beginner</option>
          <option value="Intermediate">Intermediate</option>
          <option value="Advanced">Advanced</option>
        </select>

        <select className="filter-select" value={tutorFilter} onChange={e => setTutorFilter(e.target.value)}>
          <option value="all">All Tutors</option>
          {tutors.map(t => <option key={t.id} value={t.id}>{t.name}</option>)}
        </select>

        {/* Sorting */}
        <select className="filter-select md:ml-auto" value={sortOrder} onChange={e => setSortOrder(e.target.value)}>
          <option value="popular">Most Popular</option>
          <option value="newest">Newest</option>
          <option value="price_asc">Price: Low to High</option>
          <option value="price_desc">Price: High to Low</option>
        </select>
      </div>

      {loading ? (
        <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
          {[1,2,3,4,5,6].map(i => <CourseCardSkeleton key={i} />)}
        </div>
      ) : filteredCourses.length === 0 ? (
        <div className="text-center py-12 bg-white rounded-xl border border-dashed border-gray-300">
          <p className="text-gray-500 font-medium">{t('course_no_results')}</p>
          <button onClick={() => {setSearchTerm(''); setLevelFilter('all'); setOwnershipFilter('all'); setTutorFilter('all')}} className="text-brand-purple font-bold mt-2 hover:underline">Clear Filters</button>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <>
          <div className="grid md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6">
            {displayedCourses.map(course => {
              const isOwned = purchasedIds.includes(course.id)
              return (
                <div key={course.id} className={`bg-white rounded-2xl overflow-hidden border transition group flex flex-col relative ${isOwned ? 'border-green-200 shadow-sm' : 'border-gray-100 hover:shadow-md'}`}>
                  <div className="h-48 overflow-hidden relative bg-gray-100">
                    <img src={course.image_url || 'https://placehold.co/600x400/6a11cb/ffffff?text=Course'} alt={course.title} className="w-full h-full object-cover group-hover:scale-105 transition duration-500" />
                    <div className="absolute top-4 right-4 bg-white/90 backdrop-blur px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs font-bold text-brand-dark flex items-center gap-1">
                      <Video size={12} className="text-blue-500" /> {course.level || 'All Levels'}
                    </div>
                    <div className="absolute top-4 left-4">
                      <WishlistButton productId={course.id} />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  
                  <div className="p-6 flex-1 flex flex-col">
                    <div className="mb-2">
                      <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-brand-dark line-clamp-1">{course.title}</h3>
                      {course.tutors && <p className="text-xs text-gray-500 flex items-center gap-1 mt-1"><User size={12}/> {course.tutors.name}</p>}
                    </div>
                    <p className="text-sm text-gray-500 mb-4 line-clamp-2 flex-1">{cleanDescription(course.description)}</p>
                    <div className="mt-4 pt-4 border-t border-gray-50 flex items-center justify-between">
                      {isOwned ? (
                        <Link href={`/corsi/${course.id}`} className="w-full bg-green-600 text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold hover:bg-green-700 transition flex items-center justify-center gap-2">
                          <PlayCircle size={16} /> {t('course_access')}
                        </Link>
                      ) : (
                        <>
                          <span className="text-2xl font-bold text-brand-purple">{course.price}</span>
                          <button onClick={() => initiateCheckout(course)} className="bg-brand-dark text-white px-4 py-2 rounded-lg text-sm font-bold hover:bg-gray-800 transition flex items-center gap-2">
                            <ShoppingCart size={16}/> {t('course_enroll')}
                          </button>
                        </>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>
              )
            })}
          </div>

          {/* Pagination */}
          {totalPages > 1 && (
            <div className="flex justify-center items-center gap-4 mt-12">
                <button 
                    disabled={page === 1} 
                    onClick={() => setPage(p => p - 1)} 
                    className="p-3 bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-full hover:bg-gray-50 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transition shadow-sm"
                >
                    <ChevronLeft size={20} />
                </button>
                <span className="font-bold text-gray-600 text-sm">Page {page} of {totalPages}</span>
                <button 
                    disabled={page === totalPages} 
                    onClick={() => setPage(p => p + 1)} 
                    className="p-3 bg-white border border-gray-200 rounded-full hover:bg-gray-50 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-not-allowed transition shadow-sm"
                >
                    <ChevronRight size={20} />
                </button>
            </div>
          )}
        </>
      )}
      
      <style jsx>{`
        .filter-select {
          @apply px-4 py-2 border border-gray-200 rounded-xl outline-none bg-white text-gray-600 text-sm font-bold focus:border-brand-purple cursor-pointer;
        }
      `}</style>
    </div>
  )
}
